### [1] [Data-driven prediction of atomic-oxygen erosion yield in polymers from bulk physical properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07547)
*Youngwoo Choi,Doeun Koo,Seungbum Hong,Hyun Jung Kim*

Main category: physics.space-ph

TL;DR: 该研究评估了一个基于 MISSE-2 任务 37 种聚合物训练的回归集成模型（regression ensemble）对原子氧（atomic oxygen, AO）侵蚀的预测能力；其对 9 种后 MISSE-2 聚合物的 R^2(log) 为 -0.037，但所有预测均在两倍因子内，与训练集算术均值基线（arithmetic-mean baseline）表现相当，提示目标范围狭窄导致判别力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低地球轨道（low Earth orbit）中原子氧会侵蚀聚合物表面，但相关耐久性数据有限，亟需评估现有轨道数据筛选模型的泛化能力与可靠性。

Method: 采用基于 MISSE-2 的 37 种聚合物训练的回归集成模型，输入为 8 个选定体相性质（bulk properties）和 5 个元素组成比（elemental-composition ratios）；对 9 种后 MISSE-2 聚合物、4 个 PLAVIS 牌号及一种成分未公开的未知聚合物进行预测，并与地面测量对比。

Result: 归档的留一法（leave-one-out）R^2(log) 为 0.768，但该估计以使用完整训练表进行特征选择为条件；对 9 种后 MISSE-2 聚合物 R^2(log) = -0.037，全部预测在两倍因子内，而算术均值基线同样满足该容差。对未知聚合物的预测侵蚀产率（erosion yield）与地面测量相差在 1.92 倍以内。

Conclusion: PLAVIS 测量记录相互矛盾，无法给出确定的残余-侵蚀排序或地面验证评分；热残余（thermal residue）、SEM 对比度（SEM contrast）与表面化学（surface chemistry）提供了不同观测证据，但未确立保护层机制。结果支持在测量记录经协调、输入前瞻性固定的条件下进一步评估轨道数据筛选模型。

Abstract: Atomic oxygen (AO) erodes polymer surfaces in low Earth orbit, but durability data are limited. We evaluated a regression ensemble trained on 37 MISSE-2 polymers using eight selected bulk properties and five elemental-composition ratios. Its archived leave-one-out R^2(log) was 0.768; this estimate is conditional on feature selection using the complete training table. For nine post-MISSE-2 polymers, the ensemble gave R^2(log) = -0.037 and all predictions were within a factor of two. The training arithmetic-mean baseline also met that tolerance for all nine, highlighting the limited discrimination provided by their narrow target range. Ground-based comparisons included four PLAVIS grades. For an out-of-training mystery polymer with undisclosed composition, the predicted erosion yield was within a factor of 1.92 of the ground-based measurement. Conflicting PLAVIS measurement records prevent a definitive residue-erosion ordering or ground-validation score. Thermal residue, SEM contrast and surface chemistry provide distinct observational evidence; they do not establish a protective-layer mechanism. The results support further evaluation of orbital-data screening models with reconciled measurement records and prospectively fixed inputs..

</details>

### [2] [Gaussian free field with logarithmic singularities and reduced Green's energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07272)
*Mingchang Liu,Titus Lupu,Hao Wu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文研究多边形域（polygonal domains）中带对数奇点（logarithmic singularities）的高斯自由场（Gaussian Free Field）的等值线。奇点情形下的等值线律相对无奇点情形绝对连续，其 Radon-Nikodym 导数与约化格林能量（reduced Green's energy）相关，并可由含布朗气泡（Brownian bubbles）与布朗桥（Brownian bridges）的泊松点过程表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将无奇点情形下已建立的等值线结果推广到非零奇点情形，并刻画奇点对等值线律及局部渐近行为的影响。作者还试图建立与共形场论（conformal field theory）中顶点算子（vertex operators）相关量的联系。

Method: 方法以多边形域中带对数奇点的高斯自由场为对象，将奇点情形与无奇点情形进行对比。分析依赖 F. Viklund 与 D. W. Nyström [VN24] 引入的约化格林能量，并借助布朗运动（Brownian motion）表示刻画等值线：无奇点情形涉及布朗环（Brownian loops）与布朗游弋（Brownian excursions）的泊松点过程，奇点情形额外涉及布朗气泡与布朗桥的泊松点过程。

Result: 主要结果为：奇点情形下等值线律对无奇点情形律绝对连续，Radon-Nikodym 导数由约化格林能量给出。一个或两个奇点的特殊情形给出共形半径（conformal radius）的矩母函数（moment-generating functions），可解释为自由玻色子点（free-boson point）处共形场论中顶点算子的单点与两点关联函数。虽然 Radon-Nikodym 导数在奇点附近有界远离零，等值线在奇点附近的渐近行为与无奇点情形显著不同，所有等值线保持接近奇点的渐近概率不同。

Conclusion: 结论表明奇点虽仅通过有界的 Radon-Nikodym 导数改变整体测度结构，却显著改变等值线近奇点的局部渐近概率。等值线可用含布朗气泡与布朗桥的布朗运动表示统一描述，且一二奇点情形与共形场论顶点算子关联函数存在对应。

Abstract: We analyze the level lines of the Gaussian Free Field with logarithmic singularities in polygonal domains. We focus on the (none-zero) singular case and compare it to the singular-free setting. The law of the level lines for the field in singular case is absolutely continuous with respect to the law for the singular-free case, with a Radon-Nikodym derivative related to the reduced Green's energy which is introduced by F. Viklund and D. W. Nyström~[VN24]. These level lines admit a Brownian motion representation. In the singular-free case, such representation involves Poisson point processes of Brownian loops and excursions; while for the singular case, it additionally includes Poisson point processes of Brownian bubbles and bridges. The special cases of one or two singularities yield moment-generating functions of the conformal radius, which can be interpreted as one-point and two-point correlation functions of vertex operators in conformal field theory at the free-boson point. While the Radon-Nikodym derivative is bounded away from the singularities, the behavior of the level lines near the singularities is markedly different from the singular-free case, with different asymptotic probabilities for all level lines to remain close to the singularity.

</details>


### [3] [Shape theorem for excited random walks on the integer lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07724)
*Tuan-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文研究 $\mathbb Z^d$（$d\geq2$）上"朝中心激发的随机游走（random walk excited to the center）"其访问顶点集合的极限形状，证明经 $n^{-1/(d+1)}$ 缩放后在 Hausdorff 距离下依概率收敛到一个显式的 $\ell^1$ 球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在回答 Kozma（2007）提出的问题，即确定此类含"饼干（cookie）"机制的随机游走其访问范围（range）的极限形状。该模型刻画了首次访问非原点顶点时带朝原点偏置、之后恢复无偏的游走行为。

Method: 模型设定：每个非零顶点放置一个"饼干"；游走者首次访问该顶点时消耗饼干，并以朝原点（origin）的偏置选择下一步；访问原点或再次访问非零顶点时无饼干可用，转为均匀随机移动到邻居。在此基础上分析到时刻 $n$ 所访问顶点集合在 $n^{-1/(d+1)}$ 缩放下的渐近形态。

Result: 证明到时刻 $n$ 访问的顶点集合，经 $n^{-1/(d+1)}$ 重标度（rescaled）后，在 Hausdorff 距离（Hausdorff distance）意义下依概率（in probability）收敛到一个显式的 $\ell^1$ 球（$\ell^1$ ball）。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了朝中心激发随机游走访问范围的极限形状，并由此回答了 Kozma（2007）提出的公开问题。

Abstract: We study the limit shape of the range of the random walk excited to the center on $\mathbb Z^d$, with $d\geq2$. A cookie is placed at each nonzero vertex. On its first visit to such a vertex, the walker consumes the cookie and takes its next nearest-neighbor step with a bias toward the origin. On a visit to the origin, or on later visits to a nonzero vertex, no cookie is available and the walker moves to a neighbor uniformly at random. We prove that the set of vertices visited up to time $n$, rescaled by $n^{-1/(d+1)}$, converges in probability in Hausdorff distance to an explicit $\ell^1$ ball. This answers a question raised by Kozma (2007).

</details>


### [4] [Infinite-mean Galton-Watson trees have zero random-interchange threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08149)
*Andreas Klippel,Benjamin Lees,Christian Mönch*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在后代分布几乎必然有限且后代均值无穷的Galton-Watson树上，随机交换过程（random interchange process）关于无限环（infinite cycles）的临界参数为零（条件于存活）。结合arXiv:2503.03319中有限均值的严格不等式，得到后代均值在(1,∞]的局部有限Galton-Watson树的二分法：无穷均值情形下环临界参数与链临界参数恰好相等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在刻画Galton-Watson树上随机交换过程的临界行为，特别是环（loop）与链（link）临界参数之间的关系。已有工作（arXiv:2503.03319）给出有限均值下的严格不等式，本文填补无穷均值这一端的情形，以形成完整的二分法（dichotomy）图景。

Method: 证明思路是将树上的问题归约（reduce）为一个有限置换估计（finite permutation estimate）。该估计由随机曲面（random surfaces）的剥皮估计（peeling estimates）导出，从而把树模型与随机曲面的组合/几何工具联系起来。

Result: 在几乎必然有限后代且无限后代均值的Galton-Watson树上，条件于存活时，随机交换过程对无限环的临界参数为零。与有限均值的严格不等式结果合并后，得到：对后代均值属于(1,∞]的局部有限Galton-Watson树，环与链的临界参数在无穷均值情形下完全一致，构成二分性结论。

Conclusion: 无穷后代均值下无限环的临界参数为零，与有限均值情形形成鲜明对比，二者共同刻画了局部有限Galton-Watson树上随机交换过程的临界参数二分法。证明表明树问题可通过有限置换估计与随机曲面剥皮估计建立联系。

Abstract: We prove that, on a Galton-Watson tree with almost surely finite offspring and infinite offspring mean, the random interchange process has critical parameter zero for infinite cycles, conditionally on survival. Combined with the finite-mean strict inequality established in \emph{arXiv:2503.03319}, this gives a dichotomy for locally finite Galton-Watson trees with offspring mean in $(1,\infty]$: the loop and link critical parameters coincide exactly in the infinite-mean case. The proof reduces the tree problem to a finite permutation estimate derived from peeling estimates for random surfaces.

</details>


### [5] [A Program for Fluctuating Kinetic Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08247)
*Zhengyan Wu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文提出涨落动理学理论框架，用广义梯度结构统一处理VFP、Landau与Boltzmann方程的非线性Lévy型涨落修正，并推导主涨落动理学方程及多种应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有涨落-耗散关系基于线性Onsager结构与经典涨落-耗散定理，难以统一描述动理学PDE中的非线性Lévy型涨落修正。

Method: 将VFP、Landau、Boltzmann方程分解为动理学输运项与由非线性对偶耗散势生成的广义梯度结构，提出非线性Lévy型涨落-耗散关系；在正则倾斜类上比较广义梯度结构的典型作用量与所得SPDE的Freidlin-Wentzell型作用量；并给出对偶耗散势的充分条件。

Result: 两者作用量一致，支持新涨落-耗散关系并导出主涨落动理学方程；中心累积量具Lévy-Khintchine表示，Lévy流可由高斯白噪声与补偿Poisson随机测度实现；导出α-稳定Lévy型Dean-Kawasaki方程；涨落Boltzmann方程在特定标度下其Hamiltonian收敛到Landau-Lifshitz-Navier-Stokes方程的Hamiltonian。

Conclusion: 该框架为动理学的非线性涨落修正提供了统一的热力学与随机分析基础，并揭示了动理学涨落与涨落流体力学之间的标度关系。

Abstract: A program for fluctuating kinetic theory is proposed in this paper to study nonlinear Lévy-type fluctuation corrections to kinetic PDEs in a unified way. The Vlasov--Fokker--Planck (VFP), Landau, and Boltzmann equations can each be decomposed into a kinetic transport term and a generalized gradient structure generated by a nonlinear dual pair of dissipation potentials. Going beyond the linear Onsager structure and the classical fluctuation--dissipation relation, we propose a new nonlinear Lévy-type fluctuation--dissipation relation that identifies the thermal fluctuations associated with this generalized gradient structure. On a regular tilted class, the canonical action of the generalized gradient structure agrees with the Freidlin--Wentzell-type action predicted by the resulting SPDE. This consistency gives thermodynamic support for the new nonlinear fluctuation--dissipation relation and leads to a master fluctuating kinetic equation. We further establish a sufficient condition on the nonlinear dual dissipation potential under which its centered cumulant admits a Lévy--Khintchine representation, and show that the resulting Lévy current can be realized through Gaussian white noise and a compensated Poisson random measure. As an application of this framework, an \(α\)-stable Lévy-type Dean-Kawasaki equation is derived. Furthermore, by rescaling the fluctuating Boltzmann equation under a distinguished relation between the noise intensity and the hydrodynamic scaling parameter, we prove that its Hamiltonian converges to the Hamiltonian of the Landau--Lifshitz--Navier--Stokes equations. This reveals a scaling relation between kinetic fluctuations and fluctuating hydrodynamics.

</details>


### [6] [Uniform Bessel bounds for endpoint crossings in the persistent random walk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06935)
*Arjun Pemmasani*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 研究高尔顿板（Galton board）中弹球方向带有持续概率 $p$ 的相依性模型，确定使中间箱与端箱概率相等的 $p$ 值。结果显示 $N$ 次反弹时平局对应平均约 $\log N$ 次转向，并给出对所有 $N\ge 2$ 显式的平局区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题由 Kagey 提出：弹球首次反弹方向公平，之后每次以概率 $p$ 重复前一次方向，需找出使中间箱与两端箱等概率的 $p$。此前的结论仅针对大 $N$ 的渐近情形，缺少对每个具体 $N$ 的精确刻画。

Method: 将中心箱概率与贝塞尔函数（Bessel function）比较，并给出对所有 $N$ 显式成立的误差界；对 $N\ge 175$ 采用解析方法，对较小的 $N$ 使用整数算术精确计算，并在 Lean 中交叉验证。此外分析其他各箱与端箱的平局以及周期边界（periodic boundary）造成的偏移。

Result: 对 $N$ 次反弹，平局出现在弹球平均约转向 $\log N$ 次时；由此得到每个 $N\ge 2$ 的显式平局区间，而非仅大 $N$ 的渐近结果。同时给出其余各箱与端箱的平局条件及周期边界导致的偏移。

Conclusion: 方向相依的高尔顿板模型其平局行为可由转向次数的对数标度刻画，并通过贝塞尔函数逼近与形式化验证获得对所有有限 $N$ 均成立的显式结果，推广了原先仅适用于大 $N$ 的结论。

Abstract: A ball bounces down a Galton board, where its first bounce's direction is fair while every later bounce repeats the previous bounce's direction with probability $p$, all the way down to some bin. We ask for which $p$ the middle bin is exactly as likely as the end bins, a question originally posed by Kagey. We find that for $N$ bounces, the tie occurs when the ball changes direction (turns) approximately $\log N$ times on average. We compare the central bin with a Bessel function, with an error bound that is explicit for all $N$. This yields an explicit interval for the tie at every $N \geq 2$, rather than just for large $N$. This is done analytically for $N \geq 175$, and for smaller $N$ exactly by integer arithmetic, cross-verified in Lean. We also find the tie of every other bin with the end bins and the shift caused by periodic boundaries.

</details>

### [7] [(Why) Jets quantise trapped acoustic modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07414)
*Brandon Yeung,Michael N. Stavropoulos,Vincent Jaunet,Peter Jordan,Oliver T. Schmidt*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究通过约十万个纹影（schlieren）快照与 LES 数据，结合 SPOD 分析确认高亚声速湍流射流势核中束缚声模态由约 O(10) 个离散频率组成，并给出 St、x、k 的量化规律与驻波共振模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亚声速湍流射流势核会束缚声模态（trapped acoustic modes），其频率位于 Strouhal 数（Strouhal number, St）宽度为 O(0.1) 的窄带内。此前有假设认为每个频带由约 O(10) 个间隔 ΔSt ∼ O(0.01) 的离散频率构成，本文旨在验证该假设并解释其频率量化机制。

Method: 针对马赫数 0.9、雷诺数 10^6 的射流，采集约 O(100 000) 个专用纹影实验与 LES 快照，应用谱本征正交分解（spectral proper orthogonal decomposition, SPOD）。SPOD 算法使用正弦窗（sine tapers）并施加射流的旋转与反射对称性；随后用局部、全局、模态与非模态稳定性理论（modal and non-modal stability theories）预测束缚模态。

Result: 纹影与 LES 中束缚的 SPOD 模态在 St-x 与 St-k 平面内均有界（x 为轴向坐标，k 为波数），该边界使模态在 St、x、k 上被量化：St 中第 n_x 个模态沿 x 与 k 具有 n_x 个波腹。稳定性理论分析显示模态与非模态稳定性之间存在竞争；仅由理论边界构建的同相驻波共振模型可预测量化的全局模态频率。

Conclusion: 研究证实了窄带由约 O(10) 个离散频率组成的假设，并表明束缚模态的频率量化可由理论边界与同相驻波共振（in-phase standing-wave resonance）模型解释，体现了模态与非模态稳定性机制的共同作用。

Abstract: High-subsonic turbulent jets trap acoustic modes in their potential core. The modes lie in narrow frequency bands that are $O(0.1)$-wide in Strouhal number, $St$. By studying a Mach 0.9, Reynolds number $10^6$ jet, this work confirms an earlier hypothesis: each band is made up of $O(10)$ discrete frequencies, $ΔSt \sim O(0.01)$ apart. To resolve them, we collect $O(100\,000)$ snapshots from dedicated schlieren experiments and large-eddy simulations (LES), and apply spectral proper orthogonal decomposition (SPOD). Our SPOD algorithm uses sine tapers and enforces the jet's rotational and reflectional symmetries. For both the schlieren and the LES, the trapped SPOD modes are bounded in the $St$-$x$ and $St$-$k$ planes, where $x$ and $k$ are respectively the axial coordinate and wavenumber. The bounds quantise the modes in $St$, $x$, and $k$, such that the $n_x$th mode in $St$ has $n_x$ antinodes along $x$ and $k$. We then predict trapped modes with local, global, modal, and non-modal stability theories. We find evidence of a competition between modal and non-modal stability. From only the theoretical bounds, we build a simple in-phase standing-wave resonance model that predicts the quantised global-mode frequencies.

</details>

### [8] [Relaxation of a Vlasov gas to an inhomogeneous state due to phase space mixing in an axisymmetric potential: A Newtonian analogy of the Kerr orbital motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06913)
*Ana Laura Garc\a'ia-Perciante,Merce Guerrero,Daraío Naúañez,Olivier Sarbach*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该工作研究无碰撞 Vlasov 气体在外轴对称势中的演化：初始均匀各向同性位形会弛豫为在两势阱处聚集的非均匀末态，且因系统可积，末态可用解析方法预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于理解引力类系统中通过相空间混合（phase space mixing）实现的弛豫过程，并借可积模型获得可解析对照的末态预言；同时该模型可类比 Kerr 时空中粒子运动，用于讨论近期天体物理观测现象。

Method: 采用两条独立路线：N 粒子数值模拟（N-particle simulations）与统计方法（statistical methods）；外势为中心势加四极分量（quadrupolar component），沿对称轴形成两个势阱，且四极分量被选取为使运动方程构成可积哈密顿系统（integrable Hamiltonian system），从而可解析计算气体末态。

Result: 初始均匀且各向同性的位形演化至非均匀末态，过密区（overdensities）位于势阱处；模拟的晚期行为可与解析预言比较，并通过相空间混合理解弛豫过程。

Conclusion: 该模型构成 Kerr 时空（Kerr spacetime）中粒子运动的牛顿类比（Newtonian analogue），并讨论了其对近期观测到的天体物理现象的潜在应用。

Abstract: We explore the dynamics of a Vlasov gas propagating in an external axisymmetric potential consisting of a central potential with an additional quadrupolar component which gives rise to two potential wells along the symmetry axis. Employing independently $N$-particle simulations and statistical methods, we show that an initially homogeneous and isotropic configuration evolves to an inhomogeneous final state with overdensities located at the wells. The quadrupolar component is chosen such that the equations of motion form an integrable Hamiltonian system, which allows one to compute the final state of the gas analytically. On the one hand, this allows one to compare the late-time behaviour of the simulations with an analytic prediction and on the other hand to understand the relaxation process through phase space mixing. Our model constitutes a Newtonian analogue of the particle motion in a Kerr spacetime, and we discuss possible applications to recently observed astrophysical phenomena.

</details>


### [9] [Scalable and sequential inference of the neutron star equation of state with the Einstein Telescope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07975)
*Thibeau Wouters,Thomas C. K. Ng,Hauke Koehn,Fabian Gittins,Peter T. H. Pang,Tim Dietrich,Chris Van Den Broeck*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文提出一种混合序贯蒙特卡洛（sequential Monte Carlo）算法，通过数据与似然回火（data and likelihood tempering）实现批量的中子星状态方程（equation of state）后验自适应更新，并借助 GPU 加速，使未来引力波（gravitational-wave）观测器的大规模层级推断（hierarchical inference）成为可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来引力波观测器（如 Einstein Telescope）每年将探测数万次双中子星（binary neutron star）并合，使引力波成为探测中子星状态方程的主导手段。然而，从快速增长的事件目录中提取信息需要层级推断，而现有方法在每次新事件到来时都须从头重新开始，规模上不具可行性。

Method: 提出一种混合序贯蒙特卡洛（sequential Monte Carlo）算法，将数据回火与似然回火（data and likelihood tempering）相结合，以批（batch）方式自适应更新状态方程后验，复用既有推断结果而非从宽先验（wide prior）重新启动；并使用 GPU 硬件进行加速。

Result: 在单块 GPU 上，该方法可在数小时内从模拟的一个月约 1500 次双中子星并合数据中推断状态方程；据其预测，在当前硬件上可在数天内处理一整年的探测数据。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了序贯蒙特卡洛（sequential Monte Carlo）作为面向未来引力波观测器进行实时层级推断的可行路径。

Abstract: Future gravitational-wave observatories such as the Einstein Telescope will detect tens of thousands of binary neutron star mergers per year, making gravitational waves a dominant probe of the neutron-star equation of state in the coming decades. However, extracting this information requires hierarchical inference across a rapidly growing catalog of events, and existing methods must restart from scratch whenever a new event arrives, making them impractical at this scale. In this Letter, we introduce a hybrid sequential Monte Carlo algorithm that combines data and likelihood tempering to adaptively update the equation-of-state posterior in batches, reusing previous inference results instead of restarting from a wide prior. Accelerated by GPU hardware, our method infers the equation of state from a simulated month of around 1500 binary neutron star mergers in a few hours on a single GPU, and we project that a full year of detections could be processed in a few days on current hardware. This establishes sequential Monte Carlo as a practical route for real-time hierarchical inference with future gravitational-wave detectors.

</details>

### [10] [Time-Reversal Selection Rule for Twist Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06943)
*Peng Kang,Da Wan,Shulin Bai,Pengfei Zhang,Vincent Michaud-Rioux,Zhen Li,Yu Liu,Lei Zheng,Li-Dong Zhao,Huibin Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作指出，层状材料中随机层旋转（random layer rotations）对层间输运的抑制强度由时间反演选择定则（time-reversal selection rule）决定，并给出由能带边对称性（band-edge symmetry）固定的普适厚度定律，将堆垛无序（stacking disorder）转化为可设计的调控要素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机层旋转在层状物质中普遍存在，会强烈抑制跨层输运；由于该无序由对称操作生成，在旋转轴上并不存在，因此导电的厚度规律取决于轴上背散射（backscattering）消失的快慢，这一机制此前缺乏统一判据。

Method: 提出时间反演选择定则：层间键的每个扭转谐波（twist harmonic）或为框架旋转（frame rotation，背散射相消），或为耦合变化（coupling change，产生散射）；由此导出零集定理（zero-set theorem），推广为电子与声子的普适厚度定律；并以黑磷（black phosphorus）的第一性原理前向通道模型（forward-channel model）验证。

Result: 厚度定律由能带边对称性固定，随机扭转增量仅重标定背散射而保持其量级；在旋转轴不受保护处，能带节点（band nodes）提供第二条通道，表现为透明能量（transparent energy）、重尾无序（heavy-tailed disorder）与 $N^{-3}$ 定律。黑磷计算显示两种通道并存，64 层随机扭转薄膜相对对齐堆垛可保留的电子电导约为空穴电导的一百倍。

Conclusion: 能带边与声子支（phonon branches）的对称性决定了何种载流子能穿过无序堆垛，随机扭转薄膜可充当对称性滤波器（symmetry filter），表明堆垛无序可成为材料设计中的功能性要素。

Abstract: Random layer rotations are ubiquitous in layered matter, from turbostratic films to rotationally disordered crystals, and strongly suppress transport across the layers. Generated by a symmetry operation, this disorder is absent on the rotation axis, so the thickness laws of conduction are set by how fast backscattering vanishes there. We show that a time-reversal selection rule decides this: each twist harmonic of the interlayer bond is either a frame rotation, whose backscattering cancels, or a coupling change, which scatters. A zero-set theorem turns the rule into universal thickness laws for electrons and phonons, fixed by band-edge symmetry; random twist increments rescale the backscattering but keep its order. Where the axis is unprotected, band nodes provide a second route: a transparent energy with heavy-tailed disorder and an $N^{-3}$ law. A first-principles forward-channel model of black phosphorus shows both routes; its random-twist films act as a symmetry filter that, relative to aligned stacks, retains a hundred times more of the electron than of the hole conductance at 64 layers. The symmetry of band edges and phonon branches thus decides what crosses a disordered stack, making stacking disorder a design element.

</details>


### [11] [Interplay between spinodal decomposition and wetting in ternary to quinary mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06946)
*Sandeep Kumar,Supriyo Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 基于 Cahn–Hilliard 描述的二维相场模拟，研究 N=3、4、5 组分混合物中表面导向旋节分解的形貌演化与粗化动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多组分混合物中粒子–组分相互作用如何调控微观结构演化，并理解润湿动力学与旋节分解之间的竞争机制。

Method: 采用基于 Cahn–Hilliard 方程的二维相场模拟（phase-field simulation），将共存相之一作为填充粒子，其余组分围绕其表面发生相分离；通过改变粒子与其他组分间的界面张力来考察非润湿（nonwetting）与润湿（wetting）条件。

Result: 润湿动力学与旋节分解的竞争产生晶格、液滴与靶状（核壳）等丰富形貌；靶状图案由粒子周围交替的富组分环组成。中后期由曲率驱动的润湿层耗尽与随后的粗化导致靶状图案破裂为孤立基体畴；组分数量增加使畴粗化系统性变慢，而润湿在各情形下均加速粗化。

Conclusion: 结果表明粒子–组分相互作用可有效调控多组分混合物的相分离路径与微观结构，对工业材料到细胞生物学等体系具有潜在意义。

Abstract: We use two-dimensional phase-field simulations based on the Cahn--Hilliard description to study spinodal decomposition in N-component mixtures with N = 3, 4, 5. In particular, we consider surface-directed spinodal decomposition in which one of the coexisting phases serves as a filler particle, while the remaining components phase separate around its surface. We investigate both nonwetting and wetting conditions by varying the interfacial tensions between the particle and the other components. The competition between wetting kinetics and spinodal decomposition produces rich morphological evolution, including the formation of lattice, droplet, and target (or core-shell) morphologies. The target pattern consists of alternating component-rich rings around the particle. In the intermediate-to-late time regime, these structures undergo further morphological transitions driven by curvature-induced depletion of wetting layers and subsequent coarsening of the surrounding domains, resulting in the breakup of target patterns into isolated domains of the matrix components. We find that domain coarsening slows down systematically as the number of components increases, while wetting accelerates it in all cases. The resulting morphologies and phase-separation pathways demonstrate how particle--component interactions can be used to control microstructural evolution in multicomponent mixtures, with potential implications for systems ranging from industrial materials to cellular biology.

</details>


### [12] [CrystalJev: thinking fast and slow with atomistic foundation models for materials discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06985)
*Peng Kang,Zhen Li,Yu Liu,Lei Zheng,Huibin Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将原子尺度基础模型（atomistic foundation models）重新定位为快速决策者（fast decision-makers）而非缓慢模拟器，提出 CrystalJev 框架，通过单次前向传播输出带校准概率（calibrated probabilities）与有限样本保证（finite-sample guarantees）的类型化判断，并用信息价值理论（value-of-information）决定何时进行慢速计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子尺度基础模型虽能筛选海量假想材料，但当前被当作缓慢模拟器使用，其阈值化能量被直接采信，未充分利用其作为快速决策工具的潜力。作者主张应将其解读为决策者，从而在保证可靠性的前提下降低计算成本。

Method: CrystalJev 对未弛豫结构（unrelaxed structure）仅查询一次冻结的原子间势（frozen interatomic potential），以校准概率回答类型化问题，给出有限样本保证，并设定何时需要"慢思考"的规则；该层同时可回答电子、力学与分子问题，并借助信息价值理论把更慢的计算仅分配到决策可能改变之处。

Result: 在 65 个 Matbench Discovery 模型上，"stable" 判定实质是隐式概率，可由模型误差与候选总体解释；单次前向传播的决策效果接近一次弛豫（relaxation），成本仅为三十分之一。在包含 700 次新密度泛函（density-functional）计算的注册前瞻性测试（registered prospective test）中，仅用既有数据校准的单次预测将未见候选的稳定比例（5.8%）高估最多 2.1 个百分点。

Conclusion: 原子尺度基础模型可作为带校准不确定性的快速决策层，在极低成本下逼近弛豫级决策质量，并通过信息价值规则按需分配慢速计算；前瞻性测试表明其单次预测的偏差有限，支持该范式在材料筛选中替代将能量直接阈值化的做法。

Abstract: Atomistic foundation models triage millions of hypothetical materials but are used as slow simulators, their thresholded energies taken at face value. They are better read as fast decision-makers. CrystalJev queries a frozen interatomic potential once per unrelaxed structure and answers typed questions with calibrated probabilities, finite-sample guarantees and a rule for when to think slowly. Across 65 Matbench Discovery models, a 'stable' call is a probability in disguise, explained by a model's errors and the candidate population. Once trained, one forward pass decides nearly as well as a relaxation at a thirtieth of its cost, and a value-of-information theory sends slower computation only where decisions can change. The same layer answers electronic, mechanical and molecular questions. In a registered prospective test with 700 new density-functional calculations, single-pass forecasts calibrated only on existing data over-stated the stable fraction of unseen candidates (5.8%) by at most 2.1 percentage points.

</details>


### [13] [Identifying Plastic Inorganic Semiconductors Requires More Rigorous Criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07007)
*Qiao Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 以层状GeSe为模型体系，通过宏观压缩、弯曲、常规与偏心微柱压缩等多种测试，揭示其变形行为强烈依赖取向、尺寸与加载路径，指出单一测试难以判定塑性，需建立多方向、多尺度、多模式的统一评价框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塑性无机半导体（plastic inorganic semiconductors）的判定存在困难，因其力学响应受晶体取向（crystallographic orientation）、样品尺寸与加载条件共同影响，单一条件下的结果不足以可靠反映材料塑性。

Method: 选取层状GeSe作为模型体系，采用宏观压缩、弯曲、常规微柱压缩（conventional micropillar compression）与偏心微柱压缩（eccentric micropillar compression）四种方式考察其变形行为，并结合多种表征手段进行分析。

Result: 垂直层向的宏观压缩下GeSe可承受约23%应变而不发生断裂；沿c轴扶手椅方向（armchair direction）弯曲则产生脆性解理断裂（brittle cleavage fracture）；常规微柱压缩导致脆性碎裂，而偏心加载引入剪切分量，激活显著层间滑移（interlayer sliding）并容纳变形。

Conclusion: 上述差异响应源于键合拓扑（bonding topology）、层间范德华相互作用（van der Waals interactions）、缺陷密度、应力约束与应变路径的耦合作用；结果说明依据单一方向、尺度或测试判定塑性存在局限，支持构建涵盖多方向、多长度尺度、多加载模式与多表征技术的系统评价框架，并呼吁更严格统一的判据以指导柔性电子及曲面集成器件的材料选择。

Abstract: The identification of plastic inorganic semiconductors becomes challenging when their mechanical responses depend on crystallographic orientation, sample size, and loading conditions. Using layered GeSe as a model system, we examine its deformation behavior through macroscopic compression, bending, conventional micropillar compression, and eccentric micropillar compression. Under macroscopic compression perpendicular to the layers, GeSe sustains approximately 23% strain without fracture, whereas bending along the c-axis armchair direction produces brittle cleavage fracture. Conventional micropillar compression results in brittle fragmentation, while eccentric loading introduces a shear component that activates pronounced interlayer sliding and accommodates deformation. These contrasting responses reflect the coupled effects of bonding topology, interlayer van der Waals interactions, defect density, stress constraints, and strain path on the competition between sliding and fracture. The results highlight the limitations of identifying plasticity from a single direction, scale, or test and support a systematic evaluation framework combining multiple directions, length scales, loading modes, and characterization techniques. More rigorous and unified criteria are needed to guide reliable materials selection for flexible electronics and devices integrated on curved surfaces.

</details>


### [14] [Direct-Write Chemical Vapor Deposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07113)
*Eeshan Ketkar,Swarnabha Chattaraj,Koichi Tanaka,Samy Kouidri,Supratik Guha*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究搭建基于10 μm玻璃微喷嘴、加热基底与超高真空环境的直写化学气相沉积（direct-write chemical vapor deposition）平台，实现500 nm厚、30 μm线宽的金属铝线单程连续直写，并提出耦合Knudsen气体发射与表面扩散的输运框架以提取有效前驱体扩散长度（L_d）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直写图案化可利用电子工业标准的高纯前驱体实现局部薄膜生长，但亚100 μm特征控制受限于喷嘴设计与尚未量化的前驱体表面扩散（surface diffusion）。

Method: 采用直径10 μm的玻璃微喷嘴，安装于扫描台并置于加热基底上方、处于超高真空（ultra-high vacuum）环境；在氮化钛（titanium nitride）涂层硅片上系统改变沉积条件进行无掩模（maskless）局部生长，并引入耦合微喷嘴Knudsen气体发射与表面扩散的输运框架分析沉积轮廓。

Result: 实现500 nm厚、30 μm线宽金属铝线的单程连续直写；提取的有效前驱体扩散长度L_d随温度升高（165 °C至205 °C）由17.4 μm收缩至3.2 μm，反映前驱体表面迁移率、停留时间、衬底温度、生长时长与喷嘴高度的综合影响。

Conclusion: L_d可作为评估横向约束化学气相生长与预测特征分辨率极限的定量参数，该工作提供了测量与量化局部薄膜合成中前驱体表面输运的实用方法。

Abstract: Direct-write pattering, using chemical vapor deposition through a nozzle, enables localized film growth using high-purity precursors standard to the electronics industry. Yet, achieving sub 100 $μ\mathrm{m}$ feature control has been limited by nozzle design and unquantified precursor surface diffusion. Here, we report a direct-write chemical vapor deposition platform that employs a 10 $μ\mathrm{m}$ tip diameter glass micro-nozzle mounted on a scanning stage above a heated substrate in ultra-high vacuum. Using this approach, we demonstrate the continuous single-pass writing of 500 $\mathrm{nm}$ thick metallic aluminum lines with a 30 $μ\mathrm{m}$ linewidth on titanium nitride-coated silicon. This capability enables maskless, localized growth under systematically varied deposition conditions on a single substrate. To analyze the resulting deposit profiles, we introduce a transport framework coupling Knudsen gas emission from the micro-nozzle with surface diffusion to extract the effective precursor diffusion length, $L_d$. $L_d$ captures the combined impact of precursor surface mobility, residence time, substrate temperature ($165^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ to $205^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$), growth duration, and nozzle height, contracting from 17.4 $μ\mathrm{m}$ down to 3.2 $μ\mathrm{m}$ at elevated temperatures. These results establish $L_d$ as a quantitative parameter for evaluating laterally confined chemical vapor growth and predicting feature resolution limits. This approach provides a practical methodology to measure and quantify precursor surface transport in localized film synthesis.

</details>


### [15] [Effect of Source-to-Substrate Distance on the Optical and Electrical Properties of Thermally Deposited CdTe, CdSe and Sb$_2$S$_3$ Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07222)
*Himanshu Sharma Pathok,Prasanta Kumar Saikia*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过热蒸发（thermal evaporation）技术在15–24 cm范围内改变源-衬底距离（source-to-substrate distance, SSD），制备了碲化镉（CdTe）、硒化镉（CdSe）与硫化锑（Sb₂S₃）薄膜，并系统考察SSD对其结构、成分、光学与电学性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索一种通过工艺参数调节薄膜光电性能的途径，以评估CdTe、CdSe与Sb₂S₃薄膜作为薄膜太阳能电池（thin film solar cells, TFSCs）吸收层（absorber layer）的应用潜力，并寻找除已成熟的CdTe之外的替代吸收层材料。

Method: 采用热蒸发技术沉积CdTe、CdSe和Sb₂S₃薄膜，将源-衬底距离（SSD）设置为15、18、21和24 cm；通过X射线衍射（XRD）分析结构，EDX光谱确认成分，光学分析结合包络法（envelope method）测定膜厚与光学带隙，并进行电学表征。

Result: XRD显示CdTe与CdSe薄膜为多晶（polycrystalline），Sb₂S₃薄膜为非晶（amorphous）；EDX确认各组成元素存在。随SSD增大，光学带隙仅有微小变化，而膜厚减小；CdTe与CdSe的光学带隙低于2 eV，Sb₂S₃带隙相对较高。CdTe与CdSe薄膜带隙较低、吸收系数高，但光学透射率（transmittance）相对较低，有利于吸收层的光吸收。电学表征确认所有薄膜均呈半导体性质。

Conclusion: 结果表明，改变源-衬底距离（SSD）可有效调控CdTe、CdSe与Sb₂S₃薄膜的光学与电学性能。除已成熟的CdTe吸收层外，CdSe薄膜也显示出作为薄膜太阳能电池（TFSCs）吸收层材料的应用潜力。

Abstract: In this work, cadmium telluride (CdTe), cadmium selenide (CdSe), and antimony sulfide (Sb\(_2\)S\(_3\)) thin films were deposited using the thermal evaporation technique by varying the source-to-substrate distance (SSD) from 15 to 24 cm (15, 18, 21, and 24 cm). The influence of the SSD on the structural, compositional, optical and electrical properties of the deposited films was systematically investigated. XRD analysis revealed the polycrystalline nature of the CdTe and CdSe thin films, whereas the Sb$_2$S$_3$ thin films exhibited an amorphous nature. EDX spectroscopy confirmed the presence of the constituent elements in the deposited films. Optical analysis revealed a marginal variation in the optical band gap with increasing SSD, while the film thickness, determined using the envelope method, decreased with increasing SSD. The optical band gap energies of CdTe and CdSe were found to be below 2 eV, whereas Sb$_2$S$_3$ exhibited a relatively higher band gap. The relatively low band gap energies and high absorption coefficients of the CdTe and CdSe films make them promising candidates for absorber layer applications in thin film solar cells (TFSCs). The films exhibited relatively low optical transmittance, which is desirable for efficient light absorption in absorber layers. Electrical characterization confirmed the semiconducting nature of all the deposited thin films. Overall, the results demonstrate that the optical and electrical properties of CdTe, CdSe and Sb$_2$S$_3$ thin films can be effectively modified by varying the SSD. The findings indicate that, in addition to the well established CdTe absorber, CdSe thin films show potential for application as absorber materials in TFSCs.

</details>


### [16] [Conditions for Altermagnetism in RuO2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07494)
*Himanshu Mavani,Zhonglin He,Evgeny Y. Tsymbal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文综合实验与理论的批判性评估及第一性原理计算，指出外延应变（epitaxial strain）是稳定 RuO2 交变磁性（altermagnetism）的主导因素，并区分了载流子掺杂与局部结构/化学改性的不同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金红石型 RuO2 的磁基态（magnetic ground state）仍存争议，核心问题是：在何种结构与化学条件下该材料能够支持交变磁性（altermagnetism）。

Method: 结合对实验与理论研究的关键评估，以及针对外延应变（epitaxial strain）、点缺陷（point defects）、掺杂（doping）和界面（interfaces）的第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）。

Result: 文献中的体敏感（bulk-sensitive）测量与非磁基态一致，而薄膜中的磁性信号强烈依赖结构环境，并不必然表明存在均匀的交变磁序；计算显示衬底施加的外延应变是最有利因素，并预测出若干可稳定交变磁基态的衬底取向组合。均匀空穴掺杂（hole doping）增强磁性倾向；Ru 空位（Ru vacancies）益处不大，并可通过局部结构畸变削弱磁态；Cr 或 Mn 替代则增强 Ru 磁矩并进一步稳定受应变的交变磁相。

Conclusion: 研究区分了载流子掺杂（carrier doping）与局部结构及化学变化的不同效应，并指出了在 RuO2 薄膜中实现交变磁性的具体途径。

Abstract: The magnetic ground state of rutile RuO2 remains disputed, raising a central question: under what structural and chemical conditions can this material support altermagnetism? We address this question by combining a critical assessment of experimental and theoretical studies with first-principles calculations of epitaxial strain, point defects, doping, and interfaces. Bulk-sensitive measurements reported in the literature are consistent with a nonmagnetic ground state, while magnetic signatures in thin films depend strongly on their structural environment and do not necessarily establish homogeneous altermagnetic order. Our calculations identify substrate-imposed epitaxial strain as the leading factor favoring altermagnetism among the conditions examined and predict several substrate-orientation combinations that stabilize an altermagnetic ground state. Carrier doping and chemical substitution modify this strain-induced magnetism in distinct ways. Uniform hole doping strengthens the magnetic tendency, whereas Ru vacancies provide little benefit and can weaken the magnetic state through local structural distortions. Cr or Mn substitution, by contrast, enhances Ru magnetic moments and further stabilizes the strained altermagnetic phase. These results distinguish the effects of carrier doping from those of local structural and chemical changes and identify concrete routes to realizing altermagnetism in RuO2 films.

</details>


### [17] [Determination of Burgers-vector directions of threading edge dislocations in GaN by phase-contrast microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07837)
*Yukari Ishikawa,Maki Shimizu,Koji Sato,Kisara Matsumoto,Kazuki Ohnishi,Yongzhao Yao,Ryo Hattori*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究建立了GaN中穿透刃位错的相衬显微图像明暗衬度排布与其面内Burgers矢量方向之间的对应关系，并验证相衬显微术可作为无损光学方法测定该方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相衬显微术（phase-contrast microscopy）可快速、无损地观测GaN中的穿透位错（threading dislocations），但位错衬度与Burgers矢量之间的关系此前尚未建立，限制了该方法的应用价值。

Method: 先在Burgers矢量已由X射线形貌术（X-ray topography）独立标定的氨热法（ammonothermal）GaN中建立关系，再应用于氢化物气相外延（hydride vapor phase epitaxy）生长的GaN，并用弱束暗场TEM（weak-beam dark-field TEM）与大角度会聚束电子衍射（large-angle convergent-beam electron diffraction）验证所预测的Burgers矢量。

Result: 对所考察的穿透刃位错，将暗衬度最小值指向亮衬度最大值的方向逆时针旋转90°后，与Burgers矢量方向一致；基于光弹效应（photoelastic effect）的分析进一步表明，相衬主要反映位错周围两个面内正应变分量之和εxx + εyy。

Conclusion: 结果表明相衬显微术可作为无损光学方法，用于测定GaN中穿透刃位错的面内Burgers矢量方向。

Abstract: Phase-contrast microscopy enables rapid, nondestructive observation of threading dislocations in GaN, but the relationship between dislocation contrast and the Burgers vector has not been established. Here, we demonstrate that the in-plane Burgers-vector direction of threading edge dislocations in GaN can be determined from the arrangement of bright and dark contrast in phase-contrast microscopy images. The relationship was first established for threading edge dislocations in ammonothermal GaN whose Burgers vectors were independently determined by X-ray topography and was subsequently applied to hydride vapor phase epitaxy-grown GaN. The Burgers vector predicted from the phase contrast was verified by weak-beam dark-field TEM and large-angle convergent-beam electron diffraction. For the threading edge dislocations examined, the direction obtained by rotating the direction from the minimum of the dark contrast to the maximum of the bright contrast by 90° counterclockwise coincided with the Burgers-vector direction. Analysis based on the photoelastic effect further suggests that the phase contrast primarily reflects the sum of the two in-plane normal strain components, εxx + εyy, around the dislocation. These results demonstrate that phase-contrast microscopy can be used as a nondestructive optical method for determining the in-plane Burgers-vector direction of threading edge dislocations in GaN.

</details>


### [18] [A self-learning scientific agent for X-ray diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07862)
*Bin Cao,Huichi Zhou,Runyu Yang,Jingsong Li,Shuchen Sun,Yan Song,Hanyu Gao,Zhongwei Yu,Tong-Yi Zhang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Gan Jiang——一个面向粉末 X 射线衍射（powder X-ray diffraction）的自学习智能体（self-learning agent），依托 XMatcher、XQueryer、XDecomposer 与 WPEM 构成的衍射分析生态，将分析经验转化为可复用技能并在多平台与基准上验证其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学智能体（scientific agents）的核心挑战在于把分析经验转化为以物理证据为支撑、可复用的专门知识（expertise）。现有方法在衍射分析中难以兼顾技能复用与物理模型一致性，故需构建整合工具生态以支撑经验积累与迁移。

Method: Gan Jiang 通过诊断失败、修订技能指令与代码、并在复用前验证修订来生成可执行技能（executable skills），且不重训练语言模型（language model）、不更改底层物理模型。技能基于开发数据（development data）筛选并在留出评估（held-out evaluation）前冻结。所用引擎覆盖物相鉴定、多相分解与物理约束全谱建模（physics-constrained whole-pattern modelling）。

Result: 经开发数据筛选并冻结的技能在 FullProf、GSAS-II 与 PyWPEM 上取得高于原始专家设计技能的细化分数（refinement scores）。智能体可解析强重叠衍射峰、定量五相古埃及化妆品、追踪工作电池晶格演化并比较无序氧化物催化剂的原子构型。在 DeltaXRDbench 上于单相与多相鉴定领先；无给定成分时，单相 top-1 准确率在 MP500、RRUFF、opXRD 分别达 96.30%、81.78%、40.83%，最强对照为 58.00%、58.47%、26.45%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，整合式科学工具生态可支撑智能体从测量中提取结构知识，同时积累经验证、可迁移至新样品并可复用的分析专门知识（analytical expertise）。

Abstract: A central challenge for scientific agents is to turn analytical experience into reusable expertise grounded in physical evidence. Here we introduce Gan Jiang, a self-learning agent for powder X-ray diffraction built on a diffraction-analysis ecosystem we developed: XMatcher, XQueryer, XDecomposer and WPEM. Together, these engines span phase identification, multiphase decomposition and physics-constrained whole-pattern modelling. Gan Jiang converts analytical experience into executable skills by diagnosing failures, revising skill instructions and code, and validating revisions before reuse, without retraining the language model or changing the underlying physical models. Skills selected using development data and frozen before held-out evaluation achieve higher refinement scores than the original expert-designed skills across FullProf, GSAS-II and PyWPEM. The agent resolves strongly overlapping reflections, quantifies a five-phase ancient Egyptian cosmetic, tracks lattice evolution in an operating battery and compares atomic configurations in a disordered oxide catalyst. On DeltaXRDbench, it leads the evaluated methods in single- and multiphase identification across simulated and experimental data. Without supplied composition, single-phase top-1 accuracies reach 96.30\%, 81.78\% and 40.83\% on MP500, RRUFF and opXRD, respectively, compared with 58.00\%, 58.47\% and 26.45\% for the strongest comparator. These results demonstrate how an integrated scientific tool ecosystem can support agents that extract structural knowledge from measurements while accumulating validated analytical expertise that transfers to new samples.

</details>


### [19] [Finite-Momentum Antiferromagnetic Magnon Dynamics across the Morin Transition in Hematite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08030)
*Jiaqi Gong,Yuanzhe Tian,Jun Cui,Meiye Hou,Yuxuan Mu,Di Wang,Xiangang Wan,Di Wu,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用低波数磁拉曼光谱（low-wavenumber magneto-Raman spectroscopy）解析了赤铁矿（hematite，α-Fe₂O₃）在Morin转变（Morin transition）前后k=0与有限动量（finite-k）亚太赫兹磁振子（magnon），并提取温度依赖的各向异性场与Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya场，为赤铁矿磁子学（magnonics）提供关键材料参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赤铁矿（α-Fe₂O₃）是高速自旋电子学（spintronics）与磁子学的典型反铁磁（antiferromagnetic）平台，但其有限动量磁振子动力学与自旋重取向（spin-reorientation）机制尚未被完全理解。研究动机在于定量揭示Morin转变的驱动机制及有限动量磁振子的行为。

Method: 采用低波数磁拉曼光谱在Morin转变温度区间测量k=0及有限k的亚太赫兹磁振子；结合平行场与横向场测量及自旋波建模（spin-wave modeling），提取温度依赖的Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya场HD与单轴各向异性场HK1、HK2。

Result: 极限群速度（limiting group velocity）v₀几乎不依赖磁场，而有限k群速度vg在Morin转变与spin-flop转变附近受到强烈调制。HD约为2.0–2.3 T且温度依赖较弱；HK1随温度快速下降，HK2仅弱变化。此差异驱动有效各向异性场（effective anisotropy field）的符号反转，并定量解释了Morin转变。

Conclusion: 低波数拉曼光谱可作为有限动量反铁磁磁振子的定量探测手段；所得HD、HK1、HK2等材料参数为基于赤铁矿的磁子学器件设计提供依据。

Abstract: Hematite ($α$-Fe$_2$O$_3$) is a prototypical antiferromagnetic platform for high-speed spintronics and magnonics, yet its finite-momentum magnon dynamics and spin-reorientation mechanism remain incompletely understood. Here we use low-wavenumber magneto-Raman spectroscopy to resolve both $k=0$ and finite-$k$ sub-terahertz magnons in $α$-Fe$_2$O$_3$ across the Morin transition. We find that the limiting group velocity $v_0$ remains nearly field independent, whereas the finite-$k$ group velocity $v_{\mathrm g}$ is strongly modified near the Morin and spin-flop transitions. By combining parallel- and transverse-field measurements with spin-wave modeling, we further extract the temperature-dependent Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya field $H_{\mathrm D}$ and uniaxial anisotropy fields $H_{\mathrm{K1}}$ and $H_{\mathrm{K2}}$. We find that $H_{\mathrm D}$ is weakly temperature dependent at approximately 2.0--2.3~T, while $H_{\mathrm{K1}}$ decreases rapidly and $H_{\mathrm{K2}}$ changes only weakly. This contrast drives the sign reversal of the effective anisotropy field and quantitatively accounts for the Morin transition. Our results establish low-wavenumber Raman spectroscopy as a quantitative probe of finite-momentum antiferromagnetic magnons and provide key material parameters for hematite-based magnonics.

</details>


### [20] [Dynamic Kinetic Evaluation Favors Compositionally Diverse Multicomponent Alloy Nanoparticles for Hydrogen Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08040)
*Koki Otsuka,Anh Khoa Augustin Lu,Koji Shimizu,Satoshi Watanabe*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个整合机器学习能量预测、热力学蒙特卡洛采样、贝叶斯优化与动力学蒙特卡洛（kMC）的框架，用于在九元素成分空间中筛选85原子高熵合金（HEA）纳米颗粒以优化析氢反应（HER）性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵合金纳米颗粒虽具有化学多样性丰富的催化位点，但其庞大的设计空间使最优成分的识别十分困难，需借助高效的计算筛选方法。

Method: 采用机器学习能量预测、热力学蒙特卡洛采样、贝叶斯优化与动力学蒙特卡洛（kMC）模拟相结合的框架，对九元素成分空间中的85原子纳米颗粒模型进行析氢反应筛选，并进行循环分辨（cycle-resolved）分析。

Result: 基于吸附能的静态优化倾向于选择富Pt、少组分的成分，而基于kMC的优化则将多元成分Au33Co20Cu6Pd26Pd26列为评估候选中的最优者；循环分辨分析显示氢在富Co/Pd环境吸附、经中等结合位点迁移，并在弱结合的含Au环境中频繁发生Heyrovsky脱附。

Conclusion: 路径层面的证据支持多活性位点（multisite）反应图景，表明引入动力学（kinetics）可对静态吸附能筛选形成有效补充。

Abstract: High-entropy alloy (HEA) nanoparticles offer chemically diverse catalytic sites, but identifying optimal compositions across their large design space remains challenging. We developed a framework combining machine-learning energy prediction, thermodynamic Monte Carlo sampling, Bayesian optimization, and kinetic Monte Carlo (kMC) simulations to screen 85-atom nanoparticle models across a nine-element composition space for the hydrogen evolution reaction. Static optimization based on adsorption energies selected Pt-rich, low-component compositions, whereas kMC-based optimization ranked the multielement composition Au$_{33}$Co$_{20}$Cu$_{6}$Pd$_{26}$ highest among the evaluated candidates. Cycle-resolved analysis further showed hydrogen adsorption at Co/Pd-rich environments, migration through intermediate-binding sites, and frequent Heyrovsky desorption at weaker-binding, Au-containing environments. This pathway-level evidence supports a multisite reaction picture and illustrates how incorporating kinetics can complement static adsorption-energy screening.

</details>


### [21] [Reflection of forward volume exchange spin waves at ferromagnetic junctions interpreted via continuity of angular momentum density flux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08081)
*Luis Sánchez-Tejerina,Luis M. Moreno-Ramírez,Óscar Alejos Ducal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究从角动量密度通量（angular momentum density flux）连续性出发，解析推导了两种铁磁介质（ferromagnetic media）界面处自旋波（spin waves）的反射系数与驻波包络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为高效控制基于自旋波的器件并减少不必要的反射带来的损耗与干涉，需要清晰理解其散射机制（scattering mechanism）。研究聚焦于两种铁磁介质界面处的自旋波反射问题。

Method: 基于角动量密度通量的连续性约束，解析计算反射系数（reflection coefficient）、第一介质中的驻波包络（stationary wave envelope）以及第二介质中的阻尼传播（damped propagation），并将解析结果与全微磁学模拟（full micromagnetic simulations）进行对比。

Result: 解析表达式与全微磁学模拟结果吻合良好（excellent agreement）。

Conclusion: 角动量密度通量连续性可用于理解铁磁介质界面处的自旋波反射机制，并提供与数值模拟高度一致的解析描述。

Abstract: Magnonics investigates the dynamic excitations of magnetically ordered media, known as spin waves, and their application in functionalized devices. To efficiently control spin wave based devices minimizing losses and interferences from unwanted reflections, a clear picture of the scattering mechanism is needed. Here, we focus on the reflection of spin waves produced at the interface between two ferromagnetic media. We show that it is possible to understand the reflection mechanism based on the continuity of the angular momentum density flux. This constraint allows us to analytically compute the reflection coefficient and the stationary wave envelope in the first medium, as well as the damped propagation in the second. In addition, the analytical expressions are compared with full micromagnetic simulations, showing excellent agreement.

</details>


### [22] [Powder-in-tube confinement as a design principle for fatigue-resistant caloric materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08172)
*T. Niehoff,L. Beyer,J. Puy,F. Scheibel,J. Freudenberger,O. Gutfleisch,J. Wosnitza,T. Gottschall*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出以粉末套管法（powder-in-tube, PIT）实现机械约束（mechanical confinement），将脆性 Heusler 合金粉末封装于韧性钢鞘中，使复合材料抗疲劳寿命提升数个数量级，并揭示约束导致内部应力异质、宏观应变与相变分数解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态热效应制冷（caloric solid-state refrigeration）虽具能效优势，但包括磁热与弹热 Heusler 合金在内的候选材料本征脆性（intrinsically brittle），难以承受服役所需的循环载荷。作者旨在通过机械约束克服这一瓶颈，将脆性由淘汰性缺陷转为可控特性。

Method: 采用粉末套管法，将 87% 堆积密度的颗粒状 Heusler 合金芯嵌入韧性钢鞘（外径 2.8 mm、壁厚 0.3 mm），通过鞘层约束粉末并实现颗粒间有效载荷传递。同时开发同步原位测量技术，在加载条件下联合测量应变与交流磁化率（AC magnetic susceptibility），以解析机械复杂体系中的相变行为。

Result: 复合材料机械耐久性提升数个数量级，在 250 MPa 下承受 100,000 次载荷循环而无结构失效，并可耐受高达 700 MPa 的过载应力。原位测量表明芯部内部应力场高度异质（strongly heterogeneous），导致宏观应变与相变相分数（transforming phase fraction）解耦，马氏体相变（martensitic transition）无法仅凭力学数据追踪。

Conclusion: 机械约束可作为抗疲劳热效应材料（fatigue-resistant caloric materials）的设计原则；所提出的联合原位方法可作为表征工具，适用于复合、多孔及其他机械异质的热效应体系。

Abstract: Caloric solid-state refrigeration offers an energy-efficient alternative to vapor-compression cooling, but many of the most promising materials, including the magneto- and elastocaloric Heusler alloys, are intrinsically brittle and fail under the cyclic loading required in operation. Here, we introduce mechanical confinement via powder-in-tube (PIT) processing as a general strategy to overcome this limitation. By embedding a granular Heusler alloy core of 87 % packing density within a ductile steel sheath (2.8 mm outer diameter, 0.3 mm wall thickness), the composite confines the brittle powder and enables effective load transfer between particles. While the core remains inherently brittle, the surrounding sheath prevents catastrophic fragmentation and turns brittleness from a disqualifying property into a manageable one. We demonstrate that this approach enhances mechanical durability by several orders of magnitude, with the composites sustaining 100,000 load cycles at 250 MPa without structural failure and tolerating overload stresses up to 700 MPa. Confinement also alters the transformation itself, and to resolve this behavior in these mechanically complex systems, we developed a simultaneous in-situ measurement technique combining strain and AC magnetic susceptibility under load. It reveals that the internal stress field in the core is strongly heterogeneous. As a consequence, the macroscopic strain decouples from the transforming phase fraction, and the martensitic transition cannot be tracked by mechanical data alone. Mechanical confinement thus emerges as a design principle for fatigue-resistant caloric materials, and the combined in-situ method as a characterization tool for composite, porous, and other mechanically heterogeneous caloric systems.

</details>


### [23] [Unraveling hidden Raman modes in $α$-MnTe: Effects of long-range dipole-dipole interactions on phonon bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08221)
*Susmita Jana,Rajib Sarkar,Dhavala Suri,B. R. K Nanda*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究指出，长程（long range, LR）偶极-偶极相互作用（dipole-dipole interaction）对准确计算交变磁体（altermagnet）α-MnTe 的声子能带结构至关重要，考虑该作用后可再现实验观测的各个拉曼（Raman）特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验与理论观测到的 α-MnTe 拉曼光谱之间存在差异，该差异此前被视为未解问题。研究旨在厘清造成该差异的关键物理因素。

Method: 在声子能带结构计算中引入长程偶极-偶极相互作用的修正，并与群论表示（group-theoretical representations）分析相结合，进而计算拉曼光谱。

Result: 长程修正显著改变高频光学模式及其群论表示：针对约 175 cm⁻¹ 的争议拉曼峰，除拉曼沉默（Raman silent）的 B₁ᵤ 模式外，还引入拉曼活性（Raman-active）的纵向光学 A′ 与 A₁ 模式及横向光学 A₁ 模式；这些模式在本征体系中强度较弱，特定畸变可增强其强度。约 140 cm⁻¹ 峰源于区偏离（zone off-center）贡献的高阶拉曼过程，约 120 cm⁻¹ 峰则兼具区中心与区偏离贡献。

Conclusion: 长程偶极-偶极相互作用的引入是准确估计 α-MnTe 声子能带结构并捕捉实验拉曼特征的关键；计算结果可解释多个争议拉曼峰的来源与模式归属。

Abstract: In this study, we address the seemingly open question of disparity between the experimentally and theoretically observed Raman spectra for the altermagnetic $α$-MnTe. We establish that the consideration of long range (LR) dipole-dipole interaction is crucial to accurately estimate the phonon band structure and thereby capturing each of the experimentally observed Raman signatures. The LR correction brings about substantial changes to the high-frequency optical modes and their group-theoretical representations. Overall, the calculated Raman spectra suggest that the intensity of these modes is weak in the pristine system, while certain prescribed distortions can increase their intensity. The Raman peak around 140 cm$^{-1}$, as observed experimentally, is identified to be emerging from a higher-order Raman process due to zone off-center contributions, while the other debated 120 cm$^{-1}$ peak has both the zone center and off-center contributions.

Conclusion: 该方法为存在局域与离域电子态强杂化体系的电子-声子散射机制显微分析提供了普适且高效的工具。

Our method enables efficient orbital-resolved analysis of transport spectral functions. ### [26] [Programming electronic states in conductive metal-organic frame-works through crystallization-enabled proton management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08536)
*Hao Chen,Tongyang Zhao,Weishan Li,Jinkun Guo,Jia-Xiang Zhang,Ze-Fan Yao,Maojun Zheng,Jin-Hu Dou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种"结晶驱动原位质子管理（crystallization-enabled in situ proton-management）"策略，通过调控结晶过程中的质子环境来调节导电金属有机框架（conductive metal-organic frameworks, c-MOFs）的电子态，从而在保持骨架结构不变的前提下显著提升电导率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: c-MOFs 的电子态决定其电导率，但现有调控手段依赖重新设计骨架组分或结晶后引入外部掺杂剂（external dopants），使得电子态调控受限于结构修饰或后合成掺杂（postsynthetic doping）。因此在结晶过程中实现电子态的可控调节仍是难题。

Result: HNO3 介导结晶所得电子态与 Co-O 骨架内增强的电子保留（electron retention）一致，单晶电导率达 8.4 × 10⁻² S cm⁻¹，比 Co9HHTP4-NaOAc（1.7 × 10⁻⁵ S cm⁻¹）高出近四个数量级，且传输活化能（transport activation energy）显著降低；霍尔测量显示 Co9HHTP4-HNO3 的载流子浓度（carrier concentration）明显增加，直接证明电导率提升与载流子数量大幅增加相关。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明结晶条件可作为编程导电框架中电子态与电荷传输（charge transport）的有效参数，为 c-MOFs 电子态调控提供了不依赖结构重设计或后合成掺杂的新途径。

These results establish crystallization conditions as an active parameter for programming electronic states and charge transport in conductive frameworks.

</details>

TL;DR: 基于格林函数理论（Green's-function theory）计算双亚晶格亚铁磁体（two-sublattice ferrimagnets）中有效自旋模型参数的温度依赖性，发现亚晶格间不均衡的重整化（renormalization）导致长波长自旋波响应呈现强非单调且依赖极化（polarization）的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚铁磁材料（ferrimagnetic materials）的有限温度微磁学（micromagnetic）与多尺度建模需要准确的有效参数，但现有理解缺乏对温度依赖原子尺度相互作用与长波长自旋波刚度（spin-wave stiffness）之间直接联系的刻画。

Result: 有效相互作用参数随温度升高单调减小，但因两亚晶格磁相互作用与关联（correlations）不同而产生不均衡重整化，进而导致长波长自旋波响应强非单调且依赖极化；自旋波交换刚度（exchange stiffness）表现出强分支依赖（branch-dependent）与温度非单调性，某一极化在角动量补偿区（angular momentum compensation region）附近出现刚度增强。格林函数预测与原子尺度自旋动力学模拟定量一致。

Conclusion: 结果建立了温度依赖原子尺度相互作用、长波长自旋波刚度与亚铁磁材料有限温度微磁学及多尺度建模所需有效参数之间的直接联系。

Abstract: We compute the temperature dependence of effective spin-model parameters in two-sublattice ferrimagnets with antiparallel and inequivalent sublattice magnetizations using Green's-function theory. The effective intra- and intersublattice exchange interactions, together with the sublattice anisotropies, exhibit distinct thermal renormalizations arising from the different magnetic interactions and correlations of the two sublattices. Although these effective interaction parameters decrease monotonically with increasing temperature, their unequal renormalization produces a strongly nonmonotonic and polarization-dependent long-wavelength spin-wave response. In particular, the spin-wave exchange stiffness exhibits a strongly branch-dependent and nonmonotonic temperature dependence. One polarization develops an enhanced spin-wave stiffness in the vicinity of the angular momentum compensation region despite the monotonic thermal reduction of the underlying exchange interactions. The Green's-function predictions are in quantitative agreement with atomistic spin-dynamics simulations, which provide a numerical benchmark including thermally induced spin correlations within the underlying atomistic spin model. These results establish a direct connection between temperature-dependent atomistic interactions, long-wavelength spin-wave stiffness, and the effective parameters required for finite-temperature micromagnetic and multiscale modeling of ferrimagnetic materials.

*Ashwinee Kumar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究采用混合分子动力学/蒙特卡罗（molecular dynamics/Monte Carlo, MD/MC）模拟，考察纳米晶（nanocrystalline）Fe-18Cr-12Ni中晶界（grain boundary, GB）偏聚行为及其对塑性变形的影响，并强调晶界成分表征强烈依赖参考区域的选择。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奥氏体（austenitic）Fe-Cr-Ni合金的晶界偏聚研究多集中于辐照或敏化材料，纳米晶状态下的相关认识仍不足。

Abstract: Grain-boundary (GB) segregation in austenitic Fe-Cr-Ni alloys has been studied mainly in irradiated or sensitised material, rarely in the nanocrystalline state. Ni is depleted at the GB plane and Cr is enriched in the adjacent atomic shell; both directions are established at the production sampling budget and strengthen when one of the three microstructures is sampled further. The Cr enrichment is resolved only against the far-field grain interior; the common partition into face-centred-cubic (fcc) and non-fcc atoms places the enriched shell in its own reference. Under a constant uniaxial stress of 4.6 GPa at 300 K, the segregated polycrystals take about 16% longer than random ones to reach 50% strain; measured against annealed controls, boundary chemistry contributes about two-thirds of this gain and boundary relaxation the rest. </details>


### [29] [Phase-field insight into the nature of topological polarization structures in uniaxial ferroelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08687)
*Olga Y. Mazur,Pavel Marton,Yuri A. Genenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究采用普适三维相场模型（phase-field model），研究硫酸三甘氨酸（triglycine sulfate, TGS）中畴结构的动力学演化，指出在单轴铁电体（uniaxial ferroelectric）极性面内引入横向自由度后，可自然涌现鞍点状拓扑结构，并显著增强头对头/尾对尾畴壁的束缚电荷补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轴铁电体因简单180畴结构长期作为极化演化时间分辨研究的基准体系，但近期实验观察到复杂拓扑织构，包括难以解释的无电荷头对头与尾对尾畴壁。该文旨在为这些反常现象提供一致的理论解释。

Method: 构建普适三维相场模型，考虑硫酸三甘氨酸母相与铁电相的单斜对称性（monoclinic symmetry），在单轴铁电体极性面内允许自发极化的横向自由度，模拟畴结构形成的动力学过程。

Result: 模型显示，仅在引入极性面内横向自由度时，才会形成孤立鞍状极化结构、互联鞍状级联、鞍线及特定猴鞍（monkey saddle）等结构；这些结构的丰富多样性驱动了比单分量序参量模型更有效的头对头/尾对尾畴壁束缚电荷补偿。随畴粗化与残余束缚电荷向表面迁移，拓扑景观简化，仅残留孤立鞍点。

Conclusion: 该模型成功再现了硫酸三甘氨酸与锗酸铅（lead germanate）中的实验观测奇异结构，揭示出超越单轴铁电体传统伊辛（Ising）图像的畴拓扑。束缚电荷补偿机制可能依赖具体材料，但类似拓扑结构或可出现在其他单轴铁电体中；模型的普适表述为研究任意对称性铁电材料中的此类现象开辟了路径。

Abstract: Uniaxial ferroelectrics have long served as benchmark systems for time-resolved studies of polarization evolution due to their simple 180-domain structure. However, recent experiments in these materials have revealed complex topological textures with enigmatic features, including the unexpected charge-free head-to-head and tail-to-tail domain walls which still wait for their consistent explanation. Here, we use a universal three-dimensional phase-field model to investigate the kinetics of domain structure development in triglycine sulfate, accounting for the monoclinic symmetry of its parent and ferroelectric phases. We show that isolated saddle-like polarization structures, interconnected saddle cascades, saddle lines and specific monkey saddles can form only when transverse degrees of freedom for spontaneous polarization are allowed in the polar plane of a uniaxial ferroelectric. The abundance and diversity of saddle-like structures at the early stages of domain ordering drives a far more effective compensation of bound charges at head-to-head and tail-to-tail domain walls than those predicted by single-component order parameter models. As domains coarsen and residual bound charges migrate toward the surfaces of the sample, the topological landscape simplifies, leaving behind only isolated saddle points. The present model successfully reproduces exotic structures observed experimentally in triglycine sulfate and lead germanate and reveals a domain topology that extends beyond the conventional Ising picture of uniaxial ferroelectrics. Although the mechanisms of bound-charge compensation are likely to be material-specific, similar topological structures may emerge in other uniaxial ferroelectrics. The general formulation of our phase-field model opens the way for future studies of such phenomena across a broader class of ferroelectric materials of arbitrary symmetry.

</details>


### [30] [Small Distortions, Big Polarization: Tetragonal BaTiO3 Nanoparticles for High-Performance Piezoelectric Nanogenerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08762)
*Shivshankar Jokare,Vikash Kushwaha,Mandar Shirolkar,Shruti Kharadkar,R. Boomishankar,Smita Chaturvedi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过配体辅助溶胶-凝胶法合成具有稳定四方相畸变的无铅BaTiO₃纳米颗粒，结合压电与摩擦电协同效应，实现高输出无铅压电纳米发电机，并揭示尺寸与键合工程对性能的调控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向自供电电子器件需求，需开发高输出、无铅的压电纳米发电机（piezoelectric nanogenerator, PENG）；同时需厘清纳米颗粒尺寸与化学键合对铁电与压电性能的影响，以优化器件输出。

Result: BTO-900（900°C退火，~54nm）呈准立方四方相，BTO-1200（1200°C退火，~104nm）为明确四方相。DFT表明BTO-900具有更强的Ti-O共价性、Ti 3d-O 2p杂化、更窄HOMO-LUMO带隙及更优偶极相干性。BTO-900-PDMS器件实现~100 V开路电压、~29 μJ储能与更优电容器充电性能；优化后的BTO-1200器件则展现~649 μW cm⁻²功率密度与~181 μA短路电流。

Conclusion: BTO-900的优异性能归因于缺陷辅助电荷捕获、界面极化与阻抗控制的电荷保持。研究表明，纳米颗粒尺寸与键合工程是通向高输出、无铅压电纳米发电机用于自供电电子的有效途径。

Abstract: Lead-free ferroelectric BaTiO3 (BTO) nanoparticles with stabilized tetragonal distortion were synthesized via a ligand-assisted sol-gel method to serve as the active piezoelectric phase in high-performance hybrid piezoelectric nanogenerators (PENGs) operating with triboelectric synergy. XRD, Raman spectroscopy, and Pair Distribution Function (PDF) analysis confirmed pseudo-cubic tetragonal BTO nanoparticle, annealed at 900°C (BTO-900, ~54nm) and well-defined tetragonal BTO annealed at 1200°C (BTO-1200, ~104nm). Density Functional Theory (DFT) calculations confirmed enhanced Ti-O covalency, stronger Ti 3d-O 2p hybridization, narrower HOMO-LUMO gap, and improved dipole coherence in BTO-900, indicating superior ferroelectric polarization, piezoelectric activity, and charge retention. BTO-900-PDMS PENG device generated a high open-circuit voltage of ~100 V and stored ~29 μJ energy, and superior capacitor-charging performance of BTO-900, while the optimized BTO-1200 based device showed a higher power density of ~649 μW cm-2 and short-circuit current of ~181 μA. The superior performance of BTO-900 was attributed to enhanced defect-assisted charge trapping, interfacial polarization, and impedance-controlled charge retention. By coupling the intrinsic piezoelectric response of covalency-engineered tetragonal BTO with triboelectric charge generation at the BTO-PDMS interface, this work establishes nanoparticle size and bonding engineering as an effective route to high-output, lead-free piezoelectric nanogenerators for self-powered electronics.

</details>

### [31] [Emergent Dimensionality in Diluted Power-Law Quantum Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07856)
*G. A. Domínguez-Castro,L. Santos*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究稀释幂律跳跃（diluted power-law hopping）模型中单激发量子行走（single-excitation quantum walk）的一维到二维渡越（crossover），通过纵向扩散定义有限尺寸局域化渡越，并揭示其特征横向尺度由参与动力学的本征态横向范围决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注稀释幂律跳跃体系中维度渡越的微观机制，该情形与近期发展的极性分子、里德堡原子等具有长程相互作用的量子模拟器（quantum simulators）阵列直接相关。

Abstract: We investigate single-excitation quantum walks across the one-to-two-dimensional crossover in diluted power-law hopping models. Using arrays of $\ell$ coupled chains of length $L$, we identify the finite-size localization crossover from the longitudinal spreading of an initially localized excitation. The crossover filling is well described by an exponential approach to its two-dimensional value with increasing $\ell$, defining a characteristic transverse scale $\ell_e$, and effectively two-dimensional behavior emerges already for $\ell\ll L$. An independent spectral analysis shows that $\ell_e$ is set by the transverse extent of the eigenstates participating in the dynamics, providing a microscopic interpretation of the dimensional crossover. Our results reveal an intrinsic eigenstate scale governing emergent dimensionality in diluted systems with power-law hopping, a regime directly relevant to recently developed arrays of polar molecules, Rydberg atoms, and other quantum simulators with long-range interactions.

Abstract: A machine learning (ML) framework for phase-diagram construction in condensed matter systems with unknown phase structure is presented. First, a sparse set of configurations is generated by Monte Carlo simulated annealing in order to train a contrastive neural network that produces a low-dimensional embedding. Consequently, representative phase configurations are used as initial conditions for gradient-based optimization on a dense parameter grid, enabling efficient generation of refined datasets for high-resolution phase-diagram construction. In order to optimize the workflow, a variant of the method, in which derived descriptors are used as soft labels in the contrastive objective, is examined as well. The framework is demonstrated on a PbZrO3-inspired model potential formulated in terms of coupled order-parameter fields and used as a physically motivated testbed for exploring configuration diversity and phase behavior. The proposed approach provides a physically informed route to ML-assisted phase-diagram construction that integrates data analysis with adaptive dataset refinement.

</details>


### [33] [Classifications in modular restricted Boltzmann machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08612)
*Elena Agliari,Andrea Lepre,Edoardo Roscani*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该工作将 Hopfield 模型（Hopfield model, HM）与受限玻尔兹曼机（restricted Boltzmann machine, RBM）的对偶关系推广至模块化竞争耦合网络，并证明在平均正交查询下经验权重构成学习动力学的不动点，同时给出非渐近漂移界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化关联网络中，Hebbian 模内耦合与反 Hebbian 模间耦合已知可实现模式解缠（pattern disentanglement），但其与 RBM 对偶框架的联系、以及训练动力学是否收敛到解析可用的权重解尚不明确。

Method: 构建由 L 个 HM 组成的模块化网络，其积分表示对应 L 个隐层相互耦合的 RBM；采用一步对比散度（one-step contrastive divergence）训练该模块化 RBM，将可见层查询映射为隐层读出的 L 元标签，并分析以经验均值给出的 RBM 权重的学习动力学。

Result: 当查询由平均相互正交的 L 个模式构成时，按 HM-RBM 等价性所建议的训练集经验均值权重被证明是学习动力学的不动点，且得到残差漂移的显式非渐近界；在更困难的任务中（每个模块接收 L 个模式混合查询），数值实验表明同一权重设置依然有效，网络可联合完成分类与解缠。

Conclusion: HM-RBM 对偶性可扩展至模块化竞争耦合情形，为模块化关联网络的分类与模式解缠提供了一个具有解析不动点保证、并经数值验证的训练方案。

Abstract: We consider a modular associative neural network made of $L$ Hopfield models (HMs), coupled so that intra-module interactions are Hebbian and inter-module interactions are anti-Hebbian; this competitive coupling is known to endow the network with pattern-disentanglement capabilities. The integral representation of this system coincides with an assembly of $L$ restricted Boltzmann machines (RBMs) whose hidden layers are coupled, thereby extending the HM-RBM duality to the modular setting. We then train this modular RBM, via one-step contrastive divergence, to perform a classification task in which a query encoded on the visible layers is mapped onto an $L$-tuple of labels read off the hidden layers. When the query is composed of $L$ patterns that are mutually orthogonal on average, we prove that the RBM weights obtained as empirical means over the training dataset, as suggested by the HM-RBM equivalence, constitute a fixed point of the learning dynamics, and we derive an explicit, non-asymptotic bound on the residual drift. We then turn to a harder classification task in which each module is queried with a mixture of $L$ patterns and we show numerically that the same setting for the RBM weights still provides an effective set-up, letting the network jointly classify and disentangle the mixture.

</details>

*Seokwoo Yeo,Philipp Gubler,Su Houng Lee*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 通过单核子中间态插入计算π介子耦合的四夸克凝聚（four-quark condensates），并纳入核物质中J/ψ介子的QCD求和规则（QCD sum rule）分析，在正常核物质密度下得到−1.75 MeV的质量移动，与格点QCD两π交换势推断的量级一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四夸克凝聚的密度依赖性（density dependence）是核物质中强子算符乘积展开（operator product expansion, OPE）分析的主要不确定性来源，其确定对N–J/ψ相互作用尤为关键，而该相互作用的长程吸引在格点QCD（lattice QCD）中似可由两π交换描述。

Method: 采用单核子中间态插入（one-nucleon intermediate-state insertions）评估π介子耦合的四夸克凝聚，并将其纳入核物质中J/ψ介子的QCD求和规则分析。

Result: 在正常核物质密度（normal nuclear matter density）下得到−1.75 MeV的质量移动，与拟合格点QCD结果的两π交换势所推断的约−5 MeV吸引同量级；而此前基于因子化（factorization）的处理需引入非自然大的唯象参数才能获得可比拟的移动。

Conclusion: 结果表明单核子中间态对介质内四夸克凝聚有显著贡献，并改善了算符乘积展开与长程N–J/ψ相互作用之间的联系。

Abstract: The density dependence of four-quark condensates is a major source of uncertainty in operator product expansion analyses of hadrons in nuclear matter. Their determination is particularly relevant to the $N$--$J/ψ$ interaction, whose long-range attraction appears to be well described by two-pion exchange in lattice QCD. We evaluate the pion-coupled four-quark condensates using one-nucleon intermediate-state insertions and incorporate them into the QCD sum rule analysis for the $J/ψ$ meson in nuclear matter. At normal nuclear matter density, we find a mass shift of $-1.75$ MeV, which is of the same order as the approximately $-5$ MeV attraction inferred from a two-pion exchange potential fitted to lattice QCD results. A previous factorization-based treatment required the introduction of unnaturally large phenomenological parameters to obtain a comparable shift. Our result shows that one-nucleon intermediate states provide a significant contribution to the in-medium four-quark condensates and improve the connection between the operator product expansion and the long-range $N$--$J/ψ$ interaction.

</details>

*Dunzhi Zhou,Chengyu Zhu,Gang Qiu,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出 2D-FET-Bench V2，一个由显微图像剥离二维薄片轮廓构建的 128 项版图任务基准，用于检验语言模型智能体能否按薄片外形生成可执行 GDSII 版图；脚本参考版图全部通过，最佳配置 GPT5.6-Luna 搭配 ReAct-3 的 pass@1 为 62.3%，覆盖率 80.5%，一致性 43.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剥离二维薄片上的场效应晶体管（field-effect transistor, FET）版图通常需针对每片薄片手工绘制，逐一匹配其在光学显微照片中的位置与轮廓；据作者所知，尚无可执行基准测试语言模型智能体能否可靠完成此类薄片专用构造任务。

Method: 基准包含 128 项版图任务，由显微图像导出的薄片轮廓生成，涵盖含孔洞薄片与多薄片任务；每项任务提供文本器件规格与轮廓坐标，智能体生成带类型的多边形与路径操作并渲染为 GDSII。确定性验证器（deterministic verifier）检查几何与结构要求，另设完整性检查确保给定轮廓未被改动。

Result: 脚本参考版图通过全部 128 项任务，证明任务均可解。对六个模型及 GPT5.6-Luna 的七种工作流与脚手架变体各尝试五次：六模型面板中表现最佳的 GPT5.6-Luna + ReAct-3 通过 62.3% 的尝试，覆盖率 80.5%，五次全部通过的一致性为 43.8%。ReAct-3 较单次 Plan-and-Execute 的 pass@1 高 27.0 个百分点，但 token 消耗为其 2.46 倍。专家对覆盖任务中每项随机抽取一个通过验证的版图进行审计，跨五种 ReAct-3 配置的接受率为 56.4%–63.5%。

Conclusion: 该基准用于评估几何与结构层面的 FET 版图构造能力；结果显示智能体已具备一定可解性但可靠性有限，验证器通过与专家接受之间存在明显差距，一致性亦偏低。

Abstract: Field-effect transistor (FET) layouts on exfoliated two-dimensional flakes are typically drawn by hand for each flake, placing contacts and gates to match its position and outline in optical micrographs. To our knowledge, no executable benchmark tests whether language-model agents can perform this flake-specific construction reliably. We introduce 2D-FET-Bench V2, a benchmark of 128 layout tasks built from microscopy-derived flake contours, including hole-containing flakes and multi-flake tasks. Each task supplies a textual device specification and contour coordinates. An agent generates typed polygon and path operations rendered to GDSII. A deterministic verifier checks geometric and structural requirements, and a separate integrity check verifies that the supplied contours remain unchanged. Scripted reference layouts pass all 128 tasks, showing that every task is solvable. We evaluate six models and seven workflow and scaffold variants of GPT5.6-Luna, with five attempts per task. The best-performing configuration in the six-model panel, GPT5.6-Luna with ReAct-3, passes 62.3% of attempts and solves 80.5% of tasks at least once (coverage) and 43.8% in all five attempts (consistency). ReAct-3 exceeds the one-pass Plan-and-Execute by 27.0 pass@1 points at 2.46 times the tokens. An expert audit of one sampled verifier-passing layout per covered task, across five ReAct-3 configurations, accepts 56.4% to 63.5% of them. The benchmark evaluates geometric and structural FET layout construction.

</details>

*Mahish K. Guru,Jan Bohlen,Louam Lemjid,Marius Tacke,Roland Aydin,Noomane Ben Khalifa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨能否通过学习方式，从镁合金的微观组织与织构反推其成分与加工参数（即“逆向”合金设计）。在107组镁合金挤压条件的数据集上比较三类组织/织构描述符与两种预测任务，发现传统晶粒与织构统计量表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合金力学性能由微观组织与织构决定，而后者又由成分与加工配方决定；正向调控已成熟，但从目标结构反推配方仍依赖专家经验，因此作者追问这一逆向步骤是否可学习。

Method: 基于自建的107种镁合金（magnesium alloy）挤压条件、覆盖14种合金的数据集，每条样本含光学显微图像与X射线织构（X-ray texture）测量。比较三种描述符：传统晶粒与织构统计量、预训练图像编码器的视觉嵌入（vision embedding）、晶粒网络上的图神经网络（graph neural network）；每种描述符分别接预测头完成两项任务：由工艺预测合金成分（Task A）、由成分预测工艺参数（Task B），并采用5折交叉验证（5-fold cross-validation）。

Result: 传统描述符在留出条件下识别正确合金的准确率为65%，而“始终猜最常见合金”的基线为17%，学习型嵌入（learned embeddings）均低于30%。工艺参数可被反推但噪声更大：相较仅使用成分，加入微观组织大致将温度误差减半。由于铸造合金种类与压机设置数量有限，两任务的输出均为离散取值，且在已知选项中进行有序选择的预测头优于直接预测自由数值的预测头。

Conclusion: 从微观组织与织构逆向推断成分与工艺参数是可学习的，但当前学习型表征未超越传统统计描述符；在有限的离散选项下，约束取值且保留顺序性的预测头是更有效的建模方式。

Abstract: The mechanical properties of a metallic alloy are set by its microstructure and texture: the size and shape of its grains and the orientation of their crystals. That structure is in turn set by a recipe, the alloy composition together with the processing parameters. Alloy development runs this chain forwards, tuning the structure until a target property is met. Running it backwards, from an optimized structure to the recipe that would produce it, still relies on expert knowledge. We ask whether this backwards step can be learned. On an in-house dataset of 107 magnesium alloy extrusion conditions across 14 alloys, each with optical micrographs and an X-ray texture measurement, we compare three descriptors of microstructure and texture: conventional grain and texture statistics, a vision embedding from a pretrained image encoder, and a graph neural network on the grain network. Each is paired with prediction heads for two tasks: the alloy composition given the process (Task A), and the process parameters given the composition (Task B). Under 5-fold cross-validation, the conventional descriptors identify the correct alloy for 65% of held-out conditions, against 17% for always guessing the most common alloy, while the learned embeddings stay below 30%. The process parameters are recoverable but noisier: compared with using the composition alone, the microstructure roughly halves the temperature error. Because only a few alloys were cast and only a few press settings were used, both answers are discrete, and heads that pick from these known options, while respecting their order, worked better than heads that predict a free value.

</details>

### [37] [A New Model for the Income Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08143)
*Ekrem Aydiner*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种动力学陷阱—扩散（kinetic trap–diffusion）模型，用于解释货币交换系统中帕累托分布（Pareto distribution）的涌现。通过动力学蒙特卡罗（kinetic Monte Carlo）模拟发现，帕累托指数显式依赖温度，取值范围为 $0.5 \leq \nu(T) \leq 1.5$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动力学交换模型（kinetic exchange model）中帕累托指数由微观规则固定，难以解释不同国家与经济条件下帕累托指数的经验性差异。本文旨在引入一个可调控制参数，以自然解释帕累托指数的变化。

Method: 构建动力学陷阱—扩散模型，将温度 $T$ 作为控制参数，通过热激活扩散（thermally activated diffusion）调节交换动力学；采用动力学蒙特卡罗模拟进行数值研究，并考察非均匀初始条件下的稳健性。

Result: 帕累托指数随温度变化，范围为 $0.5 \leq \nu(T) \leq 1.5$；零财富个体比例与基尼系数（Gini index）对温度呈非单调依赖，揭示由陷阱与货币流动性竞争产生的不同动力学区制；帕累托型稳态分布在强非均匀初始条件下依然持续存在。

Conclusion: 温度依赖的帕累托指数是该陷阱—扩散框架的核心创新点，可解释帕累托指数在不同国家与经济条件下的经验变化；单一控制参数的变化即可在同一陷阱—扩散动力学下产生不同的帕累托尾部与不平等区制。

Abstract: In this study, we propose a kinetic trap--diffusion model to describe the emergence of Pareto distributions in money-exchange systems. Using kinetic Monte Carlo simulations, we show that the Pareto exponent depends explicitly on temperature and takes values in the range $0.5 \leq ν(T) \leq 1.5$. In the present framework, the temperature $T$ acts as a control parameter that regulates the exchange dynamics through thermally activated diffusion. Unlike conventional kinetic exchange models, where the Pareto exponent is fixed by microscopic rules, the proposed model generates a range of Pareto exponents as a function of $T$. The temperature-dependent Pareto exponent constitutes the central novelty of the proposed kinetic trap--diffusion framework. This feature provides a natural explanation for the empirically observed variations in Pareto exponents across different countries and economic conditions. In addition, the fraction of zero-wealth agents and the Gini index exhibit a non-monotonic dependence on temperature, revealing distinct dynamical regimes arising from the competition between trapping and money mobility. The persistence of the Pareto-like stationary distribution under strongly nonuniform initial conditions further supports the robustness of the proposed mechanism. These results show that different Pareto-tail and inequality regimes can emerge from the same trap--diffusion dynamics through changes in a single control parameter

</details>

### [38] [Spontaneous Motion Generates Reversible Nonreciprocity in an Achiral Active Elastic Ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07050)
*Wen-de Tian,Kang Chen,Tianhui Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作表明，自发旋转可使具有互易被动相互作用（reciprocal passive interactions）的活性弹性环（active elastic ring）表现出机械非互易性（nonreciprocity），且方向性可由动力学状态选择与反转，并由活性反馈调控其临界放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易机械响应通常与内建定向耦合、结构手性（structural chirality）或外部驱动相关，而一个非手性活性体能否仅凭自身运动产生并反转这种方向性尚不明确。

Method: 以活性弹性环为模型体系，通过偏心弹性力（off-center elastic forces）重新定向推进力，从而将变形反馈耦合至运动；并通过反转旋转与补偿控制进行对比分析。

Result: 反转旋转会反转反对称响应；补偿控制在保留有限方向性的同时强烈抑制近Hopf共振（near-Hopf resonance）。逼近Hopf时，所选响应随与阈值距离成反比增长，并在阈值以上变为自维持行波变形（self-sustained traveling deformation）。

Conclusion: 非互易性由动力学状态所选择，而活性反馈控制其临界放大，说明非手性活性体可经自身运动产生并切换方向性。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal mechanical response is usually associated with built-in directional couplings, structural chirality, or external driving. Whether an achiral active body can instead generate and reverse such directionality through its own motion remains less clear. Here we show that spontaneous rotation makes an active elastic ring with reciprocal passive interactions mechanically nonreciprocal. Off-center elastic forces reorient propulsion, coupling deformation back to motion. Reversing the rotation reverses the antisymmetric response, while a compensating control preserves finite directionality but strongly suppresses the near-Hopf resonance. Approaching Hopf, the selected response grows inversely with distance to onset and becomes a self-sustained traveling deformation above threshold. Thus nonreciprocity is selected by the dynamical state, while active feedback controls its critical amplification.

</details>


### [39] [Mechanics of a Model Frictional Knitted Fabric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07372)
*Laura Michel,Antoine Faulconnier,Audrey Steinberger,Samuel Poincloux,Jérôme Crassous*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究分析由弹性与摩擦纱线构成的针织线圈（knitted loop）的形态与力学性质，将摩擦细纱线圈的稳定性归约为平面弹性杆（planar elastica）问题，并用尼龙针织物拉伸实验验证模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针织物的宏观力学行为源于单个线圈的几何形态与纱线间的摩擦作用，但线圈形状与其能量、稳定性之间的定量关系尚需厘清，尤其是摩擦在静止针织物多稳态形状中的作用。

Result: 摩擦使静止（无外力）针织物中出现多种稳定线圈形状；在细纱极限下，二维弹性杆模型可预测这些静止形状，并直接给出单轴拉伸的力-变形曲线。尼龙针织物的线圈尺寸与受力测量在定性与定量层面支持该模型的适用性。

Conclusion: 平面弹性杆框架可有效描述细摩擦纱线线圈的静止形态与拉伸响应，但该模型对致密针织物（dense knitted fabrics）存在适用性局限。

Abstract: We present a study of the shapes and mechanical properties of a knitted loop made of elastic and frictional yarns. First, for non-frictional yarns, we investigate how the elastic energy of a loop varies with its shape. This allows us to quantify the energetic contributions of stretching and bending, and to identify energetically favorable loop configurations. We then consider the case of thin frictional yarns. In this case, the stability of a loop can be reduced, even in the presence of friction, to a simple planar \textit{elastica} problem. Friction gives rise to a multiplicity of stable loop shapes in a knitted fabric at rest, \textit{i.e.}, in the absence of externally applied forces. We show that, in the limit of thin yarns, a 2D \textit{elastica} model can predict these rest shapes. Furthermore, the force-deformation curves under uniaxial stretching can be obtained straightforwardly within this framework. Measurements of loop dimensions and forces during uniaxial stretching of nylon knitted fabrics allow us to assess the qualitative and quantitative validity of the planar \textit{elastica} model. Finally, we examine the limitations of this model for dense knitted fabrics.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
