Abstract: Phase-field models offer an efficient alternative to self-consistent field theory (SCFT), but quantitative predictions of block-copolymer phase behavior remain challenging. We develop a quadratic-connectivity entropic (QCE) density functional and its adaptive extension (AQCE). QCE combines quadratic nonlocal connectivity with a nonlinear binary relative-entropy term, while AQCE adds bounded interfacial stiffness that preserves the homogeneous response. In lamellar benchmarks, QCE gives the lowest mean profile error among the tested models and AQCE the lowest mean period error. Compared with the optimized phase-field (OPF) model fitted to SCFT force and stress data, AQCE reduces strong-segregation period and profile errors by more than fivefold and sixfold, respectively. AQCE further reproduces the topology of the SCFT phase diagram for AB diblocks, a more demanding test of free-energy rankings among competing morphologies. Because architecture enters through ideal-chain correlations, the same nonlinear construction extends to other A/B architectures. We demonstrate this transfer for symmetric linear ABA lamellae, accurately predicting their profiles, periods, and ordering threshold.

</details>

Abstract: We propose an algorithm for identifying low-energy minima in systems containing thousands of particles, at moderate computational cost. We focus on spherical crystals, in which particles form ordered structures on the surface of a sphere. Our method uses symmetric seed configurations, or known structures, to initialize searches across contiguous ranges of system sizes. Newly identified minima are then used to guide searches at neighbouring sizes, allowing information to propagate efficiently through configuration space. We have tested this approach on the Thomson problem, one of the few interacting-particle systems for which global optimization has been considered for systems of this size. We substantially improve on many previous solutions, particularly at larger system sizes, where systematic global optimization is computationally expensive. Since favourable packings for the Thomson problem are reflected in systems spanning atomistic to mesoscopic length scales, the newly characterized defect patterns may inform structure prediction for a broad range of problems with spherical topology.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study size fluctuations of chemically active droplets in the regime of weak reactions and strong phase separation. ### [43] [Stress Tensor in Fundamental Measure Theory of Hard-Sphere Fluids: A Thermomechanical Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08289)

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文从任意无穷小形变出发，推导了原始 Rosenfeld 基本测度理论（fundamental measure theory, FMT）对硬球流体产生的局域压力张量（local pressure tensor），其由局域项与有限程弦项（chord term）组成，并在均匀与平面受限情形下验证了热力学一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典密度泛函理论中，非局域加权密度泛函（weighted-density functional）通常难以直接获得与形变耦合的力学量。本文旨在将形变法（deformation method）从局域场论推广至非局域泛函，从而导出 FMT 对应的一致压力张量。

Method: 对无限小形变求变分：将有限距离 FMT 核（kernel）的变分通过连接其两端点直线段上局域形变梯度（local deformation gradient）的线积分表示。所得对称压力张量包含以标准 FMT 加权密度（weighted densities）表达的局域项与有限程弦项；对单分散硬球流体，利用 Rosenfeld 测度间的微分关系将其约化为闭合一体的形式。

Result: 约化后无需独立的对密度（pair density）或不均匀 Ornstein–Zernike 方程。均匀流体极限下张量重现 Percus–Yevick 压缩率状态方程；平面受限情形下，法向应力满足硬壁接触关系（hard-wall contact relation），机械表面张力与表面过剩巨势一致，分离压（disjoining pressure）等于受限巨势对狭缝宽度的导数。

Conclusion: 有限程弦项使形变法得以从局域场论扩展至非局域加权密度泛函，为 FMT 框架下的力学量（应力、表面张力、分离压）提供了自洽且热力学一致的计算途径。

Abstract: We derive the local pressure tensor generated by the original Rosenfeld fundamental measure theory (FMT) for hard-sphere fluids from an arbitrary infinitesimal deformation. The resulting symmetric pressure tensor consists of a local term expressed through the standard FMT weighted densities and a finite-range chord term. For a monodisperse hard-sphere fluid, differential relations between the Rosenfeld measures reduce the tensor to a closed one-body form. We demonstrate that no independent pair density or inhomogeneous Ornstein--Zernike equation is required. In the limit of homogeneous fluid the tensor reproduces the Percus--Yevick compressibility equation of state. In planar confinement the normal stress satisfies the hard-wall contact relation, the mechanical surface tension agrees with the surface excess grand potential, and the disjoining pressure agrees with the derivative of the confined grand potential with respect to slit width. The finite-range chord term extends the deformation method from local field theories to nonlocal weighted-density functionals.

</details>

*Andrea Bonato,Davide Marenduzzo,Enzo Orlandini*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作通过分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations）半柔性聚合物与可穿线胶体环，提出将自组装拓扑凝胶（topological gels）转化为可编程软材料的三类设计原则：缠结迁移性调控、几何限域调控与聚合物重连拓扑重写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统凝胶的物理交联（physical crosslinks）是静态且空间局域的，限制了其自适应、自修复与机械做功能力；拓扑凝胶以物理缠结（physical entanglements，如穿线、链接与机械互锁）作为可移动的动态自由度，构成新的范式，但其可编程设计原则尚待建立。

Result: 得到三种控制模式：（1）通过控制缠结迁移性调节力学响应，淬火锚定（quenched anchoring）使网络存储弹性应力并屈服，退火锚定（annealed anchoring）则允许应力通过缠结重分布而松弛并保持全局连通性；（2）通过控制几何限域的程度或类型模板宏观结构，可将三维各向同性凝胶转变为跨表面的二维网络或具有独特图谱特征（graph-spectral signatures）的准一维拓扑环；（3）通过聚合物重连（polymer reconnection）实现缠结的动态重布线，在组装后重写材料拓扑，得到以简单成对链接为主的互锁结构，而非此前在可重连柔性聚合物中观察到的高度复杂连接态。

Conclusion: 该研究为自适应软超材料（adaptive soft metamaterials）提供设计原则，并为动质体DNA（kinetoplast DNA）、染色质环网络（chromatin loop networks）等生物拓扑网络提供启示。

Abstract: Physical crosslinks in conventional gels are static and spatially localised, fundamentally limiting their capacity to adapt, self-heal, or perform mechanical work. Topological gels, which are held together by physical entanglements such as threadings, links, and mechanical interlocks, constitute a different paradigm by treating connectivity as a mobile, dynamic degree of freedom. Here, by using molecular dynamics simulations of semiflexible polymers and threadable colloidal rings, we establish some general design principles that transform self-assembled topological gels into programmable soft materials. We present three different modes of control. First, by controlling the mobility of the entanglements, one can tune the mechanical response of the material. Quenched anchoring forces the network to store elastic stress and yield, whereas with annealed anchoring the material can relax stresses by redistributing its entanglements whilst preserving global connectivity. Second, by controlling the degree or type of geometric confinement, it is possible to template macroscopic architecture, converting for instance 3D isotropic gels into surface-spanning 2D nets or quasi-1D topological rings with distinct graph-spectral signatures. Third, by allowing dynamical rewiring of entanglements through polymer reconnection, the material topology can be rewritten after assembly, yielding interlocked structures dominated by simple pairwise links rather than the highly complex linked states previously observed in reconnecting flexible polymers. By establishing a direct link between microscopic entanglement dynamics and emerging mechanical function, our work offers design principles for adaptive soft metamaterials and provides insights into biological topological networks such as kinetoplast DNA and chromatin loop networks.

</details>


### [45] [Networks of triblock ellipses: from kagome order to disordered porous networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08522)
*Susanne Wagner,Gerhard Kahl,Carina Karner*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究利用形状与相互作用各向异性（anisotropy），稳定了具有两个对极吸引补丁（patches）的三嵌段椭球（triblock elliptical particles）的多孔表面组装体，其kagome有序度可调，并发现其成核遵循单步路径。

  <summary>Details</summary>

The resulting structures display a tunable degree of crystalline kagome order, controlled by quenching the temperature and/or changing the chemical potential. In particular, we find evidence that nucleation into the kagome lattice proceeds via a one-step pathway, in contrast to the two-step route widely reported in spherical triblock systems, making triblock ellipses an interesting model system for studying how self-assembly pathways change with particle shape. Beyond ordered crystalline structures, we find disordered porous networks with complex internal geometry. To characterize this range of assemblies, we introduce a particle-patch graph that explicitly captures the localization of bonds at distinct patches. Bonding motifs follow directly from this representation, while the loops, that we identify as the pore boundaries, can be determined from the graph's planar embedding. We detect all loops as the face boundaries in the planar embedding by means of a face-walking algorithm, distinct from shortest-path approaches used in ring detection. Together with a new local and global order parameter measuring the loop network's regularity with respect to a selected loop size, this framework connects microscopic bonding motifs to mesoscopic pore properties and quantifies the tunable order of these porous assemblies.

</details>


### [46] [Nonlinear Curvature-Free Periodic Folding in Origami via Symmetry Classification of Linear Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08635)
*Nan Cheng,Leon M Kamp,Yanxin Feng,Wenqian Sun,Katia Bertoldi,D Zeb Rocklin*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作利用群论（group theory）表明，周期性折痕图案（periodic crease patterns）的折叠性质主要由其空间对称性即层群（layer groups）决定，并据此建立了对称性、网络拓扑（network topology）与可展开性（deployability）之间的统一分类框架及预测性设计准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Miura-ori 等折纸折痕图案虽可实现保持空间周期性的刚性折叠（rigid folding），但一般而言周期性折痕图案需要额外折痕并折叠成准圆柱形（quasi-cylindrical）形状，其折叠行为的统一理解尚不充分。该研究旨在从空间对称性角度解释并分类这类薄板的折叠性质。

Method: 采用群论（group theory）方法，将薄板的空间对称性刻画为层群（layer groups）；在每个对称性类别内，依据不可约表示（irreducible representation）对允许的线性形状周期模式（linear shape-periodic modes）进行约束与分类。

Conclusion: 研究表明周期性折痕薄板的折叠性质在很大程度上由其层群对称性决定，并可通过对不可约表示的归类统一刻画线性模式与非线性应变模式的存现条件，进而支撑按需性质的新型折纸薄板设计。

Abstract: Broadly studied origami crease patterns such as the Miura-ori are capable of rigid folding that maintains their spatial periodicity. However, periodic crease patterns generically require additional creases and fold into quasi-cylindrical shapes. Here, using group theory, we show that the folding properties of such sheets are largely determined by their spatial symmetries, known as layer groups. Within each symmetry class, the allowed linear shape-periodic modes of sheets are constrained and classified by the irreducible representation they belong to. Such classification further gives a sufficient condition, containing a symmetry constraint and a topological constraint, for the existence of a nonlinear strain mode. In this way, familiar special cases are placed within a unified framework that connects symmetry, network topology, and deployability. Finally, we turn our classification results into a predictive design principle that enables the rapid design of novel sheets with prescribed properties.

</details>

### [47] [Inverse construction of exact atmospheric models and the 3.7--4.4\,mHz acoustic resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07073)
*O. V. Kaptsov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文以延迟时间（travel-time）表述将静止理想气体大气中的垂直传播线性声重力波（acoustic–gravity wave）化为带势 $Q(τ)$ 的克莱因–戈尔登方程（Klein–Gordon equation），并据此构建大气剖面与共振条件，用以解释3.7与4.4 mHz大气振荡及其对固体地球的耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型爆炸性喷发与地震会激发约3.7与4.4 mHz的近地大气振荡，并耦合至固体地球；已有研究仅知常数 $Q$ 的无反射（reflectionless）剖面，缺乏对一般可解大气剖面及两支振荡阻尼差异的统一解释。

Abstract: We study discrete-time quantum walks on weighted graphs, where every edge in an edge cut set is assigned a small weight $ε>0$. For the nonzero massive-gravity parameters considered here, the resulting spacetime is not asymptotically flat, and its large-$r$ behavior is governed by the massive-gravity contributions to the shape function. The Casimir energy density $ρ= -π^2/(720 r^4)$ ensures the Null Energy Condition is violated throughout the throat region, as demonstrated by the negative behavior of $(ρ+ p_r)$ and $(ρ- |p_r|)$ across all parameter values. Analysis of the volume integral quantifier reveals that the massive gravity parameter $m_g^2 c_2$ directly regulates the exotic matter requirement, with the integrated NEC violation becoming more negative as $m_g^2 c_2$ increases from $0.1$ to $0.5$. The mechanical equilibrium is rigorously established via the generalized Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equation, where the anisotropic force precisely balances the gravitational and hydrostatic forces. The quasinormal mode spectrum for scalar perturbations is extracted using both the analytical Mashhoon method and direct numerical integration. The effective potential forms a symmetric barrier at the throat, with the peak height increasing with the multipole number $\ell$. The damping rate $|ω_I|$ grows systematically with increasing massive gravity parameter. These distinct spectral signatures provide robust theoretical markers for distinguishing Casimir wormholes from black holes in future gravitational wave observations.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波天文学最关注 $s=2$ 情形，而 $s\neq0$ 时 $Ψ^{(s)}$ 与 $Ψ^{(-s)}$ 相互独立，守恒流方法无法给出单场能量范数，因此需要新的能量范数构造以刻画伪谱。

Conclusion: QNM 谱不稳定性是 Kerr 黑洞的普遍特征，且对引力扰动比标量扰动更强；同时 $s=0$ 与 $s=2$ 的结果提示 $H^{|s|}$ 范数模式。

Abstract: We study the pseudospectrum of the Kerr black hole for gravitational perturbations with spin weight $s=2$, the case of greatest interest in gravitational-wave astronomy. In the hyperboloidal framework with two-dimensional Chebyshev collocation, we formulate the quasinormal mode problem as a non-self-adjoint eigenvalue problem. For $s\neq0$ the fields $Ψ^{(s)}$ and $Ψ^{(-s)}$ are independent, so the conserved current method yields no single-field energy norm. We overcome this with the Teukolsky--Starobinsky identities, which relate $Ψ^{(-2)}$ to a fourth-order operator on $Ψ^{(2)}$ and yield an $H^2$ energy norm after symmetrization. With the $H^1$ norm known for $s=0$, this suggests the pattern $H^{|s|}$, verified for $s=0$ and $s=2$. All results use an $ω$-independent two-field hierarchy. We find that the pseudospectrum tightens by $1.1$ to $3.5$ decades at each step of $L^2\to H^1\to H^2$, yet stays large in every norm. The $s=+2$ and $s=-2$ operators are isospectral but not isopseudospectral: at twelve of fourteen probes the $ψ_0$ resolvent exceeds the $ψ_4$ one by $0.19$ to $0.82$ decades in the $H^2$ norm, while at the two probes furthest along the QNM line the ordering reverses by $0.18$ to $0.19$ decades. The gravitational pseudospectrum exceeds the scalar one by $0.5$ to $5.2$ decades in the window minimum and by $5.9$ decades in $L^2$ at $ωM=0.47-0.17\,i$, while along the damping direction the $s=2$ resolvent is non-monotonic and the $s=0$ resolvent smooth. QNM spectral instability is therefore a universal feature of Kerr, and is stronger for gravitational perturbations.

Result: 三条纹在 d=8 给出最强的总体响应。重新优化两个方位角使噪声加权 D-最优得分（noise-weighted D-optimal score）在 d=6 最高提升 7.3%，在 d=8 最高提升 35.6%。响应矩阵可同时拟合全部 14 个或 22 个实系数分量。最佳边缘化 1σ 灵敏度达到 10^{-9} m² 与 10^{-15} m⁴，对最有利分量较此前的平方反比定律（inverse-square-law）限约提升两个数量级。

As a consequence, (i) the Bondi mass-loss formula and the evolution equation of the angular-momentum aspect are those of the minimally coupled Einstein--scalar system, a statement that is exact in $λ$ under the assumption of a smooth expansion; (ii) the coupling generates no logarithmic terms through the orders considered, even though the Horndeski representation of the theory involves logarithmic functionals; and (iii) the coupling first enters a dynamical equation in the evolution of the $O(r^{-3})$ coefficient of the angular metric, through the cross term $-2λf'(\vp_0)\,\vp_1\,\partial_u N_{AB}$ between the asymptotic scalar charge aspect and the time derivative of the news. In Newman--Penrose form, the evolution equation of the leading coefficient of $Ψ_0$ acquires the source $-12λf'(\vp_0)\,\vp_1\barΨ_4^0$, while those of $Ψ_1$ and $Ψ_2$ are unchanged. The Gauss--Bonnet invariant decays as $r^{-6}$ and affects the scalar hierarchy only from the evolution of the $r^{-5}$ coefficient onward. </details>

Abstract: Artificial viscosity can be used to deal with shocks and other discontinuities such as star surfaces, that can arise in matter. These artificial terms are multiplied by a viscosity that is non-zero only in elements that are flagged as non-smooth by a newly developed smoothness indicator. Our artificial viscosity method works well in several simple shock test cases such as with Burgers' equation and blast wave tests. For the first time, we are also able to evolve single neutron star in full general relativistic hydrodynamics. To achieve this, we use different viscosities for the momentum density than for the rest of the matter variables.

</details>

Because the Maxwell potential grows logarithmically at infinity, the chosen finite Maxwell boundary term changes both the fixed Maxwell source and the renormalized action. For the example considered below, the black-hole branch satisfying the response conditions ends at a maximum temperature, and the coexistence curve has a $T\to0^+$ endpoint at two opposite values of the spatial Maxwell source. At a nonextremal crossing, the entropy difference is $S_b-S_s=S_b$. For these two static solutions, equality of both current components requires both charges to vanish.

### [71] [Natural Quenching of Inner Viscous Torques in Accretion Disks Around Mini-Boson Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07839)
*Sandip Dutta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究在正则 mini-boson star 时空中的薄吸积盘（thin accretion disk）内黏滞力矩（viscous torque）边界条件的适用性。结果表明：由于不存在坐标意义上的 ISCO，流体比角动量在深层微观边界趋于零，导致力矩被数学性抑制（torque quenching），从而提供区分黑洞与无事件视界超轻标量结构的现象学判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Novikov-Thorne 模型假设 ISCO 处为零力矩边界条件，放宽为有限黏滞力矩可显著提升黑洞吸积流的辐射效率。该机制在 Kerr 时空已确立，但在无事件视界的奇异致密天体（horizonless compact objects）周围的热力学可行性尚缺乏研究，故本文针对正则 mini-boson star 展开探讨。

Method: 数值求解耦合的爱因斯坦-克莱因-戈尔登场方程（Einstein-Klein-Gordon field equations），并采用 Lense-Thirring 慢转近似，构造出严格满足弱能量条件（weak energy condition）的精确无事件视界背景；随后在该背景中考察内黏滞力矩边界条件下的薄盘吸积动力学。

Result: 坐标意义 ISCO 的拓扑缺失根本性改变了吸积动力学：不存在突然的测地线坠落，稳定圆轨道深入稠密标量核，盘物理内边界由开普勒角速度峰值动力学决定；由于此处流体比角动量趋近于零，标准黏滞力矩被数学性抑制，黑洞中常见的边界力矩所导致的辐射通量散度增强在正则 boson star 周围被自然压制。

Conclusion: 此种力矩抑制（torque quenching）提供了区分标准黑洞与无事件视界超轻标量结构之间稳健的现象学判别手段。

Abstract: The standard Novikov-Thorne model for geometrically thin accretion disks assumes a zero-torque inner boundary condition at the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO). Relaxing this assumption to allow a finite viscous torque---driven by magnetic stresses bridging the plunging region---can significantly enhance the radiative efficiency and high-frequency spectral emission of black hole accretion flows. While this mechanism is well-established for Kerr spacetimes, its thermodynamic viability around exotic, horizonless compact objects remains largely unexplored. In this article, we investigate the application of the inner viscous torque boundary condition to a thin disk embedded in a regular mini-boson star spacetime. By numerically integrating the coupled Einstein-Klein-Gordon field equations and employing the Lense-Thirring slow-rotation approximation, we generate an exact, horizonless background that strictly satisfies the weak energy condition. We demonstrate that the topological absence of a coordinate ISCO fundamentally alters the accretion dynamics. Without a sudden geodesic plunge, stable circular orbits penetrate deep into the dense scalar core, causing the physical inner boundary of the disk to be determined dynamically by the peak of the Keplerian angular velocity. Because the specific angular momentum of the fluid approaches zero at this deep microscopic boundary, the standard viscous torque is mathematically quenched. Consequently, the enhanced radiative flux divergence typically associated with boundary torques in black holes is naturally suppressed around regular boson stars. This torque quenching offers a robust phenomenological discriminator between standard black holes and horizonless ultralight scalar structures.

</details>


### [72] [Gravitational-wave signatures of spinning particles around rotating hairy black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07850)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是探究次级天体自旋与旋转黑洞几何形变（hair参数）如何同时印刻于强场偏心运动的引力波信号中，并评估下一代空间引力波探测器对这类效应的探测与区分能力。

Conclusion: 更延展的形变最先在Taiji中可与Kerr区分，且该结论对无自旋次级天体成立，可直接应用于天体物理EMRI。自旋引起的频移与自旋线性相关，故所用大自旋是对真实次级天体退相位（量级约一弧度）的放大示例。

Abstract: We study how the spin of a compact secondary and a gravitational-decoupling deformation of the Kerr geometry are imprinted on gravitational waves from strong-field eccentric motion around a rotating black hole. The orbit is evolved with the Mathisson--Papapetrou--Dixon equations under the Tulczyjew spin supplementary condition, and the radiation is computed with the numerical-kludge method. The deformation describes a black hole only below a critical curve in the plane of the two hair parameters. The spin of the secondary pulls the pericenter inward and, above a critical value, carries the particle into the black hole. The hair raises this capture threshold, from $S\simeq0.66$ in Kerr to $0.9$ and above close to the horizon boundary, because it reduces the effective mass near the black hole. The emitted signal remains a discrete comb of orbital harmonics: the spin strengthens the pericenter bursts and shifts power toward higher harmonics, while both the spin and the hair leave their main imprint through an accumulated dephasing. The imprint of the hair is controlled by the parameter that sets the radial reach of the deformation, disappears in the Kerr-like limit of the family, and is amplified by the spin of the secondary. For a source at $2$ Gpc with $M=10^6M_\odot$ and $μ=10M_\odot$, a $6.8$ h segment of the signal carries a signal-to-noise ratio of about $1.4$ in LISA, $2.7$ in Taiji and $0.9$ in TianQin. Within the same segment the more extended deformations become distinguishable from Kerr, first in Taiji; since this holds for a nonspinning secondary, it applies directly to astrophysical EMRIs. The spin-induced frequency shifts are linear in the spin, so the large spins used here are a magnified illustration of a dephasing that, for realistic secondaries, accumulates to a phase of order a radian over an inspiral.

### [73] [Scale-Invariant Non-Collapse from Raychaudhuri Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07879)
*Rohit Dhormare*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文为超曲面正交类时测地线汇（hypersurface-orthogonal timelike geodesic congruences）建立了一个尺度不变的空间非坍缩估计（scale-invariant spatial non-collapse estimate）。在初始空间几何非坍缩及扩张、剪切、类时里奇曲率满足一致界的前提下，得到第一爆破时刻前体积下界 Vol ≥ κ_L(τ) r³，其中 κ_L 由 Raychaudhuri 比较解与空间度量畸变共同控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作推广了此前基于 Raychaudhuri 方程的构造：原方法通过扩张的比较估计控制沿汇输运区域的体积，但未定量处理空间距离的畸变。本文的动机是补入由扩张与剪切引起的空间距离畸变的定量估计，以获得尺度不变的非坍缩结论。

Method: 假设初始空间几何在尺度 r₀ 以内非坍缩，且扩张、剪切与类时里奇曲率（timelike Ricci curvature）满足适当的均匀界；对空间距离畸变进行定量估计，并引入标量 Raychaudhuri 比较方程的解 Θ 与畸变控制量 K = Θ₀/3 + Σ₀ 来构造比较系数。

Result: 在比较解首次爆破时刻之前，得到体积下界 Vol_{h_τ}(B_{h_τ}(p_τ, r)) ≥ κ_L(τ) r³，其中 κ_L(τ) = κ₀ e^{-3Kτ} exp(∫₀^τ Θ(s) ds)。对比较障碍之前的每个有限时刻，κ_L(τ) 均为正；当比较解具有有限爆破时间时，显式比较系数在该时刻从下方趋近时趋于零。

Conclusion: 比较障碍是估计的边界，其本身并不意味着物理汇出现焦点或时空奇点。该结果在结构上类比几何流（geometric flow）中的尺度不变非坍缩估计，但在数学上区别于 Perelman 关于 Ricci 流的 κ-非坍缩定理（κ-noncollapsing theorem）。

Abstract: We establish a scale-invariant spatial non-collapse estimate for hypersurface-orthogonal timelike geodesic congruences. The result extends a previous Raychaudhuri-based construction in which a comparison estimate for the expansion was used to control the volume of domains transported along the congruence. The additional ingredient here is a quantitative estimate of the spatial distance distortion induced by the expansion and shear. Assuming that the initial spatial geometry is non-collapsed up to a scale $r_0$, and that the expansion, shear, and timelike Ricci curvature satisfy suitable uniform bounds, we obtain, before the first blow-up time of the Raychaudhuri comparison solution, \begin{equation*} \operatorname{Vol}_{h_τ}\bigl(B_{h_τ}(p_τ,r)\bigr) \geq κ_{\mathrm{L}}(τ)\, r^3, \end{equation*} where \begin{equation*} κ_{\mathrm{L}}(τ) = κ_0 e^{-3Kτ} \exp\!\left(\int_0^τΘ(s)\,ds\right), \qquad K = \frac{Θ_0}{3} + Σ_0 . \end{equation*} Here $Θ$ denotes the solution of the scalar Raychaudhuri comparison equation and $K$ controls the spatial metric distortion. For every finite time strictly before the comparison barrier, $κ_{\mathrm{L}}(τ)$ is positive. When the comparison solution has a finite blow-up time, the explicit comparison coefficient approaches zero as that time is approached from below. The comparison barrier is a boundary of the estimate and is not, by itself, a statement that the physical congruence develops a focal point or spacetime singularity. The result is structurally analogous to scale-invariant non-collapse estimates in geometric flow, but is mathematically distinct from Perelman's $κ$-noncollapsing theorem for Ricci flow.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究声称共形宇宙学与标准宇宙学在Ia型超新星观测上存在不符，作者认为此类分歧的根源在于标架转换中物理量的解释不一致，因此有必要厘清两种模型的可观测预言是否真正等价。

Result: 在共形图像（conformal picture）下，Ia型超新星并非传统意义上的"标准烛光（standard candles）"，其绝对光度（absolute luminosity）随红移（redshift）变化，原因在于局部测量单位（local measurement units）的变动。

Conclusion: 两种模型在Ia型超新星视光度上具有经典等价性；所谓观测差异并非真实分歧，而是源于标架转换中对物理量的不同解释。

Abstract: This article demonstrates the classical equivalence between standard and conformal cosmological models regarding the apparent luminosity of type Ia supernovae. We show that previous claims of observational discrepancies stem from differing interpretations of physical quantities when transitioning between Einstein and Jordan frames. Specifically, we establish that in the conformal picture, SNe Ia are not "standard candles" in the conventional sense because their absolute luminosity depends on redshift due to variations in local measurement units.

</details>


### [75] [Horizon-Scale Corrections to Black Hole Geometry from a Conserved Quantum Atmosphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07934)
*Kashif Ammar Yasir*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文首次引入守恒的霍金尺度各向异性源（Hawking-scale anisotropic source），刻画粗粒化后的近视界大气，并在固定 ADM 质量下求解爱因斯坦方程，得到确定性的"着装"平均几何（dressed mean geometry），为后续随机引力（stochastic gravity）处理提供受控背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞通常被描述为量子场传播的经典几何，或被视为超出半经典引力（semiclassical gravity）完全量子描述的对象。霍金辐射（Hawking radiation）已表明黑洞并非严格孤立系统：视界带有热尺度，粗粒化后可能与涨落、耗散及环境关联相关。视界尺度开放性（horizon-scale openness）能否在任何度规随机化处理之前直接编码进引力背景，仍是未决问题。

Method: 引入一个守恒的霍金尺度各向异性源（a conserved Hawking-scale anisotropic source），用以表示粗粒化后的近视界大气；随后在固定 ADM 质量（ADM mass）条件下求解静态黑洞的爱因斯坦方程（Einstein equations）。

Result: 所得着装几何（dressed geometry）具有重整化质量函数（renormalized mass function）、自洽移动的视界（self-consistently shifted horizon）与修正的表面引力（modified surface gravity）。由于同一 lapse 同时支配视界与类时测地线（timelike geodesics），热响应伴随相关的轨道重构：有效势（effective potential）被重整化，ISCO（最内稳定圆轨道）内移，近视界停留时间增加。

Conclusion: 该构造并未在固定施瓦西背景（Schwarzschild background）上添加朗之万力（Langevin force），而是定义了一个确定性的着装平均几何，为后续在 Einstein-Langevin 框架中处理随机度规涨落提供受控背景。这确立了确定性平均场部分（deterministic mean-field sector）作为未来处理视界尺度开放性的随机引力方案的必要第一步。

Abstract: Black holes are usually described either as classical geometries on which quantum fields propagate or as objects whose complete quantum description lies beyond semiclassical gravity. Hawking radiation already shows, however, that a black hole is not an exactly isolated system: the horizon carries a thermal scale and, after coarse graining, may be associated with fluctuations, dissipation and environmental correlations. Whether this horizon-scale openness can be encoded directly in the gravitational background, before any stochastic treatment of the metric, remains an open question. Here we answer it by introducing, for the first time, a conserved Hawking-scale anisotropic source representing the coarse-grained near-horizon atmosphere and solving the Einstein equations for a static black hole at fixed ADM mass. The dressed geometry has a renormalized mass function, a self-consistently shifted horizon and a modified surface gravity. Because the same lapse governs the horizon and timelike geodesics, the thermal response is accompanied by a correlated orbital restructuring: the effective potential is renormalized, the ISCO moves inward and the near-horizon residence time increases. The construction adds no Langevin force to a fixed Schwarzschild background; rather, it defines a deterministic dressed mean geometry, providing a controlled background on which stochastic metric fluctuations may subsequently be treated in the Einstein-Langevin framework. This establishes the deterministic mean-field sector as the necessary first step for future stochastic gravity programs addressing horizon-scale openness.

</details>


### [76] [Time-Frequency Analysis for LISA: Galactic Foreground Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07977)
*Sudhi Mathur,Neil J. Cornish*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出一个快速前向模型，将参数化的银河系引力波光度密度映射为 LISA 时间延迟干涉测量（time-delay interferometry, TDI）各通道中按年调制的未分辨混淆前景，并据此构建 Fisher 矩阵以预报银河系结构参数的测量精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA 观测中将包含来自银河系的随时间变化、循环平稳（cyclostationary）的混淆噪声，这类噪声天然适合用时频分析方法处理，但需要能够快速连接银河系模型与观测通道协方差的建模工具。

Method: 沿视线方向的光度积分在 HEALPix 网格上求值并分解为球谐函数（spherical harmonics），探测器响应则被解析分解且独立于银河系模型，从而新模型只需一次地图求值、变换与收缩。协方差计算超出标准低频极限，将 LISA 响应展开至有限臂长（finite armlength）修正的二阶与不等臂（unequal-arm）修正的一阶。

Result: 有限臂长修正使归一化调制曲线在 3 与 5 mHz 处分别改变约 0.23% 与 0.64%；不等臂修正使自通道与交叉通道分别改变约 0.074% 与 0.71%。基于该模型与一个盘-核球（disk-and-bulge）银河系模型，由时变通道协方差构建 Fisher 矩阵并预报：一年观测可将盘标度宽度与标度高度（相对银心距离）分别约束至 8% 与 12% 水平。

Conclusion: 高度相关的核球分数（bulge fraction）与核球半径无法被单独约束。所提出的快速前向模型可高效支持银河系混淆前景的建模与参数预报。

Abstract: Observations by the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will contain time-varying, cyclostationary confusion noise from the Galaxy, which is naturally handled using time-frequency analysis methods. We present a fast, forward model that maps the parameterized Galactic gravitational-wave luminosity density onto the annually modulated, unresolved confusion foreground in the complete set of time-delay interferometry channels. The line-of-sight luminosity integral is evaluated on a HEALPix grid and decomposed into spherical harmonics, while the detector response is decomposed analytically, independent of the Galaxy model, such that a new Galactic model costs a single map evaluation, transformation, and contraction. We compute the modulated covariance beyond the standard low-frequency limit, expanding the LISA response to second order in finite armlength corrections, and to first order in the unequal-arm corrections. These effects change the normalized modulation curves by up to $\sim\!0.23\%$ and $0.64\%$ for finite armlength corrections at $3$ and $5$ mHz respectively, and by $\sim\!0.074\%$ and $0.71\%$ for unequal-arm corrections in the self and cross channels respectively. Using the fast, forward model and a notional disk-and-bulge Galaxy model, we construct a Fisher matrix from the time-dependent channel covariance and forecast that one year of observation constrains the disk scale width and scale height relative to the distance to the Galactic center at the $8\%$ and $12\%$ levels respectively, and leaves the strongly correlated bulge fraction and bulge radius individually unconstrained.

</details>


### [77] [Continuum Landscape of stable extra-dimensional metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08006)
*Sergey G. Rubin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究含二次曲率项（$R^2$、$R_{AB}R^{AB}$、$R_{ABCD}R^{ABCD}$）的修改引力（modified gravity），在非均匀度规下给出新的稳定性条件，并指出满足条件的稳定解并非孤立，而是构成连续族，从而诱导出有效四维参数（effective four-dimensional parameters）的连续景观（landscape）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在含二次曲率项的修改引力框架中，额外维几何由高维场方程与辅助条件共同决定，稳定解的结构及其对有效四维物理参数的影响尚需明确。该文旨在考察非均匀度规下 radion 激发（radion excitations）对应的稳定性，并厘清稳定解是否孤立及其物理含义。

Method: 在二次曲率引力框架下，将额外维几何由高维场方程连同辅助条件确定；针对非均匀度规（inhomogeneous metrics），推导 radion 激发下的新稳定性条件，并分析稳定解在解空间中的邻域结构与空间局域扰动（spatially localized perturbations）的渐近行为。

Result: 满足稳定性条件的稳定解并不孤立：典型稳定正则度规（stable regular metric）属于其紧邻的连续稳定度规族。由于有效四维物理参数依赖于额外维几何，该稳定度规的几何景观（geometrical landscape）诱导出有效四维参数的连续景观。景观中不同静态解之间的跃迁无法由空间局域扰动引发，因为扰动在 $|x|\to\infty$ 的渐近衰减保留了区分状态的边界数据（boundary data）。此外，文中注意到可能存在的拓扑跃迁（topological transition），即从双膜（two-brane）到单膜（one-brane）构型，对应于度规函数 $r(u)$ 正则零点数目的变化。

Conclusion: 稳定解构成连续族而非孤立点，因而有效四维参数亦呈连续景观；局域扰动受边界数据守恒限制，难以驱动不同静态解间的跃迁；度规函数正则零点数的改变暗示双膜—单膜拓扑跃迁的可能性。

Abstract: We investigate modified gravity with quadratic curvature terms ($R^2$, $R_{AB}R^{AB}$, and $R_{ABCD}R^{ABCD}$). In this framework, the geometry of the extra dimensions is determined by the higher-dimensional field equations together with auxiliary conditions. For inhomogeneous metrics, we derive new stability conditions under radion excitations. The central result is that stable solutions satisfying these conditions are not isolated: a typical stable regular metric belongs to a continuous family of stable metrics in its immediate neighbourhood. Since the effective four-dimensional physical parameters depend on the extra-dimensional geometry, this geometrical landscape of stable metrics induces a continuous landscape of effective four-dimensional parameters. Transitions between distinct static solutions within the landscape cannot be induced by spatially localized perturbations, whose asymptotic decay ($|x|\to\infty$) preserves the boundary data distinguishing the states. We also note a possible topological transition from a two-brane to a one-brane configuration, associated with a change in the number of regular zeros of the metric function $r(u)$.

</details>


### [78] [Regular black holes do not violate the first law of thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08099)
*Bobir Toshmatov,Bobomurat Ahmedov,Nozima Isamadinova,Chengxun Yuan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文分析正则黑洞（regular black holes）热力学中表观温度不匹配问题，证明在完整解空间上标准第一定律成立，而因子Ξ源于沿正则度规子族的投影效应，并非需要修正熵或内能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献报道广义相对论耦合非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics）的正则黑洞存在热力学表观矛盾：沿正则度规族对质量微分所得温度与霍金温度（Hawking temperature）相差因子Ξ，第一定律似需修正熵或内能。本文旨在厘清该矛盾的来源。

Method: 研究D≥4维带电正则黑洞族，其度规在四维退化为Bardeen与Hayward几何。将源写为固定耦合、无解参数的哈密顿密度（Hamiltonian density）H(P)，理论具有双参数静态解族，正则度规构成单参数子族。通过解析方法在完整解空间上验证标准第一定律。

Result: 解析证明在整个解空间上，标准第一定律以霍金温度、面积熵与视界势成立，符合非线性电动力学的一般第一定律。因子Ξ出现在固定长度参数下沿正则度规族微分质量时，Ξ^{-1}为该投影的雅可比行列式（Jacobian），等于视界内质量占比，并给出任意正则质量函数的一般形式；进一步证明Ξ^{-1}dM_{ADM}在双参数度规族上不是闭一次形式（closed one-form）。此外指出F框架与P框架间的勒让德映射（Legendre map）在D≥5近极端解视界外某半径处退化。

Conclusion: 正则黑洞热力学中无需修正熵或内能，表观温度失配并非物理矛盾，而是沿正则度规子族投影的几何效应，该修正无法表示为局部状态函数的微分。

Abstract: Regular black holes in general relativity coupled to nonlinear electrodynamics have been reported to exhibit an apparent mismatch in their thermodynamics: the temperature obtained by differentiating the mass along the family of regular metrics differs from the Hawking temperature by a factor $Ξ$, and the first law seems to need a modified entropy or a corrected internal energy. We examine this question for an electrically charged family of regular black holes in $D\geq4$ dimensions whose metrics reduce to the Bardeen and Hayward geometries in four dimensions. The source is written as a Hamiltonian density $H(P)$ with fixed couplings and no solution parameters. This theory has a two-parameter family of static solutions, of which the regular metrics form a one-parameter subfamily. We prove analytically that on the whole solution space the standard first law of thermodynamics holds with the Hawking temperature, the area entropy and the horizon potential, in agreement with the general first law for nonlinear electrodynamics. The factor $Ξ$ appears when the mass is differentiated along the regular metric family at fixed length parameter, a variation that moves through the space of theories and also changes the conserved charge. We show that $Ξ^{-1}$ is the Jacobian of this projection, equal to the fraction of the mass inside the horizon, give its general form for an arbitrary regular mass function, and show that $Ξ^{-1}dM_{ADM}$ is not a closed one-form on the two-parameter metric family, so this correction cannot be the differential of a local state function there. We also point out that the Legendre map between the $F$ and $P$ frames degenerates at a radius that lies outside the horizon of near-extremal solutions in $D\geq5$.

</details>


### [79] [Impact of Correlated Test-Mass Noise on Stochastic Gravitational-Wave Background Searches with Space-Based Interferometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08140)
*Jing-yi Wu,Yong Tang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究考察相邻检验质量加速度噪声相关性对空间引力波背景探测的偏差影响，证明忽略该相关性会导致参数系统性偏移，而完整建模可显著提升恢复精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机引力波背景（SGWB）是未来空间干涉仪的核心科学目标，但其探测依赖将统计贡献与仪器噪声区分开来；由于二者均贡献于数据协方差，不完整的噪声模型可能被误判为SGWB信号，从而偏置推断的光谱与参数。现有噪声模型通常忽略相邻检验质量加速度噪声之间的相关性。

Method: 在单链路层面模拟相邻检验质量（TM）加速度（ACC）噪声的相关性，经由运动轨道响应传播至时间延迟干涉（TDI）观测量，并用两种贝叶斯频域模板分析所得TDI自功率谱：一种包含相关噪声协方差（Corr. Template），另一种忽略之（Non-Corr. Template）。

Result: 当模拟数据中存在相关ACC噪声时，Non-Corr. Template 呈现系统性参数偏移，且偏移随相关幅度增大而更明显；相比之下，Corr. Template 能更准确地恢复注入的SGWB与仪器噪声参数。

Conclusion: 检验质量ACC噪声的相关性可构成空间SGWB搜寻中的相关系统误差，需在贴近任务实际的噪声模型与推断流程中显式纳入。

Abstract: The stochastic gravitational-wave background (SGWB) is a major science target of future space-based interferometers because it encodes information about unresolved astrophysical populations and physical processes in the early Universe. Its stochastic nature, however, means that its detection and parameter inference rely on distinguishing its statistical contribution to the data from that of instrumental noise. In particular, both the SGWB and instrumental noise contribute to the data covariance, so an incomplete noise model can be misinterpreted as part of the SGWB and thereby bias the inferred spectrum and its parameters. Here we investigate this effect in the presence of correlations between the acceleration (ACC) noises of adjacent test masses (TMs) in each spacecraft. We simulate the correlated noise at the single-link level, propagate it through the moving-orbit response into the time-delay interferometry (TDI) observables, and analyze the resulting TDI auto spectra with two Bayesian frequency-domain templates: the Corr. Template includes the correlated-noise covariance, whereas the Non-Corr. Template neglects it. We find that, when correlated ACC noise is present in the mock data, the Non-Corr. Template exhibits systematic parameter offsets that generally become more pronounced with increasing correlation amplitude. In contrast, the Corr. Template recovers the injected SGWB and instrumental-noise parameters substantially more accurately. These results demonstrate that correlations in test-mass ACC noise can constitute a relevant systematic for space-based SGWB searches and motivate their explicit inclusion in mission-realistic noise models and inference pipelines.

</details>


### [80] [Radiating Bardeen Black-Hole as a dark matter candidate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08224)
*Alan Sebastián Morales,Juan Ignacio Musmarra,Maurico Bellini*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在扩展引力与电动力学框架下，将变分作用量的边界项视为时空的几何量子涨落，构建由新联络生成的扩展黎曼流形，并计算Bardeen黑洞（Bardeen black hole, BBH）附近磁场分量的期望值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于引入变质量与磁荷的Bardeen黑洞，并在扩展引力与电动力学模型中处理变分作用量的边界项，将其解释为时空的几何量子涨落，从而统一描述引力与电磁涨落。

Method: 采用由新联络$\hat{δΓ}^μ_{αβ}$生成的扩展黎曼流形（extended Riemann manifold），该联络在黎曼流形背景度规上同时编码引力与电磁涨落的几何信息；并使用非线性非对易量子代数（nonlinear and non-commutative quantum algebra）计算引力与电磁势对应的磁场分量期望值。

Result: 结果与一个辐射性大质量磁单极子（massive magnetic monopole）一致，其产生局限于距离$r<\sqrt{3/λ_0}$的径向磁场分量，该距离受真空能量密度（vacuum energy density）限制；场$\hat{A}^μ$描述BBH附近的巨大质量光子（massive photons），形成类似原子的引力—电磁结构。

Conclusion: 该引力—电磁结构（gravito-electromagnetic structure）可能成为早期宇宙暗物质（dark matter）的候选者。

Abstract: We consider a Bardeen black hole (BBH) with variable mass and magnetic charge within the framework of an extended model of gravity and electrodynamics, where the boundary terms in the varied action are treated as geometric quantum fluctuations of spacetime. We propose that the extended Riemann manifold is generated by a new connection, $\hat{δΓ}^μ_{αβ}$. These connection components contain geometric information regarding both gravitational and electromagnetic fluctuations on the background curved metric described by a Riemann manifold. Furthermore, we calculate the expectation values for the magnetic field components using a nonlinear and non-commutative quantum algebra for the gravitational and electromagnetic potentials. Our results are consistent with a radiating massive magnetic monopole that produces a radial component of the magnetic field confined to distances $r<\sqrt{3/λ_0}$, which are restricted by the vacuum energy density. The field $\hat{A}^μ$ describes massive photons found in the vicinity of a BBH, forming a gravito-electromagnetic structure analogous to that of an atom, which could be a candidate for dark matter in the early universe.

</details>


### [81] [Self-lensing of gravitational waves from binary extreme-mass-ratio inspirals: Extended caustics and paired flashes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08399)
*Jiewei Huang,Yifan Ou,Yehui Hou,Minyong Guo,Bin Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究双星极质量比旋进（extreme-mass-ratio inspiral）中的自透镜效应，构建克尔黑洞（Kerr black hole）强引力透镜的几何光学映射，发现焦散附近信号可被强烈放大并产生双峰特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由双星极质量比旋进中的自透镜现象所驱动，探究紧凑源绕克尔黑洞运行时产生的强引力透镜效应及其可观测特征。

Method: 在几何光学极限（geometric-optics limit）下，由零测地线积分（null-geodesic integrals）构建点对点透镜映射（lens map），并利用雅可比映射（Jacobi map）计算单个像的应变放大率（strain magnification）。

Result: 透镜信号总体被缩小（demagnified），但在克尔透镜映射的扩展焦散（extended caustics）附近可被强烈放大；在合适的源—观测者几何下，轨道每圈两次穿越焦散，产生两个放大峰，其到达时间间隔取决于黑洞自旋、观测者倾角与轨道半径，经由源运动与传播延迟共同决定。

Conclusion: 这些特征有望用于约束源—观测者几何，并探测中心黑洞的时空性质。

Abstract: We investigate strong gravitational lensing of gravitational waves from a compact source orbiting a Kerr black hole, motivated by self-lensing in binary extreme-mass-ratio inspirals. In the geometric-optics limit, we construct a point-to-point lens map from null-geodesic integrals and use the Jacobi map to compute the strain magnification of individual images. While lensed signals are generally demagnified, we show that they can be strongly amplified near the extended caustics of the Kerr lens map. For suitable source--observer geometries, the orbit crosses a caustic twice per revolution, producing two amplification peaks. Their arrival-time separation depends on the black-hole spin, observer inclination, and orbital radius through both the source motion and propagation delays. These signatures might offer a means of constraining the source--observer geometry and probing the spacetime of the central black hole.

</details>


### [82] [Analytic solutions for the gravitational spin Hall equations and black hole shadows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08428)
*Marius A. Oancea*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将引力自旋霍尔方程（gravitational spin Hall equations）在波长一阶近似下解析积分，覆盖一大类存在解析零测地线（null geodesics）解的 D 型黑洞时空（type D black hole spacetimes），并给出含偏振相关修正的黑洞阴影（black hole shadows）形状解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论（general relativity）中的几何光学近似（geometric optics approximation）以零测地线描述高频电磁波传播，但局域波包在此近似之外会获得与频率和偏振相关的修正，该修由引力自旋霍尔方程刻画。论文旨在克服既有研究对该方程缺乏解析处理的不足，提供可解析求解的框架以刻画波的传播与阴影形态。

Method: 在波长一阶（to first order in wavelength）精度下对引力自旋霍尔方程进行解析积分（analytically integrated），应用范围限于 D 型黑洞时空（type D black hole spacetimes）中零测地线（null geodesics）具有解析解的一类情形。同一框架亦被用于推导黑洞阴影（black hole shadows）形状的解析表达式。

Result: 获得一大类 D 型黑洞时空中引力自旋霍尔方程的解析积分结果，并得到含偏振相关修正（polarization-dependent corrections）的黑洞阴影形状解析表达式。

Conclusion: 引力自旋霍尔效应可在宽广的 D 型黑洞时空族中解析求解，且几何光学近似之外的偏振相关效应能被一致地纳入黑洞阴影的解析描述，为研究此类修正的可观测特征提供解析基础。

Abstract: In general relativity, the geometric optics approximation describes the propagation of high-frequency electromagnetic waves along null geodesics. Beyond this approximation, localized wave packets acquire frequency- and polarization-dependent corrections to null geodesic motion, described by the gravitational spin Hall equations. In this paper, these equations are integrated analytically, to first order in wavelength, in a large class of type D black hole spacetimes, whenever the corresponding null geodesics admit analytic solutions. The same framework yields analytic expressions for the shape of black hole shadows with polarization-dependent corrections.

</details>


### [83] [Symmetry-Driven $k$-Essence Cosmological Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18790)
*Andrés Lueiza-Colipí,Nikolaos Dimakis,Genly Leon,Andronikos Paliathanasis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文研究一类由变分对称性（variational symmetry）筛选的 $k$-essence 宇宙学模型，聚焦于动能幂律项对 quintessence 的最简修正，通过守恒律获得精确真空解并借助动力系统分析刻画完整宇宙演化相空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望以场方程容许非平凡变分对称性为准则，筛选出一类 $k$-essence 模型，并考察其中最简单的动能幂律修正形式是否能够描述宇宙加速与完整演化历史。

Method: 方法包括：利用对称性生成元引入的守恒律求精确真空解；对宇宙学场方程作动力系统分析（dynamical systems analysis）；采用 Hubble 归一化变量（Hubble-normalized variables）与 Poincaré 紧致化（Poincaré compactification），分别在有、无无压物质源的情形下确定渐近行为与不动点的存在性及稳定性条件。

Result: 研究识别出全部容许的宇宙学时期（从早期到晚期），给出各不动点的存在性与稳定性条件关于自由参数的依赖关系，刻画了该对称驱动 $k$-essence 模型的完整渐近结构，并探讨了模型描述宇宙加速的条件。

Conclusion: 该 $k$-essence 模型能够成功描述暴胀时期（inflationary epoch），并自然地退出至物质主导时期（matter-dominated era），其中暴胀子（inflaton）最终演化为暗物质源（dark matter source）。

Abstract: We investigate a family of $k$-essence cosmological models selected by the requirement that the field equations admit a nontrivial variational symmetry. Inside this general family of theories, we concentrate on the simplest modification to quintessence, induced by power-law terms of the kinetic energy. The symmetry generator introduces a conservation law which we use to obtain an exact vacuum solution. We generalize our analysis and investigate the structure of the phase-space of the cosmological field equations by performing a dynamical systems analysis, both in the absence and the presence of a pressureless matter source. We employ Hubble-normalized variables and the Poincaré compactification, so as to determine the asymptotic behavior of the model and identify all admissible cosmological epochs, from early-time to late-time behavior. We discuss in detail the existence and stability conditions of each fixed point as functions of the free parameters, and characterize the full asymptotic structure of the cosmological history as provided by this symmetry-driven $k$-essence model. We explore the conditions under which the model can describe cosmic acceleration. We find that this $k$-essence model successfully describes the inflationary epoch with a natural exit to the matter-dominated era, with the inflaton ultimately evolving to a dark matter source.

</details>


### [84] [Universal Relations Meet Causality: Bounding the Dynamical Love Number of Neutron Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08546)
*Alex Kehagias,Antonio Riotto*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究将中子星潮汐响应的因果性、实性与被动性约束转化为上半复频率平面的全纯自映射，借由 Schwarz--Pick 定理给出动态潮汐形变领先修正的上界，并与文献中最新的动态潮汐响应计算对照，发现该上界在数个百分点内被饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频率依赖的中子星潮汐响应受因果性（causality）、实性（reality）与被动性（passivity）约束，此类基本物理原理可对动态潮汐形变（dynamical tidal deformability）的领先修正施加限制；本文旨在检验该理论上界与实际计算之间的符合程度。

Method: 在适当条件下将适当重标度的潮汐响应表示为上半复频率平面的全纯自映射（holomorphic self-map），从而应用 Schwarz--Pick 定理，得到动态潮汐形变对静态潮汐形变领先修正的上界；随后将该上界与文献中最新的动态潮汐响应计算结果进行对比。

Result: 对照显示上界在数个百分点（a few per cent）以内被饱和；同时文献计算表明静态潮汐形变与其频率二阶修正之间存在紧密关联，该结果定量证实了相对论区域中 f 模式（f-mode）主导性。

Conclusion: 将静态潮汐形变与独立确定的 f 模式频率相结合，或可给出一个不依赖状态方程（equation-of-state-independent）的动态潮汐上限。

Abstract: The frequency-dependent tidal response of a neutron star is constrained by causality, reality and passivity, which turn a suitably rescaled response into a holomorphic self-map of the upper complex frequency half-plane after some suitable conditions. The Schwarz--Pick theorem then bounds the leading dynamical correction to the tidal deformability from above. In this work we confront this bound with the most recent calculations of the dynamical tidal response in the literature which have also shown that the static tidal deformability and its correction at second order in frequency are tightly correlated. We find that the bound is saturated to within a few per cent, a quantitative confirmation of f-mode dominance in the relativistic regime. The combination of the static deformability with an independent determination of the f-mode frequency thus may provide an equation-of-state-independent upper limit on the dynamical tide.

</details>

### [85] [Quantum geometry of collective pairing fluctuations in the superfluid weight of multiband superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08336)
*R. N. Kalkan,M. Iskin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文指出多带超导体（multiband superconductor）超流权重（superfluid weight）除冻结配对贡献外，还含配对振幅自洽弛豫修正；该修正源于集体配对场空间的量子几何（quantum geometry），并可由软模支的小动量曲率给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多带超导体超流权重的传统描述主要聚焦冻结配对（frozen-pairing）贡献，而配对振幅在相位扭曲下的自洽弛豫修正来源尚不明确。作者旨在揭示该项是否具有几何起源，并厘清其与配对响应、Goldstone 模之间的关系。

Method: 在平均场（mean-field）层面处理吸引型多带 Hubbard 模型，将支配配对响应的热力学 Hessian（thermodynamic Hessian）与零动量静态高斯配对涨落核（Gaussian pair-fluctuation kernel）作对照；利用规范不变性（gauge invariance）把相位扭曲联系到配对场的有限质心动量，通过从 Goldstone 模演化的本征值支的小动量曲率提取超流权重，并构造相应的量子度规（quantum metric）。

Result: 在 T_c 以下各温度，热力学 Hessian 与零动量静态高斯配对涨落核一致；超流权重由自 Goldstone 模演化本征值支的小动量曲率决定，弛豫修正表现为配对构型流形上软本征矢量的刚度加权量子度规。同一构造给出带相同几何修正的 Ginzburg-Landau 梯度系数（gradient coefficient）、相干长度张量（coherence-length tensor）及配对质量张量（pair-mass tensor）。Lieb 晶格计算验证了三种表述的等价性以及超流权重与轨道嵌入（orbital embedding）无关。

Conclusion: 多带超流权重的弛豫修正具有集体配对场空间的量子几何起源，而非 Bloch 态空间的几何效应；热力学、涨落核与软模三种表述等价，超流权重不依赖轨道嵌入，并可与 Ginzburg-Landau 梯度系数及配对质量张量统一描述。

Abstract: Beyond the frozen-pairing contribution, the superfluid weight of a multiband superconductor contains a correction from the self-consistent relaxation of the pairing amplitudes under a phase twist. We show that this correction has a quantum-geometric origin, not in the space of Bloch states but in the space of collective pairing fields. For an attractive multiband Hubbard model at the mean-field level, the thermodynamic Hessian governing the pairing response coincides with the static Gaussian pair-fluctuation kernel at zero momentum at every temperature below $T_c$. Since gauge invariance relates a phase twist to a finite center-of-mass momentum of the pair field, the superfluid weight is set by the small-momentum curvature of the eigenvalue branch that evolves from the Goldstone mode, and the relaxation correction is a stiffness-weighted quantum metric of the corresponding soft eigenvector on the manifold of pairing configurations. The same construction yields the Ginzburg-Landau gradient coefficient with the identical geometric correction, hence the coherence-length tensor, and, with the low-frequency dynamics of the kernel, the pair-mass tensor. Lieb-lattice calculations verify the equivalence of the thermodynamic, fluctuation-kernel, and soft-mode formulations and the independence of the superfluid weight from the orbital embedding.

</details>
