### [86] [Correlated Low-Energy Model of Monolayer 1H-NbS$_2$: A cRPA+DMFT Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06912)
*Karollaine C. Leite,Jorge L. B. de Faria,Luiz Antonio Ribeiro Junior,Alexandre C. Dias,Helena Bragança,Alberto S. de Arruda*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作结合第一性原理 Wannier 插值、约束随机相近似（cRPA）与 DFT+DMFT，研究单层 1H-NbS₂ 中孤立的 Nb-\(d_{z^2}\) 单带关联电子行为。所构造的最小单带模型给出重整化局域相互作用 \(U_{\mathrm{cRPA}}=1.138\ \mathrm{eV}\)，可稳定强关联金属态并重现 ARPES 与 STS \(dI/dV\) 的关键谱特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层 1H-NbS₂ 拥有分离良好、以 Nb-\(d_{z^2}\) 为主的金属性单带，被视为在单带框架下探测电子关联（electronic correlations）的理想平台。此前报道的谱展宽需引入多轨道 cRPA 给出的大 \(U\)（约 1.8 eV）、格点间库仑项（intersite Coulomb terms, \(V\)）及电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）等复杂机制，本文旨在检验一致下折叠（downfolding）的单一局域相互作用是否已足以描述其低能行为。

Method: 采用第一性原理 Wannier 插值、约束随机相近似（constrained random-phase approximation, cRPA）与 DFT+DMFT 相结合的综合方法。将 cRPA 目标子空间直接定义于孤立的 Nb-\(d_{z^2}\) 带内，构建有效单带模型，并与静态 DFT+\(U\) 进行对比。

Result: 有效单带模型准确刻画低能电子结构，重现 ARPES 与 STS \(dI/dV\) 谱中的关键实验特征，包括特征性范霍夫奇点（Van Hove singularity）。静态 DFT+\(U\) 无法描述该体系的关联金属性，而 DFT+DMFT 成功再现谱权重的重新分布（spectral weight redistribution）与动态关联。cRPA 给出的重整化局域相互作用为 \(U_{\mathrm{cRPA}}=1.138\ \mathrm{eV}\)，该相互作用稳定了强关联金属态且在降温下保持稳健。此最小单带模型可捕捉与此前更复杂处理（较大 \(U\sim1.8\ \mathrm{eV}\)、格点间库仑项 \(V\) 以及电子-声子耦合）所报道相似的谱展宽。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅凭一致下折叠得到的局域相互作用，即可在单带框架下捕捉单层 1H-NbS₂ 实验观测低能行为的关键方面，说明无需引入更大 \(U\)、格点间库仑项或电子-声子耦合亦能重现其主要关联谱特征。

Abstract: Monolayer $1\mathrm{H}\text{-}\mathrm{NbS}_2$ hosts a well-separated metallic band of predominant Nb-$d_{z^2}$ character, providing an ideal platform to probe electronic correlations within a single-band setting. In this work, we present a comprehensive study of $1\mathrm{H}\text{-}\mathrm{NbS}_2$ by combining first-principles Wannier interpolation, constrained random-phase approximation (cRPA), and DFT+DMFT. We show that an effective single-band model accurately captures the low-energy electronic structure, reproducing key experimental features from ARPES and STS $dI/dV$ spectra, including a characteristic Van Hove singularity. While static DFT+$U$ fails to describe the correlated metallic nature of the system, DFT+DMFT successfully accounts for the spectral weight redistribution and dynamic correlations. By defining the cRPA target subspace directly within the isolated Nb-$d_{z^2}$ band, we obtain a renormalized local interaction of $U_{\mathrm{cRPA}} = 1.138~\mathrm{eV}$. This interaction stabilizes a strongly correlated metallic state that remains robust upon cooling. Remarkably, this minimal single-band model captures a spectral broadening similar to that previously reported in more elaborate treatments - incorporating larger local interactions ($U \sim 1.8~\mathrm{eV}$, derived from a multiorbital cRPA construction), intersite Coulomb terms ($V$), and electron-phonon coupling - demonstrating that a consistently downfolded local interaction alone can already capture key aspects of the experimentally observed low-energy behavior of monolayer $1\mathrm{H}\text{-}\mathrm{NbS}_2$.

</details>


### [87] [Disorder into Correlation: A Microscopic Mechanism for Sub-McIntyre Avalanche Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06915)
*Shafat Shahnewaz,Hannaneh Karimi,Joe C. Campbell,Avik W. Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文指出雪崩倍增（avalanche multiplication）虽本质随机，但实验中观测到低于McIntyre局域场理论下限的过剩噪声，其微观起源尚不清晰。作者提出：局限于窄前向锥内的极性光学声子（polar-optical-phonon）发射，与使场准直热载流子扩散出锥外的弹性合金散射（elastic alloy scattering）相竞争；声子冷却受抑制使电离路径窄于死区并同步化连续电离，形成动态阶梯（dynamic staircase），从而解释Sb基合金与HgCdTe的低噪声，并将合金无序（alloy disorder）确立为设计参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雪崩倍增（avalanche multiplication）具有内在随机性，但实验测得的过剩噪声低于McIntyre局域场理论（local-field theory）所预测的最小值；该噪声抑制的微观物理起源此前尚未被充分理解。

Method: 从微观散射机制出发，分析局限于窄前向锥（narrow forward cone）的极性光学声子（polar-optical-phonon）发射，与将场准直热载流子（field-collimated hot carriers）扩散出该锥的弹性合金散射（elastic alloy scattering）之间的竞争关系。

Result: 声子冷却（phonon cooling）受抑制使电离路径窄于死区（dead space），并将连续电离同步化为动态阶梯（dynamic staircase）。

Conclusion: 该机制可解释Sb基合金与HgCdTe所表现出的低噪声特性，并使合金无序（alloy disorder）成为一项可用的设计参数（design parameter）。

Abstract: Avalanche multiplication is intrinsically stochastic, yet experiments reveal excess noise below the minimum predicted by McIntyre's local-field theory. The microscopic origin of this noise suppression remains incompletely understood. We show that polar-optical-phonon emission, confined to a narrow forward cone, competes with elastic alloy scattering that diffuses field-collimated hot carriers out of the cone. Suppressed phonon cooling narrows the ionization path beyond the dead space and synchronizes successive ionizations into a dynamic staircase. This explains the low noise of Sb-based alloys and HgCdTe and makes alloy disorder a design parameter.

</details>


### [88] [Heavy-fermion hybridization waves from Dirac spinons near a Kondo-breakdown quantum phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07146)
*Pedro M. Cônsoli,Matthias Vojta,Onur Erten*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种理论机制，解释无磁序阻挫近藤晶格（frustrated Kondo lattice）中空间调制杂化波（hybridization waves）的出现，源于重费米液体（heavy Fermi liquid）接近近藤屏蔽（Kondo screening）崩溃的量子相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受不同顺磁重费米子材料中空间调制屏蔽（spatially modulated screening）近期观测结果的启发，论文旨在为无磁序阻挫近藤晶格中此类杂化波的出现建立理论机制。

Method: 采用部分子平均场理论（parton mean-field theory）与微扰计算，应用于一个最小近藤-海森堡晶格模型（minimal Kondo-Heisenberg lattice model）。

Result: 重费米液体在接近量子相变（quantum phase transition）时自然发展出杂化波，该相变处近藤屏蔽崩溃且局域磁矩形成狄拉克自旋液体（Dirac spin liquid）；机制强度与电子及自旋子（spinon）能带的运动学条件相关，杂化波的空间周期由连接狄拉克自旋子色散节点与电子能带低能部分的动量 Q 决定。

Conclusion: 杂化波的出现与近藤屏蔽崩溃的量子相变及狄拉克自旋液体态密切相关，其空间周期性由特定动量 Q 设定，为理解顺磁重费米子材料中的空间调制屏蔽提供理论框架。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent observation of spatially modulated screening in different paramagnetic heavy-fermion materials, we propose a theoretical mechanism for the emergence of such hybridization waves in frustrated Kondo lattices with no magnetic order. By applying a parton mean-field theory and a perturbative calculation to a minimal Kondo-Heisenberg lattice model, we demonstrate that a heavy Fermi liquid can naturally develop hybridization waves when it approaches a quantum phase transition where Kondo screening breaks down and the local magnetic moments form a Dirac spin liquid. We relate the strength of this mechanism to a kinematic condition on the electronic and spinon band structures and show that the spatial periodicity of the hybridization wave is set by a momentum $\vec{Q}$ connecting the nodes in the dispersion of the Dirac spinons to low-energy portions of the electronic band structure.

</details>


### [89] [Chiral Parafermions in Floquet $\mathbb{Z}_N$ Topological Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07156)
*Melissa Will,Harald Schmid,Jan Egger,Adam Gammon-Smith,Michael Knap,Frank Pollmann*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作在三步周期驱动的广义 Kitaev 蜂窝模型（Kitaev honeycomb model）中实现了 $\mathbb{Z}_N$ Floquet 拓扑序（Floquet topological order, FTO），并研究其边界与体态的非常规动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Floquet 拓扑序将拓扑概念拓展至非平衡（non-equilibrium）情形，可催生任意子动力学嬗变（dynamical transmutation of anyons）等新现象；作者旨在构建一个可实现 $\mathbb{Z}_N$ FTO 的具体模型平台。

Method: 在含 clock 自由度的广义 Kitaev 蜂窝模型上施加三步周期驱动，获得由受约束的 parafermion 与守恒 $\mathbb{Z}_N$ 规范场（gauge field）构成的有效描述；通过关联函数、radical chiral unitary index 及动力学手段进行刻画，并在 Floquet sweet spot（驱动退化为 Clifford）处采用 stabilizer 模拟，在偏离该点时采用短时矩阵乘积态（matrix-product-state）模拟。

Result: 驱动在边界产生手性 parafermionic Floquet 边缘模（chiral parafermionic Floquet edge modes），其传播由关联函数解析，分数化量子信息输运由 radical chiral unitary index 表征，并用其动力学探测交换统计（exchange statistics）；体态中借助非平衡 loop 序参量（loop order parameter）研究任意子动力学嬗变，短时模拟表明手性边缘响应在可达时间内持续存在。

Conclusion: 结果确立了 $\mathbb{Z}_N$ Kitaev 模型作为探索内禀非平衡拓扑现象的平台，并指向基于 qudit 的量子处理器（qudit-based quantum processors）上的实现前景。

Abstract: Floquet topological order (FTO) extends topology beyond equilibrium, giving rise to phenomena, such as the dynamical transmutation of anyons. Here, we realize $\mathbb{Z}_N$ FTO in a generalized Kitaev honeycomb model with clock degrees of freedom using a three-step periodic drive. The driven system admits an emergent description in terms of constrained parafermions coupled to conserved $\mathbb{Z}_N$ gauge fields. At the boundary, the drive generates chiral parafermionic Floquet edge modes. We resolve their propagation through correlation functions, characterize their fractional quantum-information transport through the radical chiral unitary index, and use their dynamics to probe exchange statistics. In the bulk, we explore the dynamical anyon transmutation with a non-equilibrium loop order parameter. At a Floquet sweet spot, where the drive becomes Clifford, these phenomena can be accessed using stabilizer simulations. Short-time matrix-product-state simulations away from this point show that the chiral edge response persists at accessible times. Our results establish $\mathbb{Z}_N$ Kitaev models as a setting for exploring intrinsically non-equilibrium topological phenomena on qudit-based quantum processors.

</details>


### [90] [Local-moment magnets on all close-packed lattices are equivalent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07158)
*Sam Studdy,Alwyn Jose Raja,R. Ganesh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文揭示了所有密堆积（close-packed）晶格上经典磁体之间存在的一种隐藏对称性（hidden symmetry），该对称性源于紧束缚模型（tight binding model）中的类似性质。对称性保证在仅考虑最近邻（J1）与次近邻（J2）耦合时，各密堆积晶格在 Luttinger-Tisza 层面等价，并由此构建了统一的相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fcc 晶格上的海森堡反铁磁体（Heisenberg antiferromagnet）是阻挫（frustration）的典型范例，而 fcc 只是无限多具有相同阻挫局域几何的密堆积结构之一。作者希望找到能统一描述所有密堆积晶格磁性的规律，并借此解释锂、钠金属低温下的无序结构。

Method: 从紧束缚模型中的类似性质出发，推导出联系所有密堆积晶格上经典磁体的隐藏对称性。在耦合限制于最近邻（J1）与次近邻（J2）条件下，论证各密堆积晶格在 Luttinger-Tisza 层面等价，并针对海森堡、XY 与 Ising 磁体构建以 J1、J2 为变量的统一相图。

Result: 对任意给定的 J1、J2 值，所有密堆积晶格具有相同的基态能量（ground-state energy）；其基态动量（ground state momenta）投影到堆垛平面（stacking plane）后完全一致。统一相图表明，在每个相中，即便计入多 Q 螺旋（multi-Q spirals），各密堆积晶格的基态简并度（ground-state degeneracy）相同。该相图可推广至由相似耦合的堆叠三角层构成的任意磁体。

Conclusion: 经典基态以上的涨落（fluctuations）不受该对称性约束，因此不同密堆积结构可能在有序-无序选择（order-by-disorder selection）中表现出有趣的差异。

Abstract: The Heisenberg antiferromagnet on the fcc lattice is a canonical example of frustration. The fcc lattice is but one of an infinite number of close-packed structures, all of which have the same frustration-inducing local geometry. In this letter, we demonstrate a hidden symmetry that relates classical magnets on all close-packed lattices. This symmetry derives from a similar property in tight binding models that may explain the disordered low-temperature structures of lithium and sodium metals. In magnets, the symmetry holds when couplings are restricted to nearest ($J_1$) and next-nearest neighbours ($J_2$). It guarantees that all close-packed lattices are equivalent at the Luttinger-Tisza level. For any given values of $J_1$ and $J_2$, they have the same ground-state energy. Their ground state momenta are identical when projected onto the stacking plane. We construct a unified phase diagram as a function of $J_1$ and $J_2$ couplings for Heisenberg, XY and Ising magnets on any close-packed lattice. In each phase, the ground-state degeneracy is the same on all close-packed lattices, even after accounting for multi-Q spirals. This phase diagram can be easily adapted to any magnet composed of stacked triangular layers with similar couplings. Fluctuations above the classical ground state(s) are not related by any symmetry. This suggests that close-packed structures can show interesting differences in order-by-disorder selection.

</details>


### [91] [Odd-parity magnon spin polarization from chiral four-spin exchange in a honeycomb antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07291)
*Bektur Murzaliev,Mikhail Titov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在补偿蜂窝反铁磁体中，手性四自旋交换（chiral four-spin exchange）在零磁场下产生动量依赖的磁振子自旋；温度梯度通过磁振子占据重分布诱发沿Néel轴的自旋密度，且可通过翻转四自旋耦合实现符号反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索零磁场条件下由多自旋交换相互作用驱动磁振子自旋（magnon spin）产生的机制，以MnPS$_3$所启发的磁几何为背景，寻求不依赖外磁场或量子化条件的新型自旋输运来源。

Method: 对补偿蜂窝反铁磁体（compensated honeycomb antiferromagnet）建立理论模型：考虑与层法线成$θ_0$角的Néel矢量，引入手性四自旋交换并与双轴各向异性（biaxial anisotropy）相结合，分析其对子晶格间磁振子配对（intersublattice magnon pairing）及两支纵向自旋模式的混合作用。

Result: 四自旋交换在谐振阶即贡献子晶格间磁振子配对，振幅正比于$\cosθ_0\sin^2θ_0$；双轴各向异性混合两个纵向自旋扇区，产生对动量奇对称的非量子化模自旋（nonquantized mode spins），而子晶格交换关系保持磁振子能量的倒易性。温度梯度经磁振子占据重分布诱发沿Néel轴的自旋密度；反转四自旋耦合会反转自旋密度与模自旋，但不改变磁振子能量。

Conclusion: 手性四自旋交换为补偿蜂窝反铁磁体提供了一种零磁场下产生动量依赖磁振子自旋的机制，并可通过结构修饰在符合模型对称性假设的条件下实现该相互作用；MnPS$_3$为该磁几何提供了物理动机。

Abstract: Chiral four-spin exchange generates a momentum-dependent magnon spin in a compensated honeycomb antiferromagnet at zero magnetic field. For a Néel vector tilted by an angle $θ_0$ from the layer normal, the interaction contributes to intersublattice magnon pairing already at harmonic order, with an amplitude proportional to $\cosθ_0\sin^2θ_0$. Biaxial anisotropy mixes the two longitudinal-spin sectors, producing nonquantized mode spins that are odd in momentum, while a sublattice-exchange relation keeps the magnon energies reciprocal. A temperature gradient then induces a spin density along the Néel axis through a redistribution of magnon occupations. Reversing the four-spin coupling reverses this spin density and the mode spins without changing the magnon energies. MnPS$_3$ motivates the magnetic geometry; realizing the interaction requires a structural modification compatible with the symmetry assumptions of the model.

</details>


### [92] [Two-exciton bound states in the Merrifield exciton model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07915)
*Ning Wu,Runtian Li,Dazhi Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文对有限尺寸 Merrifield 链中的双激子问题给出了完整的严格解，通过构造精确的双激子 Bloch 态并采用平面波拟设处理非均匀三对角矩阵，解析划定了双激子束缚态（bound states, BSs）存在的参数区域，并证明至多存在两类束缚态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解双激子束缚态（bound states, BSs）的形成与动力学对于利用多激子现象、设计下一代有机半导体材料至关重要。尽管分子间激子-激子相互作用或多能级分子内构型等机制均可产生此类束缚态，但其底层晶格动力学的根本理解仍是关键挑战，Merrifield 激子模型在此背景下被视为刻画单格点双激发态与双粒子散射连续谱之间量子混合的重要框架。

Method: 构建一组精确的双激子 Bloch 态（Bloch states），并应用平面波拟设（plane-wave ansatz）处理由此产生的非均匀三对角矩阵（inhomogeneous tridiagonal matrices），从而在有限尺寸 Merrifield 链（Merrifield chain）中对双激子问题进行完整、严格的求解。

Result: 解析地映射出支持双激子束缚态的参数区域，证明至多只能出现两类不同的束缚态，其分别位于散射连续谱（scattering continuum）之上或之下；并定量解释了这两类束缚态在整个布里渊区（Brillouin zone）内随总波数变化的演化行为。

Conclusion: 所获得的本征能量与波函数的闭式表达式（closed-form expressions）为探索相关分子聚集体（molecular aggregates）中的激子动力学与光学响应提供了有价值的基准。

Abstract: Understanding the formation and dynamics of two-exciton bound states (BSs) is crucial for harnessing multi-exciton phenomena and designing next-generation organic semiconductor materials. While various mechanisms such as intermolecular exciton-exciton interactions or multi-level intramolecular configurations can generate these BSs, a fundamental understanding of their underlying lattice dynamics remains a key challenge. Within this context, the Merrifield exciton model stands out as a premier framework for capturing the intricate quantum mixing between single-site doubly-excited states and two-particle scattering continuum. In this work, by constructing a set of exact two-exciton Bloch states and applying a plane-wave ansatz to treat the resulting inhomogeneous tridiagonal matrices, we provide a complete, rigorous solution to the two-exciton problem in a finite-size Merrifield chain. We analytically map the parameter regions that support two-exciton BSs, proving that at most two distinct types of BSs can emerge, located either above or below the scattering continuum. Furthermore, we provide a quantitative interpretation of how these two types of BSs evolve as a function of the total wave number within the whole Brillouin zone. The obtained closed-form expressions for the eigenenergies and wave functions offer valuable benchmarks for exploring exciton dynamics and optical responses in related molecular aggregates.

</details>


### [93] [Gradual Localization of Itinerant Cobalt 3d Electrons in the Pyrochlore Ferromagnetic Metal LuInCo4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08266)
*Taiki Shiotani,Subaru Yoshimoto,Yoshikazu Tabata,Yusuke Nambu,Katsuki Kinjo,Kazuhiro Nawa,Taku J. Sato,Takeshi Waki,Hiroyuki Nakamura*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过中子衍射（neutron diffraction）测定LuInCo4的微观磁性，确认其为零场下q=0共线铁磁结构，并观测到随冷却展宽的弥散磁散射，提出以巡游Co-3d电子渐进局域化解释低温变磁转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注钴基金属烧绿石（pyrochlore）化合物中电子局域化与巡游电子铁磁性（itinerant-electron ferromagnetism）之间的相互作用，而LuInCo4的微观磁态尚需明确以理解其低温变磁转变（metamagnetic transition）的来源。

Method: 采用粉末与单晶中子衍射（powder and single-crystal neutron diffraction）测量，分析零场下的磁衍射图谱，并检验反铁磁序（antiferromagnetic order）与长周期螺旋磁调制（helical magnetic modulation）是否存在，同时考察q=0附近的弥散磁散射（diffuse magnetic scattering）随温度的演化。

Result: 粉末中子衍射图谱可由简单q=0共线铁磁结构良好描述，有序Co磁矩为0.9 μB；单晶实验表明反铁磁序与长周期螺旋磁调制均不存在。在q=0附近观测到弥散磁散射，其倒空间强度分布随冷却逐渐展宽。

Conclusion: 基于上述观测，作者提出低温变磁转变可能源于巡游Co-3d电子渐进局域化所增强的铁磁不稳定性，并认为LuInCo4可作为研究钴基金属烧绿石中电子局域化与巡游电子铁磁性相互作用的模型体系。

Abstract: We investigated the microscopic magnetic state of the itinerant-electron ferromagnet LuInCo4 with a cobalt pyrochlore lattice by means of powder and single-crystal neutron diffraction measurements. The powder neutron diffraction profiles at zero field are well described by a simple q = 0 collinear ferromagnetic structure with an ordered Co moment of 0.9 uB. Single-crystal neutron diffraction experiments further suggest the absence of both antiferromagnetic order and long-period helical magnetic modulation. In addition, diffuse magnetic scattering was observed around q = 0, with its intensity distribution gradually broadened in reciprocal space upon cooling. Based on these observations, we propose a possible microscopic origin of the low-temperature metamagnetic transition in terms of a ferromagnetic instability enhanced by gradual localization of the itinerant Co-3d electrons. These results suggest that LuInCo4 provides a model system for investigating the interplay between electron localization and itinerant-electron ferromagnetism in cobalt-based metallic pyrochlore compounds.

</details>


### [94] [Symmetry protected quantum many body scars through half-gauging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08304)
*Weslei B. Fontana,Fabrizio G. Oliviero,Yi-Ping Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究对称性缺陷（symmetry defects）插入与量子多体疤痕（quantum many-body scarring）之间的相互作用，并给出半规范化（half-gauging）情形下受对称性保护的疤痕态分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性缺陷插入与量子多体疤痕现象之间的关系尚待澄清；同时，对偶性（dualities）与半规范化操作在此类疤痕模型中的后果也值得系统探索。

Method: 采用由嵌入机制（embedding mechanism）构造的一类模型，其特征为随机矩阵形式哈密顿量（stochastic matrix form Hamiltonian），并承载零能量多体疤痕态。基于该模型族，首先分析缺陷插入后疤痕态仍存在的条件，随后对链状系统进行半规范化的详细分析。

Result: 确定了在该类模型内，缺陷插入后仍保有疤痕态（scarred states）所需的条件；半规范化在系统中引入了非平凡界面（non-trivial interface），并导致由底层对称性保护的疤痕态。

Conclusion: 关于半规范化疤痕系统的结论具有一般性，可为更高维疤痕物理（higher dimension scarred physics）及其鲁棒性（robustness）研究提供有前景的方向。

Abstract: We study the interplay between symmetry defects insertion and the phenomena of quantum many body scarring. We exploit a class of models obtained from an embedding mechanism that defines a stochastic matrix form hamiltonian, hosting zero energy many body scarred states. Such models display an interesting playground to explore the consequences of dualities, half-gauging operations and so on. We first show that inside this class of models, what are the conditions to still have scared states after defect insertion. The main result of our work is a detailed analysis of half-gauging in such chains, which introduces a non-trivial interface in our system, leading to scarred states protected by the underlying symmetries. The conclusions for half-gauged scarred systems are general and may lead to fruitful directions for higher dimension scarred physics and their robustness.

</details>


### [95] [Exchange self-energy and vertex corrections to static screening in the two-dimensional electron gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08309)
*Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文解析计算二维电子气的领头超越环图（beyond-ring）修正，包括两个交换自能图与交换顶点图，并代入RPA几何级数得到静态介电函数与屏蔽相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RPA屏蔽与Thomas-Fermi屏蔽无法刻画交换效应导致的多体修正，尤其对二维体系中Kohn反常（Kohn anomaly）与Friedel振荡的描述存在定性不足，需引入一阶库仑耦合的交换图修正。

Method: 解析计算二维电子气静态不可约极化率的领头超越环图修正：两个交换自能图（exchange self-energy diagrams）与一个交换顶点图（exchange vertex diagram），均为库仑耦合的一阶；将所得极化率代入RPA几何级数（geometric series）以获得静态介电函数（dielectric function）与屏蔽相互作用。

Result: 自能与顶点贡献各自发散，且两者发散部分均以闭式（closed form）给出；两者之和有限，并精确重现Hartree-Fock压缩率（compressibility），进而对Thomas-Fermi波矢进行重整化。交换效应将2k_F处单侧二维Kohn拐点（Kohn kink）转变为尖锐的双侧峰。实空间中使带电杂质周围的Friedel振荡比RPA大2-3倍，且在研究范围内振幅随距离增加。

Conclusion: 交换修正相对于Thomas-Fermi与RPA屏蔽在Kohn拐点形态上具有定性改进，并显著增强Friedel振荡，文中讨论了其对Kohn-Luttinger配对（Kohn-Luttinger pairing）的意义以及与先前同图工作的关系。

Abstract: We compute analytically the leading beyond-ring corrections to the static irreducible polarizability of the two-dimensional (2D) electron gas, namely the two exchange self-energy diagrams and the exchange vertex diagram, each of first order in the Coulomb coupling, and insert the resulting polarizability into the RPA geometric series to obtain the static dielectric function and screened interaction. The self-energy and vertex contributions individually diverge; we obtain both divergent pieces in closed form. Their sum is finite and reproduces exactly the Hartree-Fock compressibility, which renormalizes the Thomas-Fermi wave vector. We show that exchange converts the one-sided 2D Kohn kink at 2k_F into a sharp two-sided peak, a qualitative improvement over Thomas-Fermi and RPA screening. In real space this makes the Friedel oscillations around a charged impurity 2-3 times larger than in RPA, with an amplitude that increases with distance over the range studied; implications for Kohn-Luttinger pairing are discussed. Relation to earlier works on the same diagrams is discussed.

</details>


### [96] [Incoherent Spectral Weight Emerging from a Van Hove Singularity in a Kagome Metal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08356)
*C. -y. Lim,J. Deng,A. Korshunov,A. Kar,D. Subires,H. Li,Y. Jiang,H. Hu,E. Modin,P. Törmä,A. Kumar-Sharma,C. Shekhar,A. Louat,T. K. Kim,C. Felser,B. Andrei Bernevig,S. Blanco-Canosa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在 kagome 金属 LuFe6Ge6 中，极化依赖 ARPES 观测到 L 点处延伸数百 meV 的竖直"瀑布状"（waterfall-like）谱特征，轨道以 Fe dyz 为主，其能量起始点与 EF 下方约 0.3 eV 的范霍夫奇点（van Hove singularity）吻合；但 DMFT 显示电子关联很弱，计算能带与实验体态能带高度一致，作者提出该反常谱权重可能源自出射光电子的非弹性散射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARPES 观测到的瀑布状谱特征通常被归因于强电子关联（strong electronic correlations）或电子-玻色子耦合（electron-boson coupling）。本文旨在检验这一普遍关联，选取结构简单的 FeGe 衍生家族成员 LuFe6Ge6 作为研究体系，以厘清该谱特征的真正来源。

Method: 结合极化依赖角分辨光电子能谱（polarization-dependent ARPES）、密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）与动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT）对 LuFe6Ge6 的电子结构进行联合研究。

Result: 在 L 点观测到延伸数百 meV 束缚能的显著竖直谱特征，其轨道特征主要为 Fe dyz，能量起始点与 EF 下方约 0.3 eV 的范霍夫奇点（van Hove singularity）一致。尽管存在瀑布状谱响应，DMFT 仅给出弱电子关联，且计算电子结构与实验体态能带吻合良好。

Conclusion: 作者提出反常谱权重可能来自出射光电子的非弹性散射（inelastic scattering），并可能与范霍夫奇点附近的高态密度相关。结果表明瀑布状特征可以在无强电子关联的情况下出现，提示底层能带结构与光电子能量损失过程在其解释中的潜在作用。

Abstract: Waterfall-like spectral features observed by angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) are commonly associated with strong electronic correlations or electron-boson coupling. Here, we investigate this connection in the kagome metal LuFe6Ge6, a structurally simple member of the FeGe-derived family, using polarization-dependent ARPES, density functional theory and dynamical mean-field theory. We observe a pronounced vertical spectral feature at the L point extending over several hundred meV in binding energy. Its orbital character is identified as predominantly Fe dyz, and its energy onset coincides with a van Hove singularity located approximately 0.3 eV below EF . Despite the waterfall-like spectral response, dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) reveals only weak electronic correlations, while the calculated electronic structure closely reproduces the experimental bulk bands. We propose that the anomalous spectral weight may arise from inelastic scattering of the outgoing photoelectrons, potentially associated with the large density of states near the van Hove singularity. Our results suggest that waterfall-like features can emerge without strong electronic correlations and highlight the possible role of the underlying band structure and photoelectron energy-loss processes in their interpretation.

</details>


### [97] [Dynamical vertex approximation for retarded interactions: Application to the Hubbard-Holstein model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08512)
*Emin Moghadas,Samuele Giuli,Massimo Capone,Alessandro Toschi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作将动力学顶点近似（dynamical vertex approximation, DΓA）的梯形版本（ladder-version）推广至含推迟电子相互作用（retarded electronic interactions）的系统，提出"dynamical-$U$ DΓA"方案，并在Hubbard-Holstein模型上验证其适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有DΓA主要处理瞬时相互作用，难以刻画电子与玻色子耦合（如声子）导致的推迟相互作用；同时需要在动力学平均场（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT）的局域关联之上纳入全尺度非局域空间关联。

Method: 将最常用的梯形版本DΓA扩展至推迟相互作用情形，命名为"dynamical-$U$ DΓA"；以单一无色散声子模（dispersionless phononic mode）耦合为例，在正方与立方晶格上研究Hubbard-Holstein模型，聚焦Hubbard相互作用较强、强电子关联与声子介导相互作用相互竞争的区域。

Result: 展示了该方法的可行性：在三维模型中考察反铁磁相（antiferromagnetic phase），在二维情形考察d波超导体（d-wave superconductor），并分析电子-声子耦合（含其频率依赖）对这两种不稳定性的影响。

Conclusion: dynamical-$U$ DΓA可在包含非局域空间关联的同时处理推迟电子相互作用，适用于研究强关联电子与玻色子耦合体系中的磁性及超导不稳定性。

Abstract: We extend the dynamical vertex approximation (D$Γ$A), in its most commonly used ladder-version, to systems with retarded electronic interactions. Such an extended scheme, which we name "dynamical-$U$ D$Γ$A", allows us to study strongly interacting electrons coupled to bosons including non-local spatial correlations on all length scales on top of the purely local ones captured by dynamical mean-field theory. We demonstrate the applicability of the method in the specific case of the coupling to a single dispersionless phononic mode, by studying the Hubbard-Holstein model on the square and cubic lattices, focusing on regimes of sizable Hubbard interactions where strong electronic correlations compete with phonon-mediated interactions. As illustrative examples, we consider the antiferromagnetic phase in the three-dimensional model and the d-wave superconductor in two dimensions, analyzing the effect of the electron-phonon coupling, including its frequency dependence, on both instabilities.

</details>


### [98] [Ghost rotationally invariant slave-boson approach to response functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08575)
*Lee Tsung-Han,Melnick Corey,Lanatà Nicola,Kotliar Gabriel*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作为鬼旋转不变从玻色子（ghost rotationally invariant slave-boson, gRISB）理论提出有限温度高斯涨落（Gaussian-fluctuation）响应函数形式，并应用于二维哈伯德模型（two-dimensional Hubbard model），其磁相边界与静态自旋/电荷磁化率能再现动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT）的趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准从玻色子理论（RISB）与随机相近似（random phase approximation, RPA）在描述关联体系的响应函数时精度不足，需发展更准确的形式以逼近DMFT结果并避免数值解析延拓等问题。

Method: 在gRISB框架下构建有限温度高斯涨落形式，直接在实频轴（real-frequency axis）上计算动态磁化率；在RISB层面可回到Kotliar-Ruckenstein响应，并引入以缀饰格林函数（dressed Green's functions）和顶点修正（vertex corrections）表述响应的守恒准粒子近似（conserving quasiparticle approximation）。

Result: gRISB给出的磁相边界与静态自旋、电荷磁化率捕捉到DMFT的趋势，显著优于标准RISB与RPA；动态磁化率解析出顺磁振子（paramagnons）、低能声模（sound modes）与高能哈伯德激发（Hubbard excitations）。

Conclusion: 该形式避免了松原截断（Matsubara truncation）、随机采样（stochastic sampling）与数值解析延拓（numerical analytic continuation），为关联体系响应函数提供了实用且更接近DMFT的计算途径。

Abstract: We propose a finite-temperature Gaussian-fluctuation formalism for response functions within ghost rotationally invariant slave-boson (gRISB) theory and apply it to the two-dimensional Hubbard model. The gRISB magnetic phase boundary and static spin and charge susceptibilities capture the trends of dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT), significantly improving upon standard RISB and the random phase approximation (RPA). At the RISB level, our formalism recovers the Kotliar-Ruckenstein response. The dynamical susceptibilities resolve paramagnons, low-energy sound modes, and high-energy Hubbard excitations. Because they are evaluated directly on the real-frequency axis, the results avoid Matsubara truncation, stochastic sampling, and numerical analytic continuation. We also introduce a conserving quasiparticle approximation that expresses the response in terms of dressed Green's functions and vertex corrections.

</details>


### [99] [Chiral Central Charge from Real-Space Twist Operator Correlator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08788)
*Yunlong Zang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个基于实空间扭算符关联函数的普适公式，用于从纠缠结构直接提取二维有能隙量子系统的手性中心荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子物相（quantum phases of matter）的纠缠刻画近年进展显著，实空间公式因其理论与实验相关性而受到关注；但如何在格点上系统刻画手性拓扑相仍是待解问题。

Method: 构造 N 副本态上的扭算符（twist operator）关联函数 Z_{g,h}，其中 g,h 为置换群 S_N 元素；对满足球面条件（spherical condition）的置换对，在场论框架下推导其相位与手性中心荷的关系 Z_{g,h}/|Z_{g,h}|=(θ_gθ_h/θ_{gh})^{𝔠_-}，并在微观格点模型中进行数值检验。

Result: 关联函数的相位由手性中心荷 𝔠_- 决定，其中 θ_g^{𝔠_-} 为标号 g∈S_N 的扭缺陷（twist defect）的拓扑自旋；该构造同时适用于玻色与费米系统，并得到数值验证。

Conclusion: 结果建立了实空间纠缠结构与手性中心荷之间的普适联系，为直接在格点上刻画手性拓扑相提供了系统框架。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed substantial progress in the entanglement-based characterization of quantum phases of matter, with growing interest in real-space formulas due to their theoretical and experimental relevance. For two-dimensional gapped quantum systems, we propose a universal real-space formula for the chiral central charge based on the twist-operator correlator $Z_{g,h}=\langleψ^{(N)}\rvert\mathcal{T}_A(g)\mathcal{T}_B(h)\mathcal{T}_C(g)\mathcal{T}_C(h)\lvertψ^{(N)}\rangle$, where $\lvertψ^{(N)}\rangle=\lvertψ\rangle^{\otimes N}$ is the $N$-copy state and $g,h\in S_N$ are permutations. The twist operator $\mathcal{T}_R(g)$ permutes the $N$ replicas within region $R$ according to $g$. For pairs $(g,h)$ satisfying the spherical condition, we show that the phase of the correlator is determined by the chiral central charge $\mathfrak{c}_{-}$ through $Z_{g,h}/\left\lvert Z_{g,h}\right\rvert=(θ_gθ_h/θ_{gh})^{\mathfrak{c}_{-}}$, where $θ_g^{\mathfrak{c}_{-}}$ is the topological spin of the twist defect labeled by $g\in S_N$. The construction applies to both bosonic and fermionic systems. We derive the formula within a field-theoretic framework under suitable assumptions and further test it numerically in microscopic lattice models. Our results establish a universal connection between real-space entanglement structures and the chiral central charge, providing a systematic framework for characterizing chiral topological phases directly on the lattice.

</details>

### [100] [GEANT4-Based Comparative Study of Detector Response in Reduced and Fully Instrumented Silicon-Tungsten Sampling Calorimeter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07920)
*A. Laha,S. Choudhury,S. Muhuri,M. Mondal*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该研究基于GEANT4对硅-钨（Si-W）取样量热器进行模拟，考察1-120 GeV电磁簇射发展与探测器响应，比较单层精简原型与二十层完备原型的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限条件下，需要验证精简取样原型能否替代完备量热器用于探测器表征与测试束（test-beam）研究，因而须评估两者响应的对应关系。

Method: 采用GEANT4模拟，设置两种构型：构型I（Configuration-I）为精简顺序取样原型，单个硅探测器层置于递增钨吸收体深度下游以取样纵向簇射演化；构型II（Configuration-II）为二十层硅层与钨吸收体交替的完备取样量热器。分析纵向簇射发展、簇射极大位置、能量沉积、线性度与量热性能。

Result: 构型II实现逐事件（event-by-event）能量重建，呈现预期的线性响应与能量分辨率；构型I可复现平均纵向簇射轮廓，并与构型II的积分簇射响应呈强线性相关。虽构型I因缺少逐事件多层取样而无法直接给出量热能量分辨率，但两构型间的相关性可用于将精简原型的响应关联至完备量热器并估计其量热性能。

Conclusion: 精简原型成功复现完备探测器中所观测的电磁簇射主要特征，在完整原型建造受资源限制时，对其探测器表征与测试束研究具有实用性。

Abstract: {A GEANT4-based simulation study of a silicon--tungsten (Si--W) sampling calorimeter is presented to investigate electromagnetic shower development and detector response in the energy range of 1--120 GeV. Two detector configurations are considered: a reduced sequential-sampling prototype (Configuration-I), where a single silicon detector layer is placed downstream of progressively increasing tungsten absorber depths to sample the longitudinal shower evolution, and a fully instrumented sampling calorimeter (Configuration-II) consisting of twenty silicon layers interleaved with tungsten absorbers.
  The detector response is studied in terms of longitudinal shower development, shower maximum position, energy deposition, detector linearity, and calorimetric performance. Configuration-II provides simultaneous longitudinal sampling and allows event-by-event reconstruction of the deposited energy, yielding the expected linear response and energy resolution. Configuration-I reproduces the average longitudinal shower profiles and exhibits a strong linear correlation with the integrated shower response of Configuration-II. Although the absence of event-by-event multi-layer sampling prevents a direct determination of calorimetric energy resolution in Configuration-I, the observed correlation between two configurations is used to relate the response of the reduced prototype to that of the fully instrumented calorimeter and to estimate the corresponding calorimetric performance.
  The results demonstrate that the reduced prototype successfully reproduces the main characteristics of electromagnetic shower development observed in the fully instrumented detector, establishing its usefulness for detector characterization and test-beam studies when the construction of a complete prototype is limited by available resources.

</details>


### [101] [Icing monitoring with thickness shear acoustic waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06860)
*Jaime del Moral,Miguel González del Val,Víctor Rico,Juan R. Sánchez-Valencia,Julio Mora,Paloma García Gallego,Francisco Carreño,Andreas Winkler,Agustín R. González-Elipe,Ana Borrás,Stefan Jacob*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该工作提出以厚度剪切模式体声波（thickness shear mode bulk acoustic waves, TSM AWs）作为选择性、鲁棒性更强的结冰监测方案，采用铌酸锂（LiNbO3）板与侧向场激励（lateral field excitation）电极构成传感平台，可同时检测冻结状态与温度变化，并通过反射系数（|S11|）的最小值及其频率演变提取结冰过程的定性与定量信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结冰会严重影响多类行业的性能与安全，而传统冰传感方法（如基于表面声波（surface acoustic waves, SAWs）的方案）存在选择性有限、易快速饱和以及恢复缓慢或耗能高等问题。因此需要一种更具选择性与鲁棒性的结冰监测替代方案。

Method: 传感平台由铌酸锂（LiNbO3）板构成，通过侧向场激励（lateral field excitation）电极激活，并由矢量或标量网络分析仪（Vector or Scalar Network Analyzer）进行电子驱动。监测对象涵盖冻结的固着液滴（frozen sessile droplets）直至航空包线（aeronautical envelopes）内近真实场景的积冰；通过追踪强剪切主导模式的反射系数幅值（即|S11|最小值对应的共振峰、形状与频率）演变来表征结冰过程，并辅以有限元（finite element）仿真阐明其物理机制。

Result: 该TSM AW系统能够可靠地检测冻结条件与温度变化，并可从强剪切主导模式的|S11|最小值共振峰、形状及频率的演变中，提取结冰过程的定性与定量信息。有限元仿真进一步阐明了支配冰监测能力的物理机制。

Conclusion: TSM AW器件有望成为新一代结构简单、轻量、高灵敏度且鲁棒的声波结冰传感器（acoustic wave icing sensors）的候选方案。

Abstract: Icing monitoring and ice detection are critical challenges across many industries where ice formation can severely degrade performance or compromise safety. Conventional ice sensing approaches, such as those based on surface acoustic waves (SAWs), often suffer from limited selectivity, fast saturation, and slow or energy-consuming recovery. In this work, we propose the use of thickness shear mode bulk acoustic waves (TSM AWs) as a selective and robust alternative for monitoring icing phenomena, ranging from frozen sessile droplets to ice accretion in near-real scenarios within aeronautical envelopes. The sensing platform consists of a LiNbO3 plate activated through lateral field excitation electrodes and driven electronically by a Vector or Scalar Network Analyzer. This system reliably detects both freezing conditions and temperature variations. Tracking the evolution of the magnitude of the reflection coefficient, specifically, the resonance peak and shape and frequency of|S11| minimum, of a strongly shear dominant mode provides a reliable means to characterize and extract both qualitative and quantitative information about icing processes. Finite element simulations further elucidate the physical mechanisms governing the ice monitoring capabilities. These results position TSM AW devices as promising candidates for a new generation of simple, light-weight, highly sensitive, and robust acoustic wave icing sensors.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Proposing one-sided tetrons as topological qubits for Majorana-based quantum computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07169)
*Tudor D. Stanescu,Sumanta Tewari*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种由四根平行马约拉纳纳米线共接于同一超导骨干构成的“单侧 tetron”拓扑量子比特方案，通过改变几何构型显著抑制有限长度下马约拉纳零模（MZMs）的波函数交叠与能量劈裂，从而提升宇称寿命与量子比特相干性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一维 SM-SC 混合纳米线（含 Rashba 自旋轨道耦合与外磁场）中，有限长度导致的末端马约拉纳模式波函数交叠会产生能量劈裂（energy splitting）。在双侧 tetron（a two-sided tetron）中，该劈裂限制涉及双线马约拉纳对的宇称寿命（parity lifetime）及量子比特相干性，进而损害基于测量的编织（measurement-based braiding）与读出操作。

Method: 提出单侧 tetron（one-sided tetron）：由四根平行马约拉纳纳米线在一个公共端通过超导骨干（SC backbone）连接构成。为研究其基本杂化机制，进一步考虑简化的双线子系统，即单侧 tetron 的一半，称为 doublon。

Result: 在拓扑相中，自由线端承载四个 MZMs 以定义拓扑量子比特，而位于相对端的马约拉纳模式通过骨干杂化并获得有限能量。在 doublon 中，骨干（bridge）使连接端的两个马约拉纳模式发生强杂化并偏离零能，自由端模式则形成近零能费米子。存活的 MZM 对的劈裂相比双侧 tetron 的劈裂能量可降低约两个数量级，从而大幅提升宇称寿命。

Conclusion: 单侧 tetron 对马约拉纳交叠的抑制作用相当于将对应双侧 tetron 的长度加倍，为提升拓扑量子比特的相干性与操作性能提供了几何构型层面的改进路径。

Abstract: One-dimensional SM-SC hybrid nanowires with Rashba spin-orbit coupling and applied Zeeman field can support Majorana zero modes (MZMs) at the ends of the wire. In finite-length systems, residual wave-function overlap between the end modes produces an energy splitting. In a two-sided tetron qubit, composed of a pair of parallel Majorana wires coupled by a trivial SC backbone in the middle, this splitting limits the parity lifetime associated with Majorana pairs involving both wires and the coherence of the qubit, adversely affecting measurement-based braiding and readout operations. Here, we propose a one-sided tetron made of four parallel Majorana wires connected at a common end by an SC backbone. In the topological phase, the free wire ends host four MZMs that define the topological qubit, while the Majoranas localized near the opposite ends hybridize through the backbone and acquire finite energy. To study the basic hybridization mechanism, we consider a simpler two-wire subsystem, i.e., half of a one-sided tetron, which we call a doublon. In a doublon, the backbone, or bridge, strongly hybridizes the two Majorana modes at the connected end and shifts them away from zero energy, while the modes at the free ends form a near-zero energy fermion. We show that the splitting of the surviving MZM pair can fall by about two orders of magnitude compared to the splitting energies in a two-sided tetron, thereby dramatically increasing the parity lifetimes. Thus, the one-sided tetron ensures a suppression of the Majorana overlap comparable to doubling the length of the corresponding two-sided tetron.

</details>


### [103] [Composite Fermion Theory of Fractional Chern Insulators in Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07174)
*Peize Ding,Andrew J. Millis,Yongxin Zeng*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文针对菱形堆叠石墨烯多层中分数陈绝缘体态的成因，提出复合费米子描述，通过通量附着变换与平均场近似解释莫尔势与库仑相互作用如何稳定FCI基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菱形堆叠石墨烯多层中分数量子反常霍尔效应（fractional quantum anomalous Hall effect）的实验观测，引发了关于分数陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator, FCI）态本质及莫尔势（moiré potential）作用的问题，尚缺乏相应理论描述。

Method: 构建复合费米子（composite fermion, CF）描述：在通量附着变换（flux-attachment transformation）与平均场近似下，CF谱形成朗道能级，并被莫尔势进一步劈裂；同时考虑CF之间的相互作用。

Result: 在Jain序列对应的莫尔填充因子处，整数个CF能带被占据，形成具有分数化量子化霍尔响应的不可压缩基态。当附着通量与菱形石墨烯导带贝里曲率（Berry curvature）方向相反时，CF朗道能级间距宽、跨层离域，态密度降低，从而保护CF平均场态并放大莫尔势对远离掺杂电子层的效应；CF间相互作用进一步增大莫尔诱导带隙。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了菱形石墨烯中FCI态的本质，并为后续研究提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The experimental observation of the fractional quantum anomalous Hall effect in rhombohedral graphene multilayers has raised questions about the nature of the fractional Chern insulator (FCI) states and the role of the moiré potential. In this Letter we develop a composite fermion (CF) description and discuss how moiré modulation and Coulomb interactions stabilize the FCI ground states. Under a flux-attachment transformation and mean-field approximation, the CF spectrum forms Landau levels that are further split by the moiré potential. At moiré filling fractions in the Jain sequence, an integer number of CF bands are occupied, resulting in an incompressible ground state with fractionally quantized Hall response. Remarkably, when the attached flux opposes the Berry curvature of the conduction band of rhombohedral graphene, the CF Landau levels are widely spaced in energy and delocalized across layers, leading to a reduced density of states that helps protect the CF mean-field state and amplifying effects of the moiré potential acting on the layer away from the doped electrons. Interactions between CFs further enlarge the moiré-induced band gap and stabilize the FCI ground state. Our work sheds light on the nature of the FCI states in rhombohedral graphene and provides a new theoretical framework for further studies.

</details>


### [104] [Adjoint-State Identifiability of Piezo-Tunable Valley Splitting in 2D Magnetic Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07179)
*Suhas Suresh Bharadwaj*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出一个经典的、偏微分方程约束（PDE-constrained）的多尺度反演框架，用于量化二维磁性异质结中应变可调谷效应（valley effects）在器件层面的可辨识性（identifiability），并以二硫化钼/三溴化铬异质结为示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论文献常在微观层面预测应变调控的谷劈裂（valley splitting），但很少评估这些量子预测在真实宏观器件中是否仍具有统计可恢复性。因此需要一个数学过滤器，在复杂物理制备之前判定理论预测的二维材料的真实实验可行性。

Method: 建立完全经典、偏微分方程约束的多尺度反演框架；通过第一性原理结构弛豫（first-principles structural relaxations）确认手性（chiral）C₃ 点群对称性，将交换—应变耦合张量约化为单一标量；采用偏微分方程约束的伴随态（adjoint-state）架构连接连续介质弹性动力学（continuum elastodynamics）与谷分辨反常霍尔输运（valley-resolved anomalous Hall transport）。

Result: 密度泛函理论（DFT）给出耦合估计 η≈-0.07 meV，其 95% 置信区间与零一致。以该耦合量级对照已确立的热噪声（thermal noise）与速度饱和（velocity saturation）极限，界定出介于 262.0 与 22,337.6 V/cm 之间的安全工作窗口。

Conclusion: 该有界操作窗口并非断言一个已确认的非零材料属性，而是充当精确的诊断阈值。部署这一严格的统计可辨识性框架，为在复杂物理制备前判定理论预测二维材料的真实实验可行性提供了必要的数学过滤手段。

Abstract: Controlling valley degrees of freedom with mechanical strain is a promising approach for solid-state information processing. Current theoretical literature routinely predicts strain-tuned valley splitting at the microscopic level but rarely evaluates whether these quantum predictions remain statistically recoverable in realistic macroscopic devices. This manuscript establishes a fully classical, partial differential equation-constrained multiscale inverse framework for quantifying the device-level identifiability of predicted strain-tunable valley effects in two-dimensional magnetic heterostructures, demonstrated here for a molybdenum disulfide and chromium tribromide heterostructure. First-principles structural relaxations confirm a chiral $C_3$ point-group symmetry which mathematically reduces the relevant exchange-strain coupling tensor to a single scalar. A partial differential equation-constrained adjoint-state architecture successfully bridges continuum elastodynamics to valley-resolved anomalous Hall transport. Density functional theory yields a coupling estimate of $η\approx -0.07$ meV whose 95% confidence interval is consistent with zero. Evaluating this specific coupling magnitude against established thermal noise and velocity saturation limits defines a safe operating window bounded between 262.0 and 22,337.6 V/cm. Rather than asserting a confirmed nonzero material property this bounded operational window functions as a precise diagnostic threshold. Deploying this rigorous statistical identifiability framework provides a necessary mathematical filter to determine the true experimental viability of theoretically predicted two-dimensional materials before complex physical fabrication.

</details>


### [105] [Contact-Governed Macroscopic Signatures of Strain-Induced Valley Sorting in MoS$_2$ Field-Effect Transistors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07203)
*Suhas Suresh Bharadwaj*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作将应变单层MoS₂的量子谷偏转张量（quantum valley-deflection tensor）嵌入宏观泊松-漂移-扩散（Poisson-drift-diffusion）模型，实现含接触的完整器件模拟，发现应变诱导的谷选择性输运（valley-selective transport）几乎完全由源漏金属-半导体界面（metal-semiconductor interface）决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应变工程（strain engineering）被认为是控制单层过渡金属硫族化合物（transition metal dichalcogenides, TMDs）中谷选择性输运的主要途径，但该量子效应能否在完整接触器件中存续尚不明确；既有量子输运模型仅局限于孤立的纳米尺度片段，无法判断整个器件是否、以及在何处（体区或接触区）呈现应变诱导的谷分选。

Method: 将应变单层MoS₂的量子谷偏转张量直接嵌入宏观泊松-漂移-扩散模型，构建包含接触的完整器件框架；以300 nm单层MoS₂晶体管、3 nm HfO₂栅介质、77 K、局域6.14 T赝磁场（pseudo-magnetic strain field）为对象进行模拟，并通过对仅接触区（15 nm）应变与仅沟道内部应变的对比实验，以及传输线分析（transmission-line analysis）加以验证和归约。

Result: 模拟显示开态电流被抑制63.6%，但该抑制并非体区属性：仅对15 nm接触区施加应变即可完整复现该抑制，而仅对沟道内部施加应变时抑制几乎消失；观测该效应还需隧穿透明、简并掺杂的接触，因为经典欧姆势垒（Ohmic barrier）会淹没该拓扑信号。传输线分析将该行为归约为单一谷-拓扑接触电阻（valley-topological contact resistance, R_c,V）及可预测的反比长度标度律（inverse-length scaling law）。

Conclusion: 金属-半导体界面是真实二维材料器件中工程化与探测谷选择性输运的决定性位置；该框架适用于任何应变工程二维材料异质结构（2D-material heterostructure），并可通过标准多长度接触结构进行实验检验。

Abstract: Strain engineering is a leading route to control valley-selective transport in monolayer transition metal dichalcogenides. Whether this quantum effect survives inside a fully contacted device remains an open question. Quantum transport models capture the underlying valley deflection but are limited to isolated nanoscale fragments, leaving it unclear if a full device registers strain-induced valley sorting in the bulk, at the contacts, or at all. Here, the quantum valley-deflection tensor of strained monolayer MoS$_2$ is embedded directly into a macroscopic Poisson-drift-diffusion model, extending this physics to a complete, contact-inclusive device. Applying this framework to a 300~nm monolayer MoS$_2$ transistor with a 3~nm HfO$_2$ gate dielectric at 77~K, under a localized 6.14~T pseudo-magnetic strain field, shows the resulting 63.6\% suppression of the ON-state current is not a bulk property. It is set almost entirely by the source and drain metal-semiconductor interfaces. The full suppression is reproduced when only the 15~nm contact regions are strained and nearly vanishes when the channel interior alone is strained, identifying the metal contact as the dominant site of strain-induced valley physics. Observing it requires tunneling-transparent, degenerately-doped contacts, since classical Ohmic barriers overwhelm the topological signal. A transmission-line analysis reduces this behavior to a single valley-topological contact resistance ($R_{c,V}$) and a predictive inverse-length scaling law. This framework applies to any strain-engineered 2D-material heterostructure and can be tested with standard multi-length contact structures. Ultimately, these results identify the metal-semiconductor interface as the decisive location for engineering and detecting valley-selective transport in real 2D-material devices.

</details>


### [106] [Theory of resonance Raman profiles in transition metal dichalcogenide monolayers: Interference effects due to inter-valley phonon scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07316)
*Daniel Groll,Daniel Wigger,Tilmann Kuhn*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作以微观模型研究单层TMDC中谷间散射与应变对共振拉曼散射的调控，发现K-Q谷间散射导致一阶与三阶拉曼过程的相消干涉，从而抑制WSe₂的入射共振，与实验一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层过渡金属硫族化合物（transition metal dichalcogenides, TMDCs）的光学性质受激子-声子耦合（exciton-phonon coupling）强烈影响，钨基材料中谷间散射（inter-valley scattering）尤为显著。K-Q谷间距的应变可调性使该类材料成为半导体光力学（semiconductor optomechanics）的研究平台，因此有必要厘清谷间散射与应变对共振拉曼散射的作用机制。

Method: 采用基于激子-声子相互作用有效形变势耦合（deformation potential coupling）的微观模型，考察经hBN封装的单层WSe₂中A₁模式的共振拉曼谱（resonance Raman profile）。

Result: 电子K谷与Q谷之间的谷间散射引起一阶与三阶拉曼过程之间的相消干涉（destructive interference），导致WSe₂中入射共振（incoming resonance）被抑制，该结果与近期已发表的实验数据相符。

Conclusion: 谷间散射诱导的拉曼过程干涉是解释单层钨基TMDC共振拉曼谱特征的关键机制，支持将此类材料用于应变可调的半导体光力学研究。

Abstract: The optical properties of monolayer transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDCs) are strongly impacted by exciton-phonon coupling, especially inter-valley scattering in the case of tungsten-based materials. Together with strain-tunability of the energetic K-Q valley separation this material class therefore provides a platform for semiconductor optomechanics. Here we investigate the influence of inter-valley scattering and strain on resonance Raman scattering in monolayer TMDCs using a microscopic model based on an effective deformation potential coupling for the exciton-phonon interaction. Considering exemplarily the resonance Raman profile of the A$_1$-mode in hBN-encapsulated monolayer WSe$_2$, we find that inter-valley scattering between the electronic K- and Q-valley leads to destructive interference between a first order and a third order Raman process. As a result we obtain a suppression of the incoming resonance in WSe$_2$, consistent with recently published experimental data.

</details>


### [107] [Direct synthesis of moiré graphene with twist-tuned hybridization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07429)
*Seunghyun Park,Daniel T. Larson,Zeyu Hao,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Efthimios Kaxiras,Yuan Cao,Amir Yacoby*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种基于微机电系统（MEMS）的可重构石墨烯莫尔（moiré）平台，通过机械组装与原位旋转两层独立制备的样品，实现扭转角的连续、确定性调控，并利用拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）验证莫尔势并探测其电子-声子响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转范德华（van der Waals, vdW）体系是研究超导、磁性与拓扑相等关联现象的重要平台，扭转角可连续调制哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）。但现有方法受限于一次性器件结构、角度精度不足或垂直探测几何，精确且可重复的扭转角控制仍是挑战。

Method: 采用微机电系统（micro-electromechanical system, MEMS）将两层独立制备的样品进行机械组装并原位旋转，构建可重构石墨烯莫尔平台；以拉曼光谱确认扭转诱导莫尔势的形成与稳定性，并监测纵光学（longitudinal optical, LO）声子模、双共振2D模以及近0°时Γ点G模的演化。

Result: 拉曼测量实现扭转角的连续确定性控制；莫尔势折叠电子能带结构并激活LO声子模，据此可绘制其色散关系并直接可视化莫尔动量；通过双共振2D模的演化分辨角度依赖的电子能带杂化；在近0°处观测到Γ点G模声子的涌现不对称性，揭示莫尔超晶格势与层间弛豫驱动的声子能带折叠与杂化。

Conclusion: 该平台为扭转双层石墨烯提供可重复的拉曼参考，消除了器件间差异；更广泛而言，将扭转角确立为可确定性调控的参数，用于系统研究莫尔量子材料中的关联与拓扑现象。

Abstract: Twisted van der Waals (vdW) systems provide a powerful platform for exploring emergent correlated phenomena, including superconductivity, magnetism, and topological phases. The twist angle offers a continuous tuning parameter for modifying the underlying Hamiltonian, however, precise and reproducible control remains challenging. Existing approaches are limited by single-use device architectures, restricted angular precision, or vertical probing geometries. Here we introduce a reconfigurable graphene-based moiré platform in which two independently fabricated layers are mechanically assembled and rotated in situ using a micro-electromechanical system (MEMS). Raman spectroscopy confirms the formation and robustness of the twist-induced moiré potential and enables continuous, deterministic control of the twist angle. The moiré potential folds the electronic band structure and activates the longitudinal optical (LO) phonon mode, allowing us to map its dispersion and directly visualize the moiré momentum. Angle-dependent electronic band hybridization is resolved through the evolution of the double-resonant 2D mode. Near \(0^\circ\), an emergent asymmetry of the \(Γ\)-point G-mode phonon reveals phonon band folding and hybridization driven by the moiré superlattice potential and interlayer relaxation. Our platform provides a reproducible Raman reference for twisted bilayer graphene while eliminating device-to-device variability. More broadly, it establishes the twist angle as a deterministically controllable parameter for systematic studies of correlated and topological phenomena in moiré quantum materials.

</details>


### [108] [Theory of Magic Angles in Twisted Bilayer Graphene: from Non-Abelian Gauge Fields to Flat Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07734)
*Leonardo A. Navarro-Labastida,Gerardo G. Naumis*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在 chiral continuum model（手性连续模型）框架下，将手性哈密顿量的平方视为非阿贝尔 SU(2) 赝磁场中的 2×2 Schrödinger 算符，由此导出的能量求和规则解释了第一魔角与后续魔角的本质差异，并给出魔角间距的渐近规律 α_{m+1}-α_m→3/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene）在离散的魔角处于电荷中性点形成近乎完美的平带，该现象此前缺乏统一的理论解释。本文旨在手性连续模型内给出一个统一描述，并厘清为何第一魔角在性质上区别于其余魔角。

Method: 核心对象是手性哈密顿量（chiral Hamiltonian）的平方，即电子在层间隧穿（interlayer tunneling）生成的非阿贝尔 SU(2) 赝磁场（pseudo-magnetic field）中运动的 2×2 Schrödinger 算符。方法包括推导能量求和规则（层间耦合分解为有界重叠通道与无界电流通道）、通过 moiré 势及其三胞磁周期性的重标度（rescaling）分析零模，并将 AA 点附近的零模问题映射为有效磁场 B_eff=3α 下的最低朗道能级（lowest-Landau-level）问题，另以阿贝尔化变形作为反例检验。

Result: 层间耦合的两个通道随耦合 α 的不同标度行为解释了第一魔角的定性特殊性；结合重标度得到渐近规律 α_{m+1}-α_m→3/2，两类宇称零模各以周期 3 重复并相互交错。AA 点附近高阶零模为宽度 1/√(3α) 的相干朗道态，其导心（guiding centre）收敛于 ±(π/3)q_μ，动能与束缚能处于均分状态。将耦合变形为阿贝尔形式会消除魔角序列，表明非阿贝尔结构不可或缺。相邻魔角之间，Γ 与 M 点的能带反转（band inversion）产生陈数（Chern number）C=±2 的相，量子几何度规（quantum metric）在 θ≈0.43° 处达到尖锐极大。

Conclusion: 该统一理论表明魔角现象源于非阿贝尔赝磁场结构，其阿贝尔对应物不复现魔角序列。Γ 与 M 点的能带反转及量子度规在 θ≈0.43° 的尖锐极大，标示出第一魔角之外在关联相与拓扑相方面具有前景的参数区间。

Abstract: At a discrete set of ``magic'' twist angles, twisted bilayer graphene develops two bands at charge neutrality that are almost perfectly flat. We present a unified theory of this phenomenon within the chiral continuum model. The central object is the square of the chiral Hamiltonian, a $2\times2$ Schrödinger operator for an electron moving in a non-Abelian $SU(2)$ pseudo-magnetic field generated by the interlayer tunneling. From it we derive an energy sum rule in which the interlayer coupling splits into a bounded overlap channel and an unbounded current channel; their different scaling with the coupling $α$ explains why the first magic angle is qualitatively different from the rest. The asymptotic rule $α_{m+1}-α_m\to3/2$ follows from a rescaling of the moiré potential together with its three-cell magnetic periodicity, with the two parity families of zero modes each repeating with period $3$ and interleaved with each other. Near the AA point the zero-mode problem becomes a lowest-Landau-level problem in an effective field $B_{\rm eff}=3α$, so that the high-order zero modes are coherent Landau states of width $1/\sqrt{3α}$ whose guiding centres converge to $\pm(π/3) q_μ$, with kinetic and confinement energies in equipartition. Deforming the coupling into an Abelian one removes the magic-angle sequence, which shows that the non-Abelian structure is essential. Between consecutive magic angles, band inversions at $Γ$ and $M$ produce phases with Chern numbers $C=\pm2$, and the quantum metric reaches a sharp maximum at $θ\approx0.43^\circ$, identifying a promising regime for correlated and topological phases beyond the first magic angle.

</details>


### [109] [Ferroaxial Electronic Response to Local Lattice Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07801)
*Ken Uchino,Yuuki Ogawa,Satoru Hayami*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文以二维方格紧束缚模型与线性响应理论研究铁轴（ferroaxial）体系对晶格变形的电子响应。将对称应变与反对称局域旋转视为独立微扰，发现局域旋转诱导的特征对角电四极子（electric quadrupole）响应由带内（intraband）过程主导，且随铁轴晶体场近似线性增长并在无铁轴序时消失，从而证明反对称局域旋转构成铁轴态特有的电子响应通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统弹性响应通常仅以对称应变（symmetric strain）讨论，但位移梯度（displacement gradient）还包含描述局域晶格旋转的反对称分量；该自由度在铁轴体系中的电子响应机制尚不明确，故需将其与对称应变区分并独立处理。

Method: 构建二维方格晶格上的紧束缚模型（tight-binding model），结合线性响应理论（linear response theory），将对称应变与反对称局域旋转作为独立微扰，并通过形变对电子跃迁振幅（hopping amplitude）的调制在微观层面引入其效应。

Result: 局域旋转诱导出与对称剪切应变（symmetric shear strain）所生成者不同的特征对角电四极子；该旋转诱导响应由带内贡献主导，随铁轴晶体场近似线性增大，在无铁轴序时消失。相比之下，对称剪切诱导响应在无铁轴序时仍有限，且同时包含带内与带间（interband）贡献。

Conclusion: 反对称局域旋转提供了铁轴态所特有的、区别于对称应变的独立电子响应通道。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate electronic responses to lattice deformation in a ferroaxial system using a tight-binding model on a two-dimensional square lattice and linear response theory. While conventional elastic responses are commonly discussed in terms of symmetric strain, the displacement gradient also contains an antisymmetric component describing a local lattice rotation. Focusing on this rotational degree of freedom, we treat the symmetric strain and antisymmetric local rotation as independent perturbations and incorporate their effects microscopically through deformation-induced modulations of the electronic hopping amplitudes. We find that the local rotation induces characteristic diagonal electric quadrupoles that are distinct from those generated by the symmetric shear strain. The rotation-induced response is dominated by the intraband contribution and increases approximately linearly with the ferroaxial crystal field, vanishing in the absence of ferroaxial order. In contrast, the symmetric-shear-induced response remains finite without ferroaxial order and contains both intraband and interband contributions. These results demonstrate that the antisymmetric local rotation provides a distinct electronic response channel characteristic of the ferroaxial state.

</details>


### [110] [Negative charging energy in LaAlO$_3$/SrTiO$_3$ quantum dots induced by SrTiO$_3$ polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07878)
*J. Czarnecki,A. Sierant,B. Szafran,P. Szumniak,T. S. Jespersen,G. Meucci,T. Jansen,R. Citro,A. Maiellaro,N. Bergeal,M. Bibes,M. Saluzzo,A. Kalaboukhov,P. Wójcik*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出 LaAlO₃/SrTiO₃ 量子点中负充电能的微观机制，归因于 SrTiO₃ 顺电响应产生的静电镜像电荷（image charge）。采用自洽组态相互作用方法，发现可通过限域势与镜像电荷有效距离调控稳定的电子配对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于解释 LaAlO₃/SrTiO₃ 量子点中观测到的负充电能（negative charging energy）这一现象，并寻找其微观物理起源。作者关注 SrTiO₃ 的介电响应是否可通过镜像电荷效应提供该机制。

Method: 采用自洽组态相互作用方法（self-consistent configuration-interaction approach），同时考虑库仑排斥（Coulomb repulsion）与镜像电荷相互作用（image-charge interactions）。模型中的关键参数为限域势（confinement potential）与有效镜像电荷距离，后者由界面二维电子气（two-dimensional electron gas）的厚度决定。

Result: 计算得到一个稳健的电子配对（electron pairing）区间，其强度可通过限域势和有效镜像电荷距离进行调节。有效镜像电荷距离由界面二维电子气厚度设定。

Conclusion: 结果表明，介电镜像电荷效应（dielectric image-charge effects）可作为在氧化物量子点及其他具有强介电响应材料中实现可控电子配对的途径。

Abstract: We propose a microscopic mechanism for the negative charging energy in LaAlO$_3$/SrTiO$_3$ quantum dots, arising from the electrostatic image charge associated with the paraelectric response of SrTiO$_3$. Using a self-consistent configuration-interaction approach that accounts for both Coulomb repulsion and image-charge interactions, we find a robust regime of electron pairing whose strength is tunable through the confinement potential and the effective image-charge distance, set by the thickness of the interfacial two-dimensional electron gas. Our results establish dielectric image-charge effects as a route to controllable electron pairing in oxide quantum dots and other materials with a strong dielectric response.

</details>


### [111] [Calculation of structural symmetry and applicability of small AkBlCm nanoclusters (A=Rb, Cs; B=Pb, Sn, Bi; C=Cl, Br, I) as building blocks for synthesis of larger-size nanoclusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08380)
*Roman Makarevych,Igor Dmytruk,Yevhen Hrabovskyi,Iryna Pundyk,Nataliia Bashmakova,Nataliya Berezovska*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本报告分析三元小纳米团簇（small nanoclusters）AkBlCm（A - Rb, Cs；B - Pb, Sn, Bi；C - Cl, Br, I）的结构对称性、结合能（binding energy）与稳定性，及其作为构建更大尺寸纳米团簇单元（building blocks）的适用性。研究发现原子半径及其在团簇内的比例与稳定性之间存在明确关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在评估三元小纳米团簇的结构对称性与稳定性，并探讨其作为构筑更大尺寸纳米团簇的结构单元（building blocks）的潜在适用性。

Method: 首先基于几何方法（几何途径，geometrical approach）进行初步空间建模，将原子间距取为相应离子半径（ionic radii）之和；随后通过量子化学计算（quantum-chemical calculations）进行几何优化（geometry optimization）与结合能（binding energy）计算。计算使用Gaussian09软件包，采用密度泛函理论（Density Functional Theory, DFT），包括Perdew-Burke-Ernzerhof（PBE）广义梯度近似（generalized gradient approximation）与Becke-3-Lee-Yang-Parr（B3LYP）杂化交换关联泛函（exchange-correlation functionals），并全程采用SDD与LANL2DZ标量相对论有效势基组（scalar-relativistic effective basic potentials）。

Result: 计算得到了原子半径、其在纳米团簇内的比例与团簇稳定性之间的明确关系，并对此进行了分析。

Conclusion: 原子半径及其在团簇中的比例与三元小纳米团簇的稳定性存在明确关联，该关系可为评估此类团簇作为更大尺寸纳米团簇构筑单元（building blocks）的适用性提供依据。

Abstract: In this report we analyze charasteristics of structural symmetry of small AkBlCm nanoclusters (A - Rb, Cs; B - Pb, Sn, Bi; C - Cl, Br, I), binding energies, stability, and, ultimately, applicability as building blocks for construction of larger-size nanoclusters. Preliminary spatial modeling of ternary compound nanoclusters has been carried out on the basics of geometrical approach, namely interatomic distances taken as a sums of corresponding ionic radii. The geometry optimization and binding energies of the modeled structures have been determined through quantum-chemical calculations. Geometry optimization has been performed with Gaussian09 software package using Density Functional Theory (DFT) within the generalized gradient approximation of the Perdew-Burke-Ernzerhof (PBE) and hybrid approximation of the Becke-3-Lee-Yang-Parr (B3LYP) exchange-correlation functionals. The Stuttgart/Dresden (SDD) and Los Alamos National Laboratory 2 Double Zeta (LANL2DZ) scalar-relativistic effective basic potentials were employed throughout the calculations. As a result of these calculations, a clear relationship between the atomic radii, their ratios within the nanoclusters and their stability has been obtained and analyzed.

</details>


### [112] [Interfacial Charge Transfer and Morphology Govern Magnetism in Ultrathin VOx/MoSe2 Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08494)
*Yasinthara Wadumesthri,Nalaka Kapuruge,Raphael B. de Oliveira,Bruno Ipaves,Guilherme S. L. Fabris,Kinga Lasek,Florence A. Nugera,Jianjun Pan,Xiaomei Jiang,Douglas S. Galvão,Humberto Rodríguez Gutiérrez*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究在单层 MoSe₂ 上沉积超薄 VOₓ 薄膜，证明界面形成可显著增强磁响应，并揭示界面电荷转移与纳米尺度形貌分别主导局域磁矩与集体磁动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过界面相互作用（interfacial interactions）在二维半导体（two-dimensional semiconductors）中工程化磁性，可替代替位掺杂（substitutional doping），并在同一异质结（heterostructure）中整合磁性与光电功能。

Method: 结合结构、光谱、磁性表征与第一性原理（first-principles）分析，研究沉积于单层 MoSe₂ 上的超薄 VOₓ 薄膜及其界面；并采用唯象模型（phenomenological model）描述形貌对集体磁动力学的影响。

Result: VOₓ/MoSe₂ 界面形成后磁响应显著增强；有限 VOₓ 团簇（clusters）具内禀磁矩，并因 MoSe₂ 向氧化物的电子转移而进一步增强，该电荷转移与异质结光致发光（photoluminescence）中增强的三子（trion）贡献一致。形貌亦强烈调控磁性：由非连续团簇向连续薄膜的转变导致性质不同的温度依赖——连续薄膜表现为常规热退磁（thermal demagnetization）与矫顽力软化，团簇态则随温度升高出现磁化增强与矫顽力非单调变化。

Conclusion: 结果建立统一图像：界面电荷转移增强 VOₓ 局域磁矩，而纳米尺度形貌通过有效翻转势垒（effective reversal barriers）分布及其热激活可及性支配集体磁动力学，为氧化物/过渡金属硫属化物（transition-metal dichalcogenide）异质结中的磁性调控提供互补途径。

Abstract: Engineering magnetism in two-dimensional semiconductors through interfacial interactions offers an attractive alternative to substitutional doping and provides opportunities for integrating magnetic and optoelectronic functionality within the same heterostructure. Here, we investigate ultrathin VOx films deposited on monolayer MoSe2 and demonstrate a pronounced enhancement of the magnetic response upon formation of the VOx/MoSe2 interface. Combined structural, spectroscopic, magnetic, and first-principles analyses support a consistent picture in which finite VOx clusters possess intrinsic magnetic moments that are further enhanced by electron transfer from MoSe2 to the oxide. The predicted charge transfer is also consistent with the increased trion contribution observed in the photoluminescence response of the heterostructure. Beyond this interfacial enhancement, the magnetic behavior is strongly governed by the morphology of the ultrathin oxide. A crossover from discontinuous clusters to more continuous films produces qualitatively different temperature dependences: the continuous-film regime exhibits conventional thermal demagnetization and coercive softening, whereas the clustered regime displays an unusual increase in magnetization and nonmonotonic coercivity with increasing temperature. These contrasting responses are captured by a phenomenological model in which morphology determines the collective magnetic dynamics through a distribution of effective reversal barriers and their thermally activated accessibility. Together, the results establish a unified picture in which interfacial charge transfer enhances the local magnetic moments of VOx, while nanoscale morphology governs their collective magnetic dynamics, providing complementary routes for controlling magnetism in oxide/transition-metal dichalcogenide heterostructures.

</details>


### [113] [Ultra-small-angle graphene twistronics with monolayer spacers: From interlayer hybridisation and moiré effects to high-temperature magnetotransport oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08505)
*Benjamin Fuller,Haowei Fan,Agustin Montemurro,Yuyuan Pan,Alexey I. Berdyugin,Vladimir I. Falko,Xiao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文基于小角度扭转石墨烯多层结构（含单层六方氮化硼 hBN 间隔层）的莫尔超晶格（moiré superlattice）与层间杂化（interlayer hybridisation）分析，预测其光谱特征与量子输运效应，并提出计算高效的理论模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解含 hBN 间隔层的扭转石墨烯体系中莫尔超晶格与层间杂化如何决定能带与量子输运行为，并为石墨烯/hBN/石墨烯莫尔体系寻找计算高效的建模途径。

Method: 研究对象涵盖带间隔层的扭转双层（twisted bilayer）与双双层（double bilayers）结构；双双层考虑有无反演对称性（inversion symmetry）两种堆叠序，并对比含与不含面内周期电荷分布（in-plane periodic charge distribution）的自洽 Hartree 计算结果。同时通过面外位移电场（out-of-plane displacement field）调参分析微带（minibands）演化。

Result: 识别出微带可被位移电场增强的参数区间，并在广泛密度与位移电场范围内标定微带边缘与范霍夫奇点（van Hove singularities）。发现体系存在 Lifshitz 转变（Lifshitz transitions），费米线（Fermi lines）在莫尔布里渊区（moiré Brillouin zones）中形成网络，且布朗-扎克振荡（Brown-Zak oscillations）在低磁场与升高温度下向其汇聚。

Conclusion: 层间杂化在同一参数空间中影响超高迁移率结构的面内磁输运（in-plane magneto-transport）；层间杂化的半经典磁振荡可导致电阻率出现 1/B 周期量子振荡，并可持续至高温。

Abstract: Based on the analysis of moiré superlattice characteristics and interlayer hybridisation in small-angle twisted graphene multilayers with a monolayer hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) spacer, we predict spectral features and quantum transport effects in these systems. Here, we consider both a twisted bilayer and double bilayers with a spacer, discuss double bilayers in two stacking orders - with and without inversion symmetry - and also compare the results obtained with self-consistent Hartree calculations with and without in-plane periodic charge distribution, offering computationally efficient models for these graphene/hBN/graphene moiré systems. We note parametric intervals where the appearance of minibands can be enhanced by tuning with an out-of-plane displacement field, and identify the miniband edges and van Hove singularities across a broad range of densities and displacement fields. These system host Lifshitz transitions at which the Fermi lines form a network across moiré Brillouin zones, and to which Brown-Zak oscillations converge at low magnetic field and elevated temperatures. We analyse interlayer hybridisation across the same parameter space, discuss how hybridisation affects in-plane magneto-transport in ultra-high-mobility structures, and predict that semiclassical magneto-oscillations of interlayer hybridisation can lead to 1/B-periodic quantum oscillations of resistivity persistent to high temperature.

</details>


### [114] [Quasiparticle-resolved variational theory of Andreev spin qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08557)
*Teodor Iličin,Rok Žitko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出量子点约瑟夫森结（quantum-dot Josephson junction）中奇宇称双态（odd-parity doublet）的连续变分描述，保留至多两个 Bogoliubov 准粒子（quasiparticle）组态，并与数值重整化群（numerical renormalization group）结果对照，揭示了双准粒子相干对自旋相关约瑟夫森耦合（spin-dependent Josephson coupling）的关键贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安德烈夫自旋量子比特（Andreev spin qubit）虽储存单个未配对自旋，但其与超导相位（superconducting phase）的耦合可能由相干的虚拟准粒子对主导，这一机制此前缺乏紧凑的理论刻画。

Method: 构建含库仑相互作用（Coulomb interaction）与自旋依赖背景隧穿（spin-dependent background tunneling）的量子点约瑟夫森结连续变分理论，保留至多两个 Bogoliubov 准粒子组态以获得能量与波函数的解析表达，并以数值重整化群计算作为基准并分析误差来源。

Result: 得到常规与自旋相关约瑟夫森耦合的闭式表达；双准粒子组态虽概率很小，但通过与零准粒子分量的相干，贡献了领先自旋相关约瑟夫森耦合的恰好一半（即使无库仑排斥），并在排斥增强时占主导。自旋轨道诱导的量子点—电极间自旋转移（spin transfer）产生对两个量子比特态共有的约瑟夫森电流贡献，当量子点与电极塞曼能（Zeeman energy）不同时在零相位偏置处有限。还给出了虚拟准粒子与电极内自旋密度的局域化分布图像。

Conclusion: 该结果将强关联量子点模型与安德烈夫自旋量子比特电路所用的有效哈密顿量（effective Hamiltonian）相连接，并阐明波函数结构如何决定其耦合性质。

Abstract: An Andreev spin qubit stores a single unpaired spin, yet its coupling to the superconducting phase can be dominated by coherent virtual quasiparticle pairs. We develop a continuum variational description of the odd-parity doublet in a quantum-dot Josephson junction with Coulomb interaction and spin-dependent background tunneling. Retaining configurations with up to two Bogoliubov quasiparticles provides compact analytical expressions for energies and wavefunctions. We benchmark the results against numerical renormalization group calculations and identify sources of error. We obtain closed-form expressions for the conventional and spin-dependent Josephson couplings. Despite their small probability, two-quasiparticle configurations, through coherence with the zero-quasiparticle component, supply exactly half of the leading spin-dependent Josephson coupling even without Coulomb repulsion and dominate it as the repulsion grows. Spin-orbit-induced spin transfer between the dot and the leads produces a Josephson-current contribution that is common to both qubit states and finite at zero phase bias when the dot and lead Zeeman energies differ. We also map the localization of the virtual quasiparticles and the spin density in the leads. The results connect strongly correlated quantum-dot models to effective Hamiltonians used for Andreev-spin-qubit circuits and clarify how the wavefunction structure determines their couplings.

</details>


### [115] [Geometric Photon-drag Effect in Unconventional Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08767)
*Bristi Ghosh,Vivek Pandey,Malay Bandyopadhyay,Pankaj Bhalla,Snehasish Nandy*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作基于速度规范下的密度矩阵形式，计入有限光子动量导致的非垂直光学跃迁，推导了刻画光子拖拽（photon-drag）光电流的非线性注入电导与位移电导，并在含Rashba自旋轨道耦合的非传统磁体（p_x波与d_{x^2-y^2}波）中研究其响应。核心发现是：尽管反演对称性破缺，传统光电流在q=0极限下于两相中均恒为零，故直流光电流完全由光子拖拽驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子拖拽效应（photon-drag effect）能将光子携带的有限动量转移给载流子，即使在反演对称（inversion symmetry）系统中也能产生直流光电流，为非线性光学响应提供独特机制。作者旨在厘清非传统磁体中对称性与量子几何（quantum geometry）对光子拖拽光电流的调控作用，并区分其与传统光电流（photogalvanic）贡献的差异。

Method: 采用速度规范（velocity gauge）下的密度矩阵形式（density-matrix formalism），并计入有限光子动量驱动的非垂直光学跃迁（nonvertical optical transitions），推导非线性注入电导与位移电导（injection and shift conductivities）。随后在存在Rashba自旋轨道耦合（Rashba spin-orbit coupling, RSOC）的p_x波与d_{x^2-y^2}波非传统磁体中计算上述光子拖拽响应。

Result: 在两种非传统磁相中，反演对称性虽破缺，但传统光电流响应在q=0极限下经布里渊区积分后恒为零，因而非线性直流光电流完全由光子拖拽驱动。两种磁体表现出由对称性主导的定性差异：p波磁体仅支持线性注入电导与圆位移电导，而同时破坏时间反演与反演对称性的d波交错磁体（altermagnet）则允许全部线性与圆分量。

Conclusion: 该结果表明交错磁体（altermagnets）可作为调控对称性选择性非线性光电流的候选平台，并有望应用于偏振敏感光电探测（polarization-sensitive photodetection）、非线性光电子学与量子几何光电（quantum geometric photogalvanics）。

Abstract: The photon-drag effect is a nonlinear optical phenomenon in which the finite momentum carried by incident photons is transferred to charge carriers, thereby generating a dc photocurrent even in systems with inversion symmetry. Within the density-matrix formalism in the velocity gauge, and accounting for nonvertical optical transitions driven by finite photon momentum, we derive the nonlinear injection and shift conductivities that characterize the photon-drag photogalvanic response. We investigate these photon-drag responses in unconventional $p_x$-wave and $d_{x^2-y^2}$-wave magnets in the presence of Rashba spin-orbit coupling (RSOC), highlighting the distinct roles of symmetry and quantum geometry in shaping their nonlinear optical behavior. The key finding of our work is that, despite the broken inversion symmetry, the conventional photogalvanic response vanishes identically in the $q=0$ limit after Brillouin-zone integration in both unconventional magnetic phases, resulting in a nonlinear dc photocurrent entirely photon-drag driven. The two unconventional magnets exhibit qualitatively distinct responses governed by their symmetry properties: the $p$-wave magnet supports only linear injection and circular shift conductivities, whereas the $d$-wave altermagnet, which simultaneously breaks time-reversal and inversion symmetries, admits all linear and circular components. Our findings establish altermagnets as a promising platform for controlling symmetry-selective nonlinear photocurrents and may enable applications in polarization-sensitive photodetection, nonlinear optoelectronics, and quantum geometric photogalvanics.

</details>


### [116] [Quantum twisting microscopy as a momentum-resolved probe of quantum geometry and sublattice symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08783)
*Lucas V. Pupim,Mathias S. Scheurer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出使用亚晶格极化针尖的量子扭转显微镜（quantum twisting microscope, QTM），以探测二维平带体系（flat-band systems）中布洛赫态（Bloch states）的动量依赖性与量子几何（quantum geometry）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二维平带体系中，布洛赫态的动量依赖性是决定相互作用诱导相（interaction-induced phases）出现的关键因素，但现有的动量分辨探测手段难以直接提取量子几何张量（quantum geometric tensor），因此亟需新的探测方法。

Method: 作者提出采用亚晶格极化针尖的量子扭转显微镜（QTM），沿扫描动量路径探测亚晶格泡利矩阵（sublattice Pauli matrices）的期望值，并据此提取沿扫描方向的量子度规分量（quantum metric component）；在可使用多于一种亚晶格极化时，还可获得完整的量子几何张量。

Result: 该方法可实现对石墨烯基莫尔体系（graphene-based moiré systems）中不同候选不稳定性的关键对称性进行直接测量，并能提取量子度规分量乃至完整量子几何张量。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了QTM方法的潜力并拓展其适用性；若在未来实验中实现，将为扭转双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene）及相关体系的相关相（correlated phases）对称性特征与量子几何提供前所未有的探测途径。

Abstract: In two-dimensional (2D) flat-band systems, as engineered, for instance, by stacking and twisting van der Waals materials, the momentum dependence of the Bloch states is a key factor in determining the interaction-induced emergence of different phases. Hence, methods of probing this form of ''quantum geometry'' are crucial to understanding these systems. Yet, extracting the momentum dependence of the underlying quantum geometric tensor from measurements is challenging in 2D. Here, we propose employing a quantum twisting microscope (QTM) with a sublattice-polarized tip to fill this gap. We show how expectation values of sublattice Pauli matrices can be probed across the scanned momentum path. This allows for the direct measurement of crucial symmetries characterizing the different candidate instabilities in graphene-based moiré systems. We furthermore show that the quantum metric component along the scan direction can also be extracted; the full quantum geometric tensor is also accessible, given that more than one sublattice polarization can be used. Our work illustrates the potential and extends the versatility of the QTM approach. If implemented in future experiments, this would provide unprecedented access to the symmetry signatures of the correlated phases and to the underlying quantum geometry in twisted bilayer graphene and related systems.

</details>
