### [117] [Probing departures from Schwarzschild geometry with the S301 star and kilometre-baseline interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07123)
*Rodrigo P. Silva,Alexandre C. M. Correia,Paulo J. V. Garcia*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: S301 恒星的近心点距仅约为 S2 的十分之一，为探测黑洞自旋与偏离史瓦西度规（Schwarzschild metric）开辟新窗口；作者构建后牛顿长期哈密顿框架并计算单轨道最大投影天体测量偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S301 的极小近心点距离使观测进入探测黑洞自旋及偏离史瓦西几何的新参数区间，需要相应理论工具与可观测偏差估计。

Method: 针对稳态、轴对称时空（stationary, axisymmetric spacetimes）中的束缚测地线，发展后牛顿长期哈密顿量（post-Newtonian secular Hamiltonian）框架，并计算一个轨道周期内的最大投影天体测量偏差（maximum projected astrometric deviation）。

Result: 在预期的 GRAVITY+ 精度下，S301 可探测若干非旋转几何（nonrotating geometries）及可观测的自旋诱导偏差；公里基线干涉测量（kilometre-baseline interferometry）则能探测更小的偏差。

Conclusion: S301 可作为检验人马座 A*（Sgr A*）时空几何的有力探针（powerful test）。

Abstract: The S301 star reaches a pericentre one tenth that of S2, opening a new regime for probing black-hole spin and departures from Schwarzschild. We develop a post-Newtonian secular Hamiltonian framework for bound geodesics in stationary, axisymmetric spacetimes and compute the maximum projected astrometric deviation over one orbit. At the expected GRAVITY+ precision, S301 can probe several nonrotating geometries and sizeable spin-induced deviations, while kilometre-baseline interferometry can probe much smaller departures, establishing S301 as a powerful test of the spacetime geometry of Sgr A*.

</details>


### [118] [Molecular hydrogen formation on dust: The impact of gas-dust drift on formation efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07178)
*Stefan Reissl,Simon C. O. Glover,Ralf S. Klessen,Liam S. Morrissey,Mordecai-Mark Mac Low*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 该研究通过事件驱动动力学蒙特卡洛（event-driven kinetic Monte Carlo）模拟，考察气-尘漂移（gas-dust drift）对尘埃颗粒表面分子氢（H₂）形成的双重影响：漂移提高碰撞率但降低粘附概率（sticking probability），二者竞争决定最终形成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星际介质中H₂主要在尘埃颗粒表面形成，且动态活跃环境中存在气-尘相对运动。已有研究多关注颗粒温度与表面性质对H₂形成的影响，而气-尘漂移的作用尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 采用事件驱动动力学蒙特卡洛模型，追踪球形硅酸盐（silicate）与碳质（carbonaceous）颗粒上的单个H原子，涵盖吸附（adsorption）、表面迁移（surface migration）、热脱附（thermal desorption）以及Langmuir-Hinshelwood（LH）与Eley-Rideal（ER）反应。漂移以平移麦克斯韦速度分布（shifted Maxwellian velocity distribution）描述，并对比恒定粘附概率与依赖撞击能量的粘附概率两种情形。

Result: 漂移导致吸附H原子与H₂形成在颗粒表面呈各向异性（anisotropic）分布。在恒定粘附假设下，漂移增强碰撞率从而提升H₂形成，效率可达ε=0.3–0.4；在能量依赖粘附下，强漂移反而抑制两种材料上的形成，效率降至ε=0.01–0.03。碳质颗粒在更高尘埃温度下仍保持高效。ER反应在大部分参数空间占主导，并随漂移增强而更趋重要。

Conclusion: 气-尘漂移下碰撞率的增强并不必然提升H₂形成效率，其净效应取决于粘附概率对撞击能量的依赖。动态活跃环境的模型应同时纳入气-尘相对速度及其对粘附的影响。

Abstract: Molecular hydrogen is predominantly formed on dust-grain surfaces in the interstellar medium, where relative gas-dust motion can arise in dynamically active environments. While the dependence of H$_2$ formation on grain temperature and surface properties is well studied, the impact of gas-dust drift has received little attention. We investigate how gas-dust drift modifies H$_2$ formation, focusing on the competition between the drift-enhanced H-atom collision rate and reduced sticking at higher impact energies. We use an event-driven kinetic Monte Carlo model that follows individual H atoms on spherical silicate and carbonaceous grains, including adsorption, surface migration, thermal desorption, and Langmuir-Hinshelwood (LH) and Eley-Rideal (ER) reactions. Drift is described by a shifted Maxwellian velocity distribution, and we compare constant and impact-energy-dependent sticking probabilities. Drift produces increasingly anisotropic distributions of adsorbed H and H$_2$ formation across the grain surface. Assuming constant sticking, increasing drift enhances H$_2$ formation through the higher collision rate, with efficiencies up to $ε=0.3-0.4$. With energy-dependent sticking, strong drift instead suppresses formation on both materials, reducing efficiencies to $ε=0.01-0.03$. Carbonaceous grains remain efficient to higher dust temperatures than silicate grains. ER reactions dominate over most of the investigated parameter space and become increasingly important at strong drift as the reduced surface population suppresses LH reactions. Thus, enhanced collision rates under gas-dust drift do not necessarily increase H$_2$ formation. Models of dynamically active environments should account for both relative gas-dust velocities and their effects on sticking.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [All-Orders Classical Soft Graviton Theorems in the Newtonian Limit and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07134)
*Debanjan Karan,Babli Khatun,Raghu Mahajan,Biswajit Sahoo,Ashoke Sen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在牛顿极限（Newtonian limit）下发现了引力波形尾部（tail）中无穷多个领头对数（leading-logarithmic）项，并指出Alessio、Di Vecchia与Heissenberg的全阶猜想（all-orders conjecture）仅在两体散射中成立，对三体及以上初末态不成立。作者进一步提出超越牛顿极限的全阶猜想，虽未证明但通过若干非平凡一致性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典软引力子定理（classical soft graviton theorem）旨在仅利用散射物体的渐近动量（asymptotic momenta）来确定引力波形早晚时段尾部（tail）的特定项。本文意在检验并推广相关全阶猜想在牛顿极限及更一般情形下的适用性。

Method: 在牛顿极限下（入射与出射粒子运动缓慢，但散射本身可以很剧烈）推导引力波形尾部项，求得无穷级数的领头对数尾部项，并将其与Alessio、Di Vecchia和Heissenberg的全阶猜想进行比对；同时提出超越牛顿极限的全阶猜想，并对其进行一致性检验。

Result: 所得结果与Alessio、Di Vecchia和Heissenberg的全阶猜想在两体散射情形下一致；但当初始或末态包含三个及以上粒子时，该猜想一般不再成立。针对超越牛顿极限的情形，作者提出的全阶猜想通过了若干非平凡的一致性检验，但未给出证明。

Conclusion: 牛顿极限下引力波形尾部存在无穷多个领头对数项，既有全阶猜想在多体情形下失效；作者提出的更一般全阶猜想虽缺乏证明，但获得了初步的非平凡佐证。

Abstract: The classical soft graviton theorem aims to determine certain terms in the early- and late-time tails of the gravitational waveform produced in a scattering process, using only the asymptotic momenta of the scattered objects. In this paper we find an infinite series of leading-logarithmic tail terms in the Newtonian limit, where the incoming and outgoing particles move slowly, but the scattering itself can be violent. The all-orders conjecture of Alessio, Di Vecchia and Heissenberg agrees with our results for two-particle scattering, but does not hold in general when the initial or final state contains three or more particles. We also propose an all-orders conjecture for these tails beyond the Newtonian limit, but do not prove it. Nevertheless, the conjecture passes several nontrivial consistency checks.

</details>


### [120] [A New Entropy Puzzle in ABJM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07135)
*Alexandre Belin,Seyed Morteza Hosseini,Palash Singh,Rita Vadala,Alberto Zaffaroni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文指出 AdS$_4\times S^7/\mathbb{Z}_k$ 中 BPS 黑洞熵随 $k$ 增大而增大、而能贡献熵的对偶规范不变算符数却随 $k$ 减小而增多的矛盾，并将其归因于 trace relation 数目随 $k$ 减小而增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 AdS$_4\times S^7/\mathbb{Z}_k$ 的 BPS 黑洞（BPS black hole）中，熵（entropy）随 orbifold 参数 $k$ 增大而增大，但基于 monopole operator 的对偶共形场论（conformal field theory）计数预期相反，二者构成一个待解释的谜题。

Method: 分析 ABJM 理论的超共形指标（superconformal index），在大电荷极限（large charge limit）下考察有限 $N$ 情形是否存在同一谜题，并提出基于 trace relation 的解释方案。

Result: 有限 $N$ 大电荷极限下同样存在该谜题；trace relation 数目随 $k$ 减小而增加，其效应压过 monopole operator 带来的算符增多效应，且主要影响 fortuitous operator，文中给出一个在小 $k$ 下消失的 fortuitous operator 的显式例子。

Conclusion: 该谜题可由 trace relation 的增多得到解决，即对偶算符计数与黑洞熵变化趋势之间的表观矛盾源于 trace relation 对 fortuitous operator 的削减作用。

Abstract: We point out a puzzle about BPS black holes in AdS$_4\times S^7/\mathbb{Z}_k$: the entropy grows when $k$ increases although the number of gauge-invariant operators in the dual conformal field theory that can contribute to the entropy grows as $k$ decreases, due to the presence of monopole operators. Analyzing the superconformal index of ABJM, we show that the same puzzle exists for finite $N$ in the large charge limit. We propose a resolution based on an increasing number of trace relations as $k$ decreases, which dominates over the other effect. We argue that this mostly affects fortuitous operators and we provide an explicit example of a fortuitous operator that disappears at small $k$.

</details>


### [121] [Checking the Schwinger-Dyson equation in the purely open matrix-tensor model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07141)
*Daniel L. Jafferis,Liza Rozenberg,Diandian Wang,Cynthia Yan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在纯开放扇区（purely open sector）中利用 Schwinger-Dyson 关系检验三维引力（three-dimensional gravity）的矩阵-张量（matrix-tensor）描述。作者枚举并分类了柱面振幅（cylinder amplitude）的拓扑贡献，并在 Virasoro TQFT 启发的计数方案下，发现与势能中两个相互作用项对应流形数量之差为 1 的猜想相符；若按不等价表面嵌入（surface embeddings）分别计数则出现不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵-张量模型被提出用于描述三维引力，但其有效性仍需在具体振幅层面加以检验。本文旨在通过柱面振幅的拓扑贡献计数，检验该描述中势能两个相互作用项对应流形数量相差 1 的猜想。

Method: 在矩阵-张量描述的纯开放扇区中使用 Schwinger-Dyson 关系（Schwinger-Dyson relation）。枚举并分类柱面振幅的拓扑贡献，并采用一种由 Virasoro TQFT 启发的计数方案（counting prescription）进行统计。

Result: 在 Virasoro TQFT 启发的计数方案下，得到与猜想一致的结果，即两个相互作用项对应的贡献流形数量之差为单位差（unit difference）。相比之下，若将不等价的表面嵌入分别计数，则得到不一致的结果。

Conclusion: 该对比表明，明确指定引力路径积分（gravitational path integral）中应包含的体拓扑（bulk topologies）集合至关重要，计数方案的选择会直接影响矩阵-张量描述与预期结果是否吻合。

Abstract: We test the proposed matrix-tensor description of three-dimensional gravity using a Schwinger-Dyson relation in its purely open sector. We enumerate and classify the topological contributions to a cylinder amplitude. Under a proposed counting prescription motivated by Virasoro TQFT, we find agreement with the conjectured unit difference between the numbers of contributing manifolds associated to the two interaction terms in the potential. By contrast, counting all inequivalent surface embeddings separately would instead give a mismatch. This comparison highlights the importance of specifying the set of bulk topologies to be included in the gravitational path integral.

</details>


### [122] [The Back-to-Back Plateau of the Energy-Energy Correlator from Weak to Strong Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07145)
*Hao Chen,Ian Moult,Bo Wang,Hua Xing Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作研究能量-能量关联子（energy-energy correlator, EEC）背靠背极限（back-to-back limit）中出现的有限平台（plateau），并在 $\mathcal N=4$ SYM 中完成弱耦合端计算与强弱耦合间的插值构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEC 背靠背极限的平台刻画了从弱耦合微扰软-共线辐射（soft-collinear radiation）到强耦合弦动力学（string dynamics）的演化，因此有必要精确刻画该平台高度的耦合依赖性。

Method: 在弱耦合端，对领头与次领头幂次对数（leading and next-to-leading-power logarithms）进行重求和（resummation）至 N$^3$LL，纳入非平面（non-planar）贡献，并将该弱耦合端点与 bootstrap 及强耦合数据结合构造插值；计算借助人机协同的智能体工作流（human-in-the-loop agentic workflow）完成。

Result: 获得了 $\mathcal N=4$ SYM 中直至 N$^3$LL 的弱耦合对数重求和结果，并据此构建了平台高度从弱耦合到强耦合的插值。

Conclusion: 弱耦合端点、bootstrap 与强耦合数据的结合可实现平台高度在强弱耦合区间的连续插值，且该复杂计算可由人机协同智能体工作流有效支撑。

Abstract: The back-to-back limit of the energy-energy correlator (EEC) exhibits a finite plateau, capturing the evolution from perturbative soft-collinear radiation at weak coupling to string dynamics at strong coupling. At weak coupling, we resum its leading and next-to-leading-power logarithms through N$^3$LL in $\mathcal N=4$ SYM, including non-planar contributions. Combining this weak-coupling endpoint with bootstrap and strong-coupling data, we construct an interpolation for the plateau height from weak to strong coupling. The calculation uses a human-in-the-loop agentic workflow.

</details>


### [123] [Deriving the $\text{AdS}_3\times\text{S}^3\times \text{T}^4$ Quantum Spectral Curve II: T-system and QSC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07147)
*Andrea Cavaglià,Davide Polvara,Nicolò Primi,Alessandro Sfondrini,Roberto Tateo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在前作所得扩展 Y-system 基础上，完整推导了纯 RR 流（Ramond–Ramond flux）下 $\text{AdS}_3\times\text{S}^3\times\text{T}^4$ 弦论的量子谱曲线（Quantum Spectral Curve, QSC），并建立其与热力学 Bethe 拟设（Thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz, TBA）形式间的精确关系，最终凝练为 $\mathbf{P}\mu$-系统（$\mathbf{P}\mu$-system）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作仅获得扩展的 Y-system，而所猜想的 QSC 尚缺乏严格推导，且 QSC 与 TBA 两种形式之间的关系尚未明确。本文旨在完成该情形下 QSC 的系统性推导并厘清其形式结构。

Method: 以先前论文中得到的扩展 Y-system 为出发点，建立 QSC 与热力学 Bethe 拟设两种形式体系之间的精确对应关系；据此推导此前仅为猜想的 QSC，并进一步得出其解所满足的附加关系；同时通过解析结构分析无质量激发（massless excitations）的编码方式。

Result: 完成了纯 RR 流下 $\text{AdS}_3\times\text{S}^3\times\text{T}^4$ 弦论 QSC 的系统推导，确立了 TBA 与 QSC 形式间的精确关联，导出了此前所猜想的 QSC，并揭示了其解所满足的额外关系；明确指出无质量激发虽未显式出现在 QSC 基本构件中，却由其解析结构所编码。

Conclusion: 推导的最终结果即所谓的 $\mathbf{P}\mu$-系统，它以少量函数及其解析延拓（analytic continuations）为语言，给出了 QSC 的一种紧凑表述。

Abstract: We complete the systematic derivation of the Quantum Spectral Curve (QSC) for strings on $\text{AdS}_3\times\text{S}^3\times \text{T}^4$ with pure Ramond--Ramond (RR) flux, building on the extended Y-system obtained in our preceding paper. We establish the precise relation between the two formalisms of the Thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz and the QSC, derive the previously conjectured QSC, and, along the way, uncover additional relations satisfied by its solutions. Our analysis also clarifies how massless excitations, although not explicit among the basic QSC building blocks, are encoded in their analytic structure. The derivation culminates in the so-called $\mathbf{P}μ$-system, a compact formulation of the QSC in terms of a small set of functions and their analytic continuations.

</details>


### [124] [Localizing $\mathcal{N}=(2,0)$ $AdS_3$ solutions of M-theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07153)
*Carolina Matté Gregory,Catherine Notman,James Sparks*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文运用等变局部化（equivariant localization）方法研究十一维超引力中 $AdS_3\times M_8$ 解，该类解对偶于二维 $\mathcal N=(2,0)$ 超共形场论（SCFT）。通过构造编码磁通及其对偶、校准 M2-膜与翘曲体积的等变闭多形式，将受保护可观测量约化为 $U(1)$ R-对称作用的固定点数据，并导出前导中心荷与引力反常的拓扑公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是建立一套统一框架，将 $AdS_3\times M_8$ 超引力解对偶的二维 $\mathcal N=(2,0)$ SCFT 的受保护可观测量，通过等变局部化技术直接由固定点数据计算，从而以拓扑方式表达中心荷与引力反常。

Method: 构造编码磁通及其对偶、校准 M2-膜（calibrated M2-branes）与翘曲体积（warped volume）的等变闭多形式（equivariantly closed polyforms），将受保护可观测量约化为 $U(1)$ R-对称作用的固定点数据，并应用于 $S^4$ 丛（over coassociative four-cycles）与 $S^2$ 丛（over compact six-manifolds）两类几何。

Result: 获得以量子化磁通（quantized fluxes）与拓扑数据表示的、前导中心荷（leading central charges）与引力反常（gravitational anomalies）的拓扑公式；复现了 $T^2\times KE_4$ 解的已知结果，并构造出一个具有量子化磁通、位于已知多项式分支之外的紧致数值解。

Conclusion: 等变局部化可为 $AdS_3\times M_8$ 解提供有效的拓扑计算途径，既能重现既有结果，又能刻画此前未涵盖的非多项式解分支，表明该方法在二维 SCFT 全息对偶的受保护量计算中具有更广适用范围。

Abstract: We apply equivariant localization to supersymmetric $AdS_3\times M_8$ solutions of eleven-dimensional supergravity, dual to two-dimensional $\mathcal N=(2,0)$ SCFTs. We construct equivariantly closed polyforms encoding the magnetic flux and its dual, calibrated M2-branes and the warped volume, reducing protected observables to fixed point data of the $U(1)$ R-symmetry action. We apply the framework to $S^4$ bundles over coassociative four-cycles and $S^2$ bundles over compact six-manifolds, obtaining topological formulae for leading central charges and gravitational anomalies in terms of quantized fluxes and topological data. We recover the known results for $T^2\times KE_4$ solutions and construct a compact numerical solution with quantized flux that lies outside the known polynomial branch.

</details>


### [125] [The s-Rule from Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07154)
*Sebastian Franco,Diego Rodriguez-Gomez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用局部 M 理论描述，将 s-rule 解释为局部 7-brane 形变全局化的障碍，并给出 T-cone 与终止于 7-brane 的五维膜之间的直接几何联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦理论为构造和研究五维量子场论提供了有力工具，其中几何工程与五膜网是两种重要描述。然而在存在 7-brane 时，如何将二者关联起来需要把 s-rule 纳入几何框架，此前尚不清晰。

Method: 聚焦终止于 7-brane 的基本三腿五膜网，基于五膜终止于 7-brane 的局部 M 理论描述，研究具有单一非本原边的三角环面图（toric diagram），并分析相应的局部复结构形变与粘合映射。

Result: 对应的局部复结构形变在与相邻环面片的交叠上良定义；当且仅当满足 s-rule 时，粘合映射作为全纯坐标的函数在相邻片区域内保持良好行为。在饱和情形下，几何为 T-cone，7-brane 形变成为其 Q-Gorenstein 光滑化。

Conclusion: s-rule 被识别为局部 7-brane 形变全局化的障碍，从而在 T-cone 与终止于 7-brane 的五膜之间建立了直接的几何联系。

Abstract: String Theory provides powerful tools for constructing and studying five-dimensional Quantum Field Theories, notably through geometric engineering and five-brane webs. Understanding how these two descriptions are related in the presence of 7-branes requires incorporating the s-rule into the geometry. To address this question, we focus on basic three-legged five-brane webs ending on 7-branes. Building on the local M-theory description of five-branes ending on 7-branes, we consider triangular toric diagrams with a single non-primitive edge. We show that the corresponding local complex-structure deformation is well defined on the overlaps with the neighboring toric patches, while the corresponding gluing map remains well behaved as a function of the holomorphic coordinates throughout the neighboring patches precisely when the s-rule is satisfied. At saturation, the geometry is a T-cone and the 7-brane deformation becomes its Q-Gorenstein smoothing. Our results thus identify the s-rule with the obstruction to globalizing the local 7-brane deformation and provide a direct geometric link between T-cones and five-branes ending on 7-branes.

</details>


### [126] [Interacting multicomponent $SU(N)$ magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07157)
*Alexios P. Polychronakos,Konstantinos Sfetsos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该研究考察由具有 $SU(N)$ 不变两体相互作用的原子构成的分子磁体，在平均场框架下推导其热力学性质，发现可呈现铁磁-反铁磁混合行为，并完整分析由两个处于 $SU(2)$ 自旋-$j$ 表示的原子组成的最简非平凡情形及其相结构与相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统磁体研究多以 $SU(2)$ 自旋体系为出发点，而冷原子等平台可实现具有更高对称性的 $SU(N)$ 相互作用。本文动机在于推广常规磁体图像，刻画原子携带不同不可约表示时相互作用强度依赖具体原子种类的磁性体系。

Method: 采用平均场方法（mean-field approach）推导此类分子磁体的热力学性质。原子态按 $SU(N)$ 的不同不可约表示（irreducible representations）变换，两体相互作用强度取决于所涉及的原子种类。并对两个处于 $SU(2)$ 自旋-$j$ 表示的原子的最简非平凡情形进行完整分析。

Result: 在平均场层面得到该分子磁体的热力学行为，发现其可表现出铁磁与反铁磁的混合行为（mixed ferro-antiferromagnetic behavior）。对双原子自旋-$j$ 情形，导出了相结构（phase structure）与相变（transitions）。

Conclusion: 该工作将常规磁体体系推广至 $SU(N)$ 对称且原子表示可不同的分子磁体，证明其可具备铁磁-反铁磁混合特征；双原子自旋-$j$ 分析为理解更一般的 $SU(N)$ 分子磁体相图提供了可解的最简范例。

Abstract: We study magnets composed of molecules with atoms interacting via $SU(N)$-invariant two-body interactions. Atoms carry states transforming in generically different irreducible representations of $SU(N)$, and the interaction strength between atoms depends on the specific atoms involved. We derive the thermodynamics of these magnets in the mean-field approach, and find that they can exhibit mixed ferro-antiferromagnetic behavior. We present a full analysis of the simplest nontrivial case of molecules consisting of two atoms in the spin-$j$ representation of $SU(2)$, which generalizes conventional magnetic systems, and derive its phase structure and transitions.

</details>


### [127] [Non-invertible SPT phases from fusion rings via Tannaka duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07165)
*Antonio Santaniello,Roberto Valandro,Massimo Zorzenon*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在(1+1)维具非可逆对称性（non-invertible symmetry）的量子场论中，仅凭拓扑线融合规则无法判断对称性是否无反常（anomaly-free）。本文基于Tannaka对偶（Tannaka duality），从融合环（fusion ring）直接提取纤维函子（fiber functor），并在Tambara--Yamagami环与阶27的两个extra-special群表示范畴共享的秩11融合环上发现多于一个纤维函子，对应多个非可逆SPT相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑线的融合规则（fusion rules）虽最易获得，却不足以判定对称性是否允许平庸能隙相（trivially gapped phases）。此类相由相应融合范畴（fusion category）的纤维函子刻画，而直接求解五边形方程（pentagon equations）寻找范畴化通常困难，故需一种更简便的判定手段。

Method: 以Tannaka对偶为基础，仅由融合环出发，通过联立求解幺半结构映射（monoidal structure maps）与张量自同构（tensor automorphisms）提取纤维函子，从而在无需直接求解五边形方程的情况下找到容许纤维函子的范畴化。该方法在已知的$\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$ Tambara--Yamagami环及阶27的两个extra-special群表示范畴共享的秩11融合环上进行检验。

Result: 在两个案例中均发现多于一个纤维函子，即存在数个非可逆SPT相。秩11融合环包含重数3的通道（multiplicity 3 channel），与自三重性（self-triality）相关。利用这些纤维函子的显式数据，导出了两个表示范畴的$F$-符号（$F$-symbols）的紧凑形式，其依赖于非退化交错双特征（non-degenerate alternating bicharacter）与一个可逆对象$ε$。基于重建数据还计算了两个不同SPT相界面上（interface）的反常代数（anomalous algebra）。

Conclusion: 仅由融合环即可通过Tannaka对偶提取纤维函子，从而绕开直接求解五边形方程，为判定非可逆对称性是否无反常提供了可行途径；并且同一融合环可对应多个纤维函子，意味着多个非可逆SPT相的存在。

Abstract: In a (1+1)-dimensional QFT with non-invertible symmetry, the fusion rules of topological lines are often the most accessible data, yet they do not fully determine whether the symmetry is anomaly-free, i.e. whether it admits trivially gapped phases. Such phases are characterized by fiber functors of the corresponding fusion category. Building on Tannaka duality, we extract fiber functors from the fusion ring alone by solving simultaneously for monoidal structure maps and tensor automorphisms. This allows us to find categorifications admitting a fiber functor without solving the pentagon equations directly. We test this procedure on the known Tambara--Yamagami ring of $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$. Our main result concerns the rank-11 fusion ring shared by the representation categories of the two extraspecial groups of order 27, which has a multiplicity 3 channel and is relevant to self-triality. In both cases we find more than one fiber functor, i.e. several non-invertible SPT phases. We use the explicit data of these fiber functors to derive the $F$-symbols of the two representation categories in a compact form depending on a non-degenerate alternating bicharacter and an invertible object $ε$. Using the reconstructed data, we compute the anomalous algebra on the interface between two different SPT phases.

</details>


### [128] [Multi-Point Affine Bialgebras for AdS/CFT Integrability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07170)
*Niklas Beisert,Egor Im,Hannes Malcha*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视 AdS/CFT 世界面散射与一维 Hubbard 模型可积结构所依赖的经典仿射李双代数（affine Lie bialgebra）的解析结构，指出既有代数关系除无穷远点与零点外还隐含额外奇点；作者借助多奇点黎曼球面上的亚纯函数与 Krichever-Novikov 代数，构造三点与四点仿射代数，并证明其构成一致的准三角李双代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究已显示，构成环代数与仿射代数基的洛朗多项式（Laurent polynomials）在展开点无穷远与零点之外还存在两个奇点位置，但原先仅以几何级数展开方式处理。本文旨在用亚纯函数（meromorphic functions）严格表述这些奇点，并厘清附加奇点可能带来的限制与扩展。

Method: 在复平面上细致分析仿射双代数关系的解析结构，考察附加奇点引起的限制与扩展。首先尝试以额外奇点作为洛朗多项式展开点来重构关系（该方法存在缺陷），随后为有理情形与三角情形分别提出三点与四点仿射代数；并基于 Krichever-Novikov 代数，为带若干穿刺点的黎曼球面上的亚纯函数选取合适基。

Result: 通过以额外奇点作为展开点的初步重构虽存不足，但最终以三点、四点仿射代数克服了遗留问题。在适当的亚纯函数基选取下，证明多点仿射代数构成一致的准三角李双代数（quasi-triangular Lie bialgebras）。

Conclusion: 附加奇点可被严格纳入仿射双代数框架：借助多穿刺黎曼球面上的亚纯函数与 Krichever-Novikov 代数基，多点仿射代数能一致地实现为准三角李双代数，从而为 AdS/CFT 世界面散射与 Hubbard 模型的可积结构提供更完备的代数基础。

Abstract: In this work, we revisit the analytical structure of the classical affine Lie bialgebra underlying the integrable structures of AdS/CFT worldsheet scattering and the one-dimensional Hubbard model. Earlier work showed that the algebraic relations implied two singularity locations in the evaluation parameter plane besides the common expansion points infinity and zero for Laurent polynomials forming the basis for loop and affine algebras. Our aim is to formulate these singularities rigorously using meromorphic functions rather than by expansion into geometric series.
  We thus carefully analyse the analytical structure of the affine bialgebra relations in the complex plane paying attention to potential restrictions and extensions due to the additional singularities. As a first attempt with some shortcomings, we reformulate the affine bialgebra relations with the extra singularities serving as the expansion points for Laurent polynomials. We finally overcome the remaining issues by proposing three-point and four-point affine algebras for the rational and trigonometric cases. With a suitable choice of basis for meromorphic functions on the Riemann sphere with several punctures based on Krichever-Novikov algebras, we show that the multi-point affine algebras form consistent quasi-triangular Lie bialgebras.

</details>


### [129] [Generalised entanglement wedges in three dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07176)
*Abhisek Sahu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在真空 AdS₃ 中于领头阶 G 下探究广义纠缠楔（generalized entanglement wedges, GWs）性质的起源，证明 GWs 可由普通纠缠楔的并与交构造，并借助积分几何（integral geometry）的 Crofton 公式将其周长与边界 CFT 区间冯诺依曼熵（von Neumann entropy）相联系，从而表明 GWs 的单调性与强次可加性源自边界 CFT 熵的强次可加性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bousso 与 Penington 提出的广义纠缠楔（GWs）具有嵌套（nesting）、单调性（monotonicity）及广义熵强次可加性（strong subadditivity）等性质；在 AdS/CFT 中普通纠缠楔的对应性质源于对偶 CFT 结构，本文追问 GWs 的性质是否具有类似起源。

Method: 在真空 AdS₃ 中于 G 的领头阶（leading order in G）展开研究：先证明静态切片上的每个 GW 都是测地凸区域（geodesically convex regions）的并集，而每个此类区域是普通纠缠楔的交；再运用积分几何（integral geometry）的 Crofton 公式将凸区域周长写为与其相交的测地线集合的测度（measure），并利用 AdS₃ 中每条测地线均为某边界区间的 Ryu–Takayanagi 面这一事实，将该测度表示为 CFT 区间冯诺依曼熵（von Neumann entropy）的表达式。

Result: 每个 GW 均由纠缠楔的并与交构成；凸区域周长可表示为 CFT 区间 von Neumann 熵的函数；CFT 熵的强次可加性保证该测度为正，而 GWs 的单调性与强次可加性则由测地线集合间的简单不等式推出。

Conclusion: GWs 的强次可加性是边界 CFT 强次可加性的直接推论。结果进一步暗示 GWs 对应于比边界子区域（boundary subregions）更一般的 CFT 子系统（subsystems）。

Abstract: Generalized entanglement wedges (GWs), proposed by Bousso and Penington for gravitating regions in general spacetimes, share many properties of ordinary entanglement wedges, including nesting, monotonicity and strong subadditivity of their generalized entropies. In AdS/CFT, the corresponding properties of entanglement wedges are consequences of the structure of the dual CFT. In this paper we ask whether the properties of GWs have a similar origin. We explore this question in vacuum AdS$_3$ at leading order in $G$. We first show that every GW on a static slice is a union of geodesically convex regions. Each such region is an intersection of ordinary entanglement wedges, so every GW is built from unions and intersections of entanglement wedges. We then use the Crofton formula of integral geometry to write the perimeter of a convex region as the measure of the set of geodesics that intersect it. In AdS$_3$ every geodesic is the Ryu--Takayanagi surface of a boundary interval, so this measure can be written in terms of the von Neumann entropies of CFT intervals. Strong subadditivity of the CFT entropy guarantees that the measure is positive. Monotonicity and strong subadditivity of GWs then follow from simple inequalities between sets of geodesics. The strong subadditivity of GWs is therefore a direct consequence of strong subadditivity on the boundary. Our results further suggest that GWs correspond to subsystems of the CFT that are more general than boundary subregions.

</details>


### [130] [Geometry of massive loops in gravitational scattering: Maximal cut, Riemann's bilinear relations and genus drop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07234)
*Arpan Bhattacharyya,Saptaswa Ghosh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究含质量媒介子（massive mediator）的经典引力散射（classical gravitational scattering）所对应两圈世界线量子场论（worldline quantum field theory）积分族的极大切割（maximal cut）几何结构，发现其在两圈即出现椭圆极大切割，并系统给出主积分、微分方程、周期矩阵、单值群与函数空间等五个主要结论，为完整含质量两圈积分奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典引力散射的微扰计算需要处理含质量媒介子的两圈世界线积分，其极大切割的几何结构（尤其是否出现椭圆曲线）此前尚不清楚，且与无质量情形可能本质不同。明确该几何结构及其积分方法学是通往完整含质量两圈积分的必要一步。

Method: 在全局 Baikov 表示（global Baikov representation）下进行连续两次积分，将极大切割归约为椭圆曲线（elliptic curve）的周期；使用分部积分（integration-by-parts）结合反射（reflection）分解积分块，用 Laporta 归约与交叠理论（intersection theory）两种途径推导微分方程并构造其间的有理映射；以 Appell 与 Beta 函数计算周期矩阵，推导扭曲黎曼周期关系（twisted Riemann period relations），研究微分系统的全局单值群（monodromy），并以椭圆多重对数（elliptic multiple polylogs）刻画迭代积分的函数空间，亦在碰撞参数空间分析贝塞尔扭曲微分（Bessel-twisted differential）。

Result: 含质量的两圈世界线积分在极大切割处已给出椭圆曲线，而在无质量极限与静态极限下退化为节点曲线（nodal curve）；分部积分生成五个主积分，经反射拆分为三维椭圆块与二维多重对数块；周期矩阵以 Appell 与 Beta 函数表达并获得九个二次恒等式；建立了满足黎曼双线性关系的全局单值群关系；给出以椭圆多重对数表示的迭代积分函数空间；碰撞参数空间中傅里叶变换后的切割为椭圆曲线上的贝塞尔扭曲微分，且具有额外对合对称性，其奇偶性决定周期落在有理或椭圆商上。

Conclusion: 上述结果均针对极大切割（即齐次解），但系统性揭示了含质量两圈积分的椭圆几何与积分方法学框架，为攻克完整含质量两圈积分提供了重要路径。

Abstract: We study a representative two-loop family of worldline quantum field theory integrals relevant for classical gravitational scattering with a massive mediator and investigate the geometric structure of its maximal cut. We find that, unlike the massless case, the massive loops already give rise to an elliptic maximal cut at two loops, which degenerates into a nodal curve in the massless limit and in the static limit. In the global Baikov representation, two successive integrations reduce the maximal cut to a period of an elliptic curve. We further establish five primary consequences of this study: (a) The integration-by-parts method generates five master integrals, which, under reflection, split into a three-dimensional elliptic block and a two-dimensional polylog block. We derive the differential equation in two ways, one by Laporta reduction and subsequently by intersection theory in two cocycle bases, and construct the rational map between them. (b) The period matrices in arbitrary dimension have been computed in terms of Appell and Beta functions. Furthermore, we derive twisted Riemann period relations that yield nine quadratic identities among them. (c) We study the global monodromy relation of the differential system that satisfies Riemann's bilinear relation. (d) We explicitly derive the function space of the iterated integrals in terms of elliptic multiple polylogs. (e) In impact-parameter space, the Fourier-transformed cut is a Bessel-twisted differential on the elliptic curve, which we show has extra involution symmetries. Thus, the parity of the differential further decides which quotient - rational or elliptic its periods live on. All results presented concern the maximal cut and, therefore, the homogeneous solutions, but they open an important pathway to tackling the full massive two-loop integral.

</details>


### [131] [The QCD phase diagram and critical end point at finite magnetic field from holography with neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07353)
*Shaojie Liu,Yehong Zhang,Hui Liu,Xuanmin Cao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该研究利用五维爱因斯坦–麦克斯韦–伸缩子（Einstein–Maxwell--dilaton, EMD）模型，结合神经网络形式的重整化群径向演化方法，在零重子化学势下考察磁场驱动的量子色动力学（quantum chromodynamics, QCD）热相变临界终点（critical end point, CEP）。模型校准至零磁场与有限磁场下的格点熵，给出临界点位置约为 (eB_c, T_c) ≃ (7.22 GeV², 95.9 MeV)，并预测相边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探明磁场如何改变 QCD 热相变的性质，即从连续过渡（crossover）转为一级相变（first-order transition），并确定其临界终点。该问题关系到 QCD 相图（phase diagram）在有限磁场下的结构，而相边界本身在摘要中被称为模型的预测结果。

Method: 采用五维爱因斯坦–麦克斯韦–伸缩子（EMD）全息模型（holographic model），并以神经网络形式（neural-network formulation）实现全息径向演化（holographic radial evolution）。通过将模型校准至零磁场与有限磁场下的格点熵（lattice entropy）来固定参数。利用温度转折点（temperature turning points）的合并、比热发散（divergent specific heat）与声速平方（squared sound speed）趋零作为临界终点的判据；以 s/T³ 在过渡区的拐点定义 crossover 温度，以等压 Maxwell 构造（equal-pressure Maxwell construction）确定一级相变共存线（coexistence line）。

Result: 随磁场增大，热相变由 crossover 转变为一阶相变；温度转折点合并、比热发散与声速平方趋零共同指示 (eB_c, T_c) ≃ (7.22 GeV², 95.9 MeV)。由 s/T³ 拐点定义的 crossover 线与等压 Maxwell 构造给出的一阶共存线在 CEP 相交。相变级次的变化与格点结果在定性上一致；相边界为校准后模型的预测。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，经格点熵校准的 EMD 全息模型可定性再现磁场引起的 QCD 相变级次变化，并给出临界终点位置与相边界的模型预测。相边界属于模型的预测性输出，而非直接由格点数据确定。

Abstract: We study the magnetic-field-driven critical end point (CEP) of the QCD thermal transition at zero baryon chemical potential using a five-dimensional Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton (EMD) model. We calibrate the model to lattice entropy at zero and finite magnetic fields using a neural-network formulation of holographic radial evolution. As the magnetic field increases, the thermal transition changes from a crossover to a first-order transition. The merger of temperature turning points, a divergent specific heat, and a vanishing squared sound speed identify $(eB_c,T_c)\simeq(7.22\,\mathrm{GeV}^{2},95.9\,\mathrm{MeV})$. The transition-region inflection of $s/T^3$ defines the crossover temperature, while an equal-pressure Maxwell construction determines the first-order coexistence line. The two lines meet at the CEP. The change of transition order agrees qualitatively with lattice results. The phase boundary is a prediction of the calibrated model.

</details>


### [132] [Quantum Algorithms for Finding Vacua in the ADK Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07439)
*Shirabe Endo,Yuta Hamada*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究在 Arkani-Hamed、Dimopoulos 与 Kachru 提出的弦景观（string Landscape）玩具模型中，利用量子算法寻找具有小宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）的真空态。通过构造函数值预言机（function-value oracle）并利用真空能的线性结构，将问题归约为碰撞问题（collision problem），从而将查询复杂度（query complexity）从 $\mathcal{O}(2^{N/2})$ 降至 $\mathcal{O}(2^{N/3})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弦景观中寻找小宇宙学常数真空是重要但计算困难的问题；经典伪多项式算法（pseudo-polynomial classical algorithm）代价较高，因此探索量子加速途径具有动机。

Method: 首先构造函数值预言机并用于 Grover 搜索；随后指出真空能的线性性质可将问题约化为比特串两个分块之间的碰撞问题，据此构造两种量子算法。

Result: Grover 搜索的查询复杂度为 $\mathcal{O}(2^{N/2})$，其中 $N$ 为标量场数目；基于碰撞结构的两种量子算法查询复杂度为 $\mathcal{O}(2^{N/3})$。

Conclusion: 与 Denef 和 Douglas 的伪多项式经典算法相比，在物理上有意义的参数取值下，所提量子算法显著降低了计算成本。

Abstract: We study quantum algorithms for finding a vacuum with a small cosmological constant in the toy model for string Landscape proposed by Arkani-Hamed, Dimopoulos, and Kachru. First, we construct a function-value oracle and use it for Grover search with query complexity $\mathcal{O}(2^{N/2})$, where $N$ is the number of scalar fields. Then, we point out that, by exploiting the linearity of the vacuum energy, the problem can be reduced to a collision problem between two blocks of the bit string. With this structure, we construct two quantum algorithms with query complexity $\mathcal{O}(2^{N/3})$. Compared with the pseudo-polynomial classical algorithm of Denef and Douglas, the quantum algorithms yield a large reduction in computational cost for the physically motivated parameter values.

</details>


### [133] [Soft-photon theorem near static spherical horizons in generic dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07498)
*Kai-Yu Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在一般静态球对称时空的非极端分叉 Killing 视界附近研究 Maxwell 理论，基于 Eddington–Finkelstein 正则坐标构建视界相空间与大 U(1) 荷，并推导软光子关系，将其与平直空间软光子定理进行维度相关的比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解非极端视界附近的规范场相空间结构及其大规范对称性（large gauge symmetry），并厘清视界软光子关系与平直空间软光子定理（soft photon theorem）之间的关系如何依赖时空维度。

Method: 在 D>3 维一般静态球对称时空的非极端分叉 Killing 视界（bifurcate Killing horizon）附近施加近视界边界条件，采用正则 Eddington–Finkelstein 坐标以保留辐射数据与库仑数据；构建视界相空间与大 U(1) 荷，并将视界场按 Killing 频率与球谐函数（spherical harmonics）展开，由大 U(1) Ward 恒等式导出软光子关系，同时以相同球谐函数与对易子归一化表达平直空间领头软光子定理。

Result: 在零无穷远处，辐射衰减行为依赖时空维度，奇数 D 时涉及半整数幂，而有限半径处的非极端视界允许普通整数幂展开的辐射数据。D=4 时，视界软光子关系与平直空间关系具有相同的软频率依赖和相同的硬荷项角向依赖；D>4 时，平直空间关系含有额外的维度相关频率因子与角向因子。

Conclusion: 视界附近辐射数据的幂次展开结构与零无穷远不同，且视界与平直空间软光子关系仅于 D=4 一致，在 D>4 出现维度相关的偏离，表明软定理的视界—平直空间对应具有维度依赖性。

Abstract: We study Maxwell theory near a nonextremal bifurcate Killing horizon in a general static, spherically symmetric spacetime of dimension $D>3$. We impose near-horizon boundary conditions in a regular Eddington--Finkelstein chart that retain both radiative and Coulombic data, and construct the associated horizon phase space and large-$U(1)$ charge. At null infinity, the radiative falloff depends on the spacetime dimension and involves half-integer powers in odd $D$, whereas a nonextremal horizon at finite radius supports radiative data with an ordinary integer-power expansion. Expanding the horizon fields in Killing frequency and spherical harmonics, we derive the associated soft-photon relation from the large-$U(1)$ Ward identity. For comparison, we express the flat-space leading soft-photon theorem using the same spherical harmonics and commutator normalization as the horizon modes. In $D=4$, the two relations have the same soft-frequency dependence and the same angular dependence of the hard-charge terms, whereas for $D>4$ the flat-space relation contains additional dimension-dependent frequency and angular factors.

</details>


### [134] [Spin-1 Bulk Local States in AdS/CFT and Carrollian Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07548)
*Peng-Xiang Hao,Kotaro Shinmyo,Kenta Suzuki,Yu-ki Suzuki,Shunta Takahashi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在 AdS₃/CFT₂ 及三维 Carrollian 全息框架下构建了有质量自旋-1 场的体局部态（bulk local states），通过保持体点不变的等距群协变性将其分解为两个确定螺旋度（helicity）分支，并证明其内积与 AdS₃ 有质量矢量两点函数一致；在 Carrollian 理论中求解相同条件，经平坦极限得到诱导模（induced module），支持诱导表示作为 Carrollian 全息中有质量体激发的载体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有体局部态（bulk local states）的重建多集中于标量场，带自旋的体局部态尚未被完整构造，从态本身到体-体传播子（bulk-to-bulk propagator）及两种描述之间的极限关系均缺失。本文旨在填补这一空白，在 AdS₃/CFT₂ 与三维 Carrollian 全息中构建有质量自旋-1 场的体局部态。

Method: 利用保持体点不变的等距变换（isometries）协变性确定矢量的三个分量，将解拆分为两个具有确定螺旋度（helicity）的分支；将有质量矢量的 HKLL 表示（HKLL representation）按边界后裔（boundary descendants）展开，逐项重现三个分量态；在 Carrollian 理论的诱导表示（induced representation）中求解相同条件，并取生成元的平坦极限（flat limit）。

Result: 两个分支共同描述宇称不变（parity-invariant）的 Proca 场，单独一个分支对应 Maxwell–Chern–Simons 理论中唯一传播模式。在平坦极限下，最高权模（highest weight module）变为诱导模，AdS 的态与格林函数（Green's functions）化为其 Carrollian 对应物，且内积重现 AdS₃ 有质量矢量两点函数。

Conclusion: 这是首个带自旋的体局部态构造，从态本身完整贯通到体-体传播子及连接两种描述的极限，并支持将诱导表示视为 Carrollian 全息中有质量体激发的归属载体。

Abstract: We construct the bulk local states of a massive spin-1 field in AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$ and three-dimensional Carrollian holography. In AdS, covariance under the isometries that leave a bulk point invariant determines the three components of the vector there and splits the solution into two branches of definite helicity, whose inner products reproduce the massive vector two-point function of AdS$_3$. By expanding the massive vector HKLL representation in boundary descendants, one can reproduce the three component states term by term. The two branches together describe the parity-invariant Proca field, and one alone the single propagating mode of Maxwell--Chern--Simons theory. The same conditions are solved in the induced representation of the Carrollian theory, where the solution again splits into two branches. Under the flat limit of the generators the highest weight module becomes the induced module, and the AdS states and Green's functions become their Carrollian counterparts, which supports the induced representation as the home of massive bulk excitations in Carrollian holography. This is the first bulk local state built with spin, complete from the state itself to its bulk-to-bulk propagator and to the limit relating the two descriptions.

</details>


### [135] [Thermodynamics and mechanical laws of causal-diamond horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07746)
*Carlos R. Ordóñez,Gustavo Valdivia-Mera*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文为闵可夫斯基时空（Minkowski spacetime）中的因果菱形（causal diamond）视界（horizon）建立统一的热力学与力学框架，导出熵—面积关系及局部第一、第二定律，并验证第零定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果菱形是闵可夫斯基时空因果结构的基本构件，但其视界是否具备与黑洞视界类似的自洽热力学与力学定律结构尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用欧几里得正则性（Euclidean regularity）确定温度 $T_D=1/(\pi\alpha)$；利用正则化的 Gibbons–Hawking–York 贡献得到 $I_D^E=-A_0/(4G)$；对穿越未来视界局部区域的弱物质扰动进行 Raychaudhuri 分析，将 Killing 能量通量（Killing-energy flux）与面积响应关联。

Result: 平衡几何能量为零给出熵—面积关系 $S_D=A_0/(4G)$；在分叉面（bifurcation surface）附近得到领头阶的局部物理过程第一定律，且对满足零能量条件（null energy condition）的经典物质得到局部第二定律；均匀共形表面引力（conformal surface gravity）确立第零定律。

Conclusion: 因果菱形内部可存在自洽的视界定律结构；零温仅在 $\alpha\to\infty$ 极限下趋近，此时有限因果视界消失。推导在 $(2+1)$ 维显式给出，物理结果对 $d\geq3$ 的球对称因果菱形保持相同形式。

Abstract: We develop a unified thermodynamic and mechanical framework for causal-diamond horizons in Minkowski spacetime. Euclidean regularity fixes the temperature $T_D=1/(πα)$, where $2α$ is the temporal extent of the diamond. The regulated Gibbons--Hawking--York contribution gives $I_D^E=-A_0/(4G)$, with $A_0$ the area of the horizon bifurcation surface, and the vanishing equilibrium geometric energy then yields the entropy--area relation $S_D=A_0/(4G)$. For weak matter perturbations crossing a localized portion of the future horizon, a Raychaudhuri analysis relates the associated Killing-energy flux to the area response, yielding a local physical-process first law at leading order near the bifurcation surface and, for classical matter satisfying the null energy condition, a local second law. Uniform conformal surface gravity establishes the zeroth law, while zero temperature is approached only in the limit $α\to\infty$, in which the finite causal horizon disappears. These results show that a coherent horizon-law structure can arise within a causal diamond, a fundamental building block of the causal structure of Minkowski spacetime. The derivation is explicit in $(2+1)$ dimensions, while the physical results retain the same form for spherical causal diamonds in $d\geq3$.

</details>


### [136] [Spontaneous Hawking Radiation from Dynamical Black-Hole Formation in Quantum Spin Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07794)
*Shunichiro Kinoshita,Keiju Murata,Daisuke Yamamoto,Ryosuke Yoshii*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作证明动力学黑洞形成过程中自发产生的霍金辐射（Hawking radiation）可在局域量子自旋模型（local quantum spin model）中实现，并通过局域自旋可观测量定量复现连续场论预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将弯曲时空量子场论（quantum field theory in curved spacetime）中的粒子创生与实验可及的多体可观测量相连接，为量子模拟提供微观路径。

Method: 以对应早期真空的基态为初态（无预置激发），沿演化几何驱动的动力学展开；在连续场论中通过入模与出模分解之间的非平凡Bogoliubov混合（Bogoliubov mixing）描述霍金粒子创生，并推导重整化局域能量密度（renormalized local energy density）。

Result: 所识别的局域自旋可观测量其期望值定量复现连续场论预言，包括对表面引力（surface gravity）的依赖；该一致性维持于有限时间窗口，窗口时长随系统尺寸对数增长，揭示从普适连续霍金物理到格点截断动力学的过渡。

Conclusion: 结果确立了弯曲时空量子场论量子模拟的微观途径，将场论粒子创生直接关联至实验可及的多体可观测量。

Abstract: We show that Hawking radiation spontaneously generated by dynamical black-hole formation can be realized in a local quantum spin model. Starting from the ground state corresponding to the early-time vacuum, with no initially prepared excitations, we follow the dynamics induced by the evolving geometry. In the corresponding continuum quantum field theory, Hawking particle creation is associated with nontrivial Bogoliubov mixing between the in- and out-mode decompositions. We derive the resulting renormalized local energy density in the continuum quantum field theory, which corresponds to an energy-weighted integral of the Hawking spectrum, and identify its counterpart as a local spin observable whose expectation value quantitatively reproduces the continuum prediction, including its dependence on the surface gravity. The agreement persists over a finite time window whose duration grows logarithmically with system size, revealing the crossover from universal continuum Hawking physics to lattice-cutoff dynamics. These results establish a microscopic route to quantum simulation of quantum field theory in curved spacetime, connecting field-theoretic particle creation directly to experimentally accessible many-body observables.

</details>


### [137] [Reheating surface volume renormalization and eternal inflation under broken scale invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07807)
*Min-Seok Seo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究标度不变性（scale invariance）微破缺如何修正“无止境暴胀”（no-eternal inflation）判据；两种独立方法一致表明，只要 $\varepsilon=n_s-1<0$（符合观测），再加热面体积的分形结构在宏观尺度失效，从而放松无止境暴胀条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cotler、Ivo 与 Maldacena 曾指出，在标度不变性下，永恒暴胀（eternal inflation）可被解释为再加热面体积重整化（renormalization）因标度维度（scaling dimension）定义不良而失败；而观测显示标度不变性存在轻微破缺，以谱指数偏离 1 的 $\varepsilon=n_s-1$ 表征，故需考察该破缺对无止境暴胀条件的影响。

Method: 采用两种独立途径：其一，考察正规序再加热面体积算符（normal ordered reheating surface volume operator）在曲率扰动（curvature perturbation）平移下的行为；其二，分析粗粒化（coarse-graining）结构以获取标度维度。

Result: 两种途径共同显示，只要 $\varepsilon$ 为负（与观测一致），再加热面体积的分形（fractal）结构在宏观尺度上便发生破缺，从而对无止境暴胀条件起到缓和（moderating）作用。

Conclusion: 标度不变性的微破缺（$\varepsilon<0$）会削弱无止境暴胀条件，说明将永恒暴胀理解为再加热面体积重整化失败的图像需纳入谱指数偏离 1 的修正。

Abstract: Recently, Cotler, Ivo, and Maldacena have shown that under scale invariance, eternal inflation is interpreted as a failure of the reheating surface volume renormalization due to an ill-defined scaling dimension. Meanwhile, observations suggest that scale invariance is slightly broken, which is parametrized by deviation of the spectral index from unity, $\varepsilon=n_s-1$. Motivated by this, we study how the scale invariance breaking effect modifies the no-eternal inflation condition as the renormalizability of the reheating surface volume. For this purpose, we adopt two independent approaches: investigating the behavior of the normal ordered reheating surface volume operator under a shift of the curvature perturbation, and analyzing the coarse-graining structure to obtain the scaling dimension. Both approaches commonly reveal that as long as $\varepsilon$ is negative, which is consistent with observations, the fractal structure of the reheating surface volume breaks down at macroscopic scale, moderating the no-eternal inflation condition.

</details>


### [138] [On connection between associators and 6j-symbols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08044)
*Elena Lanina,Artem Novokhatnii,Alexey Sleptsov,Radomir Stepanov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文提出一个将 Knizhnik–Zamolodchikov 方程得到的有理化 Drinfeld associators 与 $\mathcal{U}_q(\mathfrak{g})$ 的量子 $6j$-symbols 相联系的新公式 $U_q = U \cdot \Phi^{KZ}_{\mathbb{Q}}$，并在多个李代数与表示情形下进行了证明或扰动验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确阐述研究动机，仅指出所研究的问题是关联有理化 Drinfeld associators 与量子 $6j$-symbols 的关系。因此动机信息在摘要中未给出。

Method: 提出新公式 $U_q = U \cdot \Phi^{KZ}_{\mathbb{Q}}$，将 KZ 方程得到的有理化 Drinfeld associators 与量子群 $\mathcal{U}_q(\mathfrak{g})$ 的量子 $6j$-symbols 相关联，并通过对具体李代数与表示的计算进行验证。

Result: 在 $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ 李代数、基本表示与第一对称表示张量立方情形下给出了证明；在 $\mathfrak{sl}_N$ 的 $[2]\otimes[1]\otimes[1]$、$[2]\otimes[2]\otimes[1]$ 以及带重数的非平凡情形 $[2,1]^{\otimes 3}$、$[2,2]^{\otimes 3}$ 做了扰动检验；并在 $\mathfrak{so}_5$ 的基本表示张量立方情形进行了验证。

Conclusion: 该公式在若干具体算例中得到支持，包括一个严格证明与多个扰动检验，涵盖具重数（multiplicities）的非平凡（non-trivial）情形。

Abstract: We propose a new formula relating the rationalized Drinfeld associators obtained from the Knizhnik--Zamolodchikov equation to the quantum $6j$-symbols of the algebra $\mathcal{U}_q(\mathfrak{g})$: $ U_{q} = U \cdot Φ^{KZ}_{\mathbb{Q}} $. We prove this relation in the case of the $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ Lie algebra for the tensor cube of the fundamental and the first symmetric representation. We also check it perturbatively for the $\mathfrak{sl}_N$ algebra for representations $[2]\otimes [1] \otimes [1]$, $[2]\otimes [2] \otimes [1]$, the non-trivial cases with multiplicities --- $[2,1]^{\otimes 3}$, $[2,2]^{\otimes 3}$, and in the case of the $\mathfrak{so}_5$ Lie algebra for the tensor cube of the fundamental representation.

</details>


### [139] [Conformal Anomalies and Generalised Energy Conservation in De Sitter Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08113)
*Zong-Zhe Du*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文表明：在偶数维扩展庞加莱时空（extended Poincaré spacetime）中，对于对偶算符具有整数共形维度的体离散系列场（bulk discrete series fields），德西特（dS）散射振幅的广义能量守恒（Generalised Energy Conservation, GEC）条件恰好等价于树级接触相互作用（tree-level contact interactions）层面不存在边界共形反常（boundary conformal anomalies，含 beta 函数贡献）。相比之下，AdS/CFT 的全息一致性仅要求共形反常的局域性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清德西特散射中 GEC 条件与全息一致性（holographic consistency）之间的关系，特别是二者对共形反常约束的强弱差异。该问题涉及 dS 与 AdS/CFT 两种框架下一致性要求是否等价或孰更严格。

Method: 方法上同时实施 GEC bootstrap 与全息 bootstrap，考察涉及三个相同共形耦合标量（conformally coupled scalars）与一个离散系列标量（discrete series scalar）的四点相互作用。并将全息分析结果与共形场论关联函数（conformal field theory correlators）所预期的重整化条件（renormalisation conditions）进行比对。

Result: 结果发现了一类新颖且唯一的四点相互作用，其形式由上述两个 bootstrap 共同确定。GEC 的解构成全息一致解的测度为零子集（measure-zero subset）；全息分析与共形场论关联函数的预期重整化条件精确一致。此外，某一特定四点构型的约束可能有助于检验尺度分离的非超对称 AdS₄ 真空（scale-separated non-supersymmetric AdS₄ vacua）的全息一致性。

Conclusion: 结论为：在整数共形维度情形下，树级接触相互作用层面的 GEC 条件严格对应于边界共形反常（含 beta 函数贡献）的消失，而 AdS/CFT 全息一致性仅施加反常局域性这一较弱要求，故 GEC 约束显著强于全息一致性要求。该四点相互作用结果亦可为特定 AdS₄ 真空的全息一致性检验提供判据。

Abstract: We show that for bulk discrete series fields whose dual operators have integer conformal dimensions, the Generalised Energy Conservation (GEC) condition for de Sitter (dS) scattering amplitudes defined in even-dimensional extended Poincaré spacetime corresponds precisely to the absence of boundary conformal anomalies, including beta-function contributions, at the level of tree-level contact interactions. In contrast, holographic consistency in AdS/CFT enforces only the locality of the conformal anomaly.
  We find novel, unique four-point interactions involving three identical conformally coupled scalars and one discrete series scalar by performing both GEC and holographic bootstraps. This demonstrates that the solutions to GEC form a measure-zero subset of the holographically consistent solutions. Our holographic analysis agrees precisely with the expected renormalisation conditions for conformal field theory correlators. In addition, the constraint for a particular four-point configuration may be relevant for testing holographic consistency of scale-separated non-supersymmetric AdS$_4$ vacua.

</details>


### [140] [What a knot sees at a root of unity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08242)
*Ya. Kononov,A. Morozov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: HOMFLY-PT 多项式在 \(q^2\) 为单位根时呈现与纽结无关的普适因子化结构，该因子由杨图的 \(m\)-core 与每个 \(m\)-ribbon 贡献的同一因子 \(H_{[m]}\) 组成，从而解释了长期存在的因子化谜题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HOMFLY-PT 多项式描述 Chern-Simons 理论中 Wilson 平均对两个独立复参数 \(q\)、\(A\) 的解析延拓，并依赖纽结与规范群表示；此前存在一个未解释的因子化谜题（factorization puzzle），本文旨在揭示其背后的隐藏结构。

Method: 当 \(q^2\) 为本原 \(m\)-次单位根（primitive \(m\)-th root of unity）时，作者将相关奇异性定位于投影算子（projector），其残余表现为辫子 \(X_m\)（线缆内的 \(m\)-次幂和），可在其他股线间自由移动；证明借助 Murnaghan-Nakayama 规则与 Adams 运算（Adams operation）。

Result: \(H_R\) 因子化为杨图 \(R\) 的 \(m\)-core 对应多项式与每个 \(m\)-ribbon 对应的同一因子 \(H_{[m]}\) 之积；在所计算的全部例子中，该因子恰为 \(A^m\) 处的特殊多项式。当 \(q=\pm1\) 时，因子化源于线缆化 HOMFLY 的奇异性，该奇异性对所有表示相同。

Conclusion: 该隐藏结构的发现为旧的因子化谜题提供了统一解释，表明因子化是单位根处 HOMFLY-PT 多项式的普适（与纽结无关）性质。

Abstract: HOMFLY-PT polynomials describe analytic continuation of Wilson averages in Chern-Simons theory to arbitrary complex values of two independent parameters $q$ and $A$. They also depend on the knot and representation of the gauge group. However, when $q^2$ is a primitive $m$-th root of unity, e.g. $q=\pm e^{iπ/m}$, these polynomials exhibit a universal (knot-independent) factorization: $H_R$ becomes the product of the polynomials for the $m$-core of the Young diagram $R$ and of one and the same factor $H_{[m]}$ for every $m$-ribbon of $R$. In all the examples which we computed this factor is just the special polynomial at $A^m$. At $q=\pm1$ factorization follows from the singularity of the cabled HOMFLY, which is the same for all representations. At $m>1$ the relevant singularity is in the projector, and what survives of it is a finite sum of braids $X_m$ -- the $m$-th power sum inside the cable, which can be freely moved through the other strands. For the proof we use the Murnaghan-Nakayama rule and the Adams operation. Discovery of this hidden structure explains the old factorization puzzle.

</details>


### [141] [Segre determinants and MHV gravity amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08252)
*Joris Koefler,Emanuele Ventura,Hongmiao Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种 Segre-Veronese 构造（Segre-Veronese construction），用多项式解刻画树图 MHV 引力振幅（tree-level MHV gravity amplitudes）分子所满足的插值条件，并证明当 n≥7 时可给出与 Hodges 分子线性无关的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画 Hodges 公式（Hodges' formula）所给出的树图 MHV 引力振幅分子的插值条件，并探索其多项式解的结构；此前 Mai 与 Zhang 已建立 n=7,8 时存在独立解的结果。

Method: 采用 Segre-Veronese 构造（Segre-Veronese construction），以 Segre 行列式（Segre determinants）生成满足插值条件的多项式解。

Result: 五点和六点的 Hodges 分子（Hodges numerators）可由 Segre 行列式得到；对于每个 n≥7，适当的 Segre 行列式给出与 Hodges 分子线性无关的解。

Conclusion: Segre-Veronese 构造为 Hodges 分子的插值条件提供统一的多项式解框架，并将 n=7,8 的既有独立解结果推广至所有 n≥7。

Abstract: We describe a Segre-Veronese construction of polynomial solutions to the interpolation conditions of the numerators of tree-level MHV gravity amplitudes given by Hodges' formula. The five- and six-point Hodges numerators arise from Segre determinants, while for every $n\geq7$ suitable Segre determinants lead to solutions linearly independent of the Hodges numerator. The existence of such independent elements for $n=7,8$ was recently established by Mai and Zhang.

</details>


### [142] [Relative-Entropy Flow Across the Page Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08397)
*Minghui Yu,Xian-Hui Ge*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于复制技巧（replica trick）构建量子相对熵（quantum relative entropy）计算框架，用于分析Jackiw-Teitelboim黑洞的蒸发过程，揭示Page转变可被重新表述为竞争性半经典描述之间相对熵成本的流动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在为粗粒化（coarse-grained）半经典描述所固有的信息论成本提供定量刻画，并寻求对Page转变（Page transition）的信息论诊断方式。

Method: 方法上采用复制技巧构建量子相对熵的计算框架，并计算精确辐射态（exact radiation state）与Hawking鞍点态（Hawking saddle state）及复制虫洞鞍点态（replica wormholes saddle state）之间的相对熵。

Result: 结果表明：Hawking鞍点的相对熵随蒸发单调增加，反映维持该描述的热力学成本递增；复制虫洞鞍点的相对熵则下降，说明连通几何（connected geometries）描述逐渐更为精确；两者之差的符号恰在Page时间（Page time）发生反转。

Conclusion: 研究将Page转变重述为竞争性半经典描述之间相对熵成本的流动，揭示了粗粒化黑洞蒸发中有效不可逆性（effective irreversibility）涌现背后的信息论结构。

Abstract: We develop a computational framework for the quantum relative entropy based on the replica trick and apply it to analyze the evaporation process method of Jackiw-Teitelboim black holes. We show that the relative entropies between the exact radiation state and the Hawking saddle state and the replica wormholes saddle state quantify information-theoretic costs inherent to coarse-grained semiclassical descriptions. During evaporation, the relative entropy for the Hawking saddle increases monotonically, reflecting a progressively higher thermodynamic cost of sustaining the Hawking description. Conversely, the relative entropy associated with the replica wormholes saddle decreases, which indicates that the description of connected geometries becomes progressively more accurate. Moreover, the sign of the difference between the two quantities reverses precisely at the Page time, providing an information-theoretic diagnosis for the transition between competing gravitational saddles. Our results recast the Page transition as a flow of relative entropy costs between competing semi-classical descriptions, revealing an information-theoretic structure underlying the emergence of effective irreversibility in coarse-grained black hole evaporation.

</details>


### [143] [QCD_2: 't Hooft equation vs. relativistic Schroedinger equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08673)
*Andrei Smilga*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文比较二维QCD（2-dimensional QCD）中介子谱质量的两种计算方法，在半经典近似下结果一致，但WKB前渐近修正存在差异，且该差异随激发态数n增大迅速衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在检验用相对论哈密顿量薛定谔方程求解介子质量这一简化方法，与精确的积分Bethe-Salpeter方程（Bethe-Salpeter equation）结果之间的等价性范围，特别是超出领头阶的修正行为。

Method: 在't Hooft极限（N→∞, g²→0, g²N=const）下考察含质量为m的基本夸克的二维QCD；分别通过求解积分Bethe-Salpeter方程，以及求解相对论哈密顿量 H=2√(p̂²+m²)+σ|x|（弦张力σ=g²N/4）的薛定谔方程来获取介子态质量。

Result: 两种方法在领头半经典近似（semiclassical approximation）下给出相同结果；但WKB前渐近修正（preasymptotic WKB corrections）在两种方法中不同，对基态（ground state）有重要作用。当n=3时，两者预测的相对差异不超过2.5%，并随n增大迅速减小。

Conclusion: 简化薛定谔方程方法在高激发态下与Bethe-Salpeter方程结果高度接近，但低激发态（尤其基态）需谨慎对待，因两者的前渐近修正存在显著差别。

Abstract: We discuss 2-dimensional $QCD$ with a fundamental quark of mass $m$ in the 't Hooft limit ($N \to \infty, \, g^2 \to 0, \, g^2N =$ const). The spectrum of this theory includes a chain of meson states. The masses of these states can be evaluated by solving the proper integral Bethe-Salpeter equation. An alternative simple procedure consists in solving the Schrödinger equation for the relativistic Hamiltonian $$ \hat H\ =\ 2\sqrt{\hat p^2 + m^2} + σ|x| $$ with the string tension $σ= g^2N/4$. We compare the results obtained by these two methods. In the leading semiclassical approximation, they coincide.
  We show, however, that the preasymptotic WKB corrections to the masses calculated in these two ways are different. For the ground state, they play an essential role. But already for $n=3$, the relative difference between the two predictions does not exceed 2.5\% and rapidly falls off as $n$ increases.

</details>


### [144] [Projected Yang-Mills Theory and the MacDowell-Mansouri Analogy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08566)
*Pedro D. Alvarez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究由正交复结构定义的退化曲率动能算子的 $SO(2n)$ 规范理论，区分固定投影、无约束伴随 Higgs 场与严格约束于 $SO(2n)/U(n)$ 轨道的 Higgs 场，并在约束情形下证明存在局部等价于 $SU(n)$ Yang--Mills 理论的正则弱曲率分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退化曲率动能算子（degenerate curvature kinetic operator）对应的动能像为 $\su(n)$，而伴随真空的稳定子（stabilizer）为 $U(n)$，这一结构差异使得 Higgs 场的约束方式成为关键问题，需要判定哪些场空间能给出自洽的物理自由度计数。

Method: 作者区分三类场设定：固定投影算子（fixed projectors）、无约束伴随 Higgs 场（unrestricted adjoint Higgs fields）与严格约束于轨道 $SO(2n)/U(n)$ 的 Higgs 场；对带正 Higgs 动能系数的约束理论，分析陪集联络（coset connection）与中心联络（central connection），并进行非线性 Dirac 分析（nonlinear Dirac analysis）。此外用统一的 Clifford 对合（Clifford involution）表达 Stelle--West Higgs--引力作用量，并引入投影 Pontryagin 理论（projected Pontryagin theories）与满秩等变完备化（full-rank equivariant completion）。

Result: 在约束理论中，陪集联络为辅助场，中心联络为零；非线性 Dirac 分析给出正则弱曲率分支（regular weak-curvature branch），四维下含 $2(n^2-1)$ 个物理自由度，局部等价于 $SU(n)$ Yang--Mills 理论。无约束理论中的正规 Higgs 位移（normal Higgs displacements）会改变动能秩（kinetic rank），故该计数不适用于该场空间。文中亦刻画了可能的代数分支（algebraic branches）。

Conclusion: 仅当 Higgs 场被精确约束于 $SO(2n)/U(n)$ 轨道且 Higgs 动能系数为正时，退化动能理论才具备与 $SU(n)$ Yang--Mills 理论局部等价的正则弱曲率分支；无约束场空间因动能秩可变而不具备同一自由度计数。Clifford 对合表述与投影 Pontryagin 理论、满秩等变完备化为进一步构造提供框架。

Abstract: We study $SO(2n)$ gauge theories with a degenerate curvature kinetic operator defined by an orthogonal complex structure. The kinetic image is $\su(n)$, while the adjoint vacuum has stabilizer $U(n)$. We distinguish fixed projectors, unrestricted adjoint Higgs fields, and Higgs fields constrained exactly to the orbit $SO(2n)/U(n)$. For the constrained theory with a positive Higgs kinetic coefficient, the coset connection is auxiliary and the central connection is null. A nonlinear Dirac analysis establishes a regular weak-curvature branch with $2(n^2-1)$ physical degrees of freedom in four dimensions, locally equivalent to $SU(n)$ Yang--Mills theory. Normal Higgs displacements in the unrestricted theory can change the kinetic rank, so this count does not extend to that field space. We also characterize possible algebraic branches. We express the Stelle--West Higgs--gravity action through a common Clifford involution, and introduce projected Pontryagin theories and a full-rank equivariant completion.

</details>


### [145] [Connected Geometries in Gravitational Path Integrals, and Normalized No-Boundary Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08596)
*Jean-Luc Lehners*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 将路径积分（path integral）限制为对连通且允许的几何（connected, allowable geometries）求和，可显著缓解引力波函数归一化（normalization of gravitational wave functions）相关的疑难。该方案在经典跃迁（classical transitions）中给出合理结果，并提供非平凡的无边界概率（no-boundary probabilities）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波函数的归一化存在若干令人费解的特征，构成该研究的核心问题。作者试图通过修改路径积分的求和范围来缓解这一困难。

Method: 把路径积分限制为仅对连通且允许的几何（connected, allowable geometries）求和，并考察边界条件（boundary conditions）与积分围道（integration contours）对相关问题的影响。

Result: 该规定（prescription）应用于经典跃迁（classical transitions）时给出合理结果，同时提供非平凡的无边界概率（no-boundary probabilities），与早先的预期一致。

Conclusion: 限制为连通、允许几何的路径积分方案可实质缓解归一化疑难，且边界条件与积分围道对这些问题具有额外影响。

Abstract: We argue that some of the puzzling features associated with the normalization of gravitational wave functions can be substantially alleviated by restricting the path integral to a sum over connected, allowable geometries. This prescription gives sensible results when applied to classical transitions and it provides non-trivial no-boundary probabilities, in line with old expectations. We highlight the additional impact of both boundary conditions and integration contours on these issues.

</details>

### [146] [Electrical Networks and Symplectic Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07539)
*David Kogan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文将圆形平面电网（circular planar electrical network）的加权丛林计数（weighted grove counts）与辛群（symplectic group）的不变量理论相联系，构造 $\mathrm{Sp}(2n)$-不变张量 $Z_G$，并在 $\mathrm{Sp}(2)$ 与 $\mathrm{Sp}(4)$ 情形下给出基展开与系数解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 Kenyon--Wilson 丛林公式刻画平面电网的节点划分计数，但缺乏与不变量理论（invariant theory）的系统联系；本文旨在以辛群不变张量为框架统一理解加权丛林计数。

Method: 对给定平面电网 $G$ 关联一个 $\mathrm{Sp}(2n)$-不变张量 $Z_G$；在 $\mathrm{Sp}(2)=\mathrm{SL}(2)$ 情形于 Temperley--Lieb 基（非交叉匹配索引）中展开，在 $\mathrm{Sp}(4)$ 情形给出双丛林叠加的约化规则（reduction rules），并证明由 3-非交叉匹配索引的张量构成不变量空间的基。

Result: $\mathrm{Sp}(2)$ 情形下 $Z_G$ 的系数与 Kenyon--Wilson 丛林公式相关；$\mathrm{Sp}(4)$ 情形下证明 3-非交叉匹配索引张量构成 $\mathrm{Sp}(4)$-不变张量空间的基，且 $Z_G$ 在该基下的系数（至归一化）等于约化双丛林（reduced double groves）的加权计数。

Conclusion: 加权丛林计数可由辛群不变张量的基展开系数自然表达，为非交叉匹配与 3-非交叉匹配提供统一的不变量理论解释，并为更高阶 $\mathrm{Sp}(2n)$ 情形提供可推广的框架思路。

Abstract: Consider a finite planar graph with positive real edge weights and designated boundary vertices, called nodes. Such a graph is called a circular planar electrical network. A grove is a spanning forest in which every component contains at least one node. The connected components of a grove determine a partition of the nodes. We relate weighted grove counts to invariant theory for the symplectic group.
  To a planar electrical network $G$, we associate an $\mathrm{Sp}(2n)$-invariant tensor $Z_G$. For $\mathrm{Sp}(2)=\mathrm{SL}(2)$, we expand $Z_G$ in the Temperley--Lieb basis indexed by noncrossing matchings and relate its coefficients to the Kenyon--Wilson grove formulas. For $\mathrm{Sp}(4)$, we give reduction rules for superpositions of two groves and prove that the tensors indexed by $3$-noncrossing matchings form a basis of the space of $\mathrm{Sp}(4)$-invariant tensors. The coefficients of $Z_G$ in this basis are weighted counts of reduced double groves, up to normalization.

</details>


### [147] [Positivity of the basic hypergeometric series and the MacMahon identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07549)
*Alexandr Garbali*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文研究满足特定约束的非负整数 $q$ 幂参数的基本超几何级数 ${}_{n+1}\\varphi_{n}$，经归一化后可表示为正多项式，并给出该多项式的三种计算形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基本超几何级数在归一化条件下可化为正多项式，但其多种组合解释与显式表达形式之间的关系值得进一步刻画，尤其与 MacMahon 恒等式及多重集排列统计量的联系。

Method: 对上下参数为非负整数 $q$ 幂且满足特定约束的 ${}_{n+1}\\varphi_{n}$ 进行归一化；推导该多项式的三种公式：MacMahon 恒等式的推广形式、以 inversion 统计量与 mstc 统计量推广为对象的生成函数、以及包含 $2n$ 个 $q$-二项式系数的有限和。

Result: 得到三个公式：其一将无穷二项级数等同于多重集排列的 des 与 maj 统计量的归一化生成函数，推广了 MacMahon 恒等式；其二为 inversion 统计量与 mstc 推广统计量的多重集排列生成函数；其三为含 $2n$ 个 $q$-二项式系数的有限和。

Conclusion: 满足参数约束的归一化基本超几何级数可统一为多重集排列统计量的生成函数，并可由有限 $q$-二项式系数之和显式表示，从而建立超几何级数与排列统计量之间的多重联系。

Abstract: The basic hypergeometric series ${}_{n+1}φ_{n}$ whose upper and lower parameters are non-negative integer powers of $q$ satisfying a certain constraint can be, after a normalisation, computed as a positive polynomial. We derive three formulas for this polynomial. The first formula is a generalisation of the MacMahon identity in which an infinite binomial series is identified with a normalised generating function of des and maj statistics of multiset permutations. The second formula is a generating function of multiset permutations for the inversion statistic and a generalisation of the statistic mstc. The third formula is a finite sum containing $2n$ $q$-binomial coefficients.

</details>

### [148] [$\mathcal{PT}$-symmetry as Effective Time Reversal Symmetry for Anderson Localization in a Collinear Antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06951)
*Kim-Khuong Huynh,Takuma Ogasawara,Motoi Kimata,Sofie Søby Leiszner,Nhu-Quynh Thi Phan,Michael Anthony Quintero,Frej Søren Rattenborg,Denis Arčon,Bo Brummerstedt Iversen,Katsumi Tanigaki*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该研究指出，在反铁磁体 BaMn$_2$Bi$_2$ 中，尽管长程磁序破坏了常规时间反演对称性，$\mathcal{PT}$ 对称性仍作为有效时间反演对称性主导 Anderson 局域化，其稳健性由交换相互作用与磁晶各向异性共同决定，而非哈密顿量的精确 $\mathcal{PT}$ 对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规时间反演对称性在反铁磁体的长程磁序下被破坏，但局域化行为的对称性起源仍待澄清。研究旨在揭示 $\mathcal{PT}$ 对称性在反铁磁体 Anderson 局域化中的有效时间反演对称性角色。

Method: 通过测量磁电导（magnetoconductance），并施加纵向与横向磁场，从实验上比较其对 $\mathcal{PT}$ 相关磁亚晶格（magnetic sublattices）及量子干涉（quantum interference）、跃迁振幅（hopping amplitudes）的影响。

Result: 磁电导遵循由轨道耦合电荷载流子与磁矢势（magnetic vector potential）破坏时间反演对称性所确立的规律；横向磁场通过使 $\mathcal{PT}$ 相关磁亚晶格倾斜而显著削弱量子干涉与跃迁振幅，而沿亚晶格取向的纵向弱扰动对局域化影响甚微。

Conclusion: $\mathcal{PT}$ 强制简并（$\mathcal{PT}$-enforced degeneracies）的稳健性由交换相互作用与磁晶相互作用支配，这与全哈密顿量精确 $\mathcal{PT}$ 对称性在任意有限场下即被破坏形成对比。

Abstract: We show that $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetry acts as the effective time-reversal symmetry governing Anderson localization in the antiferromagnet BaMn$_2$Bi$_2$, whereas the general time-reversal symmetry is broken by its long-range magnetic order. The magnetoconductance follows the laws established from breaking time-reversal symmetry throuhg the orbital coupling of charge carriers to the magnet vector potential, however, the degrees of symmetry breaking are qualitatively modulated by the magnetocrystalline anisotropy stabilizing $\mathcal{PT}$-related sublattices. The quantum interferences and hoping amplitudes governing the phenomena are drastically impaired by transverse magnetic fields, which readily cant $\mathcal{PT}$-related magnetic sublattices. Accordingly, when the $\mathcal{PT}$-related texture is weakly perturbed by longitudinal fields orienting along the sublattices, the localization is minutely affected. The robustness of the $\mathcal{PT}$-enforced degeneracies is thus governed by exchange and magnetocrystalline interactions, in contrast to the exact $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetry of the full Hamiltonian, which is broken by any finite field.

</details>
