# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [A numerical and efficient model for thermo-field electron emission calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07013)
*Salvador Barranco Cárceles,Andreas Kyritsakis,Anthony Ayari*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: GETELEC-3 是一个开源代码，用一维定态薛定谔方程（1D-TISE）的直接数值解替代 Fowler-Nordheim、Murphy-Good 及 JWKB 类解析式，用于热场电子发射（thermo-field electron emission）计算。Noumerov 算法最快且解的不确定度低于 1%，配合并行能量内核可将电流密度计算降至毫秒级；神经网络对半经典参考对数残差建模，精度优于 0.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子源在尺寸缩小、采用新材料或追求更高性能时，原有解析公式（Fowler-Nordheim、Murphy-Good 及其基于 JWKB 的扩展）常被用于其推导条件之外，导致适用性不足。因此需要直接求解一维定态薛定谔方程的数值方法，以在更广泛条件下获得可靠结果。

Method: 实现 GETELEC-3 开源代码，对 1D-TISE 采用多种求解方法并比较速度、鲁棒性与解的不确定度；采用 Noumerov 算法与可并行推进所有电子能量的优化内核。针对复杂势垒，训练神经网络拟合半经典参考（semiclassical reference）对数残差以加速计算。

Result: Noumerov 算法被证明最快，解的不确定度低于 1%；结合优化内核，电流密度计算成本降至数毫秒，与 JWKB 评估相当。神经网络方法在计算成本仅为一部分的情况下，重现电流密度的误差优于 0.4%。

Conclusion: 该代码面向金属与半导体，接受任意势与态密度（density of states），并提供用户友好界面与可执行文件，旨在使严谨的电子发射计算与数据分析更易于普及。

Abstract: As electron sources are reduced in size, fabricated in new materials, or pushed for performance, the analytical formulations for thermo-field electron emission, namely Fowler-Nordheim and Murphy-Good equations and their JWKB-based extensions, are increasingly applied out of the range of conditions for which they were derived. We present GETELEC-3, an open-source code that replaces them with a direct numerical solution of the one-dimensional time-independent Schrödinger equation (1D-TISE). Several methods to solve the 1D-TISE have been implemented to test for speed, robustness, and solution's uncertainty. The Noumerov algorithm is found to be quickest, with the solution uncertainty below 1%, and together with an optimised kernel that advances all electron energies in parallel, the cost of calculating the current density is reduced to a few milliseconds - comparable to the JWKB evaluation. To further boost the calculation speed, especially in complicated barriers, a neural network has been trained on the residual of the logarithm of the semiclassical reference and it reproduces the current density better than 0.4% at a fraction of the computational cost. The code is developed for metals and semiconductors, accepts arbitrary potentials and density of states, and comes with a user-friendly interface and compiled executable to democratise rigorous electron emission calculations and data analysis.

</details>


### [150] [Analytical results for the Shannon entropy of the critical transverse-field Ising chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08591)
*M. A. Rajabpour*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作直接在 n=1 处处理临界横场 Ising 链（critical transverse-field Ising chain）的局域基 Shannon 熵（local-basis Shannon entropy），通过将 Born 分布表示为独立长程 Bernoulli 边的奇次边界，分离出边缘项与条件圈空间项，并在给定假设下构造全阶解析层级，重建已知周期常数与 γ₁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域基 Shannon 熵含普适次领头信息，但临界横场 Ising 链的 Shannon 点在常规 Rényi 方法（Rényi approach）下是奇异的，无法直接取 n→1 极限。因此需要一种绕开该奇异性的直接 n=1 处理方式，以提取普适常数与边缘贡献。

Method: 将完整计算基 Born 分布表示为独立长程 Bernoulli 边的奇次边界；用 Shannon 链式法则（chain rule）把熵拆为显式独立边项与条件圈空间项。对周期链分离解析边缘反常，并按连通顶点支撑（linked vertex support）组织常数项；对区间情形用 ghost vertex 消去平方对数，并将条件圈项作全支撑分解为物理线与 ghost 连通扇区。

Result: 在所述有限部分匹配与森林假设下，每个非零连通系数由显式有限维积分表示，给出全阶解析层级；四点项以闭式给出，前几个连通扇区已几乎饱和既有周期常数，该常数亦由精确有限尺寸分布独立重建。区间情形边缘贡献为 γ_E = log2/4 − 1/16；首个完整增广连通系数以闭式求得，其线与 ghost 边界层反常精确抵消，并由精确有限尺寸概率独立重建得 γ₁ = 0.060020(3)。

Conclusion: 结果表明 Shannon 点的奇异性可通过 Bernoulli 边边界表示与链式法则分解加以规避，从而在 n=1 直接获得全阶解析层级与闭式系数。边缘常数与 γ₁ 的独立重建支持该层级框架的自洽性。

Abstract: Local-basis Shannon entropies of critical wave functions contain universal subleading information, but the Shannon point of the critical transverse-field Ising chain is singular in the conventional Rényi approach. We treat it directly at $n=1$ by representing the complete computational-basis Born distribution as the odd-degree boundary of independent long-range Bernoulli edges. The Shannon chain rule separates the entropy into explicit independent-edge and conditional cycle-space terms. For the periodic chain this isolates the analytic edge anomaly and organizes the remaining constant by linked vertex support. Conditional on the stated finite-part matching and forest assumptions, every nonvanishing linked coefficient is represented by an explicit finite-dimensional integral, giving an all-orders analytic hierarchy; the four-vertex term is evaluated in closed form, and the first few linked sectors already nearly saturate the established periodic constant, which is also reconstructed independently from the exact finite-size distribution. For an interval, the exterior reduces exactly to one ghost vertex and the explicit squared logarithms cancel, leaving the analytic edge contribution $γ_E=\log2/4-1/16$. The conditional cycle term has an exact all-support decomposition into physical-line and ghost-linked sectors; conditional on closure of the boundary forest subtraction, these sectors define an all-orders hierarchy of renormalized line and monomer--dimer boundary periods for the remaining logarithmic coefficient. The first complete augmented linked coefficient is evaluated in closed form, with an exact cancellation between its line and ghost boundary-layer anomalies; an independent reconstruction from exact finite-size probabilities recovers the established coefficient $γ_1=0.060020(3)$.

</details>


### [151] [Heterogeneous diffusion with recruitment and mortality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08022)
*Esther D. Gutiérrez,Alexander López,R. K. Singh,Sébastien Fumeron,Malte Henkel,Trifce Sandev*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究具有位置依赖扩散系数（position-dependent diffusion coefficient）的异质扩散过程（heterogeneous diffusion process），并引入相互独立的招募（recruitment）与死亡（mortality）过程。结果表明系统在长时间下趋于非平衡稳态（non-equilibrium stationary state），均方位移（mean squared displacement）饱和值不再依赖招募率，并可通过有效指数（effective exponent）编码底层输运机制；同时推导出首次通过时间（mean first-passage time），发现固定招募—死亡率之比下存在最优死亡率使搜索时间最小化，并将该"死亡率辅助搜索"（mortality-assisted search）效应映射至传染病传播情境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将已有的"随机重置（stochastic resetting）下的异质扩散"框架推广至更一般的粒子生成与消亡并存情境，从而刻画伴随粒子产生与湮灭的系统（如细菌生长、反应性流体中的示踪扩散、拥挤环境中的细胞群体动力学）。

Method: 在位置依赖扩散系数的基础上，引入相互独立的招募与死亡率（当两者相等时退化为随机重置下的异质扩散），通过解析推导长时间稳态、均方位移饱和行为及到达目标的首次通过时间，并结合数值评估进行验证；进一步将招募与死亡重新解释为感染（infection）与移除（removal）过程以应用于传染病传播。

Result: 长时间极限下系统达到非平衡稳态，均方位移饱和至与招募率无关的数值；该饱和极限通过对扩散系数施加的有效指数直接编码底层输运机制，并刻画了向稳态区域的非平凡转变。首次通过时间分析显示，在固定招募—死亡率之比时存在使搜索时间最小的最优死亡率，数值评估证实了该效应；在传染病应用中，适度的移除率反而可能加速（而非延迟）感染到达新的空间区域。

Conclusion: 该框架推广了随机重置下的异质扩散，揭示了死亡率辅助搜索与稳态饱和的机制性联系，并表明其在流行病学之外，对同时存在粒子产生与消亡的系统（如细菌生长、反应性流体中的示踪扩散、拥挤环境中的细胞群体动力学）具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: We study a heterogeneous diffusion process with a position-dependent diffusion coefficient in the presence of independent recruitment and mortality. This framework generalises heterogeneous diffusion under stochastic resetting, which is recovered as the special case of equal recruitment and mortality rates. At long times, the system approaches a non-equilibrium stationary state with a mean squared displacement that saturates to a value independent of the recruitment rate. Crucially, this saturation limit directly encodes the underlying transport regime via an effective exponent applied to the diffusion coefficient, and we characterise the non-trivial transition to this stationary regime. We further derive the mean first-passage time to a target and show that, for a fixed recruitment-to-mortality ratio, an optimal mortality rate minimises the search time. This "mortality-assisted search" effect is confirmed by numerical evaluation. Reinterpreting recruitment and mortality as infection and removal processes, we apply the model to epidemic spreading and show that a moderate removal rate can counter-intuitively accelerate, rather than delay, the arrival of infection at a new spatial region. Beyond epidemiology, our framework offers broad relevance to systems featuring concurrent particle creation and destruction, such as bacterial growth, tracer diffusion in reactive fluids, or cellular population dynamics in crowded environments.

</details>


### [152] [Algebraic Tensor Network Renormalization and Holographic duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08787)
*Chenqi Meng,Tian Lan,Zhengcheng Gu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作提出 R-TNR 框架，将融合范畴 R 的范畴约束嵌入局部张量空间，并在 Loop-TNR 下保持，从而在无需精细调节的初始张量下稳定流向无能隙不动点，并高精度恢复扇区分辨的共形场论数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涌现广义对称性（emergent generalized symmetries）与全息原理（holographic principle）对理解量子相变（quantum phase transitions）至关重要，而带广义对称性的张量网络重整化（tensor-network renormalization）已被提出作为重构共形场论（conformal field theory）的离散时空框架。然而，该框架仍缺少完整全息表述，且现有形式无法直接容纳一般具有模协变性（modular covariant）的扇区配分函数（sector partition functions）。

Method: 本文构建 R-TNR，将融合范畴 R 的范畴约束（categorical constraints）置入局部张量空间，并在 Loop-TNR 过程中保持这些约束。对合适的 R 选择，所构造的初始张量在 R-Loop-TNR 下流向稳定的无能隙不动点（gapless fixed points）。

Result: 在 Ising、三临界 Ising（tricritical Ising）与三态 Potts 共形场论实例中，广泛的初始参数范围均导向各实例中同一无能隙不动点。方法按任意子扇区（anyon sector）解析共形塔（conformal towers），并以极高精度给出标度维数（scaling dimensions）、共形自旋（conformal spins）及扇区分辨结构常数（sector-resolved structure constants）的幅度，精度优于以往方法。

Conclusion: 结果实现了基于稳定对称-拓扑序（stable symmetry-topological-order）的拓扑自举（topological bootstrap）：有限的范畴与几何输入，连同无需精细调节的初始张量，足以恢复红外共形场论及其扇区分辨共形数据。

Abstract: Emergent generalized symmetries and the holographic principle play very important roles in understanding quantum phase transitions. In recent years, tensor-network renormalization with generalized symmetry and the corresponding fixed-point tensor formulation have been proposed as a discrete spacetime framework for reformulating conformal field theory. Nevertheless, a complete holographic formulation within this framework is still lacking, and the current formulation does not directly accommodate sector partition functions that are modular covariant in general. In this work, we formulate $\mathcal{R}$-TNR, which builds the categorical constraints of a fusion category $\mathcal{R}$ into local tensor spaces and preserves these constraints under Loop-TNR. For suitable choices of $\mathcal{R}$, the initial tensors constructed here flow under $\mathcal{R}$-Loop-TNR to stable gapless fixed points. In the Ising, tricritical Ising, and three-state Potts conformal field theory examples, a broad range of initial parameters leads to the same gapless fixed point in each example. The method resolves conformal towers by anyon sector and yields scaling dimensions, conformal spins, and magnitudes of sector-resolved structure constants with extremely high accuracy compared to previous methods. These results realize a topological bootstrap based on stable symmetry-topological-order in which finite categorical and geometric input, together with an initial tensor that need not be fine-tuned, suffices to recover the infrared conformal field theory and its sector-resolved conformal data.

</details>


### [153] [Spatial density, first passage times, and entropy of run and tumble particles in a bistable potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08029)
*R. K. Singh,Erez Aghion*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究热浴中双稳态势（bistable potential）内“跑动-翻滚粒子（run and tumble particle, RTP）”的动力学，给出稳态位置分布及其在拉普拉斯空间（Laplace space）的含时精确解，并据此分析弛豫、首达时间与粗粒化（coarse-graining）性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTP是活性物质（active matter）中的基本模型，其在双稳态势中的弛豫与稳态性质如何刻画，以及能否用更简化的双态过程描述其动力学，尚需精确解层面的检验。研究亦关注粗粒化描述是否保留非平衡热力学信息（如熵产生（entropy production））。

Method: 在热浴环境下求解双稳态势中RTP的稳态位置分布精确形式，以及其在拉普拉斯空间中的含时形式；利用该结果分析空间概率密度的弛豫特性，并求解平均首达时间（mean first passage time）的精确形式与首达时间分布的长时行为。

Result: 空间概率密度从中心向外收敛到稳态，弛豫前沿（relaxation front）随时间以弹道式（ballistically）推进；该弹道行为出现在密度尾部，即粒子距原点距离大于刻画RTP稳态的长度尺度时。同时得到平均首达时间的精确形式及首达时间分布的长时行为。

Conclusion: 基于上述结果，可将该动力学粗粒化为二分马尔可夫过程（dichotomous Markov process），其为一大类双稳态过程的范式。粗粒化伴随信息损失：RTP在稳态下具有有限熵产生，而其粗粒化描述中熵产生恒为零。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of a run and tumble particle (RTP) in a bistable potential, in contact with a heat bath. We find the exact form of the steady state position distribution as well as its time-dependent form in Laplace space. We use this result to study relaxation properties of the spatial probability density and find that it converges to the steady state from the center outwards, with the relaxation front moving ballistically in time. Physically this ballistic behavior arises when observing relaxation properties at the tails of the density, where the distance of the particle from the origin is larger than the length-scale describing the steady state of the RTP. We also find the exact form of the mean first passage time, and the long-time behavior of the first passage time distribution. This allows us to coarse-grain the dynamics as a dichotomous Markov process, a paradigm for a wide class of bistable processes. Coarse-graining, however, comes at the cost of a loss of information: while the RTP possesses a finite entropy production even at steady state, it identically vanishes for its coarse-grained description.

</details>


### [154] [Closed analytical form of many-body free volume and thermodynamics of monodisperse hard disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08265)
*Victor M. Pergamenshchik,Taras Bryk,Andrij Trokhymchuk*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作给出了硬盘模型自由体积（free volume）的封闭精确几何表示：任意构型下自由体积可由至多五个排斥盘的相交面积（intersection areas of exclusion disks）解析表达，由此建立从粒子坐标到配分函数与熵的直接几何路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬盘模型（hard disk model）是排除体积物理、液体结构与二维有序的基础参考体系，但因其自由体积涉及高度非平凡的多体几何（many-body geometry），热力学量的解析表述长期困难。

Method: 将自由体积表示为至多五个排斥盘相交面积的解析函数；证明 N 盘配分函数（partition function）可分解为条件自由体积之积，并识别低密度流体（extensive cavities）与高密度流体（intensive private cells）两种极限渐近形式；再以代表性平衡构型计算几何量。

Result: 所得理论在几乎整个密度范围内（直至密堆积 close packing）重现已知硬盘状态方程（equation of state）；在中间密度揭示出混合流体区，其中局域笼状缺陷（caged defects）在液相—六角相共存（liquid hexatic coexistence）之前的密度区间提供额外构型熵；五盘相交面积被证明是六角有序的敏感局域标量度量，可补充取向序参量（orientational order parameter）。

Conclusion: 自由体积的精确多体几何表述为硬盘热力学提供了直接解析途径，并凸显多体关联（many-body correlations）在二维有序与状态方程描述中的核心作用。

Abstract: The hard disk model is a fundamental reference system for excluded volume physics, liquid structure, and 2D ordering. Yet, despite its apparent simplicity, an analytical formulation of its thermodynamics has remained difficult because the free volume available to disks centers has a highly nontrivial many-body geometry. Here we show that this geometry admits a closed and exact representation: for any given hard disk configuration, the free volume can be analytically expressed through intersection areas of up to five exclusion disks. This provides a direct geometrical route from particle coordinates to the configurational partition function and entropy. We prove that the N disk partition function factorizes into a product of conditional free volumes and identify its two limiting asymptotic forms: the low density fluid regime controlled by extensive cavities and the high density fluid regime controlled by intensive private cells. Using the geometric measures computed from representative equilibrium particle configurations, the resulting theory reproduces the established hard disk equation of state over almost the entire density range up to close packing. At intermediate densities, the analysis reveals a mixed fluid regime in which localized caged defects provide an additional configurational entropy within the density interval preceding liquid hexatic coexistence. Finally, the five disk intersection area is shown to be a sensitive local scalar measure of hexagonal ordering, complementing the orientational order parameter and emphasizing the central role of many-body correlations.

</details>


### [155] [Hamiltonian curl-forces in systems coupled to multiple thermal reservoirs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08423)
*Omer Chor,Aljaž Godec,Oren Raz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究通过正则变换（canonical transformation）将多热库系统映射为单热库中被确定性旋度力（curl-force）驱动的粒子，揭示多温度系统中电流的力学起源，并对N维欠阻尼Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程给出稳态分布与熵产生率的显式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个不同温度热库耦合的系统通常维持非平凡环流（circulation），其非平衡本质的力学机制缺乏直观解释。研究旨在通过等价映射为此类系统提供力学图像，并量化旋度力在驱动系统偏离热平衡中的作用。

Method: 采用正则变换将多热库系统映射为单热库中受确定性、速度无关、非保守旋度力驱动的粒子，该力可用含各向异性动能项的Hamiltonian描述；并针对可精确求解的N维欠阻尼Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程推导稳态分布与熵产生率。

Result: 尽管具有Hamiltonian结构，系统并不弛豫到对应的Boltzmann分布，而是真正处于非平衡态。映射为多温度系统中的电流提供了力学解释：环流方向可由确定性力场定性预测；对N维欠阻尼Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程得到了稳态分布与熵产生率的显式表达式，凸显力场（广义）旋度在驱动非平衡中的作用。此外，非保守力可用于使任意多热库系统精确达到平衡，并获得相应的平衡分布。

Conclusion: 多热库系统的非平衡环流可等价地由单热库环境中的非保守旋度力刻画，力场旋度是驱动系统偏离热平衡的关键量；该映射既提供力学直观，也带来精确求解与人工平衡化多热库系统的可能性。

Abstract: We investigate the statistical mechanics of a system coupled to multiple heat reservoirs at different temperatures, a setting which typically sustains nontrivial circulation. By applying a canonical transformation, we map the system onto that of a particle coupled to a single reservoir yet driven by a deterministic curl-force---a reversible, velocity-independent non-conservative force with non-vanishing curl---that can be described by a Hamiltonian with an anisotropic kinetic energy term. Despite the Hamiltonian structure, the system does not relax to the corresponding Boltzmann distribution, and is genuinely out of equilibrium. The mapping provides a mechanical interpretation of the currents in multi-temperature systems, as we demonstrate by means of two examples. In particular, the direction of circulation is qualitatively predictable from the deterministic force field. For the exactly solvable case of an $N$-dimensional underdamped Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process, we derive explicit expressions for the steady-state distribution and the entropy production rate, highlighting the role of the (generalized) curl of the force field in driving the system out of thermal equilibrium. Finally, we show how a non-conservative force can be used to exactly equilibrate any system coupled to multiple reservoirs, and obtain the corresponding equilibrium distribution.

</details>


### [156] [Replica Fragmentation and Glassy Dynamics in Parity Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08503)
*Han Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 将独立训练但共享数据与协议的 Transformer 视为“副本（replicas）”，通过真实一致性 m、自置信度 q_self 与副本间一致性 q_cross 刻画其在“局部畴壁（domain walls）重构二元串”任务上的学习相态，识别出记忆化（memorization）、退化（retreat）与恢复（recovery）三种有限尺寸机制，并给出副本碎裂与残差反相关的判别特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究同一数据与训练协议下独立训练的网络为何会实现不同函数，以及这种副本间分歧（confident disagreement）能否被用作有限尺寸下的“类玻璃（glass-like）”相变诊断量；同时希望区分持续记忆化与正在进行中的退化—恢复动力学。

Method: 以局部畴壁重构二元串为任务，训练多个独立 Transformer 副本；定义并测量真实对齐 m、自置信度 q_self、跨副本一致性 q_cross，以及自—跨差值 q_self − q_cross；在总体与位置依赖的真实对齐被扣除后，进一步分析副本对之间残差的相关性。

Result: 训练集较小时，副本对全部训练样本预测正确，但对未见输入仍高置信度地给出错误预测（记忆化）。训练集较大时，副本可先泛化而后退化：输出开始偏离真实而置信度仍高，“已学习/未学习”边界向更短字符串后退，随后多数副本又随边界推进而恢复。q_self − q_cross 可清晰区分记忆化、退化、恢复三种机制；残差相关结构亦不同：记忆化副本残差弱且均匀相关，退化阶段反相关副本对占比最大（一方优于其平均表现时，另一方往往更差）。

Conclusion: 上述有限尺寸观测可将持续记忆化与正在进行的退化—恢复动力学区分开来；热力学极限与长时间极限，以及向其他学习任务的推广，仍有待研究。

Abstract: We study how independently trained Transformer neural networks reconstruct a binary string from its local domain walls. Runs sharing the data and training protocol can realize different functions. We treat them as replicas and measure truth alignment $m$, prediction confidence $q_{\mathrm{self}}$, and cross-replica agreement $q_{\mathrm{cross}}$. Confident disagreement defines the finite-size replica fragmentation that we call glass-like. With small training sets, replicas predict all training examples correctly but remain confident in incorrect predictions for unseen inputs, a regime we call memorization. With larger sets, runs can generalize and then retreat. Retreat occurs when outputs start to deviate from truth while confidence remains high. The frontier between learned and unlearned outputs recedes toward shorter strings. Many later recover as the frontier advances again. The self--cross gap $q_{\mathrm{self}}-q_{\mathrm{cross}}$ clearly distinguishes the three learning regimes of memorization, retreat, and recovery. With overall and position-dependent truth alignment subtracted, their residual correlations also differ: memorizing replicas have weakly and uniformly correlated residuals, while retreat has the largest fraction of replica pairs whose residuals are anti-correlated. That is, on the inputs where one replica of such a pair does better than its average, the other tends to do worse. These finite-size observations distinguish persistent memorization from ongoing retreat--recovery dynamics. The thermodynamic and long-time limits, and extensions to other learning tasks, remain open.

</details>


### [157] [Fast free-energy estimation for complex systems with nonreversible simulated tempering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08598)
*Yoshihiko Nishikawa,Koji Hukushima*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种基于 Wang–Landau 型反馈的模拟回火（simulated tempering）自由能估计方法，通过引入温度空间中的非可逆动力学（nonreversible dynamics）与每步多次温度更新，显著加快自由能估计的收敛，并在三维 Edwards–Anderson 自旋玻璃（spin-glass）模型上验证其可扩展至 L≃64。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统的自由能估计通常收敛缓慢，尤其在大尺寸自旋玻璃等强阻挫系统中计算成本高。该研究旨在提升在动态过程中（on-the-fly）进行自由能估计的收敛效率与可扩展性。

Method: 采用带 Wang–Landau 型反馈的模拟回火算法进行自由能估计，并在温度空间中引入非可逆动力学（nonreversible dynamics）以及每步执行多次温度更新（multiple temperature updates per step）。随后将该算法应用于三维 Edwards–Anderson 自旋玻璃模型进行检验。

Result: 算法在三维 Edwards–Anderson 自旋玻璃模型上可将自由能精确估计到大系统尺寸 L≃64；利用估计所得自由能进行后续的平衡模拟回火（equilibrium simulated tempering）模拟，可在覆盖自旋玻璃转变温度的宽温度范围内快速采样 Boltzmann 分布。其收敛时间随系统尺寸的增长远慢于另一已知的动态自由能估计算法。

Conclusion: 在温度空间中引入非可逆动力学与多温度更新，可有效加速 Wang–Landau 型反馈模拟回火的自由能估计收敛，并使后续平衡采样在大尺寸自旋玻璃体系中保持高效率，具备优于已知同类算法的可扩展性。

Abstract: We discuss free-energy estimation for complex systems using simulated tempering with Wang--Landau-type feedback. We show that introducing nonreversible dynamics in temperature space and multiple temperature updates per step significantly accelerates the convergence of the free-energy estimate. We then apply the algorithm to the three-dimensional Edwards-Anderson spin-glass model. Our results show that the algorithm can precisely estimate the free energy up to a large system size $L \simeq 64$, and that subsequent equilibrium simulated tempering simulations with the estimated free energy can rapidly sample from the Boltzmann distributions over a wide temperature range that covers the spin-glass transition temperature. The convergence time of our algorithm grows much more slowly with system size than that of another known algorithm for on-the-fly free-energy estimation.

</details>

Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于 BESIII 采集的 27.12 亿 ψ(3686) 事例，首次以 6.1σ 显著性观测到电磁 Dalitz 衰变 ψ(3686) → μ⁺μ⁻η′，测得分支比为 (4.1±1.0±0.4)×10⁻⁷，并与矢量介子主导模型预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在测量 ψ(3686) 的电磁 Dalitz 衰变（electromagnetic Dalitz decay）ψ(3686) → μ⁺μ⁻η′，并借助该过程检验矢量介子主导模型（vector meson dominance model）及轻子味普适性（lepton flavor universality）。

Method: 利用 BEPCII 对称正负电子对撞机上 BESIII 探测器采集的 (2712.4±14.3)×10⁶ 个 ψ(3686) 事例，进行电磁 Dalitz 衰变 ψ(3686) → μ⁺μ⁻η′ 的搜寻与分支比测量。

Result: 以 6.1σ 统计显著性首次观测到该衰变，分支比为 (4.1±1.0_stat.±0.4_syst.)×10⁻⁷；其与辐射衰变 ψ(3686) → γη′ 分支比的比值为 (3.3±0.9)×10⁻³；结合此前 BESIII 测得的 ψ(3686) → e⁺e⁻η′ 分支比，μ 子与电子道之比为 0.22±0.07。

Conclusion: 两个比值均在 1σ 内与矢量介子主导模型的预言一致，未发现轻子味普适性的显著破坏。

Abstract: Utilizing $(2712.4 {\pm} 14.3)\times10^{6}~ψ(3686)$ events collected by the BESIII detector at the symmetric $e^+ e^-$ collider BEPCII, we report the first observation of the electromagnetic Dalitz decay $ψ(3686) \to μ^+ μ^-η^{\prime} $ with a statistical significance of 6.1$σ$. The branching fraction is determined to be $\mathcal{B}(ψ(3686) \to μ^+ μ^- η^{\prime})=(4.1 \pm 1.0_{\rm stat.} \pm 0.4_{\rm syst.})\times 10^{-7}$. The ratio to the branching fraction of the radiative decay $ψ(3686) \to γη^{\prime}$ is estimated to be $(3.3\pm0.9)\times10^{-3}$, which is consistent with the prediction of the vector meson dominance model within $1σ$. Furthermore, using the branching fraction of $ψ(3686) \to e^+ e^- η^{\prime}$ previously measured by the BESIII experiment, the ratio between the muon and the electron channels is evaluated to be $0.22\pm0.07$, which is consistent with the calculation of the vector meson dominance model within $1σ$, and no significant violation of lepton flavor universality is found.

</details>


### [159] [Search for rare charm decays at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07773)
*Xiaokang Li,for the BESIII Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII 实验利用最大的粲偶素（charmonium）与阈值开粲（threshold open-charm）数据样本搜寻稀有及破坏对称性的衰变，本文综述了粲偶素弱衰变、粲味改变中性流（flavour-changing neutral currents, FCNC）、带电轻子味破坏（charged-lepton flavour violation, CLFV）与玻色对称性破坏（Bose-symmetry violation）方面的近期搜寻，未发现显著信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 借助 BESIII 在粲偶素与阈值开粲能区最大的数据样本，检验标准模型（Standard Model）及其扩展所预言的稀有与对称性破坏过程，提升相关分支比与有效相互作用的实验约束。

Method: 基于 BESIII 采集的大统计量数据，对粲偶素弱衰变、粲介子与粲偶素稀有衰变、矢量与赝标量介子衰变中的带电轻子味破坏，以及玻色对称性破坏进行搜寻，并以分支比上限与有效相互作用约束形式给出结果。

Result: 弱衰变搜寻改进了若干分支比（branching fraction）上限；稀有粲介子与粲偶素衰变研究对味改变中性流进行了检验；带电轻子味破坏搜寻给出了矢量与赝标量介子衰变中的上限并约束了有效相互作用；所有搜寻均未发现显著信号。中性 K 介子（neutral-kaon）上限低于某一所提议定域性（locality）情形的预期，J/ψ 上限接近标准模型 CP 破坏所预言的能标。

Conclusion: BESIII 的系列稀有与对称性破坏衰变搜寻未观测到显著信号，但已在多个通道给出具有竞争力的上限，其中中性 K 介子与 J/ψ 的结果对特定新物理场景与标准模型 CP 破坏预期形成有意义的实验约束。

Abstract: The BESIII experiment searches for rare and symmetry-violating decays with the largest charmonium and threshold open-charm data samples. This contribution reviews recent searches for charmonium weak decays, charm flavour-changing neutral currents, charged-lepton flavour violation, and Bose-symmetry violation. The weak-decay searches have improved several branching-fraction limits, while studies of rare charm-meson and charmonium decays probe flavour-changing neutral currents. Searches for charged-lepton flavour violation set limits in vector and pseudoscalar meson decays and constrain effective interactions. No significant signals are found in any of these searches. The neutral-kaon limits fall below the expectations of a proposed locality scenario, and the $J/ψ$ limit approaches the scale predicted for Standard Model CP violation.

</details>


### [160] [HybridMC: a fast detailed simulation framework for optical calorimeter technologies at the LHCb ECAL Upgrade II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08054)
*Marco Pizzichemi,Liupan An,Federico Betti,Aleksandar Bordelius,Loris Martinazzoli,Philipp Roloff,Matteo Salomoni,Yanxi Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出 HybridMC，一种将簇射发展（shower development）与光学输运（optical transport）解耦的光学量能器（optical calorimeter）快速模拟框架，在保持全光线追踪（ray-tracing）级别光学细节的同时，将 CPU 时间降低两个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 LHCb ECAL Upgrade II 预期的亮度与粒度下，Geant4 标准光线追踪对闪烁光与切伦科夫（Cherenkov）光子的逐光子追踪使单次电磁簇射（electromagnetic shower）的 CPU 时间相比仅模拟能量沉积增加可达三个数量级，对探测器研发与大样本生产均不可承受。同时光学光子不能简单舍弃，因为升级所针对的几十皮秒（picosecond）量级时间分辨率由光子在光探测器上的到达时间分布决定。

Method: HybridMC 将簇射发展（shower development）与光学输运（optical transport）解耦：能量沉积由不含光学光子传播的标准 Geant4 模拟处理，光输运则由参数化的汇总蒙特卡洛（summary Monte Carlo）步骤再现，其概率密度函数（probability density function）来自一次性的全光线追踪光学标定（optical calibration）。

Result: 蒙特卡洛对蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo-to-Monte Carlo）验证显示，该方法与全光线追踪在升级版 LHCb ECAL 所有模块类型上统计一致，涵盖单光子到达时间至重建能量与时间分辨率。CPU 时间降低超过两个数量级，模块相关加速因子为 150 至 480。与 DESY II 和 CERN SPS 的测试束（test-beam）数据比较表明，框架重现了真实原型测得的能量与时间分辨率的动量依赖，仅余一个小的常数项偏差。

Conclusion: HybridMC 保留了基于生成模型的快闪模拟器（flash simulator）无法触及的微观光学细节，且其 CPU 成本与在标准计算基础设施上开展大规模生产相兼容。

Abstract: The detailed Monte Carlo simulation of optical calorimeters is dominated by the generation and tracking of scintillation and Cherenkov photons, which in standard Geant4 ray-tracing increases the CPU time per electromagnetic shower by up to three orders of magnitude compared to simulating only the energy deposition. At the luminosity and granularity foreseen for the LHCb ECAL Upgrade II, this cost becomes prohibitive for both detector R&D and large-scale sample production. The optical photons cannot simply be discarded, because the time resolution of a few tens of picoseconds targeted by the upgrade to mitigate pile-up is driven by their arrival-time distribution at the photo-detectors. This paper presents HybridMC, a fast and detailed simulation framework for optical calorimeters that decouples shower development from optical transport. Energy deposition is handled by a standard Geant4 simulation without optical photon propagation, while light transport is reproduced by a parametrised summary Monte Carlo step based on probability density functions extracted from a one-time full ray-tracing optical calibration. A Monte Carlo-to-Monte Carlo validation shows good statistical agreement with full ray-tracing for all module types of the upgraded LHCb ECAL, from individual photon arrival times up to the reconstructed energy and time resolutions. The CPU time is reduced by more than two orders of magnitude, with module-dependent acceleration factors from 150 to 480. A comparison to test-beam data from DESY II and the CERN SPS shows that the framework reproduces the momentum dependence of the measured energy and time resolutions of a real prototype, up to a small residual constant term. HybridMC retains the microscopic optical detail not accessible to flash simulators based on generative models, at a CPU cost compatible with large-scale production on standard computing infrastructure.

</details>


### [161] [Gain fluctuations in a uniform avalanche diode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08468)
*Werner Riegler*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该文推导了均匀雪崩二极管中由 m 对初始电子-空穴对所产生的总雪崩尺寸（total avalanche size）的显式概率分布，并给出可生成全部原始矩（raw moments）的算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅指出所处理的问题对象：任意位置产生的 m 对初始电子-空穴对在均匀雪崩二极管（uniform avalanche diode）中所引发的总雪崩尺寸，其概率分布此前缺乏显式表达。

Method: 采用解析推导的方式，针对均匀雪崩二极管中在任意位置生成的 m 对初始电子-空穴对（primary electron-hole pairs），求出总雪崩尺寸的显式概率分布；同时构造一个算子（operator），用于生成全部原始矩（raw moments）。

Result: 得到了总雪崩尺寸的显式概率分布，并得到一个可生成所有原始矩的算子；摘要未给出具体的分布形式或矩的表达式。

Conclusion: 研究在解析层面刻画了均匀雪崩二极管中雪崩尺寸的完整概率描述与矩生成结构；摘要未涉及实验验证或应用推论。

Abstract: We derive the explicit probability distribution of the total avalanche size produced by m primary electron-hole pairs created at an arbitrary position in a uniform avalanche diode. We also derive an operator that generates all raw moments.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Parameter degeneracy and information loss in inverse flavor mapping for high-energy astrophysical neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35132)
*Zi-Qiang Chen,Zhi-zhong Xing,Ye-Ling Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究高能天体物理中微子通量的源风味比与望远镜测量风味比之间的逆映射问题，分析$\det P=0$参数简并条件（含$\mu$-$\tau$反射对称情形），给出对$f_e$、$f_\mu$与$\eta_e$、$\eta_\mu$的约束，并用IceCube全天中微子数据（5 TeV–10 PeV）演示源风味分布的映射程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由望远镜测量的中微子风味比反推源风味比在天体物理中至关重要，但逆映射$\eta=P^{-1}f$存在不可避免的参数简并和信息损失，尤其在当前中微子振荡数据允许的$\det P=0$极限附近，因此需要研究该简并条件及其对反推的限制。

Method: 通过参数关联分析探讨$\det P=0$的条件（包括$\mu$-$\tau$反射对称情形），推导该条件下$f_e$、$f_\mu$相对$\eta_e$、$\eta_\mu$的约束关系，并基于IceCube全天中微子通量数据（5 TeV至10 PeV），在源具有共同风味组成的假设下进行风味分布映射示例分析。文中$P$由四个轻子风味混合参数构成。

Result: 给出了$\det P=0$（含$\mu$-$\tau$反射对称）对应的参数关联条件及其对$f_e$、$f_\mu$与$\eta_e$、$\eta_\mu$的约束形式，并在IceCube 5 TeV–10 PeV全天中微子通量数据下，展示在共同源风味组成假设下源风味分布可被映射的程度。

Conclusion: 逆风味映射$\eta=P^{-1}f$在$\det P=0$附近受参数简并与信息损失所限，本文刻画了该极限下的参数关联与约束条件，并借助IceCube数据说明源风味分布的可恢复程度受此简并情形制约。

Abstract: Given a high-energy astrophysical neutrino flux, its source flavor ratios $η= \{η^{}_e, η^{}_μ, η^{}_τ\}$ are correlated with the ones $f = \{f^{}_e, f^{}_μ, f^{}_τ\}$ measured at a neutrino telescope via $f = P η$, where the elements of $P$ consist of four lepton flavor mixing parameters. But realistic {\it inverse} flavor mapping $η= P^{-1} f$ encounters unavoidable parameter degeneracy and information loss, especially in or near the $\det P = 0$ limit allowed by current neutrino oscillation data. In this work we explore the parameter correlation condition for $\det P = 0$ including the $μ$-$τ$ reflection symmetry case, formulate the corresponding constraints on $f^{}_e$ and $f^{}_μ$ versus $η^{}_e$ and $η^{}_μ$, and illustrate to what extent the source flavor distribution can be mapped by using the recent IceCube all-sky neutrino flux data ranging from 5 TeV to 10 PeV under the assumption that the relevant sources have a common flavor composition.

</details>


### [163] [Magnus: neutrino oscillation probabilities for any Hermitian Hamiltonian, any number of flavors, and any matter profile](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07159)
*Mauricio Bustamante*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: Magnus 是一个开源 Python 代码，利用 Magnus 展开（Magnus expansion）高效、通用地计算变密度介质中的中微子振荡概率（neutrino oscillation probability），在高精度下速度快于现有公开代码，并使研究者无需自写求解器即可更换物理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子振荡测量需计算一种味产生、另一种味被探测的概率；真空与恒定密度下有精确公式，但地球、太阳、超新星等路径密度变化时没有。常规方法（等密度台阶近似、通用求解器积分演化方程）在高精度下均变慢，而分析需要在多能量、方向与参数取值下反复计算，且每个新实验或模型往往需重写并验证求解器。

Method: 采用 Magnus 展开（Magnus expansion），其误差随步长的高次幂下降，计算成本由密度变化快慢而非路径所含振荡次数决定。代码接受任意密度剖面、任意味数与任意厄米哈密顿量（Hermitian Hamiltonian），如非标准相互作用（non-standard interactions）、惰性中微子（sterile neutrinos）、洛伦兹不变性破坏（Lorentz-invariance violation）与赝狄拉克中微子（pseudo-Dirac neutrinos）；在存在更省算力途径时自动采用，例如密度缓慢变化（如太阳）时跟踪瞬时本征态（instantaneous eigenstates）并仅在共振处应用该展开。

Result: 除振荡概率外还返回相位平均（phase-averaged）概率，用于描述太阳与天体物理中微子。单次概率计算约需数毫秒，批量扫描（batched scans）每点成本降低一至两个数量级；在与公开代码的对比中，Magnus 在变化剖面的高精度情形下最快。

Conclusion: Magnus 将研究新实验或新模型的工作重心从编写求解器转移到指定其物理内容，从而缓解变密度中微子振荡计算在高精度与多参数需求下的效率瓶颈。

Abstract: Interpreting neutrino oscillation measurements requires computing the probability that a neutrino born with one flavor is detected with another. In vacuum and in matter of constant density, this probability has exact formulas. Where the density varies along the path, as in the Earth, the Sun, or a supernova, it does not. There, the usual approaches either replace the profile by constant-density steps, whose error falls only as the square of their width, or integrate the evolution equation with a general-purpose solver, which must shorten its steps to meet a tighter tolerance. Both become slow at high accuracy, yet an analysis needs the probability at many energies, directions, and parameter values. Each new experiment or model often requires writing and validating a new solver. We present Magnus, an open-source Python code that avoids these limitations. It uses the Magnus expansion, whose error falls as a high power of its step width and whose cost is set by how fast the density varies, not by how many oscillations the path holds. Magnus accepts any density profile, any number of flavors, and any Hermitian Hamiltonian, such as those of non-standard interactions, sterile neutrinos, Lorentz-invariance violation, and pseudo-Dirac neutrinos. Where a cheaper route exists, it takes it automatically. For example, where the density varies slowly, as in the Sun, it follows the instantaneous eigenstates and applies the expansion only at resonances. Besides oscillating probabilities, Magnus returns phase-averaged ones, which describe solar and astrophysical neutrinos. A typical probability takes a few milliseconds, and batched scans are one to two orders of magnitude cheaper per point. Among the public codes we compared, Magnus is the fastest at high accuracy on a varying profile. Magnus shifts the effort of studying a new experiment or model from writing a solver to specifying its physics.

</details>


### [164] [Compositeness and decay properties of the flavor partner of $D^{*}_{s0}(2317)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07171)
*J. Sánchez-Illana,R. Molina,Pan-Pan Shi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于底夸克（bottom）扇区中 $D_{s0}(2317)$ 的重味伴态 $B_{s0}(5700)$ 的实验发现，采用隐规范形式（hidden-gauge formalism）进行 $B^{(*)}\bar{K}^{(*)}$ 耦合道计算，预测该扇区存在五个新态，并评估 $B_{s0}$、$B_{s1}$ 的等旋破缺衰变宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验在底夸克扇区发现了 $D_{s0}(2317)$ 的重味伴侣态 $B_{s0}(5700)$，为检验该扇区中分子态（molecular state）图像及相关束缚态谱提供了新的实验依据。

Method: 在隐规范形式下执行 $B^{(*)}\bar{K}^{(*)}$ 耦合道计算；将 $B\bar{K}$ 相互作用产生的 $J^P=0^+$ 束缚态质量固定为观测值以标定参数，并据此预测其余态；通过等旋破缺衰变模式 $B_s^0\pi^0$ 与 $B_s^{*0}\pi^0$ 计算 $B_{s0}$ 与 $B_{s1}$ 的衰变宽度。

Result: 预测出五个 $J^P=\lbrace 0,1,2\rbrace^+$ 新态，其中三个为 $J^P=1^+$，质量分别为 $5740$、$6005$ 与 $6065$ MeV。由于这些态相对 $B^{(*)}\bar{K}$ 阈有较大束缚能，与裸态（bare component）耦合可使纯分子模型下约 $50$ keV 的衰变宽度减至 $10-20$ keV；考虑重夸克自旋破坏（heavy-quark-spin violating）贡献后，$B_{s1}$ 的衰变宽度小于 $B_{s0}$。

Conclusion: 该工作将 $B_{s0}(5700)$ 解释为由 $B\bar{K}$ 相互作用生成的束缚态，并预言该扇区存在多个新态；裸态耦合与重夸克自旋破坏效应显著改变衰变宽度，可作为区分分子态与其他内部结构的判据。

Abstract: In view of the recent experimental finding of the heavy quark flavor partner of the $D_{s0}(2317)$ in the bottom sector, the $B_{s0}(5700)$, we perform the $B^{(*)}\bar{K}^{(*)}$ coupled-channel calculation in the hidden-gauge formalism. By fixing the mass of the bound state generated by the $B\bar{K}$ interaction with $J^P=0^+$ to the observed state, we also predict five new states with $J^P=\lbrace 0,1,2\rbrace^+$ in this sector. In particular, three of them have $J^P=1^+$ and masses $5740$, $6005$ and $6065$ MeV, respectively. In addition, we evaluate the decay width of the $B_{s0}$ and $B_{s1}$ states through the isospin breaking decay mode $B_s^0π^0$ and $B_s^{*0}π^0$, respectively. We find that, because these states have large binding energies with respect to the $B^{(*)}\bar{K}$ threshold, the coupling to the bare component can reduce this decay width by more than half of the decay width, from $50$ keV in the purely molecular model to $10-20$ keV, when the $B_{s0}$ is coupled to the bare component. We also observe that, after considering heavy-quark-spin violating contributions, the decay width for the $B_{s1}$ becomes smaller than for the $B_{s0}$.

</details>


### [165] [Probing axion-like particles through associated gauge-boson recoil and polarization at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07312)
*Amandip De,Monoranjan Guchait,Arnab Roy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究在高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）上通过 $pp\to Va$（$a\to\gamma\gamma$）过程探测类轴子粒子（ALPs），利用动量依赖的 V-V-a 相互作用带来的反冲与极化特征提升对电弱 ALP 耦合的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类轴子粒子（axion-like particles, ALPs）出现在许多有充分动机的标准模型（Standard Model, SM）扩展中，可与电弱规范玻色子（$V = W^\pm/Z$）通过高维算符产生特征相互作用，因而值得在现有伴随产生搜索基础上进一步探索其探测潜力。

Method: 在 $\sqrt{s}=14~{\rm TeV}$、积分亮度 $\mathcal{L}=3~{\rm ab}^{-1}$ 的 HL-LHC 条件下，研究 $pp\to Va$、$a\to\gamma\gamma$ 过程，ALP 质量范围 $20~{\rm GeV}\leq m_a\leq 200~{\rm GeV}$；采用 cut-based 选择与 XGBoost 多变量分析，并利用动量依赖的 V-V-a 相互作用诱导的反冲与极化特征。

Result: 这些观测量在双光子质量峰之外能对 SM 背景提供稳健区分，提升对电弱 ALP 耦合的灵敏度至 $C_{WW} \gtrsim 5.6 \times 10^{-3}~\mathrm{TeV}^{-1}$ 与 $C_{BB} \gtrsim 2.8\times 10^{-2}~\mathrm{TeV}^{-1}$。

Conclusion: 该分析通过利用反冲与极化特征扩展了现有伴随产生搜索；此外，若在数据中观测到共振峰，相同观测量可帮助区分 ALP 类信号与同质量的希格斯类（Higgs-like）自旋为 0 态。

Abstract: Axion-like particles (ALPs) appear in many well-motivated extensions of the Standard Model (SM) and can exhibit characteristic interactions with electroweak gauge bosons ($V = W^\pm/ Z$) through higher-dimensional operators. We explore the production of ALPs ($a$) in association with $V$ at the HL-LHC, with $\sqrt{s}=14~{\rm TeV}$ and an integrated luminosity of $\mathcal{L}=3~{\rm ab}^{-1}$. We study the process $pp\to Va$, with $a\toγγ$, for ALP masses in the range $20~{\rm GeV}\leq m_a\leq 200~{\rm GeV}$. This analysis extends existing associated-production searches by exploiting characteristic recoil and polarization features induced by the momentum-dependent $\rm {V-V-a}$ interaction. Using both cut-based selections and an XGBoost multivariate analysis, we demonstrate that these observables provide robust discrimination against SM backgrounds beyond the diphoton mass peak, improving the sensitivity to electroweak ALP couplings to $C_{WW} \gtrsim 5.6 \times 10^{-3}~\mathrm{TeV}^{-1}$ and $C_{BB} \gtrsim 2.8\times 10^{-2}~\mathrm{TeV}^{-1}$. We further show that, if a resonance peak is observed in data, the same observables can help distinguish an ALP-like signal from a Higgs-like spin--0 state of the same mass.

</details>


### [166] [Born-Oppenheimer Effective Field Theory as a unified framework for all the XYZ exotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07400)
*Tommaso Scirpa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文讨论基于量子色动力学（quantum chromodynamics, QCD）导出的玻恩-奥本海默有效场论（Born-Oppenheimer effective field theory, BOEFT）在含两个重夸克强子系统中的应用，旨在通过耦合薛定谔方程统一描述普通与奇异夸克偶素动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于揭示难以捉摸的 XYZ 奇异强子（XYZ exotic hadrons）模式，并借助尺度分离（scale separation）与对称性原理，建立从 QCD 直接导出的统一理论框架。

Method: 采用 BOEFT 形式，对含两个重夸克的强子系统建立耦合薛定谔方程（coupled Schrödinger equations），其势能可在格点（lattice）上计算；具体应用于 χ_{c1}(3872)、T_{cc}(3875)^+ 及其底夸克对应态。

Result: BOEFT 提供了对普通与奇异夸克偶素（包括混杂态 hybrids、四夸克态 tetraquarks、五夸克态 pentaquarks 等）动力学的统一描述；文中还评估了阈值效应（threshold effects）对夸克偶素谱的影响，并讨论了 χ_{c1}(3872) 的包容性产生截面（inclusive production cross section）。

Conclusion: BOEFT 形式可作为研究含双重重夸克强子系统的统一工具，有助于理解 XYZ 奇异强子谱及其产生机制。

Abstract: We discuss some recent applications of the Born-Oppenheimer effective field theory (BOEFT) formalism, directly derived from QCD and grounded in scale separation and symmetries, to hadronic systems containing two heavy quarks. This formalism provides a unified description of both ordinary and exotic quarkonium states (i.e., hybrids, tetraquarks, pentaquarks, ...) dynamics via coupled Schrödinger equations, whose potentials are computable on the lattice. The aim is to unveil the elusive pattern of the XYZ exotic hadrons. Here, we apply the BOEFT formalism to the $χ_{c1}(3872)$, $T_{cc}(3875)^+$, and their bottom counterparts. Moreover, we assess the threshold effects on the quarkonium spectrum and discuss the inclusive production cross section for $χ_{c1}(3872)$.

</details>


### [167] [Neutral Pseudoscalar Mesons, Hadron Light-by-Light Scattering, and the Muon Anomalous Magnetic Moment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07493)
*Cong-Cong Ye,Yu-Chen Hu,Minghui Ding,Craig D. Roberts*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文采用连续 Schwinger 函数方法（Continuum Schwinger function methods, CSM）估算赝标量介子极点对缪子反常磁矩（muon anomalous magnetic moment, $a_\mu$）的贡献，总量为 $85.1(0.8)\times 10^{-11}$，并指出该贡献的精度仍优于 5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为精确检验标准模型（Standard Model）中的 $a_\mu$，需厘清各类强子贡献，其中赝标量介子极点项（pseudoscalar meson pole contributions）是重要组成部分。现有其他当代估算存在差异，因此需要一个内部自洽的理论框架来重新评估该贡献。

Method: 使用领头阶 CSM 截断（leading order CSM truncation），并以非阿贝尔反常效应（non-Abelian anomaly effects）的参数化进行增补，从而对所有必需的赝标量—双光子跃迁形状因子（pseudoscalar–two-photon transition form factors）作内部一致的处理；该框架已通过对其他介子与核子性质的算例得到基准验证。

Result: 在 $10^{-11}$ 单位下得到：$a_\mu^{\pi^0}+a_\mu^{\eta}+a_\mu^{\eta^\prime}=84.3(0.8)$，低于某些其他当代估算；$a_\mu^{\eta_c}=0.82(0.04)$；$a_\mu^{\eta_b}$ 可忽略。总贡献为 $\sum_{\mathsf P} a_\mu^{\mathsf P}=85.1(0.8)/10^{11}$。

Conclusion: 分析表明，该极点贡献对 $a_\mu$ 的影响目前尚不能被确定到优于 5% 的精度，提示该方面的理论不确定性仍需进一步降低。

Abstract: Continuum Schwinger function methods\linebreak (CSM) are used to provide estimates of pseudoscalar meson pole contributions - $\mathsf P = π^0$, $η$, $η^\prime$, $η_c$, $η_b$ - to the muon anomalous magnetic moment. The analysis framework is benchmarked via successful applications to an array of other meson and nucleon properties. It uses the leading order CSM truncation, augmented by a parametrisation of non-Abelian anomaly effects, to deliver an internally consistent treatment of all the pseudoscalar--two-photon transition form factors required to complete such a calculation. Employing those form factor predictions, the following results are obtained (units $10^{-11}$): $a_μ^{π^0} + a_μ^η + a_μ^{η^\prime} = 84.3(0.8)$ - a value lower than some other contemporary estimates; $a_μ^{η_c} = 0.82(0.04)$; and a value of $a_μ^{η_b}$ that is negligible. The total contribution is $\sum _{\mathsf P} a_μ^{\mathsf P} = 85.1(0.8) / 10^{11}$. Our analysis suggests that this pole contribution to $a_μ$ is still not known with precision better than 5\%.

</details>


### [168] [Medium modification of pseudoscalar meson structure: Distribution amplitudes and parton distribution functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08360)
*Muhammad Ridwan,Parada Tobel Paraduan Hutauruk,Ahmad Jafar Arifi,Kazuo Tsushima*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究在光前夸克模型（light-front quark model, LFQM）框架下，结合夸克-介子耦合（quark-meson coupling, QMC）模型给出的介质内夸克性质，系统考察了核物质中赝标量介子的介质内性质与内部结构。结果显示介质效应对介子分布振幅与部分子分布有显著修正，且与EMC效应定性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将介质内夸克性质与介子内部结构在夸克自由度层面统一描述，以探究核物质环境对赝标量介子结构的修正。

Method: 方法上，采用高斯（Gaussian）与幂律（power-law, PL）两种波函数的光前夸克模型（LFQM），并把夸克-介子耦合（QMC）模型得到的介质内夸克性质引入其中，计算π±、K±、D±、B±介子的分布振幅（distribution amplitudes, DAs）与部分子分布函数（parton distribution functions, PDFs）。

Result: 结果表明，演化后的介质内π介子DA与PDF在中间x区域被强烈压低，显示介质对介子结构有实质性修正；修正后的Gegenbauer系数a_n′、参数ξ以及DA与PDF的Mellin矩的符号和量级与其他理论预言一致。π介子DA的第二Gegenbauer系数a_2^π随核密度显著增大，高斯（PL）波函数下从真空的0.096（0.057）增至正常核密度处的0.232（0.202），表明介质中π介子DA变宽、第二Gegenbauer矩增强。π介子PDF比值R_{ρ/0}^π的x依赖行为与观测到的欧洲μ子协作组（European Muon Collaboration, EMC）效应定性相似，凸显QCD演化在塑造介质修正部分子结构中的重要作用。自由空间中π±与K±的DA与现有格点QCD（lattice QCD）结果比较，符合程度合理。

Conclusion: 结论为：在LFQM中引入QMC介质内夸克性质可统一描述介质效应对介子结构的影响，介质显著改变π介子等赝标量介子的DA与PDF，且与EMC效应及既有理论、格点结果具有一致性。

Abstract: In this work, we present a comprehensive investigation of the in-medium properties and internal structure of pseudoscalar mesons within the light-front quark model (LFQM), employing both Gaussian and power-law (PL) wave functions. We incorporate in-medium quark properties obtained from the quark-meson coupling (QMC) model into the LFQM, thereby providing a unified description in terms of quark degrees of freedom. We investigate the distribution amplitudes (DAs) and parton distribution functions (PDFs) of $π^{\pm}$, $K^{\pm}$, $D^{\pm}$, and $B^{\pm}$ mesons in symmetric nuclear matter. We find that the evolved in-medium pion DA and PDF are strongly suppressed at intermediate $x$, indicating substantial medium-induced modifications to the meson structure. The signs and magnitudes of the modified Gegenbauer coefficients $a_n'$, the parameter $ξ$, and the Mellin moments of the DAs and PDFs in nuclear matter are found to be consistent with other theoretical predictions. In particular, the second Gegenbauer coefficient of the pion DA, $a_2^π$, increases substantially with nuclear density, from $0.096$ ($0.057$) in vacuum to $0.232$ ($0.202$) at normal nuclear density for the Gaussian (PL) wave function, respectively. This increase indicates a broader pion DA and an enhanced second Gegenbauer moment in the nuclear medium. We further calculate the pion PDF ratio $R_{ρ/0}^π$ and find that its $x$ dependence exhibits a behavior qualitatively similar to the observed European Muon Collaboration (EMC) effect, highlighting the important role of QCD evolution in shaping the medium-modified partonic structure. Finally, we compare the calculated free-space $π^{\pm}$ and $K^{\pm}$ DAs with available free-space lattice QCD results and find reasonable agreement.

</details>


### [169] [Missing Mass Matters: Neutrinophilic Scalar in $D$-Meson Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08606)
*Jin-Man Cai,Gang Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了轻质量中微子亲合标量（neutrinophilic scalar）φ 在 D 介子衰变 D_(s)^+ → e^+ ν̄_α φ 中的发射，指出基于积分三体衰变率的先前估计过强，并给出考虑缺失质量平方（missing-mass squared, M_miss^2）分布后的修正约束与 STCF 预期灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子亲合标量主要与中微子耦合，可通过轻子介子衰变在 10 MeV ≲ m_φ ≲ O(100) MeV 质量区间进行探测。本文旨在利用 BESIII 与未来超级陶粲装置（Super Tau-Charm Facility, STCF）的 D 介子衰变，检验此类标量并改进已有约束。

Method: 针对 D_(s)^+ → e^+ ν̄_α φ 过程，在实验所选区域内计入依赖 m_φ 的缺失质量平方（M_miss^2）分布；并扩展 M_miss^2 覆盖范围以探测更重标量。同时讨论 D_(s)^+ → μ^+ ν̄_α φ 的直接搜寻，并推导完整的三体衰变宽度解析表达式。

Result: 考虑 M_miss^2 分布后，D^+ 的约束显著弱于基于积分三体衰变率的先前估计。在完整 M_miss^2 运动学范围与 1 ab^-1 积分亮度下，STCF 预期灵敏度在 D^+ 衰变中于 m_φ ≳ 380 MeV、在 D_s^+ 衰变中于 m_φ ≳ 310 MeV 处超过现有 kaon 界限。

Conclusion: 计入缺失质量分布对 D 介子衰变中中微子亲合标量的探测灵敏度至关重要，扩展 M_miss^2 覆盖可提升对较重标量的灵敏度；STCF 在高亮度下可在较高质量区间超越现有 kaon 约束，并可通过 μ 末态直接搜寻进一步探测该标量。

Abstract: A light neutrinophilic scalar $φ$, which couples predominantly to neutrinos, can be probed through leptonic meson decays for scalar masses $10~\mathrm{MeV}\lesssim m_φ\lesssim\mathcal{O}(100)~\mathrm{MeV}$. We study neutrinophilic-scalar emission in $D$-meson decays at BESIII and the future Super Tau-Charm Facility (STCF), focusing on $D_{(s)}^+\to e^+\barν_αφ$. Accounting for the $m_φ$-dependent missing-mass squared ($M_{\rm miss}^2$) distribution within the experimentally selected region, we find the resulting $D^+$ constraint to be substantially weaker than previous estimates based on the integrated three-body decay rate. Extending the $M_{\rm miss}^2$ coverage provides sensitivity to heavier scalars. With the full kinematic range of $M_{\rm miss}^2$ and an integrated luminosity of $1~\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$, the projected STCF sensitivities surpass the existing kaon bound for $m_φ\gtrsim 380~\mathrm{MeV}$ in the $D^+$ decay and $m_φ\gtrsim 310~\mathrm{MeV}$ in the $D_s^+$ decay. We further discuss direct searches for $D_{(s)}^+\toμ^+\barν_αφ$ and their potential for probing the neutrinophilic scalar. A complete analytic expression for the three-body decay width is also derived with full dependence on the scalar and charged-lepton masses, including its behavior in the $m_φ\to 0$ and $m_\ell\to 0$ limits.

</details>


### [170] [Monopole Catalysis from Standard Model Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07150)
*Nathaniel Craig,Dan Sehayek*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究标准模型味（flavor）相关的广义对称性（generalized symmetries）及其在磁单极子（magnetic monopole）存在时的表现，指出即使重子数与轻子数在经典层面守恒，电弱反常（electroweak anomaly）仍会在单极子-费米子散射中诱导 ΔB = ΔL ∈ 3ℤ 的跃变，并在 Pati-Salam 理论与三分化（trinification）模型中演示该机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解标准模型味对称性作为广义对称性在磁单极子背景下的命运，并揭示其可能带来的可观测唯象后果。

Method: 方法上，分析味相关的广义对称性结构与磁单极子存在下的反常效应，并在 Pati-Salam 理论（Pati-Salam theory）与三分化（trinification）模型中验证该机制。

Result: 结果显示，对于任何激活电弱反常的单极子，即便重子数与轻子数经典守恒，单极子-费米子散射仍会产生 ΔB = ΔL ∈ 3ℤ 的量子数变化，构成显著的唯象学后果。

Conclusion: 结论表明，广义味对称性与磁单极子的相互作用可经由电弱反常破坏经典守恒律，且该机制在 Pati-Salam 与三分化框架中均成立。

Abstract: We study the generalized symmetries associated with Standard Model flavor and their fate in the presence of magnetic monopoles. This illuminates a striking phenomenological consequence: even when baryon and lepton number are classically conserved, the electroweak anomaly induces $ΔB = ΔL \in 3 \mathbb{Z}$ in monopole-fermion scattering for any monopole activating the anomaly. We demonstrate this mechanism in Pati-Salam theory and trinification.

</details>


### [171] [Coherent versus incoherent amplification: from parametric resonance to the kinetic regime in stimulated axion decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07155)
*Clemente Smarra,Elisa Todarello*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出轴子（axion）自发衰变为双光子的过程在玻色子占据数较大的环境中会被强烈增强。通过引入轴子场的有限动量色散（momentum dispersion），作者证明光子不稳定性受动量模式间退相干（dephasing）调制，从而统一了经典场视角下的参量共振（parametric resonance）标度 $s\propto g_{a\gamma}$ 与动理学（kinetic）视角下的 $s\propto g_{a\gamma}^2$ 两种渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子自发衰变在宇宙学时间尺度上极慢，但在大玻色占据数环境下可被显著增强。既有描述存在明显分歧：经典场方法给出光子增长指数 $s\propto g_{a\gamma}$，而基于玻尔兹曼方程（Boltzmann equations）的标准动理学描述因涉及衰变矩阵元平方而给出 $s\propto g_{a\gamma}^2$。本文动机在于厘清这两种看似矛盾标度的来源。

Method: 作者从具有有限动量色散的轴子场出发，考察其各动量模式之间的退相干如何改变光子不稳定性，并推导出一个将退相干效应编码于核（kernel）中的有效演化方程。针对轴子速度服从连续麦克斯韦-玻尔兹曼分布（Maxwell-Boltzmann distribution）的情形，得到光子增长指数的隐式表达式。

Result: 所得隐式表达式可重现两种渐近标度，并对过渡区（crossover）给出近似描述。当放大过程发生在短于轴子相干长度（coherence length）的尺度上时，恢复参量共振标度 $s\propto g_{a\gamma}$；在相反极限下则呈现有效动理学行为 $s\propto g_{a\gamma}^2$。

Conclusion: 两种标度差异源于轴子场的相干性质。过渡区的具体形式依赖轴子动量分布，但作者预期关于 $g_{a\gamma}$ 的渐近标度对具体分布假设基本不敏感。

Abstract: Although the spontaneous decay of axions into two photons is extremely slow on cosmological timescales, it can be strongly enhanced in environments with large bosonic occupation numbers. From a classical field perspective, stimulated axion decay is described by modified Maxwell equations. For a coherent axion background, these equations exhibit a parametric resonance characterized by a photon growth exponent $s\propto g_{aγ}$. By contrast, a standard kinetic description, for instance based on Boltzmann equations, involves the squared matrix element of the decay process and therefore would naturally predict a characteristic rate scaling as $s\propto g_{aγ}^2$. In this work, we show that these apparently different behaviors originate from the coherence properties of the axion field. Starting from an axion field with a finite momentum dispersion, we show how dephasing among its momentum modes modifies the photon instability and derive an effective evolution equation in which this effect is encoded in a kernel. For a continuous Maxwell-Boltzmann distribution of axion velocities, we obtain an implicit expression for the photon growth exponent that reproduces the two asymptotic regimes and provides an approximate description of the crossover. When amplification develops on scales shorter than the axion coherence length, the parametric resonance scaling $s\propto g_{aγ}$ is recovered, whereas in the opposite limit an effectively kinetic behavior with $s\propto g_{aγ}^2$ emerges. Although the detailed form of the crossover depends on the axion momentum distribution, we expect the asymptotic scaling with $g_{aγ}$ to be largely insensitive to the specific distribution assumed.

</details>


### [172] [Safety of dynamical SUSY breaking and gaugino condensation in models of spontaneous CP violation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08002)
*Norimi Yokozaki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文分析超对称（SUSY）框架下利用自发CP破坏（SCPV）解决强CP问题所需的条件：超势常数项与SUSY破缺F项须高精度为实。当二者由规范微子凝聚（gaugino condensation）和动力学SUSY破缺（DSB）动态产生时，其相位即使所有theta角为零、所有耦合为实也未必消失。文章逐一评估纯SYM、ADS、3-2、ISS与IYIT等模型，指出多数情形会经反常中介（anomaly mediation）或第二路径生成强CP相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在SUSY中通过SCPV解决强CP问题，要求超势常数项与SUSY破缺F项相位高精度为零。然而当两者由动力学机制产生时，非微扰效应可能引入无法由theta角或耦合实数性消除的复相位，因此需系统检验各动力学模型是否会破坏该要求。

Method: 在整体SUSY（global SUSY）框架下，分别考察纯SUSY Yang-Mills与Affleck-Dine-Seiberg推广中的规范微子凝聚、3-2模型、ISS模型与IYIT模型。分析多值非微扰超势的分支相位、F项相位如何经反常中介传递至胶子质量与theta-bar，以及场真空取向相对于常数项的排列。

Result: 规范微子凝聚仅在N_c - N_f ∈ {1,2}（纯SYM对应N_c = 2）时安全，否则常数项相位经反常中介到达胶子质量。3-2模型不引入相位。ISS模型在非简并夸克质量时安全，但在简并（或近简并）极限下复真空取向可产生O(1)相位；IYIT模型在约一半实汤川参数空间内介子VEV为纯虚、CP自发破缺，并可产生纯虚F项。三模型中与信使耦合的场真空均与常数项对齐，故复常数项经第二路径（区别于反常中介）生成强CP相位。

Conclusion: 在SUSY+SCPV解决强CP问题的方案中，动力学产生常数项与F项并不自动保证相位为零。规范微子凝聚、ISS简并极限与IYIT模型均可能生成不可忽略的相位，且与信使耦合场的真空对齐会提供反常中介之外的第二条强CP相位来源，因而对各模型的安全性需逐一审慎检验。

Abstract: Solving the strong CP problem with spontaneous CP violation (SCPV) in SUSY requires that the constant term of the superpotential and the SUSY-breaking F-term be real to high accuracy. When both are generated dynamically, by gaugino condensation and by dynamical SUSY breaking (DSB), their phases need not vanish even if every theta-angle vanishes and every coupling is real; e.g., the multivalued non-perturbative superpotential may sit on a branch with a complex phase. In fact, gaugino condensation, in pure SUSY Yang-Mills theory and in its Affleck-Dine-Seiberg generalization, is safe only for N_c - N_f in {1,2} (N_c = 2 for pure SYM); otherwise the phase of the constant term reaches the gluino mass through anomaly mediation. The SUSY-breaking F-term is the main source of the MSSM soft masses, including the gluino mass, so its phase reaches theta-bar as well. In the framework of global SUSY, the 3-2 model introduces no phase. The ISS model is safe for non-degenerate quark masses in that sector; in the degenerate (or nearly degenerate) limit, however, a complex vacuum orientation can generate an O(1) phase. The IYIT model can generate a purely imaginary F-term: for roughly half of the real Yukawa parameter space, the meson VEV is purely imaginary and CP is spontaneously broken. In all three, the vacuum of the field coupled to the messengers is aligned with the constant term, so a complex constant term generates a strong CP phase through a second route, separate from anomaly mediation.

</details>


### [173] [Axion Pyramid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08079)
*Chan-Gyung Park,Dahee Lee,Hyerim Noh,Jai-chan Hwang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文识别出由量子色动力学（QCD）相变诱导的轴子密度功率谱中显著的“金字塔形”增强，其出现在相变时的视界尺度附近，共动特征尺度与太阳系相当，相对冷暗物质（CDM）情形可放大约子密度扰动超过 $3.5 \times 10^{13}$ 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于揭示QCD相变对轴子密度扰动的显著放大效应，并探索该效应作为宇宙学观测手段对QCD轴子质量施加约束的可能性。

Method: 在丘顶初始条件（hilltop initial condition）下，通过考虑轴子与辐射（radiation）的耦合来触发该增强，并将初始条件与轴子质量相联系。

Result: 增强发生于相变时的视界尺度附近，共动特征长度尺度与太阳系尺度相当，轴子密度扰动相对CDM情形放大倍数超过 $3.5 \times 10^{13}$。

Conclusion: 由于初始条件与轴子质量相关，宇宙学中对“轴子金字塔（axion pyramid）”的观测有望用于约束QCD轴子质量。

Abstract: A pronounced pyramid-shaped enhancement in the axion density power spectrum induced by the QCD phase transition is identified. The enhancement occurs near the horizon scale at the transition, with a characteristic comoving length scale comparable to the size of the Solar System, and amplifies the axion density perturbation by factors exceeding $3.5 \times 10^{13}$ relative to the CDM case. Under the hilltop initial condition, coupling to radiation triggers this enhancement. The initial condition is related to the axion mass, suggesting that observation of the axion pyramid in cosmology may constrain the QCD axion mass.

</details>


### [174] [Gravitational Waves and Nambu-Goldstone Bosons from Infrared-Finite Cosmic Strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08254)
*Hajime Fukuda,Shuailiang Ge,Keisuke Harigaya*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文论证具有自发破缺整体对称性的红外有限宇宙弦（如 skyrmion lines 与 semi-local strings）其低频振荡的 Nambu-Goldstone 玻色子发射受强烈抑制，因而其引力波信号可能与局域弦（local strings）相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常只有局域弦被视为有前景的引力波源，而整体弦因长程 Nambu-Goldstone 玻色子（NGB）导致的张力红外对数发散及强烈辐射被排除。该文针对红外有限弦提出重新评估其引力波可观测性的必要性。

Method: 以理论分析为主，对比区分局域弦、具有对数红外发散张力的整体 U(1) 弦，以及 skyrmion lines 与 semi-local strings 三类弦结构在 NGB 剖面与低频振荡 NGB 发射方面的差异。

Result: yukubskyrmion lines 与 semi-local strings 不带有支撑长程 NGB 剖面所需的特征，其低频振荡的 NGB 发射被强烈抑制（摘要未给出定量细节）。

Conclusion: 红外有限宇宙弦的引力波信号可能与其对应的局域弦相可比，暗示这类整体对称性破缺模型中的弦亦可作为引力波源的候选。

Abstract: Skyrmion lines and semi-local strings are examples of infrared-finite cosmic strings that arise in theories with spontaneously broken global symmetries. Unlike global $U(1)$ strings, they do not support the long-range Nambu-Goldstone boson (NGB) profile responsible for a logarithmically infrared-divergent string tension. We argue that NGB emission from their low-frequency oscillations is strongly suppressed. Consequently, their gravitational-wave signals may be comparable to those from local strings.

</details>


<div id='math.CA'></div>

# math.CA [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Positive Bidiagonal Factorizations and Mixed-Type Chebyshev Multiple Orthogonal Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08568)
*Manuel Mañas*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文刻画了带状全正矩阵（banded totally positive matrix）何时具有正双对角分解（positive bidiagonal factorization）。有限维情形下该分解总存在；半无限情形下其存在性等价于由 Metelmann 因子决定的有限族正级数收敛，其倒数即为标量 Jacobi 连分数（Jacobi continued fraction）的极限值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限维带状全正矩阵的正双对角分解总存在，但半无限维情形缺乏存在性判据。本文旨在给出这一判据，并借此研究带状 Toeplitz 矩阵的谱与相关随机游走。

Method: 借助 Metelmann 因子构造有限族正级数并考察其收敛性，利用其倒数与标量 Jacobi 连分数极限值的联系；对带状 Toeplitz 矩阵，则通过符号多项式（polynomial symbol）的负根的有序划分构造混合型多重正交多项式（mixed-type multiple orthogonal polynomials），并应用已有的谱 Favard 定理（spectral Favard theorem）。

Result: 半无限带状全正矩阵存在正双对角分解当且仅当相关有限族正级数收敛。任意有界带状全正矩阵可在算子范数（operator norm）下由允许该分解的矩阵逼近，扰动限于固定的初始块。对带状 Toeplitz 矩阵，负根给出具有有界因子的全局分解；由负根的有序划分所得多项式的正测度矩阵支撑为单一区间，且测度关于 Lebesgue 测度绝对连续，具秩一的代数密度。

Conclusion: 上述公式进一步给出相应被杀死随机游走（killed random walk）的转移律与首达律（transition and first-return laws），将矩阵分解理论与正交多项式、随机游走谱分析相联系。

Abstract: We characterize when a banded totally positive matrix admits a positive bidiagonal factorization. In finite dimension such a factorization always exists. For semi-infinite matrices of arbitrary finite bandwidth, existence is equivalent to convergence of a finite family of positive series determined by the Metelmann factors. Their reciprocals are the limiting values of scalar Jacobi continued fractions. We also prove that every bounded banded totally positive matrix can be approximated in operator norm by matrices admitting positive bidiagonal factorizations, through perturbations confined to a fixed initial block. For banded Toeplitz matrices, the negative roots of the polynomial symbol give a global factorization with bounded factors. Ordered partitions of these roots determine explicit mixed-type multiple orthogonal polynomials. Applying the existing spectral Favard theorem, we compute their matrix of positive measures: its support is one interval, and the measure is purely absolutely continuous with an algebraic density of rank one. These formulas also give transition and first-return laws for the associated killed random walks.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.CO'></div>

# astro-ph.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Stochastic Bubble Completion in de Sitter Space: Power Spectrum, Bispectrum, and Infrared Moment Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07880)
*Hyukjung Kim,Alp Kagan Ozmen,Heeseung Zoe*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究一阶相变（first-order phase transition）中随机成核（stochastic nucleation）导致的局域完成时间涨落场，计算其在闵可夫斯基（Minkowski）与德西特（de Sitter）背景下的功率谱（power spectrum）与等边双谱（equilateral bispectrum），揭示红外（infrared）敏感性的层级结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机气泡成核使一阶相变的局域完成时间成为随机空间场，其在德西特膨胀背景下的红外行为可能与曲率扰动（curvature perturbations）和非高斯性（non-Gaussianity）相关。已有研究对有限相变时长的红外效应尚缺乏系统的功率谱与双谱刻画。

Method: 将成核历史视为随机过程，计算完成时间涨落场的功率谱与等边双谱；在永恒延展的德西特历史中分析古气泡尾（ancient-bubble tail）对空间矩的贡献，并通过数值计算验证，同时考察有限起始时间与成核历史的时间局域化（temporal localization）作为正则化手段。

Result: 在永恒德西特背景中，两点函数（two-point function）的首个有限k修正于H/β=1/5处变为尾部敏感，主导矩在H/β=1/3处发散；三点函数对应阈值分别为H/β=1/8与1/6，表明双谱比功率谱对红外更敏感。有限起始时间下古气泡尾被截断，所有空间矩保持有限，红外谱恢复解析白噪声极限 P_τ∝k^3 与 B^eq_τ∝k^6；永恒背景阈值则控制矩系数的时长依赖及向中间尾部敏感区的过渡。

Conclusion: 研究建立了描述完成时间涨落的首达（first-arrival）框架，并阐明德西特膨胀可产生不同的功率谱与双谱红外层级，这些结果与曲率扰动及非高斯性相关。

Abstract: Stochastic bubble nucleation makes the local completion time of a first-order phase transition a random spatial field. We study its power spectrum and equilateral bispectrum in Minkowski and de Sitter backgrounds, with particular emphasis on the infrared consequences of a finite transition duration. In an eternally extended de Sitter history, rare bubbles nucleated in the remote past generate an ancient-bubble tail that produces a hierarchy of infrared-sensitive spatial moments. For the two-point function, the first finite-$k$ correction becomes tail sensitive at $H/β=1/5$, while the leading moment becomes singular at $H/β=1/3$. The corresponding three-point thresholds are $H/β=1/8$ and $1/6$, showing that the bispectrum is more infrared sensitive than the power spectrum. For any finite starting time, however, the ancient-bubble tail is cut off, all spatial moments remain finite, and the strict infrared spectra recover the analytic white-noise limits ${\cal P}_τ\propto k^3$ and ${\cal B}^{\rm eq}_τ\propto k^6$. The eternal-background thresholds instead control the duration dependence of the moment coefficients and the crossover into an intermediate tail-sensitive regime, in agreement with our numerical calculations. We further show that temporal localization of the nucleation history provides a smooth regulator of the ancient-bubble tail and restores the same analytic infrared limits. These results establish a general first-arrival framework for completion-time fluctuations and clarify how de Sitter expansion can generate distinct power-spectrum and bispectrum infrared hierarchies relevant to curvature perturbations and non-Gaussianity.

</details>


### [177] [Third-order scalar induced gravitational waves during a matter-dominated era: secular growth and implications for primordial black hole domination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08045)
*Jing-Zhi Zhou*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文在物质主导（matter-dominated, MD）时期研究三阶标量诱导引力波（scalar induced gravitational waves, SIGWs），给出四类源项的闭式核函数、五重积分功率谱，并据此对原初黑洞（primordial black holes, PBHs）丰度给出比二阶强约三个量级的限制，指出高阶 SIGWs 在足够长的 MD 时期会变为非微扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 SIGWs 研究多集中于辐射主导（radiation-dominated, RD）时期及二阶过程，而高阶效应在 MD 时期的增长行为及其对早期宇宙场景（如 PBH 主导时期）的影响尚不明确，故需系统考察 MD 时期的三阶 SIGWs。

Method: 将三阶源项分为四类（三个一阶标量的组合，以及一阶标量分别与二阶张量、矢量、标量扰动的乘积），解析求得全部四个核函数（kernel functions）的闭式表达式；对一般原初功率谱导出五重积分形式的三阶功率谱，并对单色（monochromatic）谱做数值计算。

Result: MD 时期的核函数呈长期增长（secular growth），使谱快速增长，Ω^(3)_GW ∝ x^6 而 Ω^(2)_GW ∝ x^2（x ≡ kη ≫ 1）；单色谱情形下所有来自二阶矢量源的贡献恒为零。应用于 PBH 蒸发前的早期 MD 时期，由不回反（backreaction）条件 ∫d ln k Ω^tot_GW < 1 得到 Ω_PBH,f ≲ 4.6×10^-7 (m_PBH/10^7 g)^-0.48，比仅计二阶的限制强约三个量级。

Conclusion: 高阶 SIGWs 在足够长的 MD 时期不可避免地进入非微扰（nonperturbative）区域，这对包含早期物质主导的宇宙学场景具有重要影响。

Abstract: We investigate scalar induced gravitational waves (SIGWs) at third-order during a matter-dominated (MD) era. We classify the sources into four parts---built from three first-order scalars, and from a first-order scalar times the second-order tensor, vector, and scalar perturbations---and obtain all four kernel functions analytically in closed form. Unlike their radiation-dominated counterparts, the MD kernel functions grow secularly, translating into a rapid growth of the spectrum, $Ω^{(3)}_{\mathrm{GW}}\propto x^{6}$ versus $Ω^{(2)}_{\mathrm{GW}}\propto x^{2}$ at $x\equiv kη\gg1$. We derive the third-order power spectrum as a five-fold integral for a general primordial spectrum, and evaluate it numerically for a monochromatic one, where all contributions from the second-order vector source vanish identically. As an application, we consider the early MD era driven by primordial black holes (PBHs) before their evaporation. Requiring that the induced gravitational waves do not backreact on the cosmological perturbations, $\int\mathrm{d}\ln k\, Ω^{\mathrm{tot}}_{\mathrm{GW}}<1$ then bounds the initial PBH abundance as $Ω_{\mathrm{PBH},f}\lesssim4.6\times10^{-7}\, (m_{\mathrm{PBH}}/10^{7}\,\mathrm{g})^{-0.48}$, about three orders of magnitude stronger than the second-order-only bound. Our results indicate that higher-order SIGWs inevitably become nonperturbative during a sufficiently long MD era, with important implications for scenarios involving an early matter domination.

</details>


### [178] [Probing beyond the Cosmic Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08083)
*Tarun Souradeep*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文讨论宇宙视界内观测到的均匀曲率区域可能仅为极小片段，而近期超新星（supernova, SN）结果显示视界尺度可与曲率半径相当甚至更大；非平凡的全局结构可能与局部曲率尺度同量级。宇宙微波背景（cosmic microwave background, CMB）各向异性可用于探测视界尺度及其略超出处的全局结构，闭合FRW宇宙（closed FRW universe，球面几何）中椭圆拓扑（Elliptical topology）在此参数空间下预期产生高CMB各向异性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机在于：视界内观测到的均匀曲率区域或许远小于不均匀性长度尺度与极其复杂流形的全局连通尺度，而近期SN结果提示视界尺度可能接近或超过曲率半径，非平凡全局结构也可能处于局部曲率尺度量级，因此有必要探讨略超出宇宙视界的探测意义。

Method: 方法上，论文指出CMB各向异性是对视界尺度及略超出视界尺度宇宙结构敏感的有效探针，可用来探测全局结构的非平凡特征或给出有意义的限制；并以闭合FRW宇宙（球面几何）中椭圆拓扑（Elliptical topology）为例加以说明。

Result: 结果显示，在SN观测当前所偏好的闭合FRW宇宙的修订参数空间（revised parameter space）的很大一部分中，椭圆拓扑会产生高CMB各向异性信号，从而例证了CMB对全局结构的探测能力。

Conclusion: 结论是，略超出视界的探测有望揭示潜伏于视界附近或恰好在其外的非平凡全局结构；CMB各向异性可作为此类探测的敏感手段，并在闭合FRW宇宙参数空间中对椭圆拓扑等非平凡结构给出检测或限制。

Abstract: There are reasons to believe that the region of uniform curvature that we observe within our horizon is perhaps a tiny patch, much smaller than the length scales of inhomogeneity and global connectivity of an extremely complicated manifold. However, the recent supernova (SN) results suggest that the horizon scale could be comparable to or even much larger than curvature radius. Non trivial global structure too tends to be of the order of the local curvature scale. Probing (slightly) beyond the cosmic horizon can potentially reveal nontrivial global structure lurking around or just beyond the horizon scale. The cosmic microwave background (CMB) anisotropy is a sensitive probe of the universe on length scales up to and somewhats beyond the horizon scale and is perhaps poised to detect or put interesting limits on nontrivial features the global structure. This point is exemplified by high CMB anisotropy signal of the Elliptical topology in a large fraction of the revised parameter space of the closed FRW universe (spherical geometry) currently preferred by SN observations.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Dispersion for the wave equation with Robin boundary conditions on a 2D convex model domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06878)
*Diego Sánchez Sanz*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文将二维 Friedlander 模型（Friedlander model）的色散（dispersion）研究从 Dirichlet 边界条件推广至 Neumann 与 Robin 边界条件，并得到与 Dirichlet 情形相同的色散估计（dispersive estimates）及 Strichartz 估计（Strichartz estimates）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作仅处理 Dirichlet 边界条件下的二维 Friedlander 模型，Neumann 与 Robin 边界条件尚未被覆盖，故需扩展研究范围。

Method: 引入 Airy 型特殊函数（Airy-type special function），构造与 Dirichlet 情形类似的拟基本解（parametrix），但需谨慎控制新增项；随后分析该拟基本解的色散性质。

Result: 所构造拟基本解的色散估计与 Dirichlet 情形的既有结果一致。

Conclusion: 因此，Neumann 与 Robin 边界条件下亦可获得与 Dirichlet 情形相同的 Strichartz 估计，从而完成对该模型边界条件处理的推广。

Abstract: This paper presents a study of dispersion for the two-dimensional Friedlander model with Neumann and Robin boundary conditions, thereby extending previous works treating only Dirichlet boundary conditions. By considering an Airy-type special function, we obtain a parametrix construction similar to the Dirichlet case but with additional terms requiring careful control. By studying the dispersion of this parametrix, we obtain the same dispersive estimates as in previous results for the Dirichlet case, and therefore the same Strichartz estimates.

</details>


### [180] [Quantitative Stability Of Aggregation-Diffusion Under Local Attraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07264)
*Yunhong Deng*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对具有紧支撑（compactly supported）数据的聚集-扩散能量（aggregation-diffusion energy），建立其定量稳定性（quantitative stability）。研究对象为局部吸引（local attraction）下在无穷远处渐近平坦的退化扩散（degenerate diffusion），如 Riesz 核（Riesz kernels）。工作刻画了稳态处 Wasserstein Hessian 的谱下界（spectrum lower-bound），并将假设保持在唯一性所需的最小程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确说明研究动机的外部背景，仅指出关注紧支撑数据下聚集-扩散能量的定量稳定性，以及局部吸引、无穷远处渐近平坦的退化扩散这类特定情形。假设范围被有意压至唯一性所需的最小条件，暗示其目标在于在尽量弱的条件下推广稳定性结论。

Method: 分析手段为刻画稳态处 Wasserstein Hessian 的谱下界；研究对象限定为具有紧支撑数据、局部吸引且在无穷远处渐近平坦的退化扩散（如 Riesz 核）。所采用的假设被保持在唯一性所需的最小水平。

Result: 摘要未报告具体数值或显式不等式形式的结论，仅说明建立了此类聚集-扩散能量在紧支撑数据下的定量稳定性，并给出了稳态处 Wasserstein Hessian 的谱下界刻画。

Conclusion: 在该渐近平坦退化扩散与局部吸引的设定下，聚集-扩散能量具备定量稳定性，且可通过 Wasserstein Hessian 的谱下界加以刻画；相关结论在接近唯一性所需的最小假设下成立。

Abstract: In this note, we establish quantitative stability of aggregation-diffusion energy for compactly supported data. Our consideration focuses on the degenerate diffusion with local attraction that are asymptotically flat at infinity, such as Riesz kernels. We characterize the spectrum lower-bound of the Wasserstein Hessian at steady states. Assumptions are kept as minimal as that needed for uniqueness.

</details>


### [181] [Seifert type magnetohydrostatic equilibria in rotationally symmetric toroidal domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08519)
*Daniel Peralta-Salas,Radu Slobodeanu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该研究在旋转对称环面区域中构造磁流体静力学平衡态，使磁力线构成任意互素整数 $(m,n)$ 的 Seifert 纤维化（Seifert fibration）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机；仅可推断其关注在旋转对称环面域内将磁力线拓扑结构与 Seifert 纤维化（Seifert fibration）相联系以构建磁流体静力学平衡态。

Method: 采用构造性方法，在旋转对称（rotationally symmetric）环面区域（toroidal domains）内建立磁流体静力学平衡（magnetohydrostatic equilibria），并要求磁力线（magnetic lines）定义 $(m,n)$ Seifert 纤维化，其中 $m,n$ 为任意互素整数（coprime integers）。

Result: 对任意互素整数对 $(m,n)$，均构造出满足条件的磁流体静力学平衡态，且其磁力线对应 $(m,n)$ Seifert 纤维化。

Conclusion: 磁力线的 Seifert 纤维化拓扑可在旋转对称环面域内与磁流体静力学平衡相容，且该构造对任意互素整数 $(m,n)$ 成立。

Abstract: We construct magnetohydrostatic equilibria in rotationally symmetric toroidal domains whose magnetic lines define an $(m,n)$ Seifert fibration for arbitrary coprime integers $m,n$.

</details>
