### [182] [Threat Evasion and Information Propagation in Cognitive Flocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07297)
*Priyanka Iyer,Cecilia Soroco,Gerhard Gompper*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究基于含显式捕食者的增强惯性自旋模型（augmented inertial spin model）的数值模拟，探讨认知智能体集群（flocks of cognitive agents）的信息传播与集体逃逸行为，揭示转向距离的标度律、碎片化（fragmentation）的产生与同步机制，以及面对双捕食者时集群与单粒子响应的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息共享、威胁感知与集体规避体现了群体智能（swarm intelligence）的优势。研究旨在理解集群如何在含真实群体相互作用与捕食者威胁下实现信息传播与集体逃逸，以提炼适用于生物集群与机器人群体（robotic swarms）的一般性原理。

Method: 采用增强惯性自旋模型（augmented inertial spin model）并进行数值模拟，模型中引入显式捕食者；集群面向捕食者一侧的个体感知威胁，并通过局部相互作用（local interactions）触发集体转向。相互作用包含对齐（alignment）之外的局部避碰（collision avoidance）与群体凝聚（swarm cohesion）。对双捕食者情形，结合稳定性分析（stability analysis）与模拟进行研究。

Result: 在欠阻尼（under-damped）区间，增强惯性自旋模型可维持近乎线性的信息传播。集群对静止捕食者的转向距离随 $Δ_p/(v_0\sqrtη)$ 标度，反映响应强度 $Δ_p$、集群速度 $v_0$ 与信息传播阻尼 $η$ 间的最优平衡。当集群不同部分转向方向不一致时会出现碎片化（fragmentation），而快速信息传播可同步这些响应并在被追逐时维持凝聚力。对双捕食者，稳定性分析与模拟共同表明集群反应与单粒子反应形成鲜明对比，散射角（scattering angle）由集群几何、捕食者位置与探测前缘的异质响应共同塑造。

Conclusion: 信息传播、集群几何与动力学共同塑造了集体逃逸行为，为生物集群与机器人群体提供了具有普适性的原理。

Abstract: Information sharing, threat perception, and collective evasion highlight the advantages of swarm intelligence. Here, we study the behavior of flocks of cognitive agents, based on numerical simulations of the augmented inertial spin model, with an explicit predator. Each agent on the predator-facing side of the flock perceives the threat, triggering collective turns through local interactions. We show that the augmented inertial spin model sustains nearly linear information propagation in the under-damped regime, even with realistic flocking interactions such as local collision avoidance and swarm cohesion in addition to alignment. The turning distance of the flock to a stationary predator scales with the ratio $Δ_p/(v_0\sqrtη)$, identifying an optimal balance between response strength $Δ_p$, flock speed $v_0$, and damping $η$ of information propagation. Fragmentation arises when different parts of the flock turn in different directions, however, fast information propagation can synchronize these responses and maintain flock cohesion while under pursuit. For two predators, a combination of stability analysis and simulations reveals a stark contrast between flock and single-particle reactions, with scattering angle shaped by flock geometry, predator position, and the heterogeneous responses of the detecting front. Together, these results show how information propagation, flock geometry, and dynamics together shapes collective escape, providing general principles relevant to both biological flocks and robotic swarms.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [OxiGen: Oxidation-State-Aware Crystal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08296)
*Dylan John,Kim E. Jelfs,Alex M. Ganose,Eleonora Giunchiglia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 OxiGen，一种在生成过程中显式表示氧化态（oxidation states）的晶体扩散模型（crystal diffusion model），通过结构化输出层与有限状态自动机（finite-state automaton）精确推断来构造性地保证全局电荷中性，从而提升生成晶体的氧化态保真度与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型（generative models）有望通过逆向设计（inverse design）加速无机材料（inorganic materials）发现，但生成实验可实现的晶体仍具挑战性。氧化态常用于评估晶体成分有效性并指导材料发现，然而现有晶体生成模型虽能生成电荷中性（charge-neutral）的氧化态分配，却难以复现合成材料中观察到的氧化态分布。

Method: 提出 OxiGen，一种氧化态感知（oxidation-state-aware）的晶体扩散模型，在生成过程中显式表示氧化态。该模型通过带有结构化输出层（structured output layer）的设计，在有限状态自动机上执行精确推断（exact inference），从而在构造上强制满足全局电荷中性（global charge neutrality）。

Result: 经验上，OxiGen 显著提升了氧化态保真度（oxidation-state fidelity），在所评估方法中生成了最高比例的稳定、独特且新颖（stable, unique, and novel）的晶体，并在性质条件化（property conditioning）下仍保持较高的成分有效性（compositional validity）。

Conclusion: 显式建模氧化态并构造性保证电荷中性，可有效改善晶体生成模型对真实合成材料氧化态分布的复现能力，为无机材料逆向设计提供更可靠的生成方法。

Abstract: Generative models have the potential to accelerate inorganic materials discovery by enabling inverse design, but generating experimentally realisable crystals remains challenging. Oxidation states are widely used to assess the compositional validity of crystals and guide inorganic materials discovery. While existing generative models for crystals can generate materials with charge-neutral oxidation-state assignments, they poorly reproduce the distributions of oxidation states observed in synthesised materials. To address this limitation, we propose OxiGen, an oxidation-state-aware crystal diffusion model that explicitly represents oxidation states during generation. OxiGen enforces global charge neutrality by construction using a structured output layer with exact inference over a finite-state automaton. Empirically, OxiGen substantially improves oxidation-state fidelity, generates the highest rate of stable, unique, and novel crystals among evaluated methods, and maintains high compositional validity even under property conditioning.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Deep learning enables large-scale inverse design of free-form metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05021)
*Viktor A. Lilja,Khosro Zangeneh Kamali,Mikael Käll,Philippe Tassin*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种物理定制的深度学习代理模型（deep-learning surrogate model），利用电磁相互作用的局域性（locality）与对称性，实现从小尺度仿真到大面积超表面（metasurface）的泛化，从而将逆设计（inverse design）的规模从电磁仿真尺度中解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超表面（metasurface）虽具备超越传统光学的紧凑设计自由度，但逆设计需反复进行电磁仿真（electromagnetic simulation），计算成本过高，导致其大面积全自由度的设计能力长期受限。

Method: 构建物理定制的深度学习代理模型，利用电磁相互作用的局域性与对称性，从少量小尺度仿真泛化到数量级更大的超表面；该模型能捕捉非局域相互作用（nonlocal interactions），并支持任意光照条件下的梯度拓扑优化（gradient-based topology optimization）。

Result: 该模型预测光场的速度比其训练所用的传统电磁求解器（electromagnetic solver）快四个数量级以上；实验验证包括衍射角达 75° 的自由形式超光栅（free-form metagratings），以及宽度超过 1,000 个波长、含 16 亿自由度的毫米级自由形式全息超表面（holographic metasurface）。

Conclusion: 通过将电磁仿真的尺度与逆设计的尺度解耦，该方法可实现保留纳米级设计自由度的系统级自由形式光子器件（system-scale free-form photonic devices）。

Abstract: Metasurfaces are ultrathin optical elements that control light through carefully engineered structures smaller than the wavelength of light, enabling compact devices with functionalities that are difficult to achieve with conventional optics. However, exploiting their full design freedom over large areas has been prohibited by the large computational cost of repeated electromagnetic simulations required for inverse design. Here we introduce a physics-tailored deep-learning surrogate model that exploits the locality and symmetries of electromagnetic interactions to generalize from small-scale simulations to metasurfaces orders of magnitude larger. The model captures nonlocal interactions while predicting optical fields more than four orders of magnitude faster than the conventional electromagnetic solver it is trained on, enabling gradient-based topology optimization under arbitrary illumination. We validate the approach through the inverse design and experimental realization of free-form metagratings with diffraction angles up to 75 degrees, as well as a millimeter-scale free-form holographic metasurface more than 1,000 wavelengths across and comprising 1.6 billion degrees of freedom. By decoupling the scale of electromagnetic simulation from the scale of inverse design, our approach enables system-scale free-form photonic devices that retain nanoscale design freedom.

</details>


### [185] [Crossover of magnetodipole and electrodipole contributions in the broadband magneto-optics of bismuth-substituted iron garnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07595)
*Nika Gribova*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究建立了一个宏观电动力学框架，用于分离铋取代铁石榴石（bismuth-substituted iron garnet）中磁偶极（magnetodipole, MD）与电偶极（electrodipole, ED）对磁光效应（magneto-optical effects）的贡献。研究发现MD机制在太赫兹（terahertz, THz）间隙中占主导，并在中红外至近红外（MIR–NIR）区域存在MD与ED贡献相等的谱交叉点，进而揭示界面相移导致透射与反射几何下机制层级反转的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究普遍假设磁性贡献在红外频率下一致消失，且常忽略色散磁导率张量（dispersive magnetic permeability tensor）的作用。本文旨在通过严格纳入该张量，厘清MD与ED机制在不同频段及不同几何构型中的相对权重，纠正上述既有认知。

Method: 采用宏观电动力学（macroscopic electrodynamic）框架，严格引入色散磁导率张量，对铋取代铁石榴石中MD与ED对磁光效应的贡献进行分离分析。同时考察界面相移（interfacial phase shifts）对两种机制在可观测旋转（rotation）与椭偏（ellipticity）中投影的影响，并对比透射（法拉第，Faraday）与反射（极向与纵向克尔，polar and longitudinal Kerr）几何。

Result: MD机制在THz间隙中占主导；在MIR至NIR区间出现MD与ED贡献幅度精确相等的谱交叉点，否定了磁性贡献在红外频率一致消失的假设。界面相移独特地决定两种机制在透射与反射几何中的投影，产生反转的谱层级。s偏振光的横向克尔效应（transverse Kerr effect）被证明仅由非对角磁导率（off-diagonal magnetic permeability）驱动，在传统ED近似下严格为零，但在THz与MIR–NIR区间成为不受旋电（gyroelectric）贡献影响的MD活性探针，其THz响应比p偏振对应物大两个数量级。

Conclusion: MD机制在THz及MIR–NIR频段具有不可忽略的作用，且红外频率下磁性贡献并非一致消失。s偏振横向克尔效应可作为探测MD活性的稳健探针，为磁光效应中磁性贡献的实验辨识提供新途径。

Abstract: This work presents a comprehensive macroscopic electrodynamic framework isolating the magnetodipole (MD) and electrodipole (ED) contributions to the magneto-optical effects in bismuth-substituted iron garnet. By rigorously incorporating the dispersive magnetic permeability tensor, the dominance of the MD mechanism in the terahertz (THz) gap is demonstrated. In the mid-infrared (MIR) to near-infrared (NIR) domain, a fundamental spectral crossover is revealed where the magnitudes of the MD and ED contributions exactly equalize, invalidating the widespread assumption that magnetic contributions vanish uniformly at infrared frequencies. Furthermore, it is shown that interfacial phase shifts uniquely dictate how these competing mechanisms project onto observable rotation and ellipticity, yielding an inverted spectral hierarchy between transmission (Faraday) and reflection (polar and longitudinal Kerr) geometries. Most notably, the transverse Kerr effect for s-polarized light is established to be exclusively driven by the off-diagonal magnetic permeability. While strictly zero under the conventional ED approximation, the s-polarized transverse Kerr effect emerges as a robust, free of the gyroelectric contribution probe of MD activity in the THz and MIR--NIR regimes, yielding a THz response two orders of magnitude larger than its p-polarized counterpart.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [The Kähler-Einstein metric on the third Del Pezzo surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32655)
*Timothy Buttsworth,William Hadden,Elli Heyes,Daniel Platt,Toby Wiseman*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文针对四维紧致流形 $\mathbb{CP}_2\# 3\overline{\mathbb{CP}_2}$ 上存在但无闭式表达的环面 Kähler-Einstein 度量（toric Kähler-Einstein metric），结合解析与计算机辅助技术构造显式近似 Einstein 度量，并证明其与真实度量的接近程度，进而给出若干几何应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该流形虽已知容许环面 Kähler-Einstein 度量，但该度量缺乏闭式（closed form）表达，导致难以对其几何性质作出推论。因此有必要获得可显式处理的近似度量并量化其与真实度量的偏差。

Method: 采用解析技巧与计算机辅助（computer-assisted）方法相结合的策略，构造一个显式的近似 Einstein 度量 $g$；同时证明真实 Einstein 度量 $g_{\text{KE}}$ 与 $g$ 接近，并显式给出接近程度与拓扑的描述。

Result: 得到了显式描述的近似 Einstein 度量 $g$，并建立了 $g_{\text{KE}}$ 与 $g$ 之间接近性的严格结论（接近程度及拓扑均被显式刻画）。作为应用，证明了 Laplace-Beltrami 算子第一不变特征值的界，并证明该 Kähler-Einstein 度量并非处处具有正全纯截面曲率（positive holomorphic sectional curvature）。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在缺乏闭式度量的情形下，借助解析与计算机辅助手段可获得可用的近似 Einstein 度量及其与真实度量的误差控制，从而支持对该 Kähler-Einstein 度量几何性质（如特征值界与全纯截面曲率符号）的判定。

Abstract: The compact four-dimensional manifold $\mathbb{CP}_2\# 3\overline{\mathbb{CP}_2}$ is known to admit a toric Kähler-Einstein metric $g_{\text{KE}}$, but the metric is not known in closed form, which makes it difficult to draw conclusions about its geometry. In this article, we use a combination of analytic and computer-assisted techniques to produce an approximate Einstein metric $g$ described explicitly herein, and also prove that the true Einstein metric $g_{\text{KE}}$ is close to $g$, where both the closeness and the topology are described explicitly. As an application, we prove bounds on the first invariant eigenvalue of the Laplace-Beltrami operator, and prove that this Kähler-Einstein metric does not have positive holomorphic sectional curvature everywhere.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Universal critical $g$ factor for spin-1/2 Aharonov-Bohm bound states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08521)
*Vinícius Salem,Edilberto O. Silva,Fabiano M. Andrade*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作澄清了自旋-1/2 Aharonov-Bohm 哈密顿量是否存在束缚态的长期争议：Hagen 的无束缚态结论对应于奇异 Zeeman 相互作用自伴扩张族中的特定成员，而微观物理在 g=2 时恰好选择该成员。由此确认并锐化 Hagen 结论：束缚态的产生由反常磁矩决定，g_c=2 为普适临界值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-1/2 Aharonov-Bohm 哈密顿量能否支持束缚态，自 Hagen 得出“Dirac 粒子不存在此类态”以来长期存在争议。该研究旨在厘清争议根源，判定 Hagen 结论究竟针对扩张族中的哪个成员，并确定物理上被选中的是哪一个。

Method: 将 Hagen 解识别为描述奇异 Zeeman 相互作用（singular Zeeman interaction）的单参数自伴扩张族（one-parameter family of self-adjoint extensions）中的特定成员，并判断微观物理所选成员。通过将扩张参数与有限半径磁通管（finite-radius flux tube）匹配，在 g=2 处得到 ν=0，并检验其是否对所有磁通扇区成立。

Result: 匹配给出 ν=0 精确出现在 g=2，且在每个磁通扇区（flux sector）均成立，故临界 g 因子为普适值 g_c=2，不依赖于磁通扇区或正则化半径。磁通扇区控制束缚态能量深度，所得闭式表达式显示束缚随磁通整数部分显著加深。反常磁矩是奇异通道中产生束缚的充要条件。

Conclusion: Hagen 的结论得到确认并被锐化：它并非关于扩张族中任意成员的陈述，而是关于当磁矩取 Dirac 值时物理所选成员的陈述。g_c=2 具有普适性，与磁通扇区和正则化半径无关；束缚态的深浅由磁通扇区决定。

Abstract: Whether the spin-1/2 Aharonov-Bohm Hamiltonian supports bound states has remained controversial since Hagen concluded that no such states exist for a Dirac particle. Here, we show that Hagen's solution corresponds to a particular member of the one-parameter family of self-adjoint extensions that describe the singular Zeeman interaction, and we identify which member the microscopic physics selects. Matching the extension parameter to a finite-radius flux tube yields $ν=0$ precisely at $g=2$, and it does so in every flux sector. The critical $g$ factor is therefore universal, $g_c=2$, and depends on neither the flux sector nor the regularization radius. The flux sector controls the depth of the bound state energy, and we obtain a closed-form expression showing that binding deepens markedly with the integer part of the flux. Hagen's conclusion is thus confirmed and sharpened: it is not a statement about one arbitrary member of a family of extensions, but about the member that the physics selects when the magnetic moment takes its Dirac value, and an anomalous moment is a necessary and sufficient condition for binding in the singular channel.

</details>


### [188] [White paper: 1-10 Hz matter-wave interferometer to test the spin entanglement witness for quantum gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07422)
*Sougato Bose,Anupam Mazumdar,Marko Toroš,Tian Zhou,Tadeusz Adach,Niayesh Afshordi,Agya Sewara Alam,Alexandre Arbey,Navdeep Arya,Simon Baier,Peter F. Barker,Angelo Bassi,Ettore Bernardi,Lorenzo Braccini,Robert Brandenberger,Daniel Braun,Guri K. Buza,Luigi Cacciapuoti,Carlo Cepollaro,Lin-Qing Chen,Yanbei Chen,Ralph Jason Costales,Marion Cromb,Álvaro de la Cruz-Dombriz,Catalina Curceanu,Shubhang Dadhich,Debarshi Das,Saurya Das,Pratika Dayal,Subhadeep De,Ema Dimastrogiovanni,Lajos Diósi,Or Dobkowski,Kemal Döner,Brian D'Urso,Gurudev Dutt,Shafaq Gulzar Elahi,Samira Elghaayda,Matteo Fadel,Samuel Fedida,Omer Feldman,Fabiano Feleppa,Ron Folman,Joshua Foo,Paolo Fragolino,Laurent Freidel,Giulio Gasbarri,Marco Genovese,Andrew Geraci,Menachem Givon,Cisco Gooding,Jonathan M. H. Gosling,Piotr T. Grochowski,David Groswasser,Mustafa Gündoğan,Ekim Taylan Hanımeli,Bas Hensen,Dipankar Home,Richard Howl,Yonathan Japha,Maciej T. Jarema,Rainer Kaltenbaek,Adrian Kent,Eva Kilian-Rademacher,M. S. Kim,Jarosław K. Korbicz,Timothy Kovachy,Samuel Kováčik,Ohkyung Kwon,Gaetano Lambiase,Naor Levi,Iarley P. Lobo,Leon Loveridge,Adrian Lupascu,Paolo Luppi,Marta Maria Marchese,Antonino Marcianò,Aaron G. Markowitz,Chiara Marletto,Ryan J Marshman,J. D. D. Martin,Florian Millo,Gavin W. Paris,Mauro Paternostro,Alessandro Pesci,Luciano Petruzziello,Fabrizio Piacentini,Tanmay Kumar Poddar,Sofia Qvarfort,Markus Rademacher,Dennis Rätzel,Anna Chiara Rescigno,Ryan Rizaldy,Albert Roura,Carlos Sabín,Barry C. Sanders,Martine Schut,Helen M Sheehy,Suprit Singh,Aninda Sinha,Urbasi Sinha,Peter Skakunenko,Michael E Tobar,Géza Tóth,Hendrik Ulbricht,Gislaine Varão,Vlatko Vedral,Vincent Vennin,Francesca Vidotto,Giuseppe Filiberto Vitale,Marko Vojinović,Chenan Wei,Qian Xiang,Magdalena Zych*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本白皮书强调 1–10 Hz 频段对基于量子引力诱导质量纠缠（QGEM）协议的引力量子性实验室检验至关重要，其核心瓶颈在于低频环境噪声，与爱因斯坦望远镜（ET）和宇宙探测器（CE）的低频目标高度重合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QGEM 需要大质量物质波干涉仪在微米级间距下维持空间叠加与相干性，这使低频环境噪声成为核心实验挑战，因此有必要明确其频率窗口及噪声需求。

Method: 该工作为白皮书，通过给出代表性参数（m=10⁻¹⁴ kg、Δx=10 μm、τ=1 s）下的噪声量级要求，分析 QGEM 对相对加速度噪声（RAN）与引力梯度噪声（GGN）的敏感性，并与 ET 和 CE 的低频目标进行类比。

Result: 在代表性参数下，差分加速度噪声幅度谱密度需被抑制到远低于 10⁻¹⁵ m·s⁻²/√Hz 的水平，以将加速度引起的退相干控制在相关实验尺度以下。

Conclusion: 实现该量级的低频噪声抑制是 QGEM 的关键要求，其面对的 seismic 与引力梯度噪声挑战与 ET、CE 所应对的问题密切对应。

Abstract: In this white paper, we highlight the importance of the ($1-10~{\rm Hz}$) frequency range for laboratory tests of the quantum nature of gravity using the quantum gravity-induced entanglement of masses (QGEM) protocol. QGEM requires matter-wave interferometers with masses ($m\sim10^{-15}-10^{-14}~{\rm kg}$), brought within separations ($d\sim30-50~μ{\rm m}$), while maintaining spatial superpositions of ($1-20~μ{\rm m}$) and coherence for ($τ\sim 0.1 - 1~{\rm s}$). These requirements make low-frequency environmental noise a central experimental challenge and place QGEM in a regime closely related to the low-frequency goals of the Einstein Telescope (ET) and the Cosmic Explorer (CE). In particular, QGEM is sensitive to relative acceleration noise (RAN) and to gravity-gradient noise (GGN) generated by seismic and other environmental mass-density fluctuations. For representative parameters $m=10^{-14}~{\rm kg}$, $Δx=10~μ{\rm m}$, and $τ=1~{\rm s}$, the differential acceleration-noise amplitude spectral density must be suppressed well below the $10^{-15}~{\rm m\,s^{-2}/\sqrt{Hz}}$ level to keep acceleration-induced dephasing below the relevant experimental scale. Achieving this level of low-frequency noise suppression is therefore a key requirement for QGEM and closely parallels the seismic and gravity-gradient noise challenges that ET and CE address.

</details>


### [189] [Autonomous Quantum Memories from Classical Stochastic Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05589)
*Nadav Drechsler,Yuval Oreg,Dorit Aharonov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究建立了基于 CSS 码的量子记忆（quantum memory）与经典记忆之间的等价关系，通过综合征型 Lindbladian 与综合征型概率元胞自动机（SPCA）两种框架，并据此首次严格证明带 Toom 规则的 4D toric code 在局部 Pauli 噪声下构成量子记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机来自两方面：容错量子计算机需要能抵御现实噪声的记忆，以及自主多体系统（autonomous many-body systems）中量子记忆何时能涌现这一基础问题。

Method: 在异步（asynchronous）设定下，识别出一类综合征型 Lindbladian，使量子记忆问题等价于连续时间经典马尔可夫动力学（Markov dynamics）中的记忆；在同步（synchronous）设定下，引入综合征型概率元胞自动机（SPCA），并建立其经典记忆与局部量子信道下 CSS 量子记忆的一一对应。

Result: 首次严格证明带 Toom 规则的 4D toric code 在局部 Pauli 噪声低于常数阈值时为量子记忆，且同时适用于同步 PCA 型量子动力学与 Floquet Lindbladian；并恢复已知结论，如 4D toric code 在热噪声下是量子记忆。

Conclusion: 该框架将经典与量子记忆联系起来，阐明全自主量子记忆的条件，并可能指导低维可证明自主量子记忆的构造；SPCA 中的经典记忆可在 Floquet Lindbladian 下提升为量子记忆，而能否在时间无关 Lindbladian 下实现仍为开放问题。

Abstract: The study of quantum memories out of thermal equilibrium is motivated both by the need for memories that withstand realistic noise in fault-tolerant quantum computers and by the fundamental question of when quantum memory can emerge in autonomous many-body systems. We establish a general equivalence between CSS-based quantum memories subject to Pauli noise and classical memories via two associated probabilistic processes. In the asynchronous setting, where the quantum dynamics is generated by a Lindbladian, we identify a broad class of syndrome-based Lindbladians for which the quantum memory problem is equivalent to memory in continuous-time classical Markov dynamics. In the synchronous setting, we introduce syndrome-based probabilistic cellular automata (SPCAs) and establish a one-to-one correspondence between their classical memories and CSS-based quantum memories under local quantum channels. This framework provides a systematic route for deriving and constructing quantum memories from classical probabilistic processes, and recovers known results, including the fact that the 4D toric code is a quantum memory in the presence of thermal noise.
  As an application, we give the first rigorous proof that the 4D toric code with Toom's rule is a quantum memory in the presence of local Pauli noise, provided the error is below a constant threshold. We prove this both for the corresponding synchronous, PCA-type quantum dynamics and for a Floquet Lindbladian. More generally, we show that classical memory in an SPCA can be lifted to a quantum memory under Floquet Lindbladian dynamics; whether this can be done with a time-independent Lindbladian remains open. Our results provide a general framework for relating classical and quantum memory, shed light on the conditions for fully autonomous quantum memories, and may guide the construction of provable autonomous quantum memories in low dimensions.

</details>


### [190] [Kardar-Parisi-Zhang superdiffusion in chaotic quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07143)
*Rustem Sharipov,Urban Duh,Sun Woo P. Kim,Friedrich Hübner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作构造出一类满足完整 KPZ 普适类的混沌砖砌量子线路，其输运具有 z=3/2 的超扩散行为；预言由非线性涨落流体力学给出，并被张量网络模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 KPZ 超扩散多在精细调节的可积模型中实现，尚不清楚量子系统中该现象是否可在混沌、非可积情形下普遍出现。本文旨在突破这一限制，构建具有完整 KPZ 普适类的混沌量子线路。

Method: 利用与非均匀守恒密度相关联、对化学势（chemical potential）有非平庸依赖的平衡电流（equilibrium current），结合非线性涨落流体力学（nonlinear fluctuating hydrodynamics）从微观平衡性质推导传播速度、展宽尺度与电荷关联函数的普适标度形式；并用张量网络（tensor-network）模拟两格点自旋-1（qutrit）与三格点自旋-½（qubit）线路。

Result: 两格点 qutrit 线路的模拟复现了无自由参数的预言，包括完整的稳态 KPZ 剖面（profile）。三格点 qubit 线路中输运随化学势由扩散转为超扩散，但长寿命相干准粒子（coherent quasiparticles）延缓了剖面收敛至 KPZ 标度形式。

Conclusion: 结果表明，量子系统中的 KPZ 超扩散并不局限于精细调节的可积模型（integrable models），而可在混沌线路中自然实现。

Abstract: We construct a family of chaotic brickwork quantum circuits exhibiting superdiffusive transport with dynamical exponent $z=3/2$ in the full Kardar--Parisi--Zhang (KPZ) universality class. This is enabled by the equilibrium current associated with a inhomogeneous conserved density having a nontrivial dependence on the chemical potential. Using nonlinear fluctuating hydrodynamics, we derive the propagation velocity, broadening scale, and universal scaling form of the charge correlation function from microscopic equilibrium properties. Tensor-network simulations of a two-site spin-$1$ (qutrit) circuit reproduce these parameter-free predictions, including the full stationary KPZ profile. We further construct a three-site spin-$\frac12$ (qubit) circuit, where transport varies from diffusive to superdiffusive with the chemical potential, while long-lived coherent quasiparticles delay the convergence of the profile to the KPZ scaling form. Our results establish that KPZ superdiffusion in quantum systems is not restricted to fine-tuned integrable models.

</details>


### [191] [Decoupling Spectral Gaps from Entanglement Complexity in Quantum Annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07144)
*Roopayan Ghosh,Sougato Bose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作证明绝热量子退火（adiabatic quantum annealing）中能隙极小与纠缠复杂性并非必然关联，二者可独立变化，并可出现反转配对，从而说明纠缠复杂度不足以刻画退火难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指数小的谱隙（spectral gap）被视为绝热量子退火的核心障碍，但其是否必然要求相应复杂的纠缠（entanglement）尚不明确，本文旨在厘清能隙标度与纠缠复杂度之间的关系。

Method: 通过对比常规一阶与连续相变（first-order/continuous transitions）中能隙与纠缠的配对情形，并构造反转案例：交错场反铁磁 Ising 链（staggered-field antiferromagnetic Ising chain）与镜像孪生流形模型（mirror-twin manifold model）；同时推导将 Schmidt 秩（Schmidt rank）与汉明空间几何（Hamming-space geometry）相联系的界。

Result: 常规一阶相变同时具有指数小能隙与有界 O(1) 二分 Schmidt 秩（临界态仍保留全局多体关联）；连续相变则能隙多项式闭合而纠缠对数增长。反转情形亦成立：交错场反铁磁 Ising 链虽 Schmidt 秩有界但能隙多项式闭合；镜像孪生流形模型的能隙指数闭合而纠缠熵随系统尺寸对数增长。流形密度与连通性（manifold density and connectivity）被识别为决定配对方式的关键要素。

Conclusion: 纠缠复杂度本身无法刻画退火难度，需寻找能够捕捉希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）中态演化几何的量子资源，这指向更广泛的搜索方向。

Abstract: Exponentially small spectral gaps are the central obstacle to adiabatic quantum annealing, yet whether such bottlenecks demand correspondingly complex entanglement remains unclear. We show that gap scaling and entanglement complexity need not be linked. Conventional first-order transitions combine exponentially small gaps with bounded $O(1)$ bipartite Schmidt rank, even though the critical states retain global multipartite correlations, while continuous transitions combine polynomially closing gaps with logarithmically growing entanglement. We show that each pairing can also be inverted: in a known first-order transition in a staggered-field antiferromagnetic Ising chain the gap closes polynomially despite bounded Schmidt rank, while we construct a mirror-twin manifold model whose gap closes exponentially even as its entanglement entropy grows logarithmically with system size. We derive bounds connecting Schmidt rank to Hamming-space geometry, and identify manifold density and connectivity as the ingredients that determine which combination occurs. These results show that entanglement complexity alone cannot characterize annealing difficulty, and point toward a broader search for quantum resources that capture the geometry of state evolution in Hilbert space.

</details>


### [192] [Connectivity Controls Variational Accessibility in Symmetry-Preserving Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07173)
*Sahinur Reja*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作表明，哈密顿量启发式的最近邻（NN）保对称性（SP）量子线路会生成受限的动力学李代数（DLA），无法覆盖完整多体对称性扇区；仅需极少量的生成元连接修改（XXZ 加一个次近邻单激发连接，Hubbard 加一个关联双激发），即可重构完整正交 DLA，使 VQE 基态保真度接近 1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为变分量子本征求解器（VQE）设计兼具表达能力与对称性约束的量子线路，是模拟相互作用多体系统的核心难题。即便线路保持对称性，也未必能访问完整的多体对称性扇区，作者因此要显式检验这一潜在缺陷。

Method: 以自旋-1/2 的 XXZ 与 Hubbard 模型为对象，使用受哈密顿量启发的最近邻保对称性线路，分析其生成的动力学李代数（DLA）结构；并通过少量修改生成元连接（XXZ 增加一个 SP 次近邻单激发连接，Hubbard 增加一个 SP 关联双激发）来重构对称性扇区内的完整正交 DLA。

Result: 受限的动力学李代数流形在自由费米子极限附近（XXZ 的 J_z=0、Hubbard 的 U=0）能准确表示基态，但一旦引入相互作用，便暴露出不可访问的多体方向，即使线路深度很大也严重限制 VQE 性能。相反的，最小生成元连接修改可重构完整 DLA，扩大切空间并提升对降能哈密顿量方向的可达性，从而在 VQE 模拟中获得接近单位值的基态保真度。

Conclusion: 结果建立了生成元连接性与变分可达性之间的直接联系，并提出更广的线路设计原则：应识别能扩大可访问多体空间的最小保对称性生成元，而非单纯增加线路深度。该视角对凝聚态物理与量子计算交叉领域的量子多体模拟可能具有更广的相关性。

Abstract: Designing expressive quantum circuits for the variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) is a central challenge in simulating interacting many-body systems. Even symmetry-preserving (SP) circuits need not access the full many-body symmetry sector. We demonstrate this explicitly for the spin-$1/2$ XXZ and Hubbard models, where Hamiltonian-inspired nearest-neighbor (NN) SP circuits generate restricted dynamical Lie algebras (DLAs). While these restricted manifolds can accurately represent the ground state near the free-fermion limits, $J_z=0$ for XXZ and $U=0$ for Hubbard, interactions expose inaccessible many-body directions and severely limit VQE performance even at large circuit depth. Remarkably, a minimal modification of the generator connectivity can reconstruct the full orthogonal DLA within all relevant symmetry sectors studied in this work. For XXZ model, the addition of {\it only} one SP next-to-NN single-excitation connection triggers this dramatic reconstruction, whereas the Hubbard case requires a SP correlated double-excitation;--showing that the relevant connectivity can extend from the spatial lattice to many-body configuration space. This reconstruction enlarges the tangent space and accessibility of the energy-lowering Hamiltonian direction, leading to near-unit ground-state fidelity in VQE simulations. Our results establish a direct link between generator connectivity and variational accessibility and suggest a broader circuit-design principle: identify minimal symmetry-preserving generators that enlarge the accessible many-body space rather than increasing circuit depth alone. This perspective may have broader relevance for quantum many-body simulation at the interface of condensed-matter physics and quantum computing.

</details>


### [193] [A Single Conformal Coordinate Decodes Outcome-Resolved Measurement-Induced Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07995)
*Reyhaneh Khasseh,M. A. Rajabpour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在临界量子链中，对多体态做局域测量后，其普适的、按测量结果分辨的 Rényi 熵（outcome-resolved Rényi entropy）渐近地由从测量记录中提取的单个紧致共形坐标（compact conformal coordinate）所决定，从而将指数多的测量结果压缩为单一共形自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域测量会产生指数多个微观结果，每个结果对应一个不同的条件量子态；核心问题是：要从单个测量结果中提取多少信息，才能预测两个未测量区域之间剩余的纠缠。

Method: 引入“按结果分辨的 Rényi 熵”作为观测量，并从测量记录中提取单个紧致共形坐标作为解码器；用紧致玻色子理论（compact-boson theory）解释压缩机制，在自由费米子 XX 点借助精确格点恒等式揭示微观结构，并在相互作用 XXZ 链中进行数值计算。

Result: 普适熵对所有空间变化的边界剖面分量不敏感，仅保留单个相对的紧致零模（relative compact zero mode）；自由费米子 XX 点的精确格点恒等式给出了解码器的微观结构，大规模逐结果计算验证了不同子区域几何与 Rényi 指标下的标度预测；相互作用 XXZ 链的数值计算表明同一微观坐标无需依赖相互作用的重新调参即可继续解码条件熵。

Conclusion: 对该普适观测量而言，指数大的测量结果空间被约化为单个共形自由度，揭示了一种涌现的 Luttinger 液体（Luttinger-liquid）机制，用于解释按结果分辨的测量后纠缠。

Abstract: A local measurement of a critical many-body state produces exponentially many microscopic outcomes, each defining a different conditioned quantum state. We ask how much information from one such outcome is actually needed to predict the entanglement that remains between two unmeasured regions. We show that, in critical quantum chains, the universal outcome-resolved Rényi entropy is asymptotically determined by a single compact conformal coordinate extracted directly from the measurement record. Compact-boson theory explains this compression: the universal entropy is insensitive to all spatially varying components of the boundary profile, retaining only a single relative compact zero mode. At the free-fermion XX point, exact lattice identities expose the microscopic structure underlying the decoder, while large-scale outcome-by-outcome calculations confirm its scaling prediction across different subsystem geometries and Rényi indices. Numerical calculations in interacting XXZ chains further show that the same microscopic coordinate continues to decode the conditional entropy without interaction-dependent retuning. Thus an exponentially large space of measurement outcomes is reduced, for this universal observable, to a single conformal degree of freedom, revealing an emergent Luttinger-liquid mechanism for outcome-resolved postmeasurement entanglement.

</details>


### [194] [Entanglement manipulation and magic area-laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08727)
*Rafael A. Macedo,Rafael Chaves*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于近期提出的量子态分类，指出服从面积律（area law）的量子态可分为纠缠主导态与魔法主导态两类，前者对非稳定子性（non-stabilizerness，又称"magic"）满足面积律且纠缠操控高效，后者子系统非稳定子性增长快于面积律且纠缠操控需要非高效算法；并据此提出"稳定子区间"（stabilizer regime）这一受限量子相概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域相互作用的量子多体系统基态通常表现出纠缠的面积律增长，而非稳定子性（non-stabilizerness）则被认为呈体积律（volume-law）标度，从而指示内禀计算复杂性。本文旨在厘清面积律态内部在纠缠与非稳定子性这两种资源上的差异与结构。

Method: 作者沿用近期的一种量子态分类框架，对服从面积律的基态进行划分，并比较两类态在子系统非稳定子性标度行为与纠缠操控（entanglement manipulation）算法效率上的区别，进而形式化地引入"稳定子区间"（stabilizer regime）概念。

Result: 面积律态被划分为两个不同类别：纠缠主导态（entanglement-dominated states）对非稳定子性满足面积律，并存在高效的纠缠操控量子算法；魔法主导态（magic-dominated states）则表现出子系统非稳定子性的快于面积律（faster-than-area）增长，并在纠缠操控任务中需要非高效算法。

Conclusion: 在纠缠与非稳定子性两种资源的框架下，面积律态并非单一相；"稳定子区间"（stabilizer regime）被界定为一种受限量子相，其同时允许高效纠缠操控，并对魔法与纠缠二者均成立面积律。

Abstract: Physical many-body quantum systems typically interact locally, giving rise to ground states where entanglement grows with the area of the region being probed. Beyond entanglement, non-stabilizerness (or "magic") has been established as another crucial resource linked to quantum complexity and the properties of error-correcting states. Unlike entanglement, non-stabilizerness typically behaves with a volume-law scaling in ground states, indicating inherent computational complexity. In this work, we show that under a recent classification of quantum states, area-law states split into two distinct classes. Entanglement-dominated states obey an area-law for non-stabilizerness and admit efficient quantum algorithms for entanglement manipulation. Conversely, magic-dominated states exhibit a a faster-than-area growth of subsystem non-stabilizerness and require non-efficient algorithms for entanglement manipulation tasks. Within this context, we introduce the concept of the stabilizer regime, a restricted quantum phase that admits efficient entanglement manipulation and hosts area laws for both magic and entanglement.

</details>


### [195] [Observing the magic Mpemba effect in localized dynamics on a digital quantum computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08769)
*Han-Ze Li,Xianquan Yan,Yi-Rui Zhang,Jian-Xin Zhong,Shuo Liu,Ching Hua Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了一种由相互作用诱导退相位（interaction-induced dephasing）驱动的“量子魔力姆潘巴效应”（magic Mpemba effect）：初始魔力量较少的状态可反超初始魔力量较多者，并更早达到其渐近饱和值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 姆潘巴效应（Mpemba effect）挑战了“越接近平衡态则弛豫越快”的直觉。作者将这一问题推广至量子魔力（quantum magic，即 nonstabilizerness）这一通用量子计算的关键资源的生成过程，探讨是否会出现类似的“魔法排序反转”。

Method: 在相互作用的 $\ell$-比特模型（interacting $\ell$-bit model）中，对倾斜乘积态（tilted product states）进行随机相位分析（random-phase analysis）以预测魔力排序反转；并对 $\ell$-比特模型与局域自旋链（localized spin chains）进行精确模拟。同时在 IBM 超导量子处理器（IBM superconducting quantum processors）上开展实验，其中包括将时间编码于门角度（gate angles）的有效 $\ell$-比特实现，以及微观自旋模型。

Result: 随机相位分析预测魔力排序出现反转：初始魔力较少的状态反而达到更高的饱和魔力，且与其空间模式无关。精确模拟显示这些状态也更早饱和。上述两个特征均在 IBM 超导量子处理器上被观测到。微观自旋模型在局域化区域（localized regime）对任意初始模式普遍呈现该反转，而在遍历区域（ergodic regime）仅选择性地出现。

Conclusion: 研究确立了相互作用诱导退相位作为一条通往反常量子资源弛豫（anomalous quantum-resource relaxation）的路径，其机制不依赖输运（transport）或传统热化（thermalization）。

Abstract: The Mpemba effect challenges the intuition that states closer to equilibrium must relax faster. We ask whether an analogous magic-ordering reversal can occur in the generation of quantum magic (nonstabilizerness), a key resource for universal quantum computation: can a state with less initial magic overtake one with more and reach its asymptotic value sooner? Here, we uncover interaction-induced dephasing as a distinct mechanism for this magic Mpemba effect, requiring neither transport nor conventional thermalization. For tilted product states in an interacting $\ell$-bit model, a random-phase analysis predicts a reversal of the magic ordering: states with less initial magic attain higher saturated magic, independent of their spatial pattern. Exact simulations of the $\ell$-bit model and localized spin chains further show that these states also saturate earlier. We observe both features on IBM superconducting quantum processors. An effective $\ell$-bit implementation accesses long dephasing times at fixed circuit depth by encoding time in gate angles, while a microscopic spin model exhibits the reversal universally across initial patterns in the localized regime but only selectively in the ergodic regime. These results establish interaction-induced dephasing as a route to anomalous quantum-resource relaxation beyond transport-driven thermalizing dynamics.

</details>

### [196] [Characteristics of extinction behavior of ammonia partial cracking simulated fuels in counterflow premixed flames under lean condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07605)
*Jinseong Kim,Hyunchang Lee,Keeman Lee,Assaad R. Masri*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该研究对比了贫燃条件下甲烷（CH4）与16%裂解氨替代燃料（CR16）在对冲预混火焰（counterflow premixed flames）中的熄火行为，发现CR16具有更高的熄火应变率与更强的抗应变熄火能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在阐明经济上更有利的低裂解比氨燃料（low-cracking-ratio ammonia fuel）具备优异抗熄火性能的化学基础。

Method: 采用实验与基于OPPDIF的一维数值模拟相结合的方法；CH4采用GRI-Mech 3.0机理，CR16采用UCSD机理。

Result: 贫燃条件下CR16的熄火应变率与抗应变吹熄韧性（RSIB）均高于CH4；在Phi = 0.66时二者熄火应变率相同，但CR16因火焰时间更长而RSIB更高。

Conclusion: CR16在接近熄火时，通过火焰间区（interflame region）向驻定面（stagnation plane）维持H2消耗、自由基反应与放热，其氢氧化的维持与NH2 + NO、HNO等含氮反应路径耦合。

Abstract: In this study, the lean extinction behaviors of CH4 and a 16% cracked ammonia surrogate fuel, CR16, in counterflow premixed flames were compared. Experiments were conducted along with one-dimensional simulations using OPPDIF; the GRI-Mech 3.0 and UCSD mechanisms were applied to CH4 and CR16, respectively. CR16 exhibited higher extinction strain rates and resilience to strain-induced blow-out (RSIB) than those of CH4 under lean conditions. At Phi = 0.66, although both flames had the same extinction strain rate, CR16 showed a higher RSIB because of its longer flame time. Near its extinction, CR16 sustained H2 consumption, radical reactions, and heat release through the interflame region toward the stagnation plane. Hydrogen oxidation coupled with nitrogen-related pathways, including NH2 + NO and HNO reactions, supported this behavior. These results clarify the chemical basis for the superior extinction resistance of economically favorable low-cracking-ratio ammonia fuel.

</details>

### [197] [Neural networks as decision trees: an analytical solution for learning and neural selectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08228)
*Hugo Tissot,Jonas Ranft,Yves Boubenec*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个针对分段线性（piecewise-linear）前馈与循环神经网络的分析框架，将学习过程、激活区域结构与神经选择性（neural selectivity）联系起来。核心结论是：在梯度对齐学习的任何驻点，非线性网络可分解为各激活区域上的局部线性回归，并呈现决策树式的任务分解结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性神经网络能形成结构化的内部表征（internal representations），但其几何结构如何由所学任务决定仍缺乏理解。该工作旨在建立学习、激活区域结构与神经选择性之间的解析联系。

Method: 构建面向分段线性前馈与循环网络的分析框架；在梯度对齐学习的任意驻点处，将网络分解为激活区域上的局部线性回归，并分析其与对应最小二乘解（least-squares solutions）的偏差；进一步给出决策树（decision-tree）解释，并通过拟合树从输入预测网络激活模式来数值验证。

Result: 偏离最小二乘解的误差受跨区域共享的网络权重联合约束，并在低误差区间消失，从而得到任务的近似分段最小二乘分解。由此推导出神经元活跃区域决定其捕获的任务统计量，进而把神经选择性组织为不同亚群（subpopulations）。预测结果与模拟网络及两个实证神经数据集中的选择性几何高度吻合。

Conclusion: 神经基线（neural baseline）调节激活模式多样性，使网络处于从粗粒度、泛化性表征到细粒度、表达性表征的连续谱上。激活区域由此成为统一框架，用于描述非线性网络如何将任务结构分解为局部计算，以及这些计算如何通过神经活动显现。

Abstract: Nonlinear neural networks develop structured internal representations, yet how their geometry is determined by the tasks being learned remains poorly understood. Here, we develop an analytical framework for piecewise-linear feedforward and recurrent networks that links learning, activation-region structure, and neural selectivity. We show that, at any stationary point of gradient-aligned learning, a nonlinear network decomposes into local linear regressions over its activation regions. Deviations from the corresponding least-squares solutions are jointly constrained by network weights shared across regions and vanish in the low-error regime, yielding an approximate piecewise least-squares decomposition of the task. This structure admits a decision-tree interpretation, which we recover numerically by fitting trees to predict network activation patterns from the input. We further derive how the regions in which neurons are active determine the task statistics they capture, thereby organizing neural selectivity into distinct subpopulations. The resulting predictions closely match the selectivity geometry observed in simulated networks and two empirical neural datasets. Finally, we show that neural baseline regulates activation pattern diversity, placing networks along a continuum between coarse, generalizing representations and fine-grained, expressive representations. Together, these results establish activation regions as a unifying framework for describing how nonlinear networks decompose task structure into local computations and how those computations can be revealed through neural activity.

</details>

### [198] [Frequency-Dependent Breakdown of Litz-Wire Insulation Under Repetitive Bipolar Square-Wave Voltages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07012)
*Md Asifur Rahman,Farzana Islam,Mona Ghassemi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该研究测试了 14 AWG 与 16 AWG 利兹线（Litz wire）绝缘在快速边沿双极性方波电压下的介电击穿行为，频率范围 1–30 kHz。结果显示 14 AWG 平均击穿电压下降约 10.7%，16 AWG 下降约 20.0%，且 Weibull 形状参数随频率非单调变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽禁带（wide-bandgap, WBG）功率变换器产生的快速边沿双极性方波电压会加剧利兹线绝缘的电气应力，但针对具体线规构造的高频击穿数据仍显不足。本研究旨在为高频电力电子应用中的利兹线绝缘评估提供按构造区分的击穿特性数据。

Method: 采用受控高压脉冲发生器（上升时间 200 ns），在双绞线（twisted-pair）配置下对 14 AWG 与 16 AWG 两种线规进行测试，频率覆盖 1 kHz 至 30 kHz。所得击穿电压按 IEEE Std 930-2004 使用加权两参数 Weibull 方法（weighted two-parameter Weibull method）进行统计分析。

Result: 在 1 至 30 kHz 范围内，14 AWG 构造的平均击穿电压下降约 10.7%，16 AWG 下降约 20.0%。14 AWG 样品在 1 至 5 kHz 间略有上升随后下降，而 16 AWG 的平均值在整个测试频段内持续下降；Weibull 形状参数（shape parameter）随频率呈非单调变化，反映击穿电压离散程度的差异。

Conclusion: 研究给出了与线规构造相关的利兹线绝缘击穿数据，可用于高频电力电子应用中的绝缘评估。文中讨论了电荷积累（charge accumulation）、局部放电活动（localized discharge activity）与介电加热（dielectric heating）的可能贡献，但本研究中未对这些机制进行直接验证。

Abstract: This paper investigates the dielectric breakdown behavior of Litz wire insulation subjected to repetitive fast-edge bipolar square-wave voltages representative of wide-bandgap (WBG) power converter stress. Two conductor sizes, 14 AWG and 16 AWG, were tested in a twisted-pair configuration over frequencies ranging from 1 kHz to 30 kHz using a controlled high-voltage pulse generator with 200 ns rise time. The breakdown voltages obtained at each frequency were statistically analyzed using the weighted two-parameter Weibull method in accordance with IEEE Std 930-2004. Between 1 and 30 kHz, the mean breakdown voltage decreases by approximately 10.7% for the 14 AWG construction and 20.0% for the 16 AWG construction. The 14 AWG specimens exhibit a modest increase between 1 and 5 kHz followed by a decline, whereas the 16 AWG mean decreases across the tested frequencies. The Weibull shape parameter varies nonmonotonically with frequency, characterizing differences in the dispersion of the measured breakdown voltages. These findings provide construction-specific breakdown data relevant to Litz-wire insulation assessment in high-frequency power electronic applications. Possible contributions from charge accumulation, localized discharge activity, and dielectric heating are discussed, but were not directly verified in this study.

</details>
